# Chapter 55 — The definition of Meru and other sixteen modes of mansions

### Verse 55.1

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादानामतो लक्ष्म षोडशानां विशेषत:।
ज्येष्ठमध्यकनिष्ठानां यथावदभिदध्महे।।१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādānāmato lakṣma ṣoḍaśānāṃ viśeṣataḥ ।
jyeṣṭhamadhyakaniṣṭhānāṃ yathāvadabhidadhmahe ॥1॥

**English Translation:**

Of sixteen Prāsādas (mansions) a definition in particular larger, middling, smaller, we shall dilate upon.

---

### Verse 55.2

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विभज्यते यथा येन प्राग्श्रोर्ध्व विधीयते(?)।
यावद् यस्य प्रमाणं तु तथा तत् तस्य कथ्यते।।२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vibhajyate yathā yena prāgśrordhva vidhīyate(?) ।
yāvad yasya pramāṇaṃ tu tathā tat tasya kathyate ॥2॥

**English Translation:**

The way is divided and by whom is reckoned the front, centre and height as such, and the way whose extent becomes tenable that very way, a dilation is being made as such.

---

### Verse 55.3

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेरु: प्रासादराजश्च कैलासश्च हरप्रिय:।
सर्वतोभद्रकश्चैव विमानच्छदनदनौ।।३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

meruḥ prāsādarājaśca kailāsaśca harapriyaḥ ।
sarvatobhadrakaścaiva vimānacchadanadanau ॥3॥

**English Translation:**

Meru is the king of mansions Kailāsa and Harapriya, Sarvatobhadra, Vimānacchada and Nandana.

---

### Verse 55.4

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्वस्तिको मुक्तकोण्श्च श्रीवत्सो हंससंज्ञित:।
रुचको वर्धमानश्च गरुडश्च गजस्तथा।।४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

svastiko muktakoṇaśca śrīvatso haṃsasaṃjñitaḥ ।
rucako vardhamānaśca garuḍaśca gajastathā ॥4॥

**English Translation:**

Svastika, Muktakoṇa, Śrīvatsa and Haṃsa named as such. Rucaka and Vardhamāna Garuḍa and Gaja.

---

### Verse 55.5

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मृगराजश्च पद्मश्च वलभी चेति ते स्मृता:।
न त्रयस्त्रिंशतोऽधस्तान्नापि पञ्चाशत: परा।।५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mṛgarājaśca padmaśca valabhī ceti te smṛtāḥ ।
na trayastriṃśato'dhastānnāpi pañcāśataḥ parā ॥5॥

**English Translation:**

Mṛgarāja and Padma, Valabhī - they are known as such. Neither lesser than thirty three nor even beyond fifty.

---

### Verse 55.6

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

संख्या भवति हस्तानां मेरोरिति पुराविद:।
विभज्य दशधा क्षेत्रं शृङ्गं कुर्याद् द्विभागिकम्।।६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

saṃkhyā bhavati hastānāṃ meroḥ iti purāvidaḥ ।
vibhajya daśadhā kṣetraṃ śṛṅgaṃ kuryād dvibhāgikam ॥6॥

**English Translation:**

The counting becomes of hastas i.e. cubits of Meru this way postulate the Ancient experts. Having divided into ten parts the plot, the peak may one create two parted one.

---

### Verse 55.7

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कृत्वा षड्भागिकं मध्यं निर्गमस्तत्र दीयते।
भागस्य षोडशांशेन विधेयं सलिलान्तरम्।।७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kṛtvā ṣaḍbhāgikaṃ madhyaṃ nirgamastatra dīyate ।
bhāgasya ṣoḍaśāṃśena vidheyaṃ salilāntaram ॥7॥

**English Translation:**

Having created a six digited middle portion an outlet is offered therein. By a sixteenth digit of the part the water outlet deserves to be created.

---

### Verse 55.8

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदै: षोडशिभर्गभे विधेया चास्य विस्तृति:।
प्रासादभित्ति(:)पदिका पदिवस्तृतमन्तरम्(?)।।८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

padaiḥ ṣoḍaśibhargabhe vidheyā cāsya vistṛtiḥ ।
prāsādabhitti(ḥ)padikā padivastṛtamantaram(?) ॥8॥

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

The translation at the top of the page ("By sixteen steps in the centre deserves to be created the breadth of this one, the wall of the Prāsāda being quarter normed one having an intermediary space of a breadth of one square.") belongs to a verse beginning on the previous page.

---

### Verse 55.9

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदिका बाह्यभित्ति: स्यादित्येष स तदा स्मृत:।
द्विपदो वेदिकाबन्धो जङ्घा पञ्चपदोदया।।९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

padikā bāhyabhittiḥ syādityeṣa sa tadā smṛtaḥ ।
dvipado vedikābandho jaṅghā pañcapadodayā ।।9।।

**English Translation:**

The outer wall may be square normed. This way that stands remembered; two squared the ligation of Vedikā (the pedestal) and the shanks of five squared height.

---

### Verse 55.10

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदार्धेन पदार्धेन मेखलान्तरपत्रके।
शृङ्गोच्छ्रितिस्त्रिभिर्भागैर्नवभि: शिखरोच्छ्रिति:।।१०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

padārdhena padārdhena mekhalāntarapatrake ।
śṛṅgocchrititribhirbhāgairnavabhiḥ śikharocchritiḥ ।।10।।

**English Translation:**

By half a square each, with the pentad of the space of the girdle zone, the height of the top by three digits and height of the pinnacle by nine digits.

---

### Verse 55.11

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शिखरस्यास्य कर्तव्यास्तज्ज्ञै: षोडश भूमिका:।
स्कन्धोऽशैविस्तृत: षड्भिरंशेनोच्छ्रितमण्डकम्।।११।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śikharasya asya kartavyāstajjñaiḥ ṣoḍaśa bhūmikāḥ ।
skandho'śaivistṛtaḥ ṣaḍbhiraṃśenocchritamaṇḍakam ।।11।।

**English Translation:**

The tops of this one deserve to be created by the experts having sixteen storeys on the top flanks by six digits the breadth and the elevated circumference may be by a single square.

---

### Verse 55.12

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवा वंशोच्छ्रिता कार्या शिखरस्यादिविस्तृतौ।
षड्गुणेनैव सूत्रेण वेणुकोशं समालिखेत्।।१२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvā vaṃśocchritā kāryā śikharasya ādivistarṛtau ।
ṣaḍguṇenaiva sūtreṇa veṇukośaṃ samālikhet ।।12।।

**English Translation:**

The neck deserves to be created equal in height to the diagonal, of the roof within the frontal breadth and with a tape having six fold length one may carve out the bamboo beam.

---

### Verse 55.13

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तृतेरपि भद्राया: कुर्याद् द्विगुणमुच्छ्रयम्।
कुम्भं भागेन कुर्वीत प्रासादेष्वखिलेष्वपि।।१३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistṛterapi bhadrāyāḥ kuryād dviguṇamucchrāyam ।
kumbhaṃ bhāgena kurvīta prāsādeṣvakhileṣvapi ।।13।।

**English Translation:**

Twice as much as of the breadth of the Bhadrā one may create the height and one may carve out a pitcher like dome on all the mansions.

---

### Verse 55.14

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एवमेष चतु:शृङ्गश्चतुर्द्वारोपशोभित:।
मेरुर्मेरूपम: कार्यो वाञ्छता शुभमात्मन:।।१४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

evameṣa catuḥśṛṅgaścaturdvāropaśobhitaḥ ।
merurmeropamaḥ kāryo vāñcchatā śubhamātmanaḥ ।।14।।

**English Translation:**

This way this one having four roofs and equipped with four doors, Meru (mansion) having parity of Meru (mountain) deserves to be created as such, by one cherishing the well being of the self.

---

### Verse 55.15

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वस्वर्णमयं मेरुं यद् दत्त्वा पुण्यमानुयात्।
तमिष्टकाशैलमयं कृत्वा तद्धिकं भजेत्।।१५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvasvarṇamayaṃ meruṃ yad dattvā puṇyamānuyāt ।
tamiṣṭakāśailamayaṃ kṛtvā taddhikaṃ bhajet ।।15।।

**English Translation:**

Having offered Meru made of gold all over, whosoever may attain to merit to that having created as made of brick all over one may adhere to more elegantly.

---

[FLAG: The page begins mid-verse with only "मेरु:॥" visible as a label/conclusion to a verse that started on the previous page. Verse 16 appears to begin with the first full Devanagari line visible.]

### Verse 55.16

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे सप्तविंशतिपाणिकम्।
दशधा विभजेत् स स्यात् कैलासः पुण्यवर्धनः॥१६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre saptaviṃśatipāṇikam।
daśadhā vibhajet sa syāt kailāsaḥ puṇyavardhanah॥16॥

**English Translation:**

Within a field or plot square shaped as such, of twenty seven cubits when one may adhere to ten fold as such, that may be declared as Kailāsa an augmentor of merits.

---

### Verse 55.17

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ब्रह्मकोष्ठगतो गर्भः शेषं भित्त्यधकारिका।
चतुर्भागं भवेद् भद्रं मूलकर्णौ त्रिभागिकौ॥१७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

brahmakoṣṭhagato garbhaḥ śeṣaṃ bhittyadhakārikā।
caturbhāgaṃ bhaved bhadraṃ mūlakarṇau tribhāgikau॥17॥

**English Translation:**

The interior gone within the Brahmakoṣṭha with remaining one as dusky walled one. The four partitioned one may be Bhadra and the root diagonals as three partitioned ones.

---

### Verse 55.18

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सप्तभागोच्छिता जङ्घा मेखला चार्धभागिका।
भागेनान्तरपत्रं स्याद् भागेनाण्डकमुच्छितम्॥१८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

saptabhāgocchitā jaṅghā mekhalā cārdhabhāgikā।
bhāgenāntarapatraṃ syād bhāgenāṇḍakamucchitam॥18॥

**English Translation:**

The shanked part having elevation of seven digits and the middling part of half a partition may be there with the internal pattern being of one part and the elevated globe of one part.

---

### Verse 55.19

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवाग्रभागमुत्सेधाच्छिखरं दशकोच्छृतम्।
अर्धषष्ठांशविस्तारः स्कन्धः कैलाससंज्ञितेः॥१९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvāgrabhāgamutsedhācchikharaṃ daśakoccḥṛtam।
ardhaaṣaṣṭhāṃśavistāraḥ skandhaḥ kailāsasaṃjñite॥19॥

[FLAG: The last word may be कैलाससंज्ञिते or कैलाससंज्ञितेः — visarga uncertain; reading as कैलाससंज्ञिते without visarga based on visual inspection.]

**English Translation:**

In the one named Kailāsa the Skandha (the trunk normed part) or sidereal part may have breadth of half of the hexagonal part having height of a ten (cubit norm) of the roof as compared with the elevation and having the front part of the neck as such.

---

### Verse 55.20

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अस्मिन्नन्तरपत्रे तु सूत्रं दत्त्वा सुताडितम्।
त्रिगुणेन लिखेत् तेन वेणुकोशं मनोरमम्॥२०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

asminnantarapatre tu sūtraṃ dattvā sutāḍitam।
triguṇena likhet tena veṇukośaṃ manoramam॥20॥

**English Translation:**

Within the internal flank however, having introduced a tape well grooved by a threefold norm of that one, one may carve out the bamboo beam.

---

### Verse 55.21

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अष्टभूम्युच्छयः श्रीमान् मञ्जर्या च विराजितः।
उच्छ्रयश्शास्य कर्तव्यो द्विगुणः श्रियमिच्छता॥२१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

aṣṭabhūmyuccayaḥ śrīmān mañjaryā ca virājitaḥ।
ucchrayayaśśāsya kartavyo dviguṇaḥ śriyamicchatā॥21॥

[FLAG: उच्छ्रयश्शास्य — sandhi form uncertain; may be उच्छ्रयश् + शास्य; IAST rendered as ucchrayayaśśāsya tentatively. Visually reads उच्छ्रयश्शास्य.]

**English Translation:**

Eight storeyed in height endowed with glory and graced by Mañjarī a spray, the height of this one deserves to be carved out double fold by one cherishing glory for himself.

---

### Verse 55.22

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कार्यं षड्भूमिकं चास्य भद्रं भागार्धनिर्गतम्।
पाकः सिंहकर्णस्यु:(?) समाप्तिर्नायकेन च॥२२॥
कैलास एष कथितो विशेषेण हरिप्रियः।
कैलासः॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kāryaṃ ṣaḍbhūmikaṃ cāsya bhadraṃ bhāgārdhanirgatam।
pākaḥ siṃhakarṇasyu:(?) samāptirāyakena ca॥22॥
kailāsa eṣa kathito viśeṣeṇa haripriyaḥ।
kailāsaḥ॥

[FLAG: सिंहकर्णस्यु:(?) — the (?) mark appears in the original printed text; retained as-is. The word may be सिंहकर्णस्युः or similar; marked uncertain in source.]

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: No English translation is printed for verse 22 on this page — the text moves directly to verse 23.]

---

### Verse 55.23

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इदानीं सर्वतोभद्रः प्रासादः परिकीर्त्यते॥२३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

idānīṃ sarvotabhadraḥ prāsādaḥ parikīrtyate॥23॥

[FLAG: सर्वतोभद्रः — IAST: sarvotabhadraḥ; the sandhi of सर्वतस् + भद्र gives sarvotabhadra.]

**English Translation:**

### Verse 55.24

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स स्यात् षड्विंशतिं हस्तान् परम: परिमाणत:।
जठरं बाह्यसीमा च भित्तयो ह्यन्धकारिका:।।२४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sa syāt ṣaḍviṃśatiṃ hastān parama: parimāṇataḥ |
jaṭharaṃ bāhyasīmā ca bhittayo hyandha kārikāḥ ||24||

**English Translation:**

that one the larger one may be twenty six cubits in measurement, the interior and the outer boundaries and walls and Andhakārikās i.e. blind alleys.¹

> **Footnote 1:** Alley - उद्यानपथ:। वीथि - वीथी: दुर्गम मार्ग:। संवाधपथ:। Eng. Skt. Dict., V.S. Apte, p. 11. Aṇḍa-EOHA, P.K. Ācārya, p.16 means Cupola.

---

### Verse 55.25

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घोत्सेधश्च कर्णौ च यथा मेरोस्तथा स्मृता:।
तथैव भद्रविस्तारै: कार्यो भागार्धनिर्गत:।।२५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghotsedhаśca karṇau ca yathā merostathrā smṛtāḥ |
tathaiva bhadravistāraiḥ kāryo bhāgārdhanirghataḥ ||25||

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: जङ्घोत्सेधश्च — second word; स्मृता: — final ā/āḥ; भागार्धनिर्गत: — compound boundary]

**English Translation:**

The elevation of the shanks or side portions and the two corners the way they are of Meru (typed) they stand that very way remembered as such. That very way by the breadth of the bhadras it deserves to be carved out as having projection of a half a part.

---

### Verse 55.26

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रथिकैका चतुर्भागा तत: सार्धद्विभागिका।
तासां परस्परं ज्ञेयो भागभागं विधीयते(?)।।२६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

rathikaikā caturbhāgā tataḥ sārdhadivibhāgikā |
tāsāṃ parasparaṃ jñeyo bhāgabhāgaṃ vidhīyate(?) ||26||

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: सार्धद्विभागिका — सार्ध + द्वि + भागिका]

**English Translation:**

A lane of four digits and also half plus two parted one, (meant as such). Their mutual link deserves to be known and is stipulated as one part of a part.

---

### Verse 55.27

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्भागात् विस्तृतं कार्यं शिखरं सप्तमोच्छितम्।
षड्भिर्दशभिर्भागै: स्यान्मूलजा स्कन्धविस्तृति:(?)।।२७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍbhāgāt vistṛtaṃ kāryaṃ śikharaṃ saptamocchitam |
ṣaḍbhirdaśabhirbhāgaiḥ syānmūlajā skandhavistṛtiḥ(?) ||27||

**English Translation:**

Broad by six digits deserves to be created the seventh floor of height as such and the breadth of the flanked parts erected from the base, dimensioned by six or ten digits.

---

### Verse 55.28

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवार्धभागमुत्सेधादण्डकं भागमुच्छितम्।
मूलसूत्रानुसारेण च्छेद: संयुज्यते यथा।।२८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvārdhabhāgamutsedhādaṇḍakaṃ bhāgamucchitam |
mūlasūtrānusāreṇa cchedaḥ saṃyujyate yathā ||28||

**English Translation:**

A dome having a height of one digit and the elevation having half a digit of the neck. As per suitability of the basement plumb the way a truncation may become tenable.

---

### Verse 55.29

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अस्य रेखा तथा कार्या सर्वश्रेय:प्रसाधनी।
मेरोरस्य च शृङ्गाणि सिंहकर्णैर्विभूषयेत्।।२९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

asya rekhā tathā kāryā sarvaśreyaḥprasādhanī |
merorасya ca śṛṅgāṇi siṃhakarṇairvibhūṣayet ||29||

**English Translation:**

The lining of this one deserves to be created as accomplisher of all the beneficences. The roofs of this one and Meru one may decorate with lion corniced flanks.

---

### Verse 55.30

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मञ्जरीं पद्मकोशाग्रतुल्यां सर्वत्र कारयेत्।
जयं लक्ष्मीं यश: कीर्तिं सर्वानि(णी)ष्टफलानि च।।३०।।
करोति सर्वतो भद्रं सर्वतोभद्रक: कृत:।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mañjarīṃ padmakoṣāgratūlyāṃ sarvatra kārayet |
jayaṃ lakṣmīṃ yaśaḥ kīrtiṃ sarvāni(ṇī)ṣṭaphalāni ca ||30||
karoti sarvato bhadraṃ sarvato bhadrakaḥ kṛtaḥ |

### Verse 55.31

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वतोभद्र:।।
चतुर्श्रीकृते क्षेत्रे विभागशतभाजिते।।३ १।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvatobhadraḥ ।।
caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre vibhāgaśatabhājite ।।3 1।।

[FLAG: The first pāda "सर्वतोभद्र:।।" appears to be the tail end of a verse continuing from the previous page. The verse number ३१ belongs to the complete verse.]

**English Translation:**

Everywhere Mañjarī¹ or a spray similar to the tip of a lotus bud, one may create everywhere victory, glory and wealth fame, and all the cherished ends endows with Sarvatobhadra designed as such being best of all norms. In a square carved plot divided into a hundred digits.

> **Footnote 1:** A parallel line or row, EOHA, p.421 by P.K.Ācārya.

---

### Verse 55.32

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमानं विभजेत् प्राज्ञ: श्रेय:पुष्टिसुखावहम्।
भद्रैश्चतुर्भिस्तत् कुर्यात् कर्णप्राग्रीवकैस्तथा।।३ २।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vimānaṃ vibhajet prājñaḥ śreyaḥpuṣṭisukhāvaham ।
bhadraiścaturbhistat kuryāt karṇaprāgrīvakaistatthā ।।3 2।।

**English Translation:**

A wise man may originate a vimāna bearing strength and beneficence with four Bhadras and may carve that out along with flanked entrance halls.

---

### Verse 55.33

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चभूमिर्भवत्येष यदि वा त्रिविधा भवेत्।
हस्तास्त्रिंशद् भवेज्ज्येष्ठो मध्यम: पञ्चविंशति:।।३ ३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcabhūmirbhavatyeṣa yadi vā trividhā bhavet ।
hastāstriṃśad bhavejjyeṣṭho madhyamaḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ ।।3 3।।

**English Translation:**

Five storeyed one this becomes as such or if having three moded norm? A thirty cubit normed may be the largest one and twenty five cubit normed the middling normed.

---

### Verse 55.34

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्यादेकविंशतिर्हस्तात् कनीयान् षोडशाथवा।
जातिशुद्धो भवेदेको मझ्यार्वैं परो भवेत्।।३ ४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

syādekāviṃśatirhastāt kanīyān ṣoḍaśāthavā ।
jātiśuddho bhavedeко [IAST UNCERTAIN: majhyārvai] paro bhavet ।।3 4।।

[FLAG: The third pāda reads "मझ्यार्वैं" — this is unclear in the image and may be "मञ्जर्या वै" or similar. Marked uncertain.]

**English Translation:**

The smaller one may be of twenty one cubits or sixteen as such. One may be of a purified norm in species and the other having the addition of a Mañjarī a spray or parallel line or row or a projection.

---

### Verse 55.35

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मिश्रकोऽन्यो विमानानामिति संख्या त्रिधोदिता।
ज्येष्ठो मिश्रकनिर्माण: स च कौलाशभद्रकृत्(?)।।३ ५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

miśrako'nyo vimānānāmiti saṃkhyā tridhoditā ।
jyeṣṭho miśrakanimāṇaḥ sa ca kaulāśabhadrakṛt(?) ।।3 5।।

**English Translation:**

Miśraka one the other among the vimānas - This way the numbering has been illustrated as threefold normed. The largest one having structure of a mixed norm and that one as Kailāśa² is the doer of good.

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE, verse 35, pāda 4 has कैलाशभद्रवृत in the fn. 1 it is given as कैलाश p. 310.

---

### Verse 55.36

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मध्यमो जातिशुद्ध: स्यात् कनीयान् मञ्जरीयुत:।
पञ्चभागयुतं भद्रं विस्तरेण प्रकीर्तितम्।।३ ६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

madhyamo jātiśuddhaḥ syāt kanīyān mañjarīyutaḥ ।
pañcabhāgayutaṃ bhadraṃ vistareṇa prakīrtitam ।।3 6।।

**English Translation:**

The middle ordered one may be pure by nature and the smaller one equipped with a Mañjarī (projection) or flower bud stalk or sprint parallel line or row. The Bhadra equipped with five parts has been illustrated in details.

---

### Verse 55.37

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णप्राग्रीविवितार: कर्तव्यो भागसंमित:।
भागार्धं क्षोभणे कार्यं तलिपं तझलान्तरं(?)।।३ ७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇaprāgrīvivistāraḥ kartavyo bhāgasaṃmitaḥ ।
bhāgārdhaṃ kṣobhaṇe kāryaṃ talipaṃ ta[IAST UNCERTAIN: jhalāntaraṃ](?) ।।3 7।।

[FLAG: The last word of verse 37 "तझलान्तरं(?)" is marked with (?) in the original, indicating uncertainty in the source text itself.]

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

The breadth of an entrance hall on the corner deserves to be created as equal in extent to a part and a part is to be carved out in Kṣobhaṇa¹ and Talipa² as Tajhalāntara.

---

### Verse 55.38

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गुप्तकर्णं तु कर्तव्यं यदिच्छेऽलक्षणान्वितम्।
तस्माद् भद्रस्य निष्कंशं(?) भागेनैकेन कारयेत्॥३८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

guptakarṇaṃ tu kartavyaṃ yadicche'lakṣaṇānvitam ।
tasmād bhadrasya niṣkaṃśaṃ(?) bhāgenaikena kārayet ॥38॥

**English Translation:**

A guptakarṇa deserves to be carved out the are the one may desire for carving out as endowed with specific marks. From that of Bhadra, Niṣkamśa one may carve out by a part alone.

> **Footnote 1:** Kṣobhaṇa may be synonym of Kṣepaṇa, EOHA p.47.
> **Footnote 2:** Talipa may be Talpaka, p.207 EOHA, an upper storey, a room on the top of a house, a turret, a tower.

---

### Verse 55.39

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मिश्रकस्य चतुर्भागं भद्रं कुर्याद् विचक्षण:।
पञ्चभागोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा खुरपिण्डकया सह॥३९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

miśrakasya caturbhāgaṃ bhadraṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
pañcabhāgocchrītā jaṅghā khurapīṇḍakayā saha ॥39॥

**English Translation:**

Of the Miśraka four portioned one, one may create a skilled one, a Jaṅghā of height equal to five parts or digits along with Khura-piṇḍakā.

---

### Verse 55.40

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभा(गा) रथिका कार्या भूमि: स्याच्चतुरंशका।
द्वितीयार्धांशहीना च तृतीया भूमिरिष्यते॥४०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhā(gā) rathikā kāryā bhūmiḥ syāccaturaṃśakā ।
dvitīyārdhāṃśahīnā ca tṛtīyā bhūmiriṣyate ॥40॥

**English Translation:**

Two portioned Rathikā (may be lane) deserves to be created and the ground may be four digited one. The second one may be reduced by a part pentad and the third one is desired as a ground floor.

---

### Verse 55.41

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्थे(र्थी) तु त्रिभिर्भागैरर्धहीना तु पञ्चमी।
उदयो भूमिकाया य: कूटं कुर्यात् तदर्धत:॥४१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturthe(rthī) tu tribhirbhāgairardha hīnā tu pañcamī ।
udayo bhūmikāyā yaḥ kūṭaṃ kuryāt tadardhatāḥ ॥41॥

**English Translation:**

The fourth one may be reduced by three digits and the fifth one reduced by half a digit. The elevation of the storey that may be the roof of that one may create by a half of that.

---

### Verse 55.42

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अर्धेन कुम्भिकां कुर्यादुच्छालकसमन्विताम्।
ऊर्ध्वभवस्तु(?) पञ्चम्या वेदिका भागमुच्छ्रिता॥४२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ardhena kumbhikāṃ kuryāduccḥālakasaṃnvitām ।
ūrdhvabhavastu(?) pañcamyā vedikā bhāgamucchrītā ॥42॥

**English Translation:**

By a half one may create Kumbhikā or the base of a column³ equipped with the dimension of the wall. Having upper structure by a fifth one, may be a terrace elevated by a part.

> **Footnote 3:** EOHA, p.138 (Kumbhaka).

---

### Verse 55.43

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

घण्टा षड्भागविस्तारा कार्या भागद्वयोच्छ्रिता।
घण्टोत्सेधं त्रिभिर्भागैर्विभजेत् तदनन्तरम्॥४३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ghaṇṭā ṣaḍbhāgavistārā kāryā bhāgadvayocchrītā ।
ghaṇṭotsedhāṃ tribhirbhāgairvibhajet tadanantaram ॥43॥

**English Translation:**

### Verse 55.44

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागिकानि प्रकुर्वीत कण्ठग्रीवाण्डकानि च।
भागं कण्ठप्रदेश: स्याद् दण्डकाया: समुच्छ्रिति:॥४४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgikāni prakurvīta kaṇṭhagrīvāṇḍakāni ca ।
bhāgaṃ kaṇṭhapradeśaḥ syād daṇḍakāyāḥ samucchrītiḥ ॥44॥

### Verse 55.45

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

घण्टार्धेन विधातव्या द्विभागा कलशोच्छ्रितिः।
सुरसेनादिकं सर्वं कर्तव्यं पूर्ववत् तथा॥४५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ghaṇṭārdhena vidhātavyā dvibhāgā kalaśocchrtiḥ।
surasenadikaṃ sarvaṃ kartavyaṃ pūrvavat tathā॥45॥

**English Translation:**

Deserves to be carved out by half of the exetnt of a ghaṇṭā (a clock tower) having two parted height of the domes. Sūrasenā and others all deserve to be carved out as per earlier innovation.

---

### Verse 55.46

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रं मनोरमैश्चेह सिंहकर्णैर्विभूषयेत्।
पञ्चव्यासेन सूत्रेण पद्मकोशं समालिखेत्॥४६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadraṃ manoramaiśceh siṃhakarṇairvibhūṣayet।
pañcavyāsena sūtreṇa padmakośaṃ samālikhet॥46॥

**English Translation:**

The Bhadra one may got decorated by attractive Siṃha Karṇas (lion corniced turrets). By a tape of five cubits broad, one may carve out Padmakośa.

---

### Verse 55.47

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

लतातयो(?)भवेदेषां लताभिस्तं प्रकल्पयेत्।
मिश्रको मिश्रितैरङ्गैः शुद्धः स्याद् भूमिकान्वितः॥४७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

latātayo(?)bhavedēṣāṃ latābhistaṃ prakalpayeṭ।
miśrako miśritairaṅgaiḥ śuddhaḥ syād bhūmikānvitaḥ॥47॥

**English Translation:**

Of these Latātayas may be there and those one may create by latās. The Miśraka one may be such as by mixed parts the pure one as equipped with bhūmikās or storeys (the Vimāna).

---

### Verse 55.48

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमान:।

नन्दनस्य भवेत् सीमा द्वात्रिंशद्धस्तनिर्मितः।
अष्टाष्टकविभागेन चतुःषष्टिपदो हि सः॥४८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vimānaḥ।

nandanasya bhavet sīmā dvātriṃśaddhastanirmitaḥ।
aṣṭāṣṭakavibhāgena catuḥṣaṣṭipado hi saḥ॥48॥

**English Translation:**

Of Nandana may be the limitation as thirty two cubits in structure¹. By the octagonal division that may be of 64 padas i.e. squares.

> **Footnote 1:** नन्दस्य भवेत् सीमा द्वात्रिंशद्धस्त निर्मितिः for द्वात्रिंशद्धस्तनिर्मितः: GOSE, p. 311, verse 48, pāda 2 (Nandana).

---

### Verse 55.49

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागैश्चतुर्भिर्गर्भोऽस्य शेषं भित्त्यन्धकारिका।
भद्रं गर्भसमं कार्यं तदूर्ध्वेनास्य निर्गमः॥४९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgaiścaturbhirgarbho'sya śeṣaṃ bhittyandha kārikā।
bhadraṃ garbhasamaṃ kāryaṃ tadūrdhvenāsya nirgamaḥ॥49॥

**English Translation:**

By four digits becomes the centre of this one and the remaining part may have the wall enclosure or a blind alley. Bhadra deserves to be created equal in extent to that of the centre and above that is its outlet or exit door.

---

### Verse 55.50

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वौ रथौ पार्श्वतो भूयः सर्वतः कर्णसूत्रतः।
पञ्चभागोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा मेखला भागसंमिता॥५०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvau rathau pārśvato bhūyaḥ sarvataḥ karṇasūtrataḥ।
pañcabhāgocchrṭā jaṅghā mekhalā bhāgasaṃmitā॥50॥

**English Translation:**

षड्भ(भू)मिरेष भूमिः स्यादेकैका द्वादशांशका।
रेखास्कन्धाण्डकादीनां कैलासे(न) समाकृतिः॥५१॥

ṣaḍbh(bhū)mireṣa bhūmiḥ syādekaikā dvādaśāṃśakā।
rekhāskandhāṇḍakādīnāṃ kailāse(na) samākṛtiḥ॥51॥

### Verse 55.52

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नन्दनो नन्दयत्येष समृद्धा(द्ध्या) हन्ति चापद:।
नन्दन:।
चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे पञ्चविं(शति) हस्तके।।५२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

nandano nandayatyeṣa samṛddhā(ddhyā) hanti cāpadaḥ ।
nandanaḥ ।
caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre pañcaviṃ(śati) hastake ॥52॥

**English Translation:**

This Nandana gladdens and obviates calamities by prosperous norm. In a plot rendered square shaped of twenty five cubits.

---

### Verse 55.53

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सूत्रपातं तत: कुर्यात् कर्मायतमुखायतम्(?)।
तत: सीमार्धसूत्रेण सम्यग् वृत्तं समालिखेत्।।५३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sūtrapātaṃ tataḥ kuryāt karmāyatamukhāyatam(?) ।
tataḥ sīmārdhasūtreṇa samyag vṛttaṃ samālikhet ॥53॥

**English Translation:**

The plumb lining or foundation laying then one may take to having length of the worked out frame and the frontal part.² Thereafter by the plumb line of a half measuring boundary one may carve out globular or circular.

> **Footnote 2:** कर्मायतमुखायतम्(?) is obscure reading textual corruption. GOSE, verse 53, pāda 2.

---

### Verse 55.54

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ततस्तदष्टाविंशत्या भजेद् भागैर्यथापदम्।
निर्मापये शालार्धेन (त् तदर्धेन शाला)दिक्सूत्रसंश्रिता:।।५४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tatastadaṣṭāviṃśatyā bhajed bhāgairyathāpadam ।
nirmāpaye śālārdhena (t tadārdhena śālā)diksūtrasaṃśritāḥ ॥54॥

**English Translation:**

Then that by twenty eight may work out by parts as per square norm. And may make to be constructed the Halls by a half of that recumbent upon the sidereal plumb.

---

### Verse 55.55

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तासां तु मध्यगा: कार्या एकैकस्य रथास्त्रय:।
अन्ये चार्धरथा: कार्या: शालाकर्णसमाश्रिता:।।५५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tāsāṃ tu madhyagāḥ kāryā ekaikasya rathāstrayaḥ ।
anye cārdharathāḥ kāryāḥ śālākarṇasamāśritāḥ ॥55॥

**English Translation:**

In the middle of these ones may be created of one each, a triad of Rathas or chariot shaped pedestals. The others should be created as recumbent upon the corners of the halls.

---

### Verse 55.56

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागषट्कोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा भागार्धेन तु मेखला:।
भागेनान्तरपत्रं स्याद् भागं चोद्वृत्तमण्डकम्।।५६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgaṣaṭkocchritā jaṅghā bhāgārdhena tu mekhalāḥ ।
bhāgenāntarapatraṃ syād bhāgaṃ codvṛttamaṇḍakam ॥56॥

**English Translation:**

The Jaṅghā may be of the height of six parts and the Mekhalās by a half part. By a part may be the intermediary plank and one part as of elevated globular dome.

---

### Verse 55.57

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अर्धभागोच्छ्रिता जीवा विष्कम्भेन चतुष्टदाम्(?)।
शिखरस्योच्छ्रयो भागैरेकादशभिरिष्यते।।५७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ardhabhāgocchritā jīvā viṣkambhena catuṣṭadām(?) ।
śikharasyocchhrayo bhāgairekaādaśabhiriṣyate ॥57॥

**English Translation:**

The Jīvā (an arched beam) may be elevated to a half extent having four squares by an enclosure. The height of the roof is considered by parts as eleven normed.

### Verse 55.58

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वेषामेव लतिना मात्रिधा द्विगुणो हि स:(?)।
विस्ताराद् द्विगुणं सूत्रं स्कन्धाद्यं चापि षड्गुणम्।।५८।।

### Verse 55.59

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सुत्तानितं(?) समाकृष्य पद्मकोशं समालिखेत्।
स पञ्चविंशतिहस्ता ज्येष्ठ: षोडश मध्यम:।।५९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

suttānitaṃ(?) samākṛṣya padmakośaṃ samālikhet।
sa pañcaviṃśatihastā jyeṣṭhaḥ ṣoḍaśa madhyamaḥ।।59।।

**English Translation:**

Having carved out or stretched out the Padmakośa well laid out, one may carve it out admirably. That may be of twenty five cubits the larger and the middling of sixteen cubits.

---

### Verse 55.60

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कनीयान् द्वादशकरच्छस्यो (?) विजानता।
ज्येष्ठस्य भागसंख्येयमेतदर्धेन मध्यम:।।६०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kanīyān dvādaśakaracchasyо (?) vijānatā।
jyeṣṭhasya bhāgasaṃkhyeyametadardhen madhyamaḥ।।60।।

**English Translation:**

The smaller one of twelve cubits becomes laudable by the expert. This is the counting of the components of the larger one and the middling one is by a half of that.

---

### Verse 55.61

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मध्यमस्य तथार्धेन कनीयान् भागसंख्यया।
भागषट्कमिता जङ्घा ज्येष्ठस्य परिकीर्तिता।।६१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

madhyamasya tathārdhena kanīyān bhāgasaṃkhyayā।
bhāgaṣaṭkamitā jaṅghā jyeṣṭhasya parikīrtitā।।61।।

**English Translation:**

Of the middling are that very way by a half and the smaller one by a part only. The Jaṅghā of the larger one is declared as measured to the extent of Hexagonal norm.

---

### Verse 55.62

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सप्तभागोच्छ्रिता सा स्यान्मध्यमे सकनीयसि।

**IAST Transliteration:**

saptabhāgocchrītā sā syānmadhyame sakanīyasi।
sarveṣāṃ latinām eṣa kṣetreṇa vidhirīritaḥ।।62।।

**English Translation:**

In the Madhyama (middling one) along with the smaller one, that may be such as having height of seven parts. Of the Latinas i.e. the one carved out in a chain, by the circumference a modicum has been ordained.

---

### Verse 55.63

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्वस्तिकोऽयं समाख्यात: स्वस्तिश्रेयस्करो नृणाम्।
स्वस्तिक:।।
इदानीं मुक्ताकोणस्य सू(ल)क्ष्म बूम: स तु त्रिधा।।६३।।

svastiko'yaṃ samākhyātaḥ svastriśreyaskaro nṛṇām।
svastikaḥ।।
idānīṃ muktākoṇasya sū(l)kṣma brūmaḥ sa tu tridhā।।63।।

[FLAG: "स्वस्तिक:।।" appears as a label/colophon mid-verse; "सू(ल)क्ष्म" contains an uncertain reading — the translator renders it as "illustrate the definition", suggesting सूक्ष्म with possible alternate reading सुलक्ष्म.]

**English Translation:**

Svastika this one has been illustrated may be beneficent for the well being of men (Svastika). Now of the Muktakoṇa we illustrate the definition. That is three normed one.

### Verse 55.64

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षोडश द्वादशाष्टौ च हस्तसंख्या परस्य च।

jyeṣṭhaḥ ṣoḍaśabhirbhāgairmadhyo dvādaśabhirbhavet।।64।।

**English Translation:**

Sixteen, twelve and eight cubits measurement is of that one and the distinct one. The larger one may be by sixteen parts and the middling one by twelve parts.

---

### Verse 55.65

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

**English Translation:**

The smaller one may be of eight parts the Prāsāda (named) Mukta koṇaka. Of Muktakoṇa and Svastika this may be the distinctness.

---


### Verse 55.66

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चभागोन्नता जङ्घा द्वौ भागौ रथिका भवेत्॥६६॥

**English Translation:**

Svastika there is of globular norm and the other one is square shaped spoken as such. The Jaṅghā is elevated by five parts. The blind alley may be of two parts.

---

### Verse 55.67

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागैश्चतुर्भि: कर्तव्या द्वितीया तस्य भूमिका।
येषास्तवर्धार्धभागेन विधेयास्त(द्व ?)स्य भूमिका:॥६७॥

bhāgaiścaturbhiḥ kartavyā dvitīyā tasya bhūmikā |
yeṣāstavardhārdhabhāgena vidheyāsta(dva ?)sya bhūmikāḥ ||67||

By four parts derives to be created the second storey of that one. The Yeṣās by a half part deserves to be created as the storeys of that.

---

### Verse 55.68

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विधाय नवधा गर्भेस्तैस्त्रयोदशभिर्भवेत्।

मुक्तकोण:।

vidhāya navadhā garbhestaisttrayodaśabhirbhavet |

Having rendered nine fold the central part that may be by thirteen parts, the Jaṅghā by five digits reduced by 3/4. The first one may be of a shop type (Muktakoṇa).

---

### Verse 55.69

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तारं दशधा कृत्वा षड्भागं मध्यमालिखेत्।

**English Translation:**

Having created the breadth as tenfold are may carve out the centre as six parted one. The corner may be two parted one and the middle part may divided into four parts.

---

### Verse 55.70

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तद्वन्मध्ये कृतौ द्व्यंशौ भागिकौ वामदक्षिणौ।

aṅgulīkarasaṃkhyātaividheyo rathinargamaḥ ||70||

That very way in the centre, created as such the two digited norms or two pronged one partitioned as left and right normed by the countings of Aṅgulis and hastas, the outlet of the lane is to be marked out.

---

### Verse 55.71

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

**English Translation:**

By the huge well exposed entrance halls and by the columns by nicely marked ornamental works. The merit endowed one Śrīvatsa may be the comfort giving.

---

### Verse 55.72

Two aṅgulas or three aṅgulas i.e. 1.5 inch and 2.25 inch¹ or even four aṅgulas = 3 inches a structure having water spaced inside deserves to be created in Śrīvatsa and Nandana or Śrīvatsa as the gladdening one.

sarvatobhadravan mūlakarṇāvasya dvibhāgikau॥73॥

By breadths divided into ten fold norm six digited Mañjarī (a spray shower) may be there. Like the Sarvatobhadra, of this one the basement flanks may be of two digited norm.

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हंसः॥

haṃsaḥ॥

The water space or the bathroom of this one may one create like that of Śrīvatsa. Haṃsa, this one is declared as the right one endowed with auspicious marks and a bestower of comforts.

---

### Verse 55.75

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रुचकोऽप्येवमेव स्यादुदकान्तरवर्जितात्(त)म्।

रुचकः॥

rucakaḥ॥

**English Translation:**

Rucaka even may also be of the same norm deprived of the water channels. The walls may be by a quarter of digits and the centre is of the extent of the half of the breadth appreciated as such.

### Verse 55.76

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे विभजेद् दशभिः पदैः।

viddhyārdhamānākhyaṃ tatra bhrāntamanukramāt॥76॥

**English Translation:**

In the plot square shaped as such, one may divide by ten steps; one may create one known as Ardhamāna may be Vardhamāna, therein the revolving one may be as per serial order.

---

### Verse 55.77

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रस्य च भवेद् भागैश्चतुर्भिः परिविस्तृतम्।
एकेनैकेन भागेन द्वौ रथौ वामदक्षिणौ॥७७॥

bhadrasya ca bhaved bhāgaiścaturbhiḥ parivistṛtam।
ekenaikena bhāgena dvau rathau vāmadakṣiṇau॥77॥

**English Translation:**

Of Bhadra the all round breadth may be by four parts and by a single individual part two chariot shaped pedestals may be on left out right.

---

### Verse 55.78

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभागविस्तृतौ कणौ निर्गमः स्यात् कराङ्गुलैः।
वर्धमानः क्रियायुक्तो यशोलक्ष्मीं विवर्धयेत्॥७८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhāgavistṛtau karṇau nirgamaḥ syāt karāṅgulaiḥ।

**English Translation:**

Two digited in breadth the flanks and the exit route may be by cubits and inches or of the measure of the aṅgulas of a hand, Vardhamāna endowed with activity may augment fame and wealth.

---

### Verse 55.79

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ya eko rocate teṣu nyaset taṃ garuḍe sudhīḥ॥79॥

**English Translation:**

Rucaka or Vardhamāna Śrīvatsa and Haṃsa likewise, which one among them singled

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

out may be of liking, the wise one may assort it on Garuḍa.

### Verse 55.80

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पक्षावेतस्य कर्तव्यौ प्रासादार्धविनिर्गमौ।
नासिकां वैनतेयस्य त्रिगभां कारयेदपि।।८०।।
गरुड:।।

nāsikāṃ vainateyasya trigabhāṃ kārayedapi।।80।।
garuḍaḥ।।

**English Translation:**

The two flanks of this one deserve to be created having exit gates in half of the building and may carve out the nāsikā (the beak) of Garuḍa having triple moded centres (Garuḍa).

---

### Verse 55.81

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतु:षष्टिपदे क्षेत्रे प्रासादं विभजेच्छुभम्।
क्षेत्रार्धेन च सूत्रेण पृष्ठतो वृत्तमालिखेत्।।८१।।

catuḥṣaṣṭipade kṣetre prāsādaṃ vibhajecchubham।
kṣetrārdhena ca sūtreṇa pṛṣṭhato vṛttamālikhet।।81।।

**English Translation:**

In an area of sixty four square yards one may divide a mansion an auspicious one and by a tape half in extent of the area, one may carve out a circle on the rear.

---

### Verse 55.82

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागैश्चतुर्भिर्जङ्घास्य मेखलाचार्धभागिका।

गज:।।

bhāgaiścaturbhirjaṅghāsya mekhalācārdhabāgikā।
puratak sūrasoyaṃ(?) pṛṣṭhañca gajākṛtiḥ।।82।।
gajaḥ।।

**English Translation:**

Its Jaṅgā may be by four parts and the mekhalā or slope of half an extent. In the front it may be one as named Sūkara¹ and from the rear having shape like that of an elephant (Gaja).

---

### Verse 55.83

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतु:षष्टिपद: सिंहो भद्रं भागचतुष्टयम्।

**English Translation:**

Siṃha is sixty four square yard (in area) and Bhadra having a tetrad of parts and the basement flanks may be of digit twain and the centre may be of sixteen squares.

---

### Verse 55.84

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तारार्धे भवेज्जङ्घा मेखला पदिका भवेत्।

**English Translation:**

The Jaṅghā may be half in breadth and the slope (Mekhalā) may be of a single squared one. A solitary rathikā (a chariot normed lane) may have the height of a triad of Bhāgas or squares.

---

### Verse 55.85

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वतोभद्रवच्चास्य रेखाग्रीवाण्डकादिकम्।

**English Translation:**

Like that of Sarvatobhadra it may have the neck globe and the like and by Bhadras covered all over by lions, the Prāsāda may be such as called by name Siṃha.

### Verse 55.86

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सिंह:।।

पद्यस्य हस्तसंख्या स्यात् षोडश द्वादशाथवा।।८६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vikramārjavaśīlānāṃ prāsādo'yaṃ śubhāvahaḥ।
siṃhaḥ।।

padyasya hastasaṃkhyā syāt ṣoḍaśa dvādaśāthavā।।86।।

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

### Verse 55.87

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्तुल: स च कर्तव्य: सूत्रं तु स्वस्तिके यथा।
सर्वे रथा: स्मृता: पद्मपत्राकृतिमनोरमा:।।८७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vartula: sa ca kartavya: sūtraṃ tu svastike yathā।
sarve rathā: smṛtā: padmapatrākṛtimanorāmā:।।87।।

**English Translation:**

Globular that one deserves to be created and its plumb lining of the same norm as that of Svastika. The Rathas are declared as fascinating like the shape of a lotus petal.

---

### Verse 55.88

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उदकान्तरकं कुर्याच्छ्रेयसे नन्दने यथा।
पद्मक:।।
स्वस्तिकस्य यथा पूर्वं कथितं मानलक्षणम्।।८८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

udakāntarakaṃ kuryācchreayase nandane yathā।
padmaka:।।
svastikasya yathā pūrvaṃ kathitaṃ mānalakṣaṇam।।88।।

**English Translation:**

This one may be created as having water courses as in a Nandana for the sake of beneficence (Padmaka). The measurement and definition is spoken of here as in case of Svastika.

---

### Verse 55.89

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तेनैव लक्षलित: सर्वो(?)विदधीत विचक्षण:।
यथामूलविभक्तस्तु लतिषु स्वस्तिकादिषु।।८९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tenaiva lakṣalita: sarvo(?)vidadhīta vicakṣaṇa:।
yathāmūlavibhaktastu latiṣu svastikādiṣu।।89।।

**English Translation:**

By that very one the Latikās (the creeper normed flanks) may be created by the expert one having the division the suitable plinths in Latis headed by Svastikas.

---

### Verse 55.90

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यथास्कधविभागोऽपि रेखामध्यविभागत:।
स्वस्तिकाङ्को विधातव्य: शुकनाशो(सो)च्छयाच्छुभ:।।९०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

yathāskadhavibhāgo'pi rekhāmadhyavibhāgata:।
svastikāṅko vidhātavya: śukanāśo(so)cchayācchubha:।।90।।

**English Translation:**

As per even the division of the Skandha as per division of the centre of the lining the mark Svastika deserves to be carved out auspicious like the elevation of the aquiline nose of a parrot.

---

### Verse 55.91

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादानां स भागै: स्यात् सप्तभिर्विहित: श्रिये।
विमाने स धरात्र्यंशन्यून: कार्यो विपश्चिता।।९१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādānāṃ sa bhāgai: syāt saptabhirvihita: śriye।
vimāne sa dharātryaṃśanyūna: kāryo vipaścitā।।91।।

**English Translation:**

By the seven storeys of the Prāsādas stands stipulated for glory in a Vimāna (Saptabhūmi Prāsāda), that may be reduced by three digits deserving to be created as such by the expert.

---

### Verse 55.92

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कैलासो च(साच्च)तुरंशोना विधेना(या) शुकनासिका।
सर्वतोभद्रसिंहाख्यौ मेरूणां तु विशेषत:।।९२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kailāso ca(sācca)turaṃśonā vidhenā(yā) śukanāsikā।
sarvatobhadrasiṃhākhyau merūṇāṃ tu viśeṣata:।।92।।

**English Translation:**

From Kailāsa reduced by four digits, deserves to be carved out the Śukanāsikā¹ (aquiline nose of a parrot like structure). Sārvatobhadra and Siṃha named as such, are the specific modes of Merus.

> **Footnote 1:** Śukanāsikā- a part of the finial (triangular part of an upper part of a ridged roof) looking like a parrot's nose, EOHA, p.540, P.K.Ācārya.

---


### Verse 55.93

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्भिर्भागैर्विना कार्या शुकनासा विजानता।
प्रासादोच्चेन सन्धारो विमानाद्या: प्रकीर्तिता:।।९३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍbhirbhāgairvinā kāryā śukanāsā vijānatā |
prāsādoccena sandhāro vimānādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||93||

**English Translation:**

Without six digits the Śukanāsā deserves to be carved out by the one knowing one. By the height of the mansion, the Sandhāra (the basement) as also the Vimānas and the like have been illustrated.

---

### Verse 55.94

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तारार्धेन तद्भर्भो यच्छेषं तेन भित्तय:।
प्रासाद(सि ?)जङ्घोच्छ्रयेण तुल्यो गर्भतुलोदय:।।९४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistārārdhena tadbharbho yacchheṣaṃ tena bhittayaḥ |
prāsāda(si ?)jaṅghocchrayeṇa tulyo garbhatulodayaḥ ||94||

[FLAG: The reading `प्रासाद(सि ?)` includes a query mark as printed in the text.]

**English Translation:**

By the half of the breadth the centre of that one, may be there and that which remains by that the one (may be created). The height of the central beam may be similar to the height of the Jaṅghā of the Prāsāda.

---

### Verse 55.95

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सभित्तिगर्भतुल्य: स्यात् सन्धारेषु तुलोदय:।
स विधेय: पुनर्व्यासाद् यदि वा किञ्चिदुन्नत:।।९५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sabhittigarbhatulayaḥ syāt sandhāreṣu tulodayaḥ |
sa vidheyaḥ punarvyāsād yadi vā kiñcidunnataḥ ||95||

**English Translation:**

The height of the beam in the basements may be similar to the centre, having walls included. The same deserves to be created again as compared with the breadth or even if somewhat elevated.

---

### Verse 55.96

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मूलसूत्रं तु दशधा ++ (मश्य ?)समालिखेत्।
गर्भसूत्रं प्रतिष्ठाप्य सिंहकर्णं प्रकल्पयेत्।।९६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mūlasūtraṃ tu daśadhā ++ (maśya ?)samālikhet |
garbhasūtraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya siṃhakarṇaṃ prakalpayet ||96||

[FLAG: `++` and `(मश्य ?)` are printed as-is in the text, indicating an unclear/corrupt reading.]

**English Translation:**

Having divided the basic plumb line ten fold, one may carve out a fish shaped structure¹ having established one may carve out a lion flank (Siṃha Karṇa).

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, verse 96, pāda 2 has at fn. 10 कृत्वा मत्स्यम् for मश्य।

---

### Verse 55.97

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सार्धभागेन सूत्रेण मध्यमस्य समालिखेत्।
उरो द्विभागतुल्यं तु मस्तकं भागमुच्छ्रितम्।।९७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sārdhabhāgena sūtreṇa madhyamasya samālikhet |
uro dvibhāgatulyaṃ tu mastakam bhāgamucchritam ||97||

**English Translation:**

By the plumb line having half digits one may carve out the (structure) of he middling one and the mastaka (the frontal curvature) one part in height and equal to the division of the chest like structure.

---

### Verse 55.98

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अर्धेन वोच्छ्रयस्तस्या: पक्षोच्छ्राया द्विभागिका:।
उरो (लेख्य ?) च सीमान्ते सूत्रेणच्छन्दमादिशेत्।।९८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ardhena vocchrayastasyāḥ pakṣocchhrāyā dvibhāgikāḥ |
uro (lekhya ?) ca sīmānte sūtreṇacchandamādiśet ||98||

[FLAG: `(लेख्य ?)` is printed as-is in the text, indicating an uncertain reading.]

**English Translation:**

By a half may be the height of that one and the flanked heights may be two digited ones. And within the extremity carved out in the chest like structure,² one may order the Acchanda by a plumb line.

> **Footnote 2:** उरो लेख्ये च सीमान्ते correction in fn. 13, p. 315. GOSE verse 98.

### Verse 55.99

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नवधा दशधा चैव सिंहकर्मा(र्णा)वुभौ स्मृतौ।
एकषड्विस्तृतो भागा(?) उद्यात् पञ्चभागिक:।।९९।।

navadhā daśadhā caiva siṃhakarma(rṇā)vubhau smṛtau |
ekaṣaḍvistṛto bhāgā(?) udyāt pañcabhāgakaḥ ||99||

### Verse 55.100

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सूरसेनो द्वितीयस्तु स्वात् समोदयविस्तृतिः।
उदयात् सार्धविस्तीर्णं सिंहकर्णस्त्रिसंकुला(?)॥१००॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sūraseno dvitīyastu svāt samodayavistṛtiḥ।
udayāt sārdhavistīrṇaṃ siṃhakarṇastrisaṃkulā(?)॥100॥

**English Translation:**

Sūrasena the second one may there be having breadth and height similar. And having breadth half way from the height Siṃha Karṇā of saṅkula triad.

---

### Verse 55.101

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कामलान् मल्लकांश्चान्यान् सिंहकर्णात् प्रलोप(कल्प)येत्।
प्रासादानो हि सर्वेषां सर्वमेतद् विभूषणम्॥१०१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kāmalān mallakāṃścānyān siṃhakarṇāt pralopa(kalpa)yet।
prāsādāno hi sarveṣāṃ sarvametad vibhūṣaṇam॥101॥

**English Translation:**

Kāmalās and Mallakas, the other ones, one may create out of Siṃhakarṇa of all the Prāsādas, all this is the adornment.

---

### Verse 55.102

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यस्य यत्रोचितं स्थानं तत् तत्र विनिवेशयेत्।
निर्मिती वलभेस्तिर्यक् सूत्रं कुर्वीत् सप्तधा॥१०२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

yasya yatrocitaṃ sthānaṃ tat tatra viniveśayet।
nirmitī valabhestirYak sūtraṃ kurvīt saptadhā॥102॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: तिर्यक् — possibly tiryak]

**English Translation:**

Wherever may be the proper adjustment therein one may get adjusted that. In the building of the wall, the horizontal plumb lining may one create as seven fold.

---

### Verse 55.103

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चभागांश्च मुखतस्तेनैवांशेन कल्पयेत्।
मेखलान्तरपत्रे च जम्भाकुम्भकमेव च॥१०३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcabhāgāṃśca mukhatastanevāṃśena kalpayet।
mekhalāntarapatre ca jambhākumbhakameva ca॥103॥

**English Translation:**

The five parts from the opening one may create by the same digital norm. And within the intermediary flank of the slope, there may be a Jambhā Kumbhaka (a globular projection).

---

### Verse 55.104

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चभागोच्छ्रितं कुर्यात् तद्विच्छिखरमुच्छ्रितम्।
कीर्तितानि विमानानि यान्येव सुरवर्त्मनि॥१०४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcabhāgocchritaṃ kuryāt tadviccśikharamucchritam।
kīrtitāni vimānāni yānyeva suravartmani॥104॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: तद्विच्छिखर — possibly tadvicchikharamucchritam]

**English Translation:**

Like that very norm one may create the elevated top elevated by five parts, the Vimānas or Saptabhūmi Prāsādas that have been illustrated in the passage of gods or temples.

---

### Verse 55.105

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तान्येव स्थावरत्वेन प्रासादा इति विश्रुताः।
महेश्वरस्य कैलासो विष्णोस्तु गरुडाभिधः॥१०५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tānyeva sthāvaratvena prāsādā iti viśrutāḥ।
maheśvarasya kailāso viṣṇostu garuḍābhidhaḥ॥105॥

**English Translation:**

These very ones owing to stability are the palaces this way having been well known of Maheśvara (Śiva) the Kailāsa (temple) ordained and that of Viṣṇu Garuḍa normed.

---

### Verse 55.106

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कार्यः प्रजापतेः पद्मो गणनाथस्य च द्विपः।
न खल्वेतेऽन्यदेवानां विधातुमुचिताः स्मृताः॥१०६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kāryaḥ prajāpateḥ padmo gaṇanāthasya ca dvipaḥ।
na khalvete'nyadevānāṃ vidhātumucitāḥ smṛtāḥ॥106॥

**English Translation:**

Of Prajāpati i.e. Brahmā deserves to be created a Padma normed and of gaṇanāyaka i.e. Gaṇeśa the tusker normed. These types, however, are not declared as suitable for the other gods.

---

### Verse 55.107

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यस्तु त्रिविष्टपः स स्यात् सर्वदेविनकेतनः।
अस्मात् तु येऽन्ये प्रासादाः स्मृतास्तेऽनेकरूपिणः॥१०७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

yastu triviṣṭapaḥ sa syāt sarvadevinakетanaḥ।
asmāt tu ye'nye prāsādāḥ smṛtāste'nekarūpiṇaḥ॥107॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: सर्वदेविनकेतनः — possibly sarvadevanikетanaḥ]

**English Translation:**

### Verse 55.108

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वेषामेव देवानामभेदेन भवन्ति ते।
जगत्यां विस्तरः कार्यः प्रासादोच्छ्रयसंमितः॥१०८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvēṣāmeva devānāmabhedena bhavanti te।
jagatyāṃ vistaraḥ kāryaḥ prāsādocchrayasaṃmitaḥ॥108॥

**English Translation:**

They all are created in light of the non-differentiation of all gods. On the ground breadth deserves to be created equal in norm to the height of the temple.

---

### Verse 55.109

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गभर्धिनोच्छ्रयतस्या: शुभद: परिकीतित:।
मण्डपस्य षडंशार्धे(?)पञ्चमांशादथ स्मृत:॥१०९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

garbhardhinocchrayatasyāḥ śubhadaḥ parikīrtitaḥ।
maṇḍapasya ṣaḍaṃśārdhe(?)pañcamāṃśādatha smṛtaḥ॥109॥

**English Translation:**

The height as compared with the central half, of that one is declared as beneficence affording of the Maṇḍapa (the pavilion), the half of the six digits, is declared as such compared with fifth digit.

---

### Verse 55.110

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णप्रासादका: कार्या: प्रासादस्य त्रिभागत:।
पूर्वापरमुखा: कार्या एते याम्योत्तरानना:॥११०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇaprāsādakāḥ kāryāḥ prāsādasya tribhāgataḥ।
pūrvāparamukhāḥ kāryā ete yāmyottarānanāḥ॥110॥

**English Translation:**

The Karṇa Prāsādaka, (The flanked sidereal chambers) deserve to be created as per triple divisibility of the Prāsāda. These as having openings in the south and north, deserve to be created as having openings in the East and West.

---

### Verse 55.111

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऐन्द्रे याम्ये वारुणे च कौबेरे च यथाक्रमम्।
दिग्भागेषु चतुर्ष्वेषु वलभीं विनिवेशयेत्॥१११॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

aindre yāmye vāruṇe ca kaubere ca yathākramam।
digbhāgeṣu caturṣveṣu valabhīṃ viniveśayet॥111॥

**English Translation:**

Within the Aindra i.e. eastern, Yāmya (southern), Vāruṇa (western) and Kaubera (northern) as per serial order, in quarter regions of these, one may get walls incorporated.

---

### Verse 55.112

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गर्भविस्तारविस्तीर्णां द्वौ त्रिभागौ मुखायताम्।
इति बाह्यपरीवारे जङ्घा प्रासादमानत:॥११२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

garbhavistāravistīrṇāṃ dvau tribhāgau mukhāyatām।
iti bāhyaparīvāre jaṅghā prāsādamānataḥ॥112॥

**English Translation:**

Broad by the breadth of the centre, oblong normed at the opening having two triple moded parts such is Jaṅghā in the outer circumference, as per measurement of the temple.

---

### Verse 55.113

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तीर्यंगायतमारोप्य सूत्रं गर्भेण मण्डपे।
(गुरुकक्षोऽथ?) कर्तव्या गवाक्षस्तम्भसंयुता:॥११३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tīryaṃgāyatamāropya sūtraṃ garbheṇa maṇḍape।
(gurukakṣo'tha?) kartavyā gavākṣastambhasaṃyutāḥ॥113॥

**English Translation:**

Horizontal and oblong normed having superimposed the plumb line in the pavilion by the central part, Gurukakṣā then deserves to be created equipped with the columns of the widows.

---

### Verse 55.114

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादविस्तरात् कार्यो द्विगुणो मण्डप: सदा।
मण्डपस्यस(स्व)विस्तारा(द)जगती द्विगुणा बहि:॥११४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādavistārāt kāryo dviguṇo maṇḍapaḥ sadā।
maṇḍapasyasa(sva)vistārā(da)jagatī dviguṇā bahiḥ॥114॥

**English Translation:**

The Maṇḍapa or the pavilion may always be created double fold as compared with the

### Verse 55.115

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णप्रासादका: कार्या: प्रासादस्यार्धतोऽपि वा।
तेषामध्यर्धत: कुर्याद् वलभीनां निवेशनम्॥११५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇaprāsādakāḥ kāryāḥ prāsādasya ārdhato'pi vā।
teṣāmadhyardhataḥ kuryād valabhīnāṃ niveśanam॥115॥

**English Translation:**

The Karṇa Prāsādakas (or sidereal chambers) deserve to be created from half the dimension of the Prāsāda and from their additional or one and half extent one¹ may create the establishment of halls.

> **Footnote 1:** MW, p. 23. अध्यर्ध।

### Verse 55.116

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अनेन क्रमयोगेन बाह्याद् बाह्यं सुसंवृतम्।
यदा(था) हि शोभते राजा केयूराङ्गदकुण्डलै:॥११६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

anena kramayogena bāhyād bāhyaṃ susaṃvṛtam।
yadā(thā) hi śobhate rājā keyūrāṅgadakuṇḍalaiḥ॥116॥

**English Translation:**

By this very combination of serial order, the outer side in line with the outskirts nicely globular or covered may be there. The way becomes decorated with armlets and ear-peduncles.

---

### Verse 55.117

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तथा प्रासादराजोऽयं शोभते भूषणैर्निजै:।
ध्वजास्याहर्स्यातिसौम्यस्य(?)श्रीकीर्तिविजयावह:॥११७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tathā prāsādarājo'yaṃ śobhate bhūṣaṇairnijaiḥ।
dhvajāsyāharsyātisaumyasya(?)śrīkīrtivijayāvahaḥ॥117॥

**English Translation:**

That very way the Prāsādarāja i.e. the king of mansions this one gets englamoured by personal or individual decorations. And the dhvaja or upper dome or sanctum sanctorum of highly graceful norm entails glory fame and victory.

---

### Verse 55.118

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अनेन विधिना न्यस्त: प्रासाद: स्यात् सदा तृणाम्।
आदित्यं पूर्वतो न्यस्येत् कुमारं पूर्वदक्षिणे॥११८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

anena vidhinā nyastaḥ prāsādaḥ syāt sadā tṛṇām।
ādityaṃ pūrvato nyasyet kumāraṃ pūrvadakṣiṇe॥118॥

**English Translation:**

Having been designed in such a way the palace of the people, Āditya may be established in the East and Kumāra on the East-South.

---

### Verse 55.119

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दक्षिणे मातृदेवै(वां)स्तु गजास्यं दक्षिणोपरि।
विन्यसेद् वारुणे गौरीं वायव्येऽपि च चण्डिकाम्॥११९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dakṣiṇe mātṛdevai(vāṃ)stu gajāsyaṃ dakṣiṇopari।
vinyased vāruṇe gaurīṃ vāyavye'pi ca caṇḍikām॥119॥

**English Translation:**

In the south or on the right Mātṛdevas and Gajāsya on the upper right or south. In the west one may carve out Gaurī and in the northwest the Caṇḍikā.

---

### Verse 55.120

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विष्णुं कुबेरदिग्भागे तथैशान्यां महेश्वरम्।
अन्येषामपि देवानां कथ्यते तु क्रमोऽधुना॥१२०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

viṣṇuṃ kubera digbhāge tathaiśānyāṃ maheśvaram।
anyeṣāmapi devānāṃ kathyate tu kramo'dhunā॥120॥

**English Translation:**

To Viṣṇu one may place in the quarter governed by Kubea i.e. north and in the north eastern quarter Maheśvara. Of other gods even now is being dilated upon the serial order.

---

### Verse 55.121

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तत्रैशान्यां दिशि न्यसेदीशानं लोकनायकम्।
दानवानां निहन्तारं पूर्वस्यामपि वासवम्॥१२१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tatraiśānyāṃ diśi nyased īśānaṃ lokanāyakam।
dānavānāṃ nihantāraṃ pūrvasyāmapi vāsavam॥121॥

### Verse 55.22

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वैश्वानरं तथाग्नेय्यां धर्मराजं च दक्षिणे।
नैर्ऋत्यां निर्ऋतिं न्यस्येत् प्रतीच्यां तु प्रचेतसम्॥१२ २॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vaiśvānaraṃ tathāgneyāṃ dharmarājaṃ ca dakṣiṇe।
nairṛtyāṃ nirṛtiṃ nyasyct pratīcyāṃ tu pracetasam॥122॥

[FLAG: The verse number in the image reads ॥१२ २॥ — likely ॥१२२॥; spacing may be a print artefact.]

**English Translation:**

Vaiśvānara in the Āgneyi (south eastern quarter) and Dharmarāja (Yama) in the southern. In the Nairṭṭiḥ (south western quarter) Nirṛtiḥ (goddess of death) and in the western Pracetasa (Varuṇa).

---

### Verse 55.23

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वायुं वायव्यदिग्भागे कुबेरमपि चोत्तरे।
अष्टौ ह्येते महात्मानो लोकपालाः प्रकीर्तिताः॥१२ ३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vāyuṃ vāyavyadigbhāge kuberam api cottare।
aṣṭau hyete mahātmāno lokapālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ॥123॥

[FLAG: The verse number in the image reads ॥१२ ३॥ — likely ॥१२३॥.]

**English Translation:**

Vāyu (god) may be in the north western and Kubera in the north - these eight great souled ones are known as Lokapālas (guardians of the quarters).

---

### Verse 55.24

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पालयन्ति जगत् सर्वं स्वस्वस्थाने प्रतिष्ठिताः।
पुर(कर्कट ? कर्वेट)दुर्गेषु ग्रामेषु नगरेषु च॥१२ ४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pālayanti jagat sarvaṃ svasvastāne pratiṣṭhitāḥ।
pura(karkaṭa? karveṭa)durgeṣu grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca॥124॥

[FLAG: The text in parentheses "पुर(कर्कट ? कर्वेट)" appears to be an editorial note within the printed Devanagari, likely indicating a variant reading. Reproduced verbatim.]

**English Translation:**

Established in individual spots they protect the entire universe. In a Pura² (a place containing large buildings surrounded by a ditch and extending not less than one Krośa in length), Karvaṭa³ (a small market town), in forts, villages⁴ and bigger towns.

> **Footnote 2:** MW. p. 635. If it extends for half a distance it is called Kheṭa, if less than that a Karvaṭa or small market town. Any smaller number of houses is called a grāma or a village.

> **Footnote 3:** Ibid.

> **Footnote 4:** Ibid.

---

### Verse 55.25

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

क्रमेणानेन विन्यस्ताः स्युः प्रजानां सुखावहाः।
न यत्र देवताबाधस्तत्र द्वारं प्रकल्पयेत्॥१२ ५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

krameṇānena vinyastāḥ syuḥ prajānāṃ sukhāvahāḥ।
na yatra devatābādhas tatra dvāraṃ prakalpayet॥125॥

**English Translation:**

Having been established in this very order, (they) may be established as bliss affording to the subjects. And where there be no overlapping of the images there one may create a gate or portal.

---

### Verse 55.26

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादस्यानुसारेण भवेद् द्वारं शुभावहम्।
अथातः प्रोच्यते सम्यग् द्वारमानमनुक्रमात्॥१२ ६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādasyanusāreṇa bhaved dvāraṃ śubhāvaham।
athātaḥ procyate samyag dvāramānam anukramāt॥126॥

**English Translation:**

In line with the structure of the Prāsāda, the door may be auspicious normed one. Hence is being dilated upon suitably the measurement of the gate as per serial order.

---

### Verse 55.27

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ज्येष्ठमध्यकनिष्ठानां द्रव्यं स्तम्भानुसङ्गतम्।
एकहस्ते भवेद् द्वारं प्रासादे षोडशाङ्गुलम्॥१२ ७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jyeṣṭhamadhyakaniṣṭhānāṃ dravyaṃ stambhānusaṅgatam।
ekahaste bhaved dvāraṃ prāsāde ṣoḍaśāṅgulam॥127॥

**English Translation:**

The material for the larger, middling and smaller ones, is in line with the nature of the columns on one cubit in a Prāsāda of 16 Aṅgulas i.e. 12 inches.

---

### Verse 55.128

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विकरे द्विगुणं तत् स्यात् त्रिकरे द्विकरं शुभम्।
चतुष्करे चतु:षष्टिरङ्गुलानि प्रशस्यते॥१२८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvikare dviguṇaṃ tat syāt trikare dvikaraṃ śubham |
catuṣkare catuḥṣaṣṭiraṅgulāni praśasyate || 128 ||

**English Translation:**

On two Karas or cubits that may be double fold and on a three cubited spot, the two cubited one may be auspicious on a four cubited spot 64 Aṅgulas i.e. 48 Inches is appreciated as such.

---

### Verse 55.129

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अत ऊर्ध्वं प्रतिकरं त्र्यङ्गुला वृद्धिरिष्यते।
द्वारोदयकरैस्तुल्यान्यङ्गुलानि नियोजयेत्॥१२९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ata ūrdhvaṃ pratikaram tryaṅgulā vṛddhiriṣyate |
dvārodayakaraistulyānyaṅgulāni niyojayet || 129 ||

**English Translation:**

Beyond this on each cubit the increase of 3 aṅgulas i.e. 2.25 inches as such. Similar in extent to the cubits of the elevation of the gate, one may adjust the Aṅgulas i.e. 3/4th of inches.

---

### Verse 55.130

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(पोह्वाजयवान्यालर्ध?)° ध्रुवके चतुर(र ?)ङ्गुले।
विस्तारो द्विगुणस्तस्य स्तम्भपिण्डं स एव हि॥१३०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(pohvājayavānyālardha?) dhuvake catur(r?)aṅgule |
vistāro dviguṇastasya stambhapiṇḍaṃ sa eva hi || 130 ||

[FLAG: Pāda 1 is uncertain/corrupt; translator reads as "Half in extent of the thickness (bāhalya) and length of the Pedyā." Footnote 1 indicates GOSE p. 318, verse 130, pāda 1 correction: पेढ्या वाहल्यसेन्यार्धं ध्रुवके चतुरङ्गुले।]

**English Translation:**

Half in extent of he thickness (bāhalya) and length of the Pedyā (pedestal or basement) the two dhruvakas (posts)² may be four Aṅgulas i.e. 3 inches. The breadth of that is double fold of that and the column curvature is practically of the same norm.

> **Footnote 1:** पेढ्या वाहल्यसेन्यार्ध ध्रुवके चतुरङ्गुले। GOSE, p. 318, verse 130, pāda 1 correction on fn. 3 for पोहवाजय वान्यालर्ध।

> **Footnote 2:** V.S. Apte, p. 276, MW. p. 521.

### Verse 55.131

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एकद्वित्रिचतुष्पञ्चषट्सप्तककराविधि।
द्वारविस्तारभागेन स्तम्भ: सम्यग् विधीयते॥१३१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ekadvitricatuṣpañcaṣaṭsaptakakarāvidhi |
dvāravistārabhāgena stambhaḥ samyag vidhīyate || 131 ||

**English Translation:**

To the extent of one, two, three, four, five, six, seven cubits by the breadth of the door panels a column is erected quite suitably.

### Verse 55.132

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्भागेन कर्तव्या सीमास्तम्भ: प्रमाणत:(?)।
तथा स्तम्भस्य बाहुल्याच्चतुर्भागिविभागिवना(?)॥१३२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturbhāgena kartavyā sīmāstambhaḥ pramāṇataḥ(?) |
tathā stambhasya bāhulyāccaturbhāgivibhāgivanā(?) || 132 ||

[FLAG: Pāda 4 uncertain/corrupt. Footnote 3 notes GOSE p. 318, verse 132, pāda 4: तथा स्तम्भस्य बालह्वाङ्तुयांग विमात्रिकै seems to be the correction for चतुर्भागिवभागा विना; in fn. 7 it is चतुर्भाग विमाजितौ।]

**English Translation:**

In measurement the column of the extremity deserves to be created by four parts. That very way from the thickness of the column having divisibility of four parts.³

### Verse 55.133

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

+ भागौ तत्र कर्तव्यौ हीरग्रहणमुच्छ्रितम्।
भागत्रयेण कर्तव्य: पट्टस्य च समुच्छ्रय:॥१३३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

+ bhāgau tatra kartavyau hīragrahaṇamucchritam |

Two parts there deserve to be created and the curvature of a Hira (dome - diamond like) or sanctum sanctorum, elevated. And by a part triad deserves to be created the height or elevation of the Paṭṭa (pedestal) or pada (padasya - fn. 9).

### Verse 55.134

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेनैकेन नीस्यातव्यविस्तारं भत्रिभागरच्यकिम् (?)।

(bhāgenaikena tiraṃ syāt vā stambha tribhāgikaṃ pada tastadvidhāta vyaṃ tryaṅgula dvayanirgamam) i.e. by one part may be the cornered flank or of three parts of the column may it be. By a square, deserves to be created a 3 Aṅgula normed double opening.

### Verse 55.135

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

+ ड ++++ स्तार: स्तम्भतुल्य: प्रशस्यते।

The breadth of the top of the column³ is belauded as one equal in norm to the column as one Aṅgula i.e. 3/4th of an inch each of the two flanks of the pedestal, projected as such.

### Verse 55.136

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पट्टदस्य विस्तार:(?)कार्यञ्चतुर्भागविभाजित:।
भागेनैकेन चोत्सेधस्तुलाधारणमिष्यते॥१३६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

paṭṭadasya vistāraḥ (?) kāryañcaturbhāgavibhājitaḥ ।
bhāgenaikena cotsedhastulādhāraṇamiṣyate ॥136॥

[IAST UNCERTAIN: paṭṭadasya — reading uncertain]

**English Translation:**

The breadth of the pedestal, deserves to be created by the ones divided into four parts. And the elevation is to be by a single part being the supporter of beam declared as such or required as such.

---

### Verse 55.137

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तुलाधारणकोत्सेधाच्चतुर्भागविभाजितात्।
भागमेकं परित्यज्य पिण्डस्तस्य विधीयते॥१३७॥

tulādhāraṇakotsedhāccaturbhāgavibhājitāt ।
bhāgamekaṃ parityajya piṇḍastasya vidhīyate ॥137॥

**English Translation:**

From the elevation of the beams support divided into four parts having left one part the circumference of that one stands ordained.

---

### Verse 55.138

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मात्राहीना भवेन्मेढ्यां तावन्यस्येच्छलान्तले(?)।
द्वौ भागौ मूलभागेन जयन्तीपिण्डविस्तरौ॥१३८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

mātrāhīnā bhavenmēḍhyāṃ tāvanyasyecchalāntale (?) ।
dvau bhāgau mūlabhāgena jayantīpiṇḍavistarau ॥138॥

**English Translation:**

The Meḍhyā may be reduced by one digit and to that one may assort in Chalāntala. Two parts, by the root part, may be of breadth of the Jayanti piṇḍa.

---

### Verse 55.139

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इति हीरग्रहादीनां समासाऌक्ष्मकीर्तनम्।
पञ्चांशाभ्यधिकं स्तम्भविस्तारस्थेन कुम्भिका (?)॥१३९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

iti hīragrahādīnāṃ samāsāḷkṣmakīrtanam ।
pañcāṃśābhyadhikaṃ stambhavistārasthena kumbhikā (?) ॥139॥

[IAST UNCERTAIN: samāsāḷkṣmakīrtanam — ḷ/ḷ ligature uncertain; may be samāsālkṣmakīrtanam]

**English Translation:**

This way of (palaces) such as Hīragraha and the like, the definition in brief has been illustrated having an additional five parts and by one established in the breadth of the columns it may have a Kumbhikā or it may be Kumbhikā.

---

### Verse 55.140

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्वंत स्तम्भत: साधं गर्ग(?)कुम्भस्य विस्तृति:।
अथवा स्तम्भकर्णेन स्तम्भाग्रद्विगुणा क्वचित्॥१४०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kurvaṃta stambhataḥ sārdhaṃ garga(?)kumbhasya vistṛtiḥ ।
athavā stambhakarṇena stambhāgradviguṇā kvacit ॥140॥

**English Translation:**

The breadths of an Agrakumbha¹ (the frontal globe), having one and half the breadth of the column otherwise by the Stambha Karṇa (the breadth) may be redoubled than the top of the column.

> **Footnote 1:** कुर्वंत स्तम्भत: साधं अग्रकुम्भस्य विस्तृति: GOSE, fn. 5 - गर्ग कुम्भ ? corrected as अग्रकुम्भ।

---

### Verse 55.141

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पादोनस्तम्भविस्ताराद्ग्रकुम्भे समुच्छ्रिति:।
स्तम्भविस्तारकर्णाद् वा यद्वा पिण्डोऽग्रकुम्भके॥१४१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pādonastambhavistarādgrakumbhe samucchritiḥ ।
stambhavistarakarṇād vā yadvā piṇḍo'grakumbhake ॥141॥

**English Translation:**

From the breadth of the column reduced by a square the height may be in the Agrakumbha. Or from the corner of the breadth of the column the circumference may be such within the Agrakumbhaka.

---

### Verse 55.142

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्य भागान् प्रवक्ष्यामो यथाकुम्भं स युज्यते।
विभक्तोऽत्र त्रिधा पिण्डो भागेनैकेन पुत्तली॥१४२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasya bhāgān pravakṣyāmo yathākumbhaṃ sa yujyate ।
vibhakto'tra tridhā piṇḍo bhāgenaikena puttalī ॥142॥

**English Translation:**

We shall illustrate the parts of that, that suits in line with the individual Kumbha (globe). Divided herein three times here the Piṇḍa and by one part Puttalī (a puppet or female bracket figure).

---

### Verse 55.143

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्भिस्तस्य मध्यस्य (द्यखं?)पद्मं समालिखेत्।
उच्छाले पञ्चधा भक्ते त्रिभिरावर्तनं ++॥१४३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturbhistasya madhyasya (dyakhaṃ?) padmaṃ samālikhet ।
ucchāle pañcadhā bhakte tribhirāvartanaṃ ++ ॥143॥

**English Translation:**

By four the middle portion of that one becomes valid, and Padma one may get carved out. The Ucchāla having been divided into five, by three parts the circumambiation may be created or a fortification may be created.

---

### Verse 55.144

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्तनं योषव्येत् (?) किञ्चिन्न च स्वल्वं समाचरेत्।
वर्तने कुष्मकुम्भौ तु सूत्रं दत्वा सुतानितम्॥१४४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vartanaṃ yoṣavyet (?) kiñcinna ca svalvaṃ samācaret ।
vartane kuṣmakumbhau tu sūtraṃ datvā sutānitam ॥144॥

**English Translation:**

The circulation may be organised to a like extent and may not adhere to Khalva. In Varttana Kumbhakumbhas, (or Kumbha yakṣas) may be there and having offered a plumb well stretched out.

---

### Verse 55.145

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पद्मनालासमा स स्यान्न भवेत् पङ्क्तिविजिता(?)।
नवाधोच्चाहा(?)लके भक्ते वीरगण्डस्तु भागिक:॥१४५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padmanālāsamā sa syānna bhavet paṅktivijitā(?) ।
navādhoccāhā(?)lake bhakte vīragaṇḍastu bhāgikaḥ ॥145॥

**English Translation:**

That may be equal in extent to Padmamālā and there may be no paucity of arraying. The nine ones at the root divided in Kākhaka, Vīragaṇḍaka may be of one part.

---

### Verse 55.146

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एकेनैकेन भागेन विधेया पट्टिकद्विका।
(ध्वसंछाकाल?)कर्तव्यं भागद्वितयसंमितम्॥१४६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ekenaikena bhāgena vidheyā paṭṭikadvikā ।
(dhvasaṃchākāla?)kartavyaṃ bhāgadvitayasaṃmitam ॥146॥

**English Translation:**

### Verse 55.147

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तलकुम्भकपिण्डं तु पञ्चधा प्रविभाजयेत्।
भागेनैकेन पद्मं स्याद् भागेन कलशं लिखेत्॥१४७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

talakumbhakapiṇḍaṃ tu pañcadhā pravibhājayet।
bhāgenaikena padmaṃ syād bhāgena kalaśaṃ likhet॥147॥

**English Translation:**

To the Tala Kumbhaka Piṇḍa one may divide as five fold. By an individual single part the Kalaśa one may carve out.

---

### Verse 55.148

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वाभ्यां समालिखेत् कुम्भं भागेनैकेन पट्टिकाम्।
वर्तमाना चत्रा(?)कार्या° शोभा स्यादस्य यावत:॥१४८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvābhyāṃ samālikhet kumbhaṃ bhāgenaikena paṭṭikām।
vartamānā catrā(?)kāryā° śobhā syādasya yāvataḥ॥148॥

**English Translation:**

By a twain one may carve out Kumbha and by a single part Paṭṭikā and revolving round that deserves to be created whereby a glamour may get ensured.

> **Footnote 2:** वर्तमानोवताकार्या GOSE, p. 320, fn. 1

### Verse 55.149

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एष कुम्भक्रम: प्रोक्त: स्तम्भपादे व्यवस्थित:।
तलपट्टस्य पिण्डस्तु भागपट्टसमो भवेत्॥१४९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

eṣa kumbhakramaḥ proktaḥ stambhapāde vyavasthitaḥ।
talapaṭṭasya piṇḍastu bhāgapaṭṭasamo bhavet॥149॥

**English Translation:**

This stands illustrated as the serial order of the Kumbha fixed over the pedestal of the column. The Piṇḍa, however, of the Talapaṭṭa, may be equal in dimension with the bhāgapaṭṭa.

> **Footnote 3:** GOSE, p. 320, fn. 3 has तलपादस्य पिण्डं तु भारपदसमो भवेत्।

---

### Verse 55.150

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्रव्येष्वत्र हि सर्वेषु सम्यक् शोभा विवक्षिता।
न्यूनातिरिक्तमप्यस्मान्मानेष्वङ्गुलमाचरेत्॥१५०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dravyeṣvatra hi sarveṣu samyak śobhā vivakṣitā।
nyūnātiraktamapyasmānmāneṣvaṅgulamācaret॥150॥

**English Translation:**

In matter of materials in entirety, the decorative norm has been desired as being illustrated quite aptly. In point of lesser or additional the other⁴ one in point of measurement one may take into account Aṅgula i.e. 3/4the of an inch as the basic factor.

> **Footnote 4:** न्यूनातिरिक्तमन्यत्सान्मगानेष्वङ्गुलकाचरेत् ष्यत् should be न्यत् in pāda 3 of verse 150, GOSE, p. 320.

### Verse 55.151

द्वारामुदयविस्तारो द्रव्यसंस्थानमेव च।

dvārāmudayavistāro dravyasaṃsthānameva ca।
pūrvameva yathohiṣṭaṃ (ya ? ta)thā sarvamanusmaret॥151॥

**English Translation:**

Of the doors height and breadth and the adjustment of materials one may follow as earlier ordained or may follow all the proceedings).

---

### Verse 55.152

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पिण्डेन त मूलशाखाया द्वितीया प विधीयते।
सपायते सपादेन प्रत्ययदेनाथ सार्धेनरूपशाखा प्रशस्यते(?)॥१५२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

piṇḍena ta mūlaśākhāyā dvitīyā pa vidhīyate।
sapāyate sapādena pratyaydenātha sārdhenaropaśākhā praśasyate(?)॥152॥

**English Translation:**

By the Piṇḍa the first Mūla Śākhā the second one stands provided with one fourth or one and a half the Rūpaśākhā is appreciated.

> **Footnote 5:** सपादेनाथ सार्धेन रूपशाखा प्रशस्यते GOSE, p. 320, verse 152, pādas 3-4, fn. 7.

---

### Verse 55.153

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अर्धेन मूलशाखा: समा चैव बाह्यशाखा शाखां प्रकल्पयेत्(?)।
ऊर्ध्वपञ्चमशाखाया (दु?) सप्तमी नवमी च सा।।१५३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ardhena mūlaśākhā: samā caiva bāhyaśākhā śākhāṃ prakalpayet(?) |
ūrdhvapañcamaśākhāyā (du?) saptamī navamī ca sā || 153 ||

**English Translation:**

By a half of the Mūlaśākhā and Rūpaśākhā is broader quite similar to the Rūpaśākhā, one may create a bāhyaśākhā as the top of the Pañcamaśākhā. T hat may be the seventh or ninth.

> **Footnote 1:** रूपशाखा समासास्वात् न न्यूना नाधिकापि वा।

---

### Verse 55.154

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रूपशा ++++। स्यान्र न्यूना नाधिकापि च।
विस्तरार्धं तु कर्तव्य: सर्वासामेव निर्गम:।।१५४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

rūpaśā ++++| syānna nyūnā nādhikāpi ca |
vistarārdhaṃ tu kartavyaḥ sarvāsāmeva nirgamaḥ || 154 ||

**English Translation:**

The Rūpaśākhā may be the levelled one and neither lesser nor greater. Half in extent than the breadth. the outlet or opening of all deserves to be created as half in extent than the breadth.

---

### Verse 55.155

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शाखाविस्तारविस्तीर्णा(नु?न्यु)त्तरङ्गानि कारयेत्।
सार्धेन ध्रुवशाखानां पिण्डेनोदुम्बरोदय:।।१५५।।

śākhāvistāravistīrṇā(nu?nyu)ttaraṅgāni kārayet |
sārdhena dhruvaśākhānāṃ piṇḍenodumbaroudayaḥ || 155 ||

**English Translation:**

Broad as the breadth of the Śākhās (side frames) one may make to be built the Uttaraṅgas the upper blocks. By a half of the Piṇḍa, of the Dhruvaśākhās (the upper side frames) there may be the height of the threshold and the upper block.

---

### Verse 55.156

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उदुम्बरस्य पिण्डेन सिंहवक्राणि कारयेत्।
तद्र्धं विलसन्धि: स्यात् तत्समा भूमिरङ्गिका।।१५६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

udumbarasya piṇḍena siṃhavakrāṇi kārayet |
tadrdhaṃ vilasandhiḥ syāt tatsamā bhūmiraṅgikā || 156 ||

**English Translation:**

By the Piṇḍa or orb of the Udumbara (Threshold and the upper block), one may get created Siṃhacakras² the lion normed wheels. By a half of the bilasandhi may be there and similar for that aṅgikābhūmi a sidereal soil?

> **Footnote 2:** सिंह चक्राणि – fn. 12, GOSE, p. 320.

---

### Verse 55.157

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऽतलन्याससम: पट्ट: पिण्डपूर्वव्यवस्थित:।
कूटाकारैर्विचित्रैश्च शोभनै: रूपकर्मभि:।।१५७।।

'talanyāsasaṃaḥ paṭṭaḥ piṇḍapūrvavyavasthitaḥ |
kūṭākārairvicitraiśca śobhanaiḥ rūpakarmabhiḥ || 157 ||

**English Translation:**

The Paṭṭa is similar in extent to talanyāsa (the storey-adjustment) established in advance of the Piṇḍa or globe or dome, by dwelling shaped and variegated good looking artistic norms.

> **Footnote 3:** Kūṭa- EOHA, P.K.Ācārya, p.139- The peak or summit, head top : The top of a building.

पत्रजातैरनेकैश्च कण्ठं³ कुर्याद् यथेप्सितम्।

# Chapter 56 — The Illustration of sixty four Prasadas headed by Rucaka

[FLAG: The first two verses (55.159 and 55.160) appear to be the closing verses of Chapter 55, appearing before the Chapter 56 heading.]

---

### Verse 56.159

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अन्वीक्ष्य विचेत् तद्वत् स्थपति: सर्वमाचरेत्।
यदुक्तं यदनुक्तं च तत् समग्रमपि स्फुटम्।।१५९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

anvīkṣya vicet tadvat sthapatih sarvamācaret |
yaduktaṃ yadanuktaṃ ca tat samagraṃ api sphuṭam ||159||

**English Translation:**

Having examined thoroughly the Architect may bake all accordingly and may act as he does. What stands illustrated and what stands unillustrated all that stands evident.

---

### Verse 56.160

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(युक्ता)युक्तं समालोच्य यथाशोभं समाचरेत्।
आरभ्य मेरोरिति षोडशैते प्रासादमुख्या: कथिता यथावत्।
संक्षेपतो लक्ष्म तथा जगत्यां द्वारादिसम्बन्धि च(द्वा?) दारुमानम्।।१६०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

(yuktā)yuktaṃ samālocya yathāśobhaṃ samācaret |
ārabhya meroriti ṣoḍaśaite prāsādamukhyāḥ kathitā yathāvat |
saṃkṣepato lakṣma tathā jagatyāṃ dvārādisambandhi ca(dvā?) dārumānam ||160||

**English Translation:**

Having deliberated properly as per beatification he may activise. Having started from Meru, this way sixteen Prāsādas have been illustrated as such as per classification. Briefly in definition that very way on the ground has been illustrated as also relating for doors and the wood-qualities and measurements or weights of woods and measurements.

---


### Verse 56.1

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अथात: सम्प्रवक्ष्यामि प्रासादाञ् शिखरान्वितान्।
रुचकादींश्चतु:षष्टिं नामलक्षणत: क्रमात्।।१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi prāsādāñ śikharānvitān |
rucakādīṃścatuḥṣaṣṭiṃ nāmalakṣaṇataḥ kramāt ||1||

**English Translation:**

Now I shall talk of the Prāsādas equipped with roofs headed by Rucaka and the like, as sixty four as per said order of titles and defnition.

---

### Verse 56.2

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पूर्वं यानि विमानानि पञ्चोक्तान्यभवंस्तत:।
तदाकारभृत: सर्वे प्रासादा: पञ्चविंशति:।।२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pūrvaṃ yāni vimānāni pañcoktānyabhavaṃstataḥ |
tadākārabhṛtaḥ sarve prāsādāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ ||2||

**English Translation:**

Earlier whichever are the Vimānas five illustrated as such all those having shape of their individual norm, the Prāsādas are twenty five.

---

### Verse 56.3

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शिखरैर्विविधाकारैरेकेनाण्डेन भूषिता:।
केचिदण्डत्रयोपेता: केचित् पञ्चाण्डकान्विता:।।३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śikharairividhākārairekena āṇḍena bhūṣitāḥ |
kecidaṇḍatrayopetāḥ kecit pañcāṇḍakānvitāḥ ||3||

**English Translation:**

Decorated by myriad normed tops or roofs and a single circular curvature or round orbed structuring some equipped with a triad of circular norms and some of five Aṇḍakas (cupolas¹ or spires, a tapening structure in the firm of a tall cave or a pyramid rising above

> **Footnote 1:** Cupola- a small rounded dome of a roof or ceiling of a dome, the furnace for melting metals, p.298 (Concise Oxford Dictionary).
