### Verse 56.14

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ईयद्भेदेन ते ज्ञेया: प्रासादा: सर्वकामदा:।
सौवर्णा राजतश्चैव देवानां सततं प्रिया:॥१४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

īyadbhedena te jñeyāḥ prāsādāḥ sarvakāmadāḥ ।
sauvarṇā rājataścaiva devānāṃ satataṃ priyāḥ ॥14॥

**English Translation:**

By a slight variation they deserve to be known, the Prāsādas affording all desires, golden and silver made being dear to all gods.

---

### Verse 56.15

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मणिमुक्ताप्रवालाद्यैर्भूषणै: सुविभूषिता:।
रीतिकाताम्रघोषाद्यै: पिशाचोरगरक्षसाम्॥१५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

maṇimuktāpravālādyairbhūṣaṇaiḥ suvibhūṣitāḥ ।
rītikātāmraghoṣādyaiḥ piśācoragarakṣasām ॥15॥

**English Translation:**

Decorated with gems, pearls and corals and the like of ornaments and those constructed or inlaid with brass,¹ copper and bell metals² (equipments), being of Piśācas and Rākṣas.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 322, verse 16, pādas has सुभद्रोऽथ किरीटश for प्रमेयोऽथ।

> **Footnote 2:** VSA, p.199- ghoṣa- Bell metal.

---

### Verse 56.16

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

देवलोका भवन्येते कामस्वच्छन्दचारिण:।
पाताले चापि निर्दिश्: पाषाणै: स्फटिकैस्तथा॥१६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

devalokaḥ bhavantyete kāmasvacchandacāriṇaḥ ।
pātāle cāpi nirdiśaḥ pāṣāṇaiḥ sphaṭikaistatāhā ॥16॥

[FLAG: Second pāda reads पाताले चापि निर्दिश्: पाषाणै: स्फटिकैस्तथा — the word निर्दिश् is slightly unclear; may be निर्दिष्ट or similar. IAST UNCERTAIN: nirdiśaḥ]

**English Translation:**

The Devalokas or temples these happen to be acting at will and voluntary norm. In the Pātāla also they are ordained constructed with stones and marbles.

---

### Verse 56.17

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इष्टकाकाष्ठपाषाणैर्मर्त्यलोकेऽपि नन्दका:।
सुखदश्च भवन्येते कर्तु: कारयितुस्तथा॥१७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

iṣṭakākāṣṭhapāṣāṇairmarttyaloke'pi nandakāḥ ।
sukhadaśca bhavantyete kartuḥ kārayitustathā ॥17॥

**English Translation:**

Those made of bricks, wood, stones in the world of mortals are Nandakas i.e. gladdening ones. These ones bliss affording for the architect as well as the builder or one who gets them built or owners.

---

### Verse 56.18

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

लक्षणेनान्वितानेतान् कथयामो यथाविधि।
पुराणां भूषणार्थाय भुक्तिमुक्तिप्रदा नृणाम्॥१८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

lakṣaṇenānvitānetān kathayāmo yathāvidhi ।
purāṇāṃ bhūṣaṇārthāya bhuktimuktipadā nṛṇām ॥18॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: bhuktimuktipadā — the last syllable of the compound may be pradā; reading प्रदा from image.]

**English Translation:**

Equipped with auspicious marks these (Lakṣaṇas) we shall talk of as per procedure for the sake of ornamentation of the large towns endower of enjoyment and emancipation for men.

---

### Verse 56.19

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रुचको भद्रकश्चैव हंसो हंसोद्भवस्तथा।
प्रतिहंसस्तथा नन्दो नन्द्यावर्तो धराधर:॥१९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

rucako bhadrakaścaiva haṃso haṃsodbhavastathā ।
pratihaṃsastathā nando nandyāvarto dharādharaḥ ॥19॥

**English Translation:**

Rucaka and Bhadraka, Haṃsa and Haṃsodbhava Pratihaṃsa and Nanda likewise, Nandyāvartta and Dharādhara.

---

### Verse 56.20

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्धमानोऽद्रिकूटश्च श्रीवत्सोऽथ त्रिकूटक:।
मुक्तकोणो गजश्चैव गरुड: सिंह एव च॥२०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vardhamāno'drikūṭaśca śrīvatso'tha trikūṭakaḥ ।
muktakoṇo gajaścaiva garuḍaḥ siṃha eva ca ॥20॥

**English Translation:**

Vardhamāna and Adrikūṭa Śrīvatsa and Trikūṭaka Muktakoṇa and Gaja, Garuḍa and Siṃha likewise.

---


### Verse 56.11

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भवश्च विभवश्चैव पद्मो मालाधरस्तथा।
वज्रक: स्वस्तिक: शङ्कुर्मलयो मकरध्वज:।।११।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhavaś ca vibhavaś caiva padmo mālādharas tathā।
vajrakaḥ svastikaḥ śaṅkur malayo makaradhvajaḥ।।11।।

**English Translation:**

Bhava and Vibhava, Padma and Māladhara, Vajraka Svastika and Śaṅku, Malaya and Makaradhvaja.

---

### Verse 56.12

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इत्येते नामत: प्रोक्ता: प्रासादा: पञ्चविंशति:।
एतेषां रूपनिर्माणं कथयामो यथाविधि:।।१२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ity ete nāmataḥ proktāḥ prāsādāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ।
eteṣāṃ rūpanirmāṇaṃ kathayāmo yathāvidhiḥ।।12।।

**English Translation:**

This way these have been illustrated by names, the Prāsādas twenty five. The structural creation of these we shall talk of as per routine.

---

### Verse 56.13

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रुचका(द्य)ष्टादशैषां चतुरश्रा: प्रकीर्तिता:।
भवश्च विभवश्चैव चतुरश्रायतोऽथवा।।१३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

rucakā(dy)aṣṭādaśaiṣāṃ caturaśrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ।
bhavaś ca vibhavaś caiva caturaśrāyato'thavā।।13।।

**English Translation:**

[Translation appears as part of the combined block for verses 13–15 below]

---

### Verse 56.14

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पद्मो मालाधरश्चैव वृत्तावेतावुदाहृती।
मलयो मकराख्योऽथ द्वौ तु वृत्तायताविमौ।।१४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

padmo mālādharaś caiva vṛttāvetāv udāhṛtī।
malayo makarākhyo'tha dvau tu vṛttāyatāv imau।।14।।

**English Translation:**

[Translation appears as part of the combined block for verses 13–15 below]

---

### Verse 56.15

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वज्रक: स्वस्तिक: शङ्कुरित्यमष्टाश्रयस्त्रय:।
ललिता: कथिता होते बूमोऽन्यान् मिश्रकानथ।।१५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vajrakaḥ svastikaḥ śaṅkur ity am aṣṭāśrayas trayaḥ।
lalitāḥ kathitā hote brūmo'nyān miśrakān atha।।15।।

**English Translation:**

Bhava and Vibhava, Padma and Māladhara, Vajraka Svastika and Śaṅku, Malaya and Makaradhvaja. Of these Rucaka and the like eighteen square shaped have been illustrated as such. Bhava and Vibhava, however, are square shaped or oblong normed or rectangular ones. Padma and Māladhara these two are declared as globular. Malaya and the one named Makara both these are globular and oblong normed. Vajraka, Svastika and Śaṅku are this way octagonal ones, the three. The lovely ones these have been illustrated. We now shall talk of others as these of mixed structural norm.

---

### Verse 56.16

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सुभद्रो योकिटश्च(?) सर्वतोभद्र एव च।
सिंहकेसरिसंज्ञोऽन्यश्चित्रकूटो धराधर:।।१६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

subhadro yokiṭaś ca(?) sarvatobhadra eva ca।
siṃhakesarisaṃjño'nyaś citrakūṭo dharādharaḥ।।16।।

**English Translation:**

Subhadra and Kiriṭa, Sarvatobhadra as well as such and a different one named Siṃha Kesari, Citrakūṭa and Dharādhara or variegated peaked one Dharādhara.

---

### Verse 56.17

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तिलकाख्य: स्वतिलकस्तथा सर्वाङ्गसुन्दर:।
नवामी मिश्रका: प्रोक्ता: कथ्यन्ते (साधकारिका:?)।।१७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tilakākhyaḥ svatilakastathā sarvāṅgasundaraḥ।
navāmī miśrakāḥ proktāḥ kathyante (sādhakārikāḥ?)।।17।।

**English Translation:**

Tilaka named one and Svatilaka and likewise Sarvāṅga Sundara - nine these ones are declared as Miśrakas (the mixed structural ones) and now are being illustrated "Sādhikārikas" having typical individual structures.

---

### Verse 56.18

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

केसरी सर्वतोभद्रो नन्दनो नन्दिशालक:।
नन्दीशो मन्दिराख्यश्च श्रीवृक्षश्चामृतोद्भव:।।१८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kesarī sarvatobhadro nandano nandiśālakaḥ।
nandīśo mandirākhyaś ca śrīvṛkṣaś cāmṛtodbhavaḥ।।18।।

**English Translation:**

Kesarī, Sarvatobhadra, Nandana, Nandiśālaka, Nandiśa, one named Mandira, Śrīvṛkṣa and Anṛtodbhava.

---

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — verse 35 Devanagari begins on previous page; only partial Devanagari and translation visible here]

### Verse 56.35

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हरेहिरण्यगर्भस्य +++ भास्करस्य च।
मेरुरेष विधातव्यो नान्यस्य त्रिदिवौकसः॥३५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

hareḥ hiraṇyagarbhasya +++ bhāskarasya ca।
merureṣa vidhātavyo nānyasya tridivaukasaḥ॥35॥

**English Translation:**

Of Hari and Hiranyagarbha Hara¹ and of Bhāskara Meru this one as such deserves to be created and not of any other god.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 324, verse 35, Pāda 2 हरोहिरण्यगर्भस्य हरस्य भास्करस्य च।

---

### Verse 56.36

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेरो: प्रासादराजस्य देवानामालयस्य च।
कर्ता क्षत्रिय एवास्य वैश्य(श्योऽ)स्य स्थपतिर्भवेत्॥३६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

meroḥ prāsādarājasya devānāmālayasya ca।
kartā kṣatriya evāsya vaiśya(śyo'sya) sthapatir bhavet॥36॥

**English Translation:**

Of Meru the king of Palaces or temples, the builder may be a Kṣatriya and an architect as a Vaisya.

---

### Verse 56.37

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एवं विधीयमानेऽस्मिन् मेरौ द्रावपि नन्दतः।
वास्तुशास्त्रविधिज्ञोऽपि क्षत्रियः स्थपतिर्यदि॥३७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

evaṃ vidhīyamāne'smin merau drāvapi nandataḥ।
vāstuśāstravidhijño'pi kṣatriyaḥ sthapatir yadi॥37॥

**English Translation:**

This way of this one being constructed the Meru the two become happier the one a knower of the moulding of Architecture as if an Architect is a Kṣatriya.

---

### Verse 56.38

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तदास्य सत्यं शौचं च विक्रमश्च विनश्यति।
ईश्वरोऽपि यदा विप्रो मेरुप्रासादकृद् भवेत्॥३८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tadāsya satyaṃ śaucaṃ ca vikramaśca vinaśyati।
īśvaro'pi yadā vipro meruprāsādakṛd bhavet॥38॥

**English Translation:**

Then the existence, purity and prowess of this one becomes defunct, when the owner is a Brāhmaṇa the creator of Meru Prāsāda.

---

### Verse 56.39

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्तुः कारयितुः पीडा पूजा चास्य न तादृशी।
ब्राह्मणः स्थपतिश्चास्य वास्तुशास्त्रे विशारदः॥३९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kartuḥ kārayituḥ pīḍā pūjā cāsya na tādṛśī।
brāhmaṇaḥ sthapaticāsya vāstuśāstre viśāradaḥ॥39॥

**English Translation:**

There may be affliction for the creation one causing to create and there may be no respect offered to this one of the same type.

---

### Verse 56.40

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वणिक्कर्मणि वर्तेत धनवानपि यद्यसौ।
सर्वविप्रेषु निर्दिष्टः कर्ता स्थपतिरेव सः॥४०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vaṇikkarmaṇi varteta dhanavānapi yadyasau।
sarvavipreṣu nirdiṣṭaḥ kartā sthapatireva saḥ॥40॥

**English Translation:**

If he may be a rich man then he may develop in the mercantile. Among all the Brāhmaṇas the creator is called an architect.

---

### Verse 56.41

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तत्रस्था देवताः सर्वास्तस्य वृद्धिः कथञ्चन।
वास्तुशास्त्रविधिज्ञोऽपि तत्तत् कारियता यदि॥४१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tatrasthā devatāḥ sarvāstasya vṛddhiḥ kathañcana।
vāstuśāstravidhijño'pi tattat kāriyatā yadi॥41॥

**English Translation:**

Stationed therein the gods, all of them, (become favourable) and his propriety becomes ensured somehow. And the one given to the understanding of the working of Science of Architecture, even being one as creator of that and that norm.

---

### Verse 56.42

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

राजापि क्षत्रियः कर्ता मेरु(रो)भवेत् तदा।
राष्ट्रभङ्गो भवेत् तस्य प्रजा यान्ति दिशो दश॥४२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

rājāpi kṣatriyaḥ kartā meru(ro)bhavet tadā।
rāṣṭrabhaṅgo bhavet tasya prajā yānti diśo daśa॥42॥

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

A king even a creator being Kṣatriya of Meru when, may there be, the demolition of the country of that one and may even and the subserve of that one get scattered within ten quarters i.e. go helter skelter.

---

### Verse 56.43

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

क्षत्रियेण नरेन्द्रेण कर्ता स्थपतिना यदि (?)।
मेरो: पूजा भवेत् तत्र क्षत्रियोऽप्यक्षयं पदम्।।४३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kṣatriyeṇa nareṇdreṇa kartā sthāpatinā yadi (?)।
meroḥ pūjā bhavet tatra kṣatriyo'pyakṣayaṃ padam।।43।।

**English Translation:**

By a Kṣatriya king an architect if is appointed a Vaiśya,¹ then the adoration of Meru may be assumed and there of the two may be an undecayable position.

> **Footnote 1:** क्षत्रियेण नरेन्द्रेण कतो स्थपतिना यदि विशा। GOSE, p. 325, fn. 1 क्षत्रियेण नरेन्द्रेने कृतः स्थयति यदि वैश्यः। should be the correction.

---

### Verse 56.44

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एकैकस्य च यन्मानं सकर्णस्य च यद्वशम्।
प्रासादानां च सर्वेषां तत् सम्यगभिधीयते।।४४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ekaikasya ca yanmānaṃ sakarṇasya ca yadvśam।
prāsādānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tat samyagabhidhīyate।।44।।

**English Translation:**

Of a single one each whatever may be the measurement and whatever may be the circumference of the one flanked as such, of all those Prāsādas a true picture is being given.

---

### Verse 56.45

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे चतुर्भागिविवर्जिते।
भागिका सर्वतो भित्ति: शेषं गर्भगृहं भवेत्।।४५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre caturbhāgivivarjite।
bhāgikā sarvato bhattiḥ śeṣaṃ garbhagṛhaṃ bhavet।।45।।

**English Translation:**

In a field rendered square shaped divided into of four parts, the wall all over is one parted one, the remaining may be the central chamber.

---

### Verse 56.46

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्याग्रत: पुन: कार्यो भागद्वयविनिर्गत:।
विस्तारेण त्रिभागश्च प्राग्रीव: स्तम्भभूषित:।।४६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasyāgrataḥ punaḥ kāryo bhāgadvayavinigataḥ।
vistāreṇa tribhāgaśca prāgrīvaḥ stambhabhūṣitaḥ।।46।।

**English Translation:**

In front of that again is to be created projected by a part twain, in breadth tripartite and an entrance hall decorated by a column.

---

### Verse 56.47

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पीठोत्सेधस्य भागेन भवेज्जङ्घा द्विभागिका।
भागार्धं + तरं पत्रं(?)² पादेन स्याद् वरण्डका।।४७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pīṭhotsedhasya bhāgena bhavejjaṅghā dvibhāgikā।
bhāgārdhaṃ + taraṃ patraṃ(?) pādena syād varaṇḍakā।।47।।

**English Translation:**

By the part of the elevation of the pedestal, the Jaṅghā maybe two digited one. The intermediary flank may be of half a part and by a quarter may be Varaṇḍikā.³

> **Footnote 2:** भागार्द्धं वान्तरं पत्रम्। (GOSE, p.325, fn.6)

> **Footnote 3:** A wall. V.S. Apte, p. 492.

---

### Verse 56.48

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सपादांश्चतुरो भागान् शिखरस्योच्छ्रय: स्मृत:।
त्रिगुणेन च सूत्रेण पद्मकोशं समालिखेत्।।४८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sapādāṃścaturo bhāgān śikharasyocchrāyaḥ smṛtaḥ।
triguṇena ca sūtreṇa padmakośaṃ samālikhet।।48।।

**English Translation:**

Of four parts and a quarter the height of the roof becomes known. And with a triple fold plumb, one may carve out Padmakośa.

---

### Verse 56.49

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्कन्धकोशान्तरं चास्य भागै: प्रविभजेत् त्रिभि:।
भवेद् ग्रीवार्धभागेन भागेनामलसारकम्।।४९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

skandhakośāntaraṃ cāsya bhāgaiḥ pravibhajet tribhiḥ।
bhaved grīvārdhabhāgena bhāgenāmalasārakam।।49।।

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the translation at the top of the page ("And of this one the space between the Skandha and Kośa, one may divide by three parts and by the half a part of grīvā and a part may be Amalasāraka¹.") belongs to a verse begun on the previous page.]

---

### Verse 56.50

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पद्यशीर्षं च भागार्धाद् भागेन (+ कलशः ?)स्मृतः।
इत्युक्तो रुचकाख्योऽयं
रुचकः।।
भद्रकाख्योऽथ कथ्यते।।५०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

padyaśīrṣaṃ ca bhāgārdhād bhāgena (+ kalaśaḥ ?) smṛtaḥ |
ityukto rucakākhyo'yaṃ
rucakaḥ ||
bhadrakākhyo'tha kathyate || 50 ||

**English Translation:**

This way has been illustrated Rucaka. Now Bhadraka one named as such is being illustrated Padmaśīrṣa is such as by a half of the part and by a part is known the Kalaśa.

> **Footnote 1:** Fluted oblate spheroid resembling an Āmalaka fruit which resting on a dado (kaṇṭha or beki) forms the crowning element of Nāgara and Rekhā temples. (p.148, Prāsādaśikhara)

---

### Verse 56.51

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

'भवेदुचक एवात्र भद्रो भद्रत्रयान्वितः।
भद्रं द्विभागविस्तारम् पदषड्भागनिर्गमम् ।।५१।।

भद्रकः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

'bhaveduca­ka evātra bhadro bhadratrayānvitaḥ |
bhadraṃ dvibhāgavistāram padaṣaḍbhāganir­gamam || 51 ||

bhadrakaḥ ||

**English Translation:**

The Rucaka here may be equipped with Bhadra triad Bhadra is broad by a part twain having projection of six parts. The space of water may be in the centre of Bhadra and Karṇa. There it may be called Haṃsa, a fascinating Prāsāda.

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE, p.325 fn.9.
> तोयान्तरं तु भद्रस्य मध्ये स्याद्र्दकर्णयोः।
> तदाहंस इति ख्यात: प्रासादोऽतिमनोहरः।। GOSE, p.325 fn.10.

---

### Verse 56.52

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

'भद्रं तु कर्णयोर्मध्ये कारयेदुदकान्तरम्।
तदा हंसो विजानीयात् प्रासादो देवतालयः।।५२।।

हंसः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

'bhadraṃ tu karṇayormadhye kārayedudakāntaram |
tadā haṃso vijānīyāt prāsādo devatālayaḥ || 52 ||

haṃsaḥ ||

**English Translation:**

The Bhadra one may get created as having an internal water channel in the centre of the flanks. Then one may take it to be as Haṃsa the Prāsāda as an abode of god i.e. a temple.

---

### Verse 56.53

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हंसस्येव यदा कुर्याद् भद्रस्यान्ते जलान्तरम्।
तदा हंसोद्भवो नाम प्रासादः परिकीर्तितः।।५३।।

हंसोद्भवः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

haṃsasyeva yadā kuryād bhadrasya­nte jalāntaram |
tadā haṃsodbhavo nāma prāsādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 53 ||

haṃsodbhavaḥ ||

**English Translation:**

Like that of a Haṃsa when one may create an internal water channel at the end of a Bhadra, then Haṃsodbhava named as such, a Prāsāda stands illustrated.

---

### Verse 56.54

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्थानकर्णयोष्ठैवं यदा स्यादुदकान्तरम्।
प्रतिहंसस्तदा प्रोक्तः प्रासादोऽयं मनोरमः।।५४।।

प्रतिहंसः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sthānakarṇayoṣṭhaivaṃ yadā syādudakāntaram |
pratihaṃsas tadā proktaḥ prāsādo'yaṃ manoramaḥ || 54 ||

pratihaṃsaḥ ||

**English Translation:**

Within the chariot shaped flank or a lane, when there may be a water channel,

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

**English Translation:**

Pratihaṃsa that stands declared this Prāsāda fascinating for the mind.

---

### Verse 56.55

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्राग्भीवा रुचकस्यैव सीमाविस्तारविस्तृता:।
निर्गता भद्रमानेन तदा नन्द: स उच्यते।।५५।।
नन्द:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāgbhīvā rucakasyaiva sīmāvistāravistṛtāḥ ।
nirgatā bhadramānena tadā nandaḥ sa ucyate ॥55॥
nandaḥ ॥

**English Translation:**

The entrance halls of Rucaka as such, when broad in line with the breadth or stretch of the boundary projected in line with the measure of Bhadra then Nanda as such is declared that (the Prāsāda).

---

### Verse 56.56

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्राग्भीवैर्भद्रमानेन नन्दो यदि विभूष्यते।
निर्गतैर्भागमानेन चतुरश्रै: समनत:।।५६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāgbhīvairbhadramānena nando yadi vibhūṣyate ।
nirgatairbhāgamānena caturaśraiḥ samanataḥ ॥56॥

**English Translation:**

If Nanda is decorated with entrance halls in line with the measure of Bhadra, with projected square shaped structures all around by the measure of Bhadra.

---

### Verse 56.57

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्राग्भीव: पुरत: कार्य: स्तम्भद्वयविभूषित:।
नन्द्यावर्तस्तदा प्रोक्त: प्रासादो विजयावह:।।५७।।
नन्द्यावर्त:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāgbhīvaḥ purataḥ kāryaḥ stambhadvayavibhūṣitaḥ ।
nandyāvartastadā proktaḥ prāsādo vijayāvahaḥ ॥57॥
nandyāvartaḥ ॥

**English Translation:**

The entrance hall in it then should be created decorated by column twain. The Nandyavartta then declared as such becomes the Prāsāda entailing a victory.

---

### Verse 56.58

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नन्द्यावर्ते यदा कुर्याद् भद्रान्ते जलनिर्गमम्।
धराधरस्तदा ज्ञेय: प्रासादो भुवनोत्तम:।।५८।।
धराधर:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

nandyāvarte yadā kuryād bhadrānte jalanirgamam ।
dharādharastadā jñeyaḥ prāsādo bhuvanottamaḥ ॥58॥
dharādharaḥ ॥

**English Translation:**

When in Nandyavartta one may create at the end of a Bhadra, a water outlet, then Dharādhara deserve to be known the Prāsāda the best one in the world.

---

### Verse 56.59

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दशधा भाजिते क्षेत्रे चतुरश्रे समनतत:।
भागद्वयेन कर्ण: स्याद् भजेच्छेषं च सप्तधा।।५९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

daśadhā bhājite kṣetre caturaśre samanataḥ ।
bhāgadvayena karṇaḥ syād bhajecchheṣaṃ ca saptadhā ॥59॥

**English Translation:**

In a plot divided into ten segments square shaped then by a part twain or sidereal structure may there be and one may divide it into seven parts.

---

### Verse 56.60

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागत्रयेण रथको मध्यमोऽस्य विधीयते।
द्राभ्यां द्राभ्यां तु भागाभ्यां रथकौ वामदक्षिणौ।।६०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgatrayeṇa rathako madhyamo'sya vidhīyate ।
drābhyāṃ drābhyāṃ tu bhāgābhyāṃ rathakau vāmadakṣiṇau ॥60॥

**English Translation:**

By a part triad Rathaka is provided here as a middling one. By two parts each, the two Rathikas are to the left and to the right.

---

### Verse 56.61

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागस्यैव त्रिभागेन भवेदस्य विनिर्गम:।
कथितो वर्धमानोऽयं
वर्धमान:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgasyaiva tribhāgena bhavedасya vinirgamaḥ ।
kathito vardhamāno'yaṃ
vardhamānaḥ ॥

### Verse 56.1

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गिरिकूटोऽथ कथ्यते॥६ १॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

girikūṭo'tha kathyate ॥6 1॥

**English Translation:**

By a digit triad of the single part, may there be its projection or exit door. This stands known as Vardhamāna. Now Girikūṭa is being illustrated.

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — only the tail of verse 60.1 is visible; the opening Devanagari pādas are on the preceding page.]

---

### Verse 56.2

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्धमानस्य भद्रस्थमध्यसूत्रेण योजयेत्।
कर्णिसूत्रं तदग्राभ्यां न्यस्येत् सूत्रचतुष्टयम्॥६ २॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vardhamānasya bhadrasthamadhyasūtreṇa yojayet ।
karṇisūtraṃ tadagrābhyāṃ nyasyet sūtracatuṣṭayam ॥6 2॥

**English Translation:**

By a plumb within one stationed in Bhadra of Vardhamāna one may attach a sidereal plumb here. By the two tips of that one, a plumb tetrad may be adjusted.

---

### Verse 56.3

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तदुत्पत्रैस्तु भद्रस्थकर्णैः स्याच्चित्रकूटकः।
गिरिकूट:॥
कर्णान्ते च रथाने च यदि स्यात् सलिलान्तरम्॥६ ३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tadutpatraistu bhadrasthakārṇaiḥ syāccitrakūṭakaḥ ।
girikūṭaḥ ॥
karṇānte ca rathāne ca yadi syāt salilāntaram ॥6 3॥

**English Translation:**

By the Karṇas stationed on the Bhadra developed from that, may be the (Prāsāda) Citrakūṭaka) (alias Girikūṭa). At the end of the Karṇa and Ratha if there be the space of water.

---

### Verse 56.4

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्धमानस्य भवति श्रीवत्स: स्यात् तदा शुभ:।
श्रीवत्स:॥
गिरिकूटस्य संस्थाने तदूपे विनिवेशिते॥६ ४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vardhamānasya bhavati śrīvatsaḥ syāt tadā śubhaḥ ।
śrīvatsaḥ ॥
girikūṭasya saṃsthāne tadūpe viniveśite ॥6 4॥

**English Translation:**

By a plumb within one stationed in Bhadra of Vardhamāna may attach a sidereal plumb here. By the two tips of that one, a plumb tetrad may be adjusted.

If Vardhamāna assumes form Śrīvatsa the auspicious one (Śrīvatsa) at the institution of the Girikūṭa having been incorporated in that shape.

---

### Verse 56.5

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णा(नु)प्रतिरथेष्वस्य निखिलेष्वपि योजयेत्।
प्राग्वत् प्रतिरथोद्धृतसूत्राभ्यां कर्णवर्त्मना॥६ ५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇā(nu)pratiratheṣvasya nikhileṣvapi yojayet ।
prāgvat pratirathod­dhṛtasūtrābhyāṃ karṇavartmanā ॥6 5॥

**English Translation:**

One may supply Karṇas in all the Pratirathas of this one. Like the earlier norm, by the two Sūtras shaken by Pratirathas, to enroute the Karṇas.

---

### Verse 56.6

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

त्रिकूटाख्यस्तदैव स्यात् प्रासादो देवतालयः।
त्रिकूट:॥
त्रिकूटस्यैव संस्थाने भद्रकर्णपरिच्युते॥६ ६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

trikūṭākhyastadaiva syāt prāsādo devatālayaḥ ।
trikūṭaḥ ॥
trikūṭasyaiva saṃsthāne bhadrakarṇaparicyute ॥6 6॥

**English Translation:**

Trikūṭa by name that may be as such a Prāsāda an abode of gods or a temple, in the very institution of Trikūṭa devoid of the Bhadra Karṇa.

---

### Verse 56.7

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्वरूपभद्रसंस्थाने मुक्तकोण: प्रजायते।
मुक्तकोण:॥
चतुर्भि(वि)स्तृतैर्भगैः क्षेत्रे पञ्चभिरायते॥६ ७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

svarūpabhadrasaṃsthāne muktakoṇaḥ prajāyate ।
muktakoṇaḥ ॥
caturbhi(vi)stṛtairbhagaiḥ kṣetre pañcabhirāyate ॥6 7॥

**English Translation:**

In the plot surface of the Svarūpabhadra Muktakoṇa gets generated (Muktakoṇa) by the

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

**English Translation:**

four stretched out parts in a plot oblong by five (parts).

### Verse 56.68

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेन भित्ति: कर्तव्या शेषं गर्भगृहं भवेत्।
अस्य क्षेत्रार्धसूत्रेण पृष्ठतो वृत्तमालिखेत्।।६८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgena bhittiḥ kartavyā śeṣaṃ garbhagṛhaṃ bhavet ।
asya kṣetrārdhasūtreṇa pṛṣṭhato vṛttamālikhet ।।68।।

**English Translation:**

A wall deserves to be created by a part. The remaining one may be the garbha gṛha. By the half plumb line of the plot on the rear, one may draw a circle.

---

### Verse 56.69

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पुरत: शूरसेनोऽस्य पृष्ठतोऽपि गजाकृति:।
प्रासादोऽयं गजो नाम गणेशस्य विधीयते।।६९।।
गज:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

purataḥ śūraseno'sya pṛṣṭhato'pi gajākṛtiḥ ।
prāsādo'yaṃ gajo nāma gaṇeśasya vidhīyate ।।69।।
gajaḥ ।।

**English Translation:**

In the front of this one Śūrasena and in the rear Gajākṛti or tusker shaped, the Prāsāda this one is named as Gaja of Gaṇeśa it is ordained as such.

---

### Verse 56.70

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्धमानस्य संस्थाने गरुडं विनिवेशयेत्।
तस्य पक्षौ विधातव्यौ प्रासादार्धेन निर्गतौ।।७०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vardhamānasya saṃsthāne garuḍaṃ viniveśayet ।
tasya pakṣau vidhātavyau prāsādārdhena nirgatau ।।70।।

**English Translation:**

In the spot of Vardhamāna one may introduce Garuḍa. The two flanks of that deserve to be created projected by a half of the Prāsāda.

---

### Verse 56.71

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पक्षयोस्तु दशकरंस्य(?) वर्धमानं विभाजयेत्।
जातिशुद्धा रथा: कार्या: पार्श्वयोर्गरुडो भवेत्।।७१।।
गरुड:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pakṣayostu daśakaraṃsya(?) vardhamānaṃ vibhājayet ।
jātiśuddhā rathāḥ kāryāḥ pārśvayorgaruḍo bhavet ।।71।।
garuḍaḥ ।।

**English Translation:**

Of the two flanks the Vardhamāna of ten cubits one may divide. The Rathas¹ pure by the species deserve to be created on the two flanks the Garuḍa (Prāsāda) may be there (Garuḍa).

> **Footnote 1:** Ratha- A type of building, a shrine, a temple (EOHA, p.475, 473).

---

### Verse 56.72

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्धमानस्य संस्थाने प्राग्वत् कर्णौ नियोजयेत्।
द्विभागा रथिका कार्या शेषं भद्रं प्रकल्पयेत्।।७२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vardhamānasya saṃsthāne prāgvat karṇau niyojayet ।
dvibhāgā rathikā kāryā śeṣaṃ bhadraṃ prakalpayet ।।72।।

**English Translation:**

On the Institution of the Vardhamāna one may join the two Karṇas as per earlier norm two parted Rathikās deserve to be created. The remaining one may be deemed as Bhadra.

---

### Verse 56.73

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घास्य पञ्चभिर्भागै: पीठं चास्य तदर्धत:।
विरण्डञ्चर्धांशिकाद्विश(?) भागश्चान्तरपत्रयो:।।७३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghāsya pañcabhirbhāgaiḥ pīṭhaṃ cāsya tadardhatḥ ।
viraṇḍañcārdhāṃśikādviśa(?) bhāgaścāntarapatrayoḥ ।।73।।

**English Translation:**

The Jaṅghā of this one by five parts and the Pīṭha by a half of that and the provision of the internal platforms by the half of the fraction of the Varaṇḍa (a projecting wall).

---

### Verse 56.74

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तथोत्सेधत्रिभागैश्च नवभि: शिखरोच्छ्रित:।
कुम्भश्यामलसारश्च सिंहेऽस्मिन्न(स्मिन् भा)गमाग(न)त:।।७४।।
सिंह:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tathotsedhātribhāgaiśca navabhiḥ śikharocchritaḥ ।
kumbhaśyāmalasāraśca siṃhe'sminn(asminn bhā)gamāga(na)taḥ ।।74।।
siṃhaḥ ।।

### Verse 56.75

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे चतुर्भिर्भाजिते: पदै:।
सीमाविस्तारमानेन रथांस्तस्य प्रकल्पयेत्।।७५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre caturbhirbhājiteḥ padaiḥ ।
sīmāvistāramānena rathāṃstasya prakalpayet ।।75।।

**English Translation:**

In a plot square shaped, divided by four digits by the measurement of the breadth of the boundary one may create the Rathas of that.

---

### Verse 56.76

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पादेनैकेन निर्यातान् दिक्षु सर्वास्वनुक्रमात्।
प्राग्ग्रीवान् पुनरस्यैव भागद्वितयविस्तृतान्।।७६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pādenaekena niryātān dikṣu sarvāsvanukramāt ।
prāggrīvān punarasyaiva bhāgadvitayavistṛtān ।।76।।

**English Translation:**

Projected by a single part in all the directions in descending order, the entrance halls broad by a part twain (one may create).

---

### Verse 56.77

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पद्षड्भागनिर्यातान् विदध्यीत चतुर्दिशम्।
चतुर्भागायतो गर्भ: कार्योऽस्य द्वयंशविस्तृत:।।७७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

padṣaḍbhāganiryātān vidadhyīta caturdiśam ।
caturbhāgāyato garbhaḥ kāryo'sya dvaṃśavistṛtaḥ ।।77।।

**English Translation:**

In four quarters one may create projected by six parts of a square, a central part oblong by four parts deserving to be created of this one, broad by part twain.

---

### Verse 56.78

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घोत्सेध्छ पीठं च यथा भद्रे तथा भवेत्।
प्रासादो भवसंज्ञोऽयं देवतात्रितयाश्रय:।।७८।।

भव:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghotsedh[IAST UNCERTAIN: cha] pīṭhaṃ ca yathā bhadre tathā bhavet ।
prāsādo bhasaṃjño'yaṃ devatātritayāśrayaḥ ।।78।।

bhavaḥ ।।

**English Translation:**

The elevation of Jaṅghā and the Pīṭha the way it may be in Bhadra, that very way in the Prāsāda that may be known as Bhava having the refuge of deity triad.

---

### Verse 56.79

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भवस्यैव यदा कुर्याद् रथान् सजलनिर्गमान्।
द्विभङ्गं संश्रयोऽन्य: सा(?)प्रासादो विभवाभिध:।।७९।।

(त्रिलिङ्ग: ?)

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhavasya eva yadā kuryād rathān sajalanirgamān ।
dvibhaṅgaṃ saṃśrayo'nyaḥ sā(?)prāsādo vibhavābhidhaḥ ।।79।।

(triliṅgaḥ ?)

**English Translation:**

Of Bhava as such, when one may create rathas having water outlets being a different one having resort of two bhaṅgas (culverts), that Prāsāda is one named as 'Vibhava'.

---

### Verse 56.80

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अष्ठा भाजिते क्षेत्रे चतुरश्रे समन्तत:।
विदध्याद् गर्भसूत्राणि कर्णसूत्राणि च क्रमात्।।८०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

aṣṭhā bhājite kṣetre caturaśre samantataḥ ।
vidadhyād garbhasūtrāṇi karṇasūtrāṇi ca kramāt ।।80।।

**English Translation:**

In a plot divided into eight parts a square shaped as such, all around one may provide the central plumbs (garbha sūtra) as also the flanked plumbs (Karṇasūtras) in due order.

---

### Verse 56.81

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दिक्सूत्रेष्वखिलेष्वस्य सीमार्धं पदमेव च।
पदस्याष्टादशो भागस्तद्योगाद् वृत्तमालिखेत्।।८१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

diksūtreṣvakhileṣvasya sīmārdhaṃ padameva ca ।
padasyāṣṭādaśo bhāgastadyogād vṛttamālikhet ।।81।।

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page] the side plumbs (diksūtriṇi) in entirety a square half in extent of the boundary, may. And eighteenth part of the pada, one may carve out as circular owing to the on of that.

---


### Verse 56.82

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्ताराधं भवेद् गर्भो गर्भार्धास्तस्य भित्तय:।
तद्वृत्तबाह्यसूत्रेण भागान् षोडश कारयेत्।।८२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistārārdhaṃ bhaved garbho garbhārdhāstasya bhittayaḥ ।
tadvṛttabāhyasūtreṇa bhāgān ṣoḍaśa kārayet ।।82।।

**English Translation:**

The centre may be half in extent of the breadth and the walls of that may be half in extent of the centre. By the external plumb of the circle of that one, one may arrange the creation of sixteen parts.

---

### Verse 56.83

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दिक्सूत्रकर्णसूत्रेषु रथकान् सम्प्रकल्पयेत्।
द्विभागा रथिका कार्या सलिलान्तरभूषिता।।८३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

diksūtrakarṇasūtreṣu rathakān samprakalpayeत् ।
dvibhāgā rathikā kāryā salilāntarabhūṣitā ।।83।।

**English Translation:**

In the diksūtras and Karṇasūtras one may create Rathakas suitably. The Rathikā is to be created as of two parts adorned by a water space.

### Verse 56.84

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

The water space of this one, one may create like that of Śrīvatsa. In the elevation of this Jaṅghā and in the Pīṭha the roof may be likewise of that very norm.

Mālādhāra or Mālādhara deserves to be known as that which may be equal in dimensions from within and without on the plot of the Mālādhara, the area that stands developed as per earlier norm.

Divested of the watery space, Padma (named) there one may introduce. And likewise in the front, one may make to be created, the outlets or exit doors by a half of the breadth of the Karṇa.

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

Having shapes equal in norm to the Padma patras pure in species endowed with auspicious marks, the six part in an oblonged field having breadth of a square norm.

### Verse 56.88

### Verse 56.104

Within the area of the Bhadraka, in a bhadra when there may be a roof, the (Prāsāda) named Subhadra this one, by Karṇakūṭas developed as such.

### Verse 56.106

Having left out the roof in Bhadra, one may get created Siṃha therein. In line with the combination of the combined one, of the two the combined one may be called the Siṃha Kesarī.

### Verse 56.111

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उत्तमाङ्गे च यस्तद्वत् स स्यात् सर्वाङ्गसुन्दर:॥१११॥

Just as on Karṇa likewise on Bhadra that may be like the Citrakūṭa, and as the highest limb that may be of the same norm that may be Sarvāṅga Sundara.

### Verse 56.112

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रतिशृङ्गेषु सर्वेषु यदा कूटं निवेश्यते।
मिश्रक: स तु विज्ञेय: श्रीनाम्ना चान्तिकोऽन्तिक:॥११२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pratiśṛṅgeṣu sarveṣu yadā kūṭaṃ niveśyate ।
miśrakaḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ śrīnāmnā cāntikoʼntikaḥ ॥112॥

**English Translation:**

On all the Pratiśṛṅgas, when one may introduce a Kūṭa¹, that one may be considered as Miśraka by name Śrī and Antika the Antika one.

> **Footnote 1:** Kūṭa- summit based top or peak, p.139 (EOHA).

---

### Verse 56.113

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वे कूटावृता: कार्या: सर्वे कार्याश्चतुर्मुखा:।
मिश्रका बहुशृङ्गश्च कुटिसंज्ञास्ततोऽपरे॥११३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarve kūṭāvṛtāḥ kāryāḥ sarve kāryāścaturmukhāḥ ।
miśrakā bahuśṛṅgaśca kuṭisaṃjñāstato'pare ॥113॥

**English Translation:**

All deserve to be created as covered by Kūṭas and all deserve to be created as facing the four quarters. Miśrakas and Bahūśṛṅgas and Kuṭisañjñ as the other ones.

---

### Verse 56.114

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इदं नवानां मिश्राणामिह लक्षणमीरितम्।
साधारणमत: स्पष्टं ब्रूम: सम्प्रति लक्षणम्॥११४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

idaṃ navānāṃ miśrāṇāmiha lakṣaṇamīritam ।
sādhāraṇamataḥ spaṣṭaṃ brūmaḥ samprati lakṣaṇam ॥114॥

**English Translation:**

This way of nine Miśras the definitions have been illustrated. And now the normal ones and evident ones we talk of the definition of that.

---

### Verse 56.115

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे भागाष्टकविभाजिते।
तस्य मध्ये भवेद् गर्भो द्विभागो देवतालय:॥११५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre bhāgāṣṭakavibhājite ।
tasya madhye bhaved garbho dvibhāgo devatālayaḥ ॥115॥

**English Translation:**

In a plot having been carved out as square shaped one divided into octagonal segments in the midst of that there may be a central segment two segmented one, a temple.

---

### Verse 56.116

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागे निवेशयेद् भित्तिं कुर्याद् भागेन कारिकाम्।
बाह्यभित्तिं पुनर्भागे विधेयास्तस्य सिद्धये(?)॥११६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāge niveśayed bhittiṃ kuryād bhāgena kārikām ।
bāhyabhittiṃ punarbhāge vidheyāstasya siddhaye(?) ॥116॥

**English Translation:**

In a part one may erect a wall and may carve out a segmentation by a part. An outer enclosure or a fencing wall then within apart becomes creatable or erectable, for the success of the designing.

---

### Verse 56.117

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्य कर्णेषु कर्तव्या रथिकाश्च द्विभागिका:।
शेषं भद्रं प्रकर्तव्यमुदकान्तरभूषितम्॥११७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasya karṇeṣu kartavyā rathikāśca dvibhāgikāḥ ।
śeṣaṃ bhadraṃ prakartavyamudakāntarabhūṣitam ॥117॥

**English Translation:**

On the flanks of that one deserve to be created rathikās two segmented ones. The residual Bhadra deserves to be caved out decorated by a water channel.

---

### Verse 56.118

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेन निर्गतं दिक्षु सर्वास्वेष भवेद् विधि:।
चतुर्भागोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा करकश्च तदर्धक:॥११८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgena nirgataṃ dikṣu sarvāsveṣa bhaved vidhiḥ ।
caturbhāgocchrītā jaṅghā karakaśca tadardhakaḥ ॥118॥

**English Translation:**

Projected by a part in the directions in entirety, such a situation may there be and

### Verse 56.119

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वरण्डयन्तरपत्रं च भागेनैकन कल्पयेत्।
रथिकैकान्तरं तस्य सार्धभागत्रयोच्छ्रिता:(?)॥११९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

varaṇḍayantarapatraṃ ca bhāgenaikana kalpayet।
rathikaikāntaraṃ tasya sārdhabhāgatrayocchritā:(?)॥119॥

**English Translation:**

The plank within the porch (Varaṇḍī) one may cave out by a part only and the space between the Rathikā of that one may be having an elevation of three and a half parts.

> **Footnote 1:** Karika- moulding of a coloumn, p.113 (EOHA).

---

### Verse 56.120

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्भागे शिखरं मूले शेषांशकसमुच्छ्रितम्।
तस्योच्छ्रयं त्रिधा कृत्वा वेणुकोशं समालिखेत्॥१२०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍbhāge śikharaṃ mūle śeṣāṃśakasamucchritam।
tasyocchrayaṃ tridhā kṛtvā veṇukośaṃ samālikhet॥120॥

**English Translation:**

In six parts the roof, having elevation of the residual parts at the root, having created threefold the height of that one may carve out a Veṇukośa (a bamboo enclosure).

---

### Verse 56.121

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्कन्धकोशान्तरं तस्य चतुर्धा विभजेत् ततः।
पद्यशीर्षं तथा ग्री(वां)सार्धेनांशेन कारयेत्॥१२१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

skandhakośāntaraṃ tasya caturdhā vibhajet tataḥ।
padyaśīrṣaṃ tathā grī(vāṃ)sārdhenaṃśena kārayet॥121॥

**English Translation:**

Then the space between the Skandha kośa of that one may divide into four segments. And Padmasīrṣa likewise and the grīvā one may make to be carved out by one and a half digit.

---

### Verse 56.122

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्याद् भागेन भागेन कुम्भं चामलसारकम्।
भागार्धेन प्रकुर्वीत् तदूर्ध्वं बीजपूरकम्॥१२२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kuryād bhāgena bhāgena kumbhaṃ cāmalasārakam।
bhāgārdhena prakurvīt tadūrdhvaṃ bījapūrakam॥122॥

**English Translation:**

By a part each one may create a Kumbha and Amalasāraka. And by half a part, one may carve out above that a Bijapūraka.

---

### Verse 56.123

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासाद: कैसरी नाम सर्वत: सन्तितिप्रिय:।
कैसरी।
चतुरश्रं समं कृत्वा भूमिभागं विचक्षण:॥१२३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādaḥ kaisarī nāma sarvataḥ santitipriayaḥ।
kaisarī।
caturaśraṃ samaṃ kṛtvā bhūmibhāgaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ॥123॥

[FLAG: The label "कैसरी।" appears as a sub-heading or colophon label between the two pādas; it may not be part of the verse proper.]

**English Translation:**

The Prāsāda named Kaisarī has an all round efficacy for the progeny. The skilled one having created a square segment of the land quite levelled as such.

---

### Verse 56.124

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादो(द)व्यासत: कुर्याज्जगतीं द्विगुणामिह।
विदध्याञ्जगतीपीठं प्रासादार्धसमुच्छ्रितम्॥१२४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādo(da)vyāsataḥ kuryājjagatīṃ dviguṇāmiha।
vidadhyāñjagatīpīṭhaṃ prāsādārdhasamucchritam॥124॥

**English Translation:**

One may create a land redoubled one as compared with the width of the Prāsāda and one may create the plinth of the land having elevation of the half of the Prāsāda.

---

### Verse 56.125

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पीठस्योपरि संस्थाय प्रासादं विभजेत् ततः।
सर्वतोभद्रसंस्थानं हस्तसंख्या यदा भवेत्॥१२५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pīṭhasyopari saṃsthāya prāsādaṃ vibhajet tataḥ।
sarvotobhadrasaṃsthānaṃ hastasaṃkhyā yadā bhavet॥125॥

**English Translation:**

Having erected the Prāsāda over the plinth one may bifurcate, the Institution of the Sarvatobhadra, when may have a counting of cubits.

### Verse 56.126

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हस्तै: सप्तत्रिंशता तु ज्येष्ठ: साऋँ(?) रदाहत:।
मध्यम: सप्तविंशत्या प्रासाद: स्यात् कलाधिकै:॥१२६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

hastaiḥ saptatriṃśatā tu jyeṣṭhaḥ sārddh(?) rdāhataḥ।
madhyamaḥ saptaviṃśatyā prāsādaḥ syāt kalādhikaiḥ॥126॥

**English Translation:**

By thirty seven cubits the Jyeṣṭha (named) is declared as such by a half extent. A medium sized Prāsāda may be by twenty seven cubits added by Kalās.

---

### Verse 56.127

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कनीयान् पञ्चदशभि: प्रासाद: समुदीरित:।
तलच्छन्दो यदा होषां तथा चैवोर्ध्वतो गतिम्॥१२७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kanīyān pañcadaśabhiḥ prāsādaḥ samudīritaḥ।
talacchando yadā hoṣāṃ tathā caivorddhvato gatim॥127॥

**English Translation:**

The lowest one, by fifteen cubits a Prāsāda is declared as such. The Talacchanda of their's the way it may be, that very way the upward movement.

---

### Verse 56.128

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ज्येष्ठमध्यकनिष्ठानां तथा सम्यङ् निगद्यते।
चतुर्श्रीकृते क्षेत्रे शतमूलविभाजिते॥१२८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre śatamūlavibhājite॥128॥

**English Translation:**

Of larger, middling and the lowest ones, (the structure) is being illustrated correctly. In a square shaped spot, divided into a hundred rooted plinths.

---

### Verse 56.129

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

न्यसेत् तन्मध्यतो गर्भं चतुर्वर्गपदान्वितम्।
गर्भपादेन भित्ति: स्यात् तद्देवान्धकारिका॥१२९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

nyaset tanmadhyato garbhaṃ caturvargapadānvitam।
garbhapādena bhattiḥ syāt tadddevāndhakārikā॥129॥

**English Translation:**

One may assort a central part in the centre equipped with four squared pedestals. The wall may be by a quarter of the central part and that very way an Andhakārikā the blind alley.

---

### Verse 56.130

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

बाह्याभिति:स्तथवा(थैव) स्याद् दशशास्युद्विभागिका(?)।
प्रतिवर्णपदांशेन(?) षोडशेन जलान्तरम्॥१३०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bāhyābhitiḥ stathaivā(thaiva) syād daśaśāsyudvibhāgikā(?)।
prativarṇapadāṃśena(?) ṣoḍaśena jalāntaram॥130॥

**English Translation:**

The outer fence may be likewise of ten segments having bifurcation and a water channel by a sixteen spaced track by the segmentation of all the Varṇas.

---

### Verse 56.131

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेषं भद्रं(?)प्रकर्तव्यं गर्भार्धेन विनिर्गतम्।
भागार्धं क्षोभयेत् पार्श्वे निर्गमं च तथाचरेत्॥१३१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣaṃ bhadraṃ(?) prakartavyaṃ garbhārdhena vinirgataṃ।
bhāgārdhaṃ kṣobhayet pārśve nirgamaṃ ca tathācaret॥131॥

**English Translation:**

The remaining bhadra deserves to be created projected out by a half of the central part. A part half one may get rendered turbulent on the flank, and the exit door one may create that way.

---

### Verse 56.132

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेष: स्याद् भद्रविस्तार: पञ्चभागायतस्तथा।
पीठं तस्यैव कर्तव्यं सार्धद्वयसमुच्छ्रितम्॥१३२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣaḥ syād bhadravistāraḥ pañcabhāgāyatastathā।
pīṭhaṃ tasyaiva kartavyaṃ sārdhadvatyasamucchhritam॥132॥

**English Translation:**

The width of the Bhadra as additional one may be oblong normed by five parts. The Pīṭha (i.e. the roof or pedestal) of that one deserves to be created having elevation of a couple of half plus dimensions.

---

### Verse 56.133

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विगुणां च तथा जङ्घामुच्छ्रायेणास्य कल्पयेत्।
मेखलामर्धभागेन भागेनान्त(र)पत्रकम्॥१३३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dviguṇāṃ ca tathā jaṅghāmucchrāyeṇāsya kalpayet।
mekhalāmardhabhāgena bhāgenānta(ra)patrakam॥133॥

### Verse 56.134

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रथमा रथिका तत्र कार्या भागत्रयोच्छ्रिता।
द्वितीया रथिका या सा सार्धभागेन चोच्छ्रिता॥१३४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prathamā rathikā tatra kāryā bhāgatrayocchritā |
dvitīyā rathikā yā sā sārdhabhāgena cocchritā || 134 ||

**English Translation:**

the first Rathikā thus deserves to be created as having an elevation of part triad and the second Rathikā that may there be that may be elevated by part of one and a half.

### Verse 56.135

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागे भागेऽन्तरं कार्यमुपर्युपरि चोभयोः।
षड्भागान् विस्तृतं कुर्याच्छिखरं सप्तमोच्छ्रितम्॥१३५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāge bhāge'ntaraṃ kāryam uparyupari cobhayoḥ |
ṣaḍbhāgān vistṛtaṃ kuryācchikharaṃ saptamocchritam || 135 ||

**English Translation:**

On the top of the both a space deserves to be left out, in each part. And the seventh elevated roof may be created having breadth of six parts.

---

### Verse 56.136

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एवं भूमिभिरष्टाभिः कुर्यादेनं विचक्षणः।
जलनिर्गमविच्छिन्ना रथाः प्रतिरथास्तथा॥१३६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

evaṃ bhūmibhiraṣṭābhiḥ kuryādenaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |
jalanirgamavicchinnā rathāḥ pratirathāstathā || 136 ||

**English Translation:**

This way by eight the expert may create this as such; divested of water emptying channels Rathas and Pratirathas.

---

### Verse 56.137

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्गुणैः पृथक्सूत्र(त्रैः) पद्मकोशं समालिखेत्।
मञ्जरी ललिता कार्या नीलोत्पलदलाकृतिः॥१३७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturguṇaiḥ pṛthaksūtra(traiḥ) padmakośaṃ samālikhet |
mañjarī lalitā kāryā nīlotpaladalaākṛtiḥ || 137 ||

**English Translation:**

By four fold separated plumbs, one may carve out Padmakośa and a mañjarī lovely one deserves to be created of the shape of a sprout as petal of blue lotus.

---

### Verse 56.138

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवा चैकार्धभागेन(भागेना)मलसारकम्।
पद्मशीर्षं च कर्तव्यं ग्रीवामानेन धीमता॥१३८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvā caikārdhabhāgena(bhāgenā)malasārakam |
padmaśīrṣaṃ ca kartavyaṃ grīvāmānena dhīmatā || 138 ||

**English Translation:**

The grīvā may be by a part half and Amalasāraka¹ by a part and the Padmasīrṣa deserves to be carved out by the measurement of the Grīvā, by the wise one.

> **Footnote 1:** Amalasāraka- gadrooned or fluted oblate splendid resembling an āmalak fruit resting on a dado (kaṇṭha or beki) forms the crowning element of Nāgara or Rekhā temple, p.148 (Prāsādaśikhara).

---

### Verse 56.139

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सार्धभागेन सोष्णीषः पद्यस्योपरिकुम्भकः।
सर्वतोभद्र इत्युक्तो रेषानाम्(?) एष शेखरः॥१३९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sārdhabhāgena soṣṇīṣaḥ padyasyopari kumbhakaḥ |
sarvatobhadra ityukto reṣānām(?) eṣa śekharaḥ || 139 ||

**English Translation:**

Accompanied by a turban shaped (globe) is the upper Kumbhaka of the Padyā ś Sarvatobhadra this way enunciated as such, this is Śekhara of the Rathis or prāsādas of the mansion.

---

### Verse 56.140

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विधाय सर्वतोभद्रं देवानामालयं शुभम्।
लभते परमं लोकं दिवि स्वच्छन्दभाषितम्॥१४०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vidhāya sarvatobhadraṃ devānāmālayaṃ śubham |
labhate paramaṃ lokaṃ divi svacchandabhāṣitam || 140 ||

**English Translation:**

Having created Sarvatobhadra, an auspicious temple of gods one gains the highest domain in the heaven proclaimed voluntarily.

---


### Verse 56.141

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वतोभद्र:।।
चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे दशधा प्रविभाजिते।
व्यासपादेन गर्भस्त(स्यात् त)दर्धादन्धकारिका।।१४१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvatabhadraḥ ।।
caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre daśadhā pravibhājite ।
vyāsapādena garbhasta(syāt ta)darddhādandhakārikā ।।141।।

**English Translation:**

In the field square shaped as such, divided into ten parts, the garbha may be by a part of Vyāsa and Andhakārkā by a half of that.

---

### Verse 56.142

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घा स्कन्धश्च तं कर्णं(?) भद्रं चाप्यस्य यद् भवेत्।
सर्वतोभद्रवत् सर्वं तद् विधेयं चतुर्दिशम्।।१४२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghā skandhaś ca taṃ karṇaṃ(?) bhadraṃ cāpy asya yad bhavet ।
sarvatabhadravat sarvaṃ tad vidheyaṃ caturdiśam ।।142।।

**English Translation:**

That Jaṅghā, Skandhaḥ, and Karṇa, Bhadra even that may be of this one, like Sarvatobhadra all that deserves to be created as four quartered one.

---

### Verse 56.143

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्य भद्राणि सर्वाणि भित्तिभि: परिवेष्टयेत्।
भद्रे भद्रे पुनश्चास्य वर्धमानं निवेशयेत्।।१४३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasya bhadrāṇi sarvāṇi bhittibhiḥ pariveṣṭayet ।
bhadre bhadre punaś cāsya vardhamānaṃ niveśayet ।।143।।

**English Translation:**

All the Bhadras of that one may surround all over by walls and on each Bhadra of this one may incorporate Vardhamāna.

---

### Verse 56.144

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चभागास्तथा सार्धि: शिखरस्योदयो भवेत्।
सर्वतोभद्रकाकारा रथिकाश्चात्र कारयेत्।।१४४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcabhāgāstathā sārdhi: śikharasyodayo bhavet ।
sarvatabhadrakākārā rathikāścātra kārayet ।।144।।

**English Translation:**

Five parted one, with half plus, the elevation of the roof may be there and one may make to be created Rathikās of the shape of Sarvatobhadra.

---

### Verse 56.145

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्यात् षडंशा विस्तीर्णंशिखरं प्र+धोच्छ्रितम्(?)।
सर्वतोभद्रसंस्थानादेषां चास्वत्र योजयेत्(?)।।१४५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kuryāt ṣaḍaṃśā vistīrṇaṃ śikharaṃ pra+dhocchritam(?) ।
sarvatabhadrasaṃsthānādeṣāṃ cāsvatra yojayet(?) ।।145।।

**English Translation:**

One may create six digited in breadth the Śikhara (roof), having seven heights or tops or stores and from the spot of Sarvatobhadra, its Preṣā (a projection) one may make to be incorporated.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE p.334 fn.4 has- कुर्यात् षडशानिस्तीर्णम् शिखरं सत्पचोच्छ्रितम्।

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE p.334 fn.5 has- सर्वतोभद्रसंस्थानां प्रेषां चास्य्प्रयोजयेत्।।

---

### Verse 56.146

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवा चामलसारं च कुम्भश्चापि तथा भवेत्।
प्रासादो नन्दनो नाम कर्तव्यो देवतालय:।।१४६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvā cāmalasāraṃ ca kumbhaścāpi tathā bhavet ।
prāsādo nandano nāma kartavyo devatālayaḥ ।।146।।

**English Translation:**

Grīvā, Amalasāraka and Kumbhaka may be likewise of the same standard. The Prāsāda named Nandana deserves to be created as an abode of gods i.e. temple.

---

### Verse 56.147

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कृतेऽस्मिन् नन्दति स्वामी दुरितानि च निर्दहेत्।
नन्दन:।।
भक्ते द्वादशधा क्षेत्रे चतुरश्रीकृते तत:।।१४७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kṛte'smin nandati svāmī duritāni ca nirdahet ।
nandanaḥ ।।
bhakte dvādaśadhā kṣetre caturaśrīkṛte tataḥ ।।147।।

**English Translation:**

On this one having been created, the owner gets enjoyment and may scare away sins. In

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

---

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the translation "the field divided into twelve parts and rendered square shaped likewise." belongs to a verse starting on the previous page.]

---

### Verse 56.148

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सप्तवर्गपदो गर्भो भित्त्या सह विधीयते।
सपादपादिका भित्तिगर्भे(?)कुर्याद् विचक्षण:॥१४८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

saptavargapado garbho bhittyā saha vidhīyate ।
sapādapādikā bhittigarbhe(?)kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥148॥

**English Translation:**

A garbha of seven square yards, is created along with the wall. The wall as having quarter normed yards, the skilled one may create in the centre.

---

### Verse 56.149

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

बाह्याभित्तिश्च तद्वत् स्यात् तद्वच्चाप्यन्धकारिका।
पीठोच्छ्रयस्तथा जङ्घा कर्णेषु रथिकाश्च या:॥१४९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bāhyābhittiśca tadvat syāt tadvaccāpyandhakārikā ।
pīṭhocchrāyastathā jaṅghā karṇeṣu rathikāśca yāḥ ॥149॥

**English Translation:**

The outer wall may be of the similar norm, and that very normed the blind alley (andhakārka). The height of the Pīṭha and likewise the Jaṅghā and in Karṇas Rathikās that are there.

---

### Verse 56.150

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वतोभद्रकाकारान्मूलकर्णाँश्च योजयेत्।
एकैकां रथिकां चान्यां विन्यसेत् पक्षयोर्द्वयो:॥१५०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvatobhadrakākārānmūlakarṇām̐śca yojayet ।
ekaikāṃ rathikāṃ cānyāṃ vinyaset pakṣayordvayoḥ ॥150॥

**English Translation:**

One may combine or adjoin Mūlakarṇas of the shape of Sarvatobhadraka and a single each Rathikā different one may one assort on the two flanks.

---

### Verse 56.151

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतस्रो रथिकाश्चैवं कर्णे कर्णे निवेशयेत्।
शेषो भद्रस्य विस्तार: स्वविस्ताराधनिर्गतः॥१५१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

catasro rathikāścaivaṃ karṇe karṇe niveśayet ।
śeṣo bhadrasya vistāraḥ svavistārārdhanirgatāḥ ॥151॥

**English Translation:**

Four Rathikās, that very way on each Karṇa one may introduce. The remaining one the breadth of the Bhadra projected out by a half of its own breadth.

---

### Verse 56.152

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भूषयेत् सिंहकर्णैश्च भद्रव्यासार्धमुन्नतैः।
विन्यसेच्छिखरं तत्र भागैर्विस्तृतमष्टभिः॥१५२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhūṣayet siṃhakarṇaiśca bhadravyāsārdhamunnataiḥ ।
vinyasecchikharaṃ tatra bhāgairvisṛtamaṣṭabhiḥ ॥152॥

**English Translation:**

One may decorate by the Siṃhakarṇas elevated by a half the size of the Bhadra and may assort the roof therein broad by eight parts.

---

### Verse 56.153

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्गुणेन सूत्रेण वेणुकोशं समालिखेत्।
स्कन्धकोशान्तरं चास्य त्रिभिभागैर्विभाजयेत्॥१५३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturguṇena sūtreṇa veṇukośaṃ samālikhet ।
skandhakośāntaraṃ cāsya tribhibhāgairvibhājayet ॥153॥

**English Translation:**

By a four pronged thread one may carve out a Veṇukośa and may divide by three parts the space between the Skandha and Kośa.

---

### Verse 56.154

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवार्धभागिकोत्सेधा(द्?)भागेनामलसारकः।
पद्मशीर्षं तथार्धेन भागेन कलशः स्मृतः॥१५४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvārdhabhāgikotsedha(d?)bhāgenāmalasārakaḥ ।
padmaśīrṣaṃ tathārdhena bhāgena kalaśaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥154॥

**English Translation:**

The Utsedha or elevation is to be by a half part of the grīvā and by a part Amalasāraka and Padmaśīrṣa a half of that known as Kalaśa¹.

> **Footnote 1:** Pot shaped finial (Prāsādaśikhara p.148).

---

### Verse 56.155

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

त्रिपादा रथिकास्तिस्र उच्छ्रायेण प्रकीर्तिता:।

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

**IAST Transliteration:**

tripādā rathikāstisra ucchrāyeṇa prakīrtitāḥ ।

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

---


### Verse 56.155

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वतोभद्रकाकारो नन्दिशाल: प्रकीर्तित:।।१५५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvato bhadrakākāro nandiśālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ।।155।।

**English Translation:**

The three Rathikās by a square triad in height declared as such and one shaped as Bhadraka the is known as Nandīśāla.

---

### Verse 56.156

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नन्दिशाल:।।
नन्दिशालस्य संस्थाने तदूपे समवस्थिते।
तस्य भद्राणि सर्वाणि भित्तिभि: परिवेष्टयेत्।।१५६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

nandiśālaḥ ।।
nandiśālasya saṃsthāne tadūpe samavasthite ।
tasya bhadrāṇi sarvāṇi bhittibhiḥ pariveṣṭayet ।।156।।

**English Translation:**

At the spot of Nandīśāla the framework of that situated at par, one may surround by wall all the bhadra of that.

---

### Verse 56.157

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रे भद्रे तस्यैव तस्य वर्धमानं निवेशयेत्।
अर्धषट्टांसतथा भागान् स्याद् भद्रशिखरोच्छ्रय:।।१५७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadre bhadre tasyaiva tasya vardhamānaṃ niveśayet ।
ardhaṣaṭṭāṃsatathā bhāgān syād bhadraśikharocchrāyaḥ ।।157।।

**English Translation:**

On every Bhadra of that and that, one may introduce Vardhamāna. And to the extent of sixth halves, may be the height of the Bhadraśikhara¹.

> **Footnote 1:** The top of a phallus or image, p.409 (EOHA).

---

### Verse 56.158

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पीठोच्छ्रायं च जङ्घां च तथास्य शिखरोच्छ्रयम्।
नन्दिशालसमाकारं सममेव प्रकल्पयेत्।।१५८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pīṭhocchrāyaṃ ca jaṅghāṃ ca tathāsya śikharocchrāyam ।
nandiśālasamākāraṃ samameva prakalpayet ।।158।।

**English Translation:**

Pīṭhaacchrāya and Jaṅghā and likewise the Śikharocchraya of this one, one having shape similar to that of Nandiśālā, one may create of the same size or on parallel norm.

---

### Verse 56.159

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कार्योऽयं सर्वदेवानां प्रासादो नन्दिवर्धन:।
नन्दिवर्धन:।।
नन्दिवर्धनसंस्थानं पूर्ववत् परिकल्पयेत्।।१५९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kāryo'yaṃ sarvadевānāṃ prāsādo nandivardhanaḥ ।
nandivardhanaḥ ।।
nandivardhana saṃsthānaṃ pūrvavat parikalpаyet ।।159।।

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: the repeated label 'नन्दिवर्धन:।।' appears to be a section marker mid-verse; transliterated accordingly.]

**English Translation:**

This Prāsāda Nandivardhana deserves to be created as one of all the gods. The spot of Nandivardhana one may create as done earlier.

---

### Verse 56.160

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उभयो: कर्णयोर्मध्ये ये तत्र रथिके स्थिते।
तयोश्चोपरि कर्तव्यं शिखरं लक्षणान्वितम्।।१६०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ubhayoḥ karṇayormadhye ye tatra rathike sthite ।
tayoścupari kartavyaṃ śikharaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam ।।160।।

**English Translation:**

In the centre of both the Karṇas the Rathikās that are stationed, on the upper part of the two deserves to be created Śikhara (a roof) equipped with marks.

---

### Verse 56.161

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षडंशविस्तृतं चैतत् सार्धषट्कसमुच्छितम्।
चतुर्गुणेन सूत्रेण वेणुकोशं समालिखेत्।।१६१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍaṃśavistṛtaṃ caitat sārdhaṣaṭkasamuccitam ।
caturguṇena sūtreṇa veṇukośaṃ samālikhet ।।161।।

**English Translation:**

Broad by six digits and having height of six plus half, by a four time in extent a plumb or thread, one may carve out Veṇukośa.

---

### Verse 56.162

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवा चामलसारं च कुम्भकस्याश्रयो भवेत्।
कार्य: स सर्वतोभद्रसंस्थान इति निश्चय:।।१६२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvā cāmalasāraṃ ca kumbhakasyāśrayo bhavet ।
kāryaḥ sa sarvato bhadrasaṃsthāna iti niścayaḥ ।।162।।

**English Translation:**

### Verse 56.163

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मन्दिरोऽयमिति ख्यातः प्रासादः क्षितिभूषणः।
मन्दिरः॥
नन्दिवर्धनसंस्थाने तद्रूपसमवस्थिते।।१६३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mandiro'yamiti khyātaḥ prāsādaḥ kṣitibhūṣaṇaḥ |
mandiraḥ ||
nandivardhanaṃsaṃsthāne tadrūpasamavasthite || 163 ||

**English Translation:**

This temple is known as Kṣitibhūṣaṇa Prāsāda or Mandara by name is well known this Prāsāda as an ornament of the Earth. In the spot of Nandi Vardhana, having situation equal in norm to that of the Nandivardhana.

---

### Verse 56.164

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दिक्सूत्रे कर्णसूत्रे च (कुम्भ?) + रथिकाष्टकम्'।
रथिका अपि चैताः स्युर्द्विभागायतविस्तृताः।।१६४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

diksūtre karṇasūtre ca (kumbha?) + rathikāṣṭakam |
rathikā api caitāḥ syurdvibhāgāyataviṣtṛtāḥ || 164 ||

**English Translation:**

On the Diksūtra and Karṇasūtra Rathikā octagon deserves to be created. And these Rathikās may also be having breadth and length of two parts.

> **Footnote 1:** कुर्वीत रथाष्टकम्।

---

### Verse 56.165

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्भागविस्तृतिभ्रास्य शेषं शिखरमाचरेत्।
उच्छ्रयभ्रास्य कर्तव्यो भागानां सार्धसप्तकम्।।१६५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍbhāgavistṛtibhrāsya śeṣaṃ śikharamācaret |
ucchrayabhrāsya kartavyo bhāgānāṃ sārdhasaptakam || 165 ||

**English Translation:**

And the breadth of this one having six parts may be there and the remaining one may be employed on the roof and the height of this one deserves to be created of parts seven and a half.

---

### Verse 56.166

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्भागः स्कन्धविस्तारो ग्रीवा चास्य द्विभागिका।
रेखा चामलसारं च कलशश्चात्र यो भवेत्।।१६६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍbhāgaḥ skandhavistaāro grīvā cāsya dvibhāgikā |
rekhā cāmalasāraṃ ca kalaśaścātra yo bhavet || 166 ||

**English Translation:**

The six digited breadth of the Skandha and its grīvā of two digits. The Rekhā and Amalasāraka of this one and the Kalaśa of this one that may be.

---

### Verse 56.167

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वतोभद्रवत् स स्याच्छ्रीवृक्षोऽयमुदाहृतः।
श्रीवृक्षः॥
चतुःश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे चतुर्दशविभाजिते।।१६७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvatobhadravat sa syācchṛīvṛkṣo'yamudāhṛtaḥ |
śrīvṛkṣaḥ ||
catuḥśrīkṛte kṣetre caturdaśavibhājite || 167 ||

**English Translation:**

Like Sarvatobhadra that may be described as Śrīvṛkṣa. In the field square shaped as such divided into fourteen digits.

---

### Verse 56.168

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभागविस्तृताः कर्णा रथिकास्तेषु कारयेत्।
उदकान्तरविच्छिन्ना मूलकर्णेषु योजयेत्।।१६८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhāgavistṛtāḥ karṇā rathikāsteṣu kārayet |
udakāntaravicchinnā mūlakarṇeṣu yojayet || 168 ||

**English Translation:**

The Karṇas having breadth of two digits the Rathikās therein one may make to be created and divested of water channels may conjoin to the basic Karṇas.

---

### Verse 56.169

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेषं भद्रस्य विस्तारसतदर्धमपि निर्गमः।
सर्वतोभद्रमप्यस्य भद्रे भद्रे विभज्य च।।१६९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣaṃ bhadrasya vistārasatadardhamapi nirgamaḥ |
sarvatobhadramapyasya bhadre bhadre vibhajya ca || 169 ||

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

The remaining one is the breadth of the Bhadra nad a half of that is the exit door and on every Bhadra, even the Sarvatobhadra of this one, having divided.

---

### Verse 56.170

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पूर्वैर्गुणैस्तु संयुक्ते चतुर्दिक्षु निवेशयेत्।
तस्य गर्भस्तु कर्तव्य: (पदाष्टक?)विस्तृत:।।१७०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pūrvairguṇaistu saṃyukte caturdiakṣu niveśayet ।
tasya garbhastu kartavyaḥ (padāṣṭaka?)vistṛtaḥ ॥170॥

**English Translation:**

In one equipped with earlier qualities one may introduce in the four quarters, and the centre (garbha) of that which deserves to be created broad by eight steps.

---

### Verse 56.171

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सार्धभागप्रमाण: स्याद् भित्तिगर्भस्य मध्यत:।
बाह्यभित्तिस्तथैवास्य शेषं भ्रमणमाचरेत्।।१७१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sārdhabāgapramāṇaḥ syād bhittigarbhasya madhyataḥ ।
bāhyabhittistathāivāsya śeṣaṃ bhramaṇamācaret ॥171॥

**English Translation:**

The wall of the centre may be of the extent of a part plus half and the outer wall of this one may be of that norm and the remaining one may create as a circular one.

---

### Verse 56.172

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घाषड्भागमुत्सेधात् पीठं तस्य तदर्धत:।
वरण्डीना(म्)तरं पत्रं भागेनैकेन कारयेत्।।१७२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghāṣaḍbhāgamutsedhāt pīṭhaṃ tasya tadardhatāḥ ।
varaṇḍīnā(m)taraṃ patraṃ bhāgenaikena kārayet ॥172॥

**English Translation:**

The Jaṅghā may be six digited from the elevation (Utsedha) and the Pīṭha may be a half of that. And the Pattra within the Varaṇḍis one may get created by a part.

---

### Verse 56.173

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रथिकाद्वादशविस्तारानुपर्युपरि योजिता(?)।
तिस्रस्तिस्रो निवेशा: (श्या:)स्यु: कर्णे कर्णेत(य)थाक्रमम्।।१७३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

rathikādvādaśavistārānuparyupari yojitā(?) ।
tisrastisro niveśāḥ (śyāḥ)syuḥ karṇe karṇeta(ya)thākramam ॥173॥

**English Translation:**

Rathikās of twelve normed breadth created on the tops three each may be creatable in each Karṇa as per descending order.

---

### Verse 56.174

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रथमा रथिकास्तस्य कुर्याद् भागत्रयोच्छिता:।
कुर्याटु(पर्यु)पर्यन्या: पादपादिवविर्जिता:।।१७४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prathamā rathikāstasya kuryād bhāgatrayocchitāḥ ।
kuryāṭu(paryu)paryanyāḥ pādapādivavirjitāḥ ॥174॥

**English Translation:**

The first Rathikās of that one, one may create elevated by three parts, one may create over and over ahead this one reduced by a part each.

---

### Verse 56.175

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अष्टभिर्विस्तृतं भागै: सार्धेन्नवभिरुच्छ्रितम्।
सर्वतोभद्रकाकारं शिखरं तस्य कारयेत्।।१७५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

aṣṭabhirvistṛtaṃ bhāgaiḥ sārdhennavabhirucchritam ।
sarvatobhadrakākāraṃ śikharaṃ tasya kārayet ॥175॥

**English Translation:**

By eight digits broad and elevated by nine and half parts, a roof of the shape of Sarvatobhadra one may make to be carried out.

---

### Verse 56.176

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादोऽयं विमानाख्य: प्रख्यातश्चामृतोद्भव:।
विमान:।।
द्विसप्तायामविस्तारं हिमवन्तं विभाजयेत्।।१७६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādo'yaṃ vimānākhyaḥ prakhyātaścāmṛtodbhavaḥ ।
vimānaḥ ॥
dvisaptāyāmavistāraṃ himavantaṃ vibhājayet ॥176॥

**English Translation:**

This Prāsāda known as Vimāna stands well known as Amṛtodbhava i.e. born of nectar and one may get the division done of Himavanta of length of seventy two (cubits).

---

### Verse 56.177

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्धा रथिकास्तत्र कर्णे कर्णे निवेशयेत्।
द्विभागविस्तृता: सर्वा उपर्युपरि कारयेत्।।१७७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturdhā rathikāstatra karṇe karṇe niveśayet ।
dvibhāgavistṛtāḥ sarvā uparyupari kārayet ॥177॥

### Verse 56.178

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रथमा भूमिका तस्य स्याच्च भागत्रयोच्छ्रिता।
पादपादविहीनास्तु क्रमणोपरिभूमय:॥१७८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prathamā bhūmikā tasya syācca bhāgatrayocchritā।
pādapādavihīnāstu kramaṇoparibhūmayaḥ॥178॥

**English Translation:**

The first storey of that one may be having height of a part triad and in ascending order, the upper storeys as reduced by a square each.

---

### Verse 56.179

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नन्दिशालगुणैर्युक्तं शिखरं चात्र कारयेत्।
सर्वतोभद्रवन्मध्ये भूमिकाश्च समाचरेत्॥१७९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

nandiśālaguṇairyuktaṃ śikharaṃ cātra kārayet।
sarvatobhadravanmadhye bhūmikāśca samācaret॥179॥

**English Translation:**

And here one may create a roof equipped with Nandiśālaguṇas or merits of Nandiśāla and like the Sarvatobhadra, in the centre one may activise the storeys.

---

### Verse 56.180

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभागा रथिकास्तस्य सर्वे(वो) भागत्रयोच्छ्रिता:।
द्वितीयभूमि(का?)रथिका भूम्युश(च्छा)येण कारयेत्॥१८०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhāgā rathikāstasya sarve(vo) bhāgatrayocchritāḥ।
dvitīyabhūmi(kā?)rathikā bhūmyuśa(cchā)yeṇa kārayet॥180॥

**English Translation:**

Part twained Rathikās therein of that one all of them elevated by part triad, and the Rathikā of the second storey one may get created in line with the height of the storey.

---

### Verse 56.181

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शिखरस्योच्छ्रय: कार्य: सपादव्याससंमित:।
अमृतोद्धवज्जङ्घा पीठं चात्र तथा भवेत्॥१८१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śikharasyocchrayaḥ kāryaḥ sapādavyāsasaṃmitaḥ।
amṛtodbhavajjaṅghā pīṭhaṃ cātra tathā bhavet॥181॥

**English Translation:**

The elevation of the roof deserves to be created equal in extent of the breadth of a quarter size. And the Jaṅghā may be like that of the Amṛtodbhava and likewise the Pīṭha here.

---

### Verse 56.182

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जातिशुद्धो भवत्येष हिमवान् भुवनोत्तम:।
हिमवान्॥
हिमाचलस्य संस्थाने तदूपे समवस्थिते॥१८२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jātiśuddho bhavatyeṣa himavān bhuvanottamaḥ।
himavān॥
himācalasya saṃsthāne tadūpe samavasthite॥182॥

**English Translation:**

Pure in quality becomes this Himavān the excellent mansion and over the spot of the Himācala, being of that shape and well designed.

---

### Verse 56.183

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्य भद्रेषु सर्वेषु वर्धमानं च योजयेत्।
भागषट्कप्रविस्तारं तदर्धेन विनिर्गतम्॥१८३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasya bhadreṣu sarveṣu vardhamānaṃ ca yojayet।
bhāgaṣaṭkapravistāraṃ tadardhen vinirgataṃ॥183॥

**English Translation:**

In all the bhadras of that one, one may combine Vardhamāna projected out by a half of the breadth of six parts.

---

### Verse 56.184

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागै: सप्तभिरप्यस्य साधैं: स्याच्छिखरोच्छ्रय:।
शिखरस्याग्रत: स्तम्भ:(मं)सिंहकर्णे वि(णैर्वि)भूषयेत्॥१८४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgaiḥ saptabhirapyasya sārdheḥ syācchikharocchrayaḥ।
śikharasyāgrataḥ stambhaḥ(maṃ)siṃhakarṇe vi(ṇairvi)bhūṣayet॥184॥

**English Translation:**

The height of the roof even may be by seven parts plus a half on the front of the roof one may decorate a column on the Siṃhakarṇa.

---

### Verse 56.185

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दिक्सूत्रैरस्य सर्वेषु क्रियां प्राग्वत् प्रकल्पयेत्।

**IAST Transliteration:**

dikasūtrairasya sarveṣu kriyāṃ prāgvat prakalpayet।

---


### Verse 56.185

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घोत्सेधश्च कर्णश्च शिखरं चास्य यद् भवेत्॥१८५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghotsedhaśca karṇaśca śikharaṃ cāsya yad bhavet ॥185॥

**English Translation:**

On all the Diksūtras of this one the activity one may devise as that of the earlier one. Elevation of the Jaṅghā Karṇa and Śikhara of this one that may be.

---

### Verse 56.186

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हिमवत्सदृशं सर्वं विधेयं तद् विजानता।
हेमकूट इति ख्यात: प्रासादोऽयं जगत्त्रये॥१८६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

himavatsadṛśaṃ sarvaṃ vidheyaṃ tad vijānatā ।
hemakūṭa iti khyātaḥ prāsādo'yaṃ jagattraye ॥186॥

**English Translation:**

Like snow clad mountain all that deserves to be created by an expert Hemakūṭa this way well known, this is the Prāsāda in the world triad.

---

### Verse 56.187

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एष त्रिमूर्तिनिलय: कार्यो नान्यस्य कस्यचित्।
हेमकूट:॥
हिमवतुल्यसंस्थानं प्रासादं परिकल्पयेत्॥१८७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

eṣa trimūrtinilayaḥ kāryo nānyasya kasyacit ।
hemakūṭaḥ ॥
himavatulyasaṃsthānaṃ prāsādaṃ parikalpayет ॥187॥

[FLAG: The verse appears to have "हेमकूट:॥" as an interposed label/gloss between the two halves of the verse.]

**English Translation:**

This an abode of frame (lit. image)¹ triad one deserves to be created for no one else. Like snow clad mountain, styled institution one may create a mansion.

> **Footnote 1:** Hindu Trinity, Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Maheśa. V.S. Apte, p. 241. त्रिमूर्ति - Trinity.

---

### Verse 56.188

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्य मध्ये विधातव्य: सर्वतोभद्रसंज्ञित:।
वर्जनीयं तु तन्मध्ये वर्धमाननिवेशनम्॥१८८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasya madhye vidhātavyaḥ sarvatobhadrasaṃjñitaḥ ।
varjanīyaṃ tu tanmadhye vardhamānanivedanam ॥188॥

[FLAG: The last word may be वर्धमाननिवेशनम् — reading as nivedanam vs. niveśanam; visually appears to be निवेशनम्]

**English Translation:**

In the centre of that deserves to be created one named Sarvatobhadra. The introduction of Vardhamāna deserves to be ignored in the centre of that.

---

### Verse 56.189

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तत: स्थानेषु सर्वेषु खण्डरेखा निवेशयेत्।
व्यासोच्छिखैस्तत: सिंहकर्णैर्भद्रं विभूषयेत्॥१८९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tataḥ sthāneṣu sarveṣu khaṇḍarekhā niveśayet ।
vyāsocchikhaistataḥ siṃhakarṇairbhadraṃ vibhūṣayet ॥189॥

[FLAG: व्यासोच्छिखैस्तत: — second pāda reading uncertain; may be व्यासोच्छ्रैस्तत: or similar; visually reading as व्यासोच्छिखैस्तत:]

**English Translation:**

Then on all the spots one may introduce Khaṇḍarekhā and may decorate Bhadra with Karṇas having height in line with the breadth.

---

### Verse 56.190

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वं च शिखरं तस्य वर्जनीयं विचक्षणै:।
द्वे द्वे च रथिके कार्ये सपादद्वंशकोच्छिती॥१९०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūrdhvaṃ ca śikharaṃ tasya varjanīyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।
dve dve ca rathike kārye sapādadvāṃśakocchriti ॥190॥

[FLAG: सपादद्वंशकोच्छिती — reading uncertain; may be सपादद्वंशकोच्छ्रिती]

**English Translation:**

By the experts the top roof deserves to be ignored and two Rathikās each by a height of two digits added by a quarter.

---

### Verse 56.191

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तयोश्चोपरि विस्ताराच्छिखरं चतुरश्रकम्।
उच्छ्रय: पञ्चभि: साधैर्विधेय: शिखरस्य च॥१९१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tayoścopari vistārācchikharaṃ caturaśrakam ।
ucchrāyaḥ pañcabhiḥ sārdhairvidheyaḥ śikharasya ca ॥191॥

**English Translation:**

A square shaped roof breadth wise over these two having height by five added to half, deserves to be created of the roof.

---

### Verse 56.192

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दिक्सूत्रेषु च सर्वेषु क्रियामेवं प्रकल्पयेत्।
बाहारेखा तु जङ्घा च हिमवत्सदृशी स्मृता॥१९२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

diksūtreṣu ca sarveṣu kriyāmevaṃ prakalpayет ।
bāhārekhā tu jaṅghā ca himavatsadṛśī smṛtā ॥192॥

[FLAG: बाहारेखा — may be बाह्यरेखा; visually appears as बाहारेखा but translation reads "bāhyarekhā"]

**English Translation:**

In all the Diksūtras one may design a structure and the Jaṅghā having a bāhyarekhā known as of the style of Himavat (Himālaya).

---

### Verse 56.193

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कैलासोऽयमिति ख्यात: कर्तव्य: शूलपाणये।
कैलास:।।
एतस्यैव यदा भद्रमुच्छितं सिंहकर्णकै:।।१९३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

Kailāso'yamiti khyātaḥ kartavyaḥ śūlapāṇaye।
Kailāsaḥ।।
Etasyaiva yadā bhadramucchitaṃ siṃhakarṇakaiḥ।।193।।

**English Translation:**

Kailāśa this one is known deserves to be created for the trident wielding one i.e. Śiva. Of this very one which there is a bhadra elevated by Siṃhakarṇakas.

---

### Verse 56.194

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वे द्वे च रथिके तत्र दीय(ये)ते सुमनोरमे।
शेष: शिखरविस्तार: पञ्चभागसमुच्छित:।।१९४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

Dve dve ca rathike tatra dīya(ye)te sumanorāme।
Śeṣaḥ śikharavistāraḥ pañcabhāgasamucchitaḥ।।194।।

**English Translation:**

Two individual Rathikās therein are provided beautiful to the utmost extent. By the remaining ones the breadth of the Śikhara or roof, elevated to the extent of five parts.

---

### Verse 56.195

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्राग्ग्रीवकाश्च भद्रेषु भागभागविनिर्गता:।
विस्तारेण चतुर्भागा दिक्षु सर्वास्वयं विधि:।।१९५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

Prāggrīvakāśca bhadreṣu bhāgabhāgavinirgatāḥ।
Vistāreṇa caturbhāgā dikṣu sarvāsvayaṃ vidhiḥ।।195।।

**English Translation:**

And the entrance halls or balconies or porches or porticoes on the Bhadras projected part wise by width as four digited ones. This is the methodology in all the quarters or sites.

---

### Verse 56.196

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमानसदृशी चास्य बाह्यलेखा विधीयते।
गुणैरेभिस्तदा युक्त: प्रासाद: पृथिवीजय:।।१९६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

Vimānasadṛśī cāsya bāhyalekhā vidhīyate।
Guṇairebhistadā yuktaḥ prāsādaḥ pṛthivījayaḥ।।196।।

**English Translation:**

Broad by eight parts and elevated by nine and half having shape of the Sarvatobhadra, one may create the roof of that one.

---

### Verse 56.197

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पृथिवीजय:।।
भक्ते षोडशिभ: क्षेत्रे चतुरश्रे समन्तत:।
गर्भोऽष्टवर्ग: स्यात् तस्य मध्ये भित्तिद्विभागिका।।१९७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

Pṛthivījayaḥ।।
Bhakte ṣoḍaśibhiḥ kṣetre caturaśre samantataḥ।
Garbho'ṣṭavargaḥ syāt tasya madhye bhittidvibhāgikā।।197।।

**English Translation:**

In a field divided into sixteen digits being square shaped all over, the centre may be of eight vargas and in the midst of that a wall of two parts.

---

### Verse 56.198

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भ्रमणं बाह्यभित्तिश्च तत्समे एव कीर्तिते।
कर्णेषु रथिका कार्या सलिलान्तरभूषिता।।१९८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

Bhramaṇaṃ bāhyabhittiśca tatsame eva kīrtite।
Karṇeṣu rathikā kāryā salilāntarabhūṣitā।।198।।

**English Translation:**

The Brāhmaṇa or promenading spot and the outer wall, are taken to be of the same structure. In Karṇas Rathikās deserve to be created decorated within the interior of water or space of water.

---

### Verse 56.199

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तत्तुल्यायामविस्तारा रथिका: स्युस्तथापरा:।
तद्वत् तृतीयरथिका भद्रं चतुष्पदायतम्।।१९९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

Tattūlyāyāmavistārā rathikāḥ syustathāparāḥ।
Tadvat tṛtīyarathikā bhadraṃ catuṣpadāyatam।।199।।

**English Translation:**

Having breadth and length of the similar norm, may there be Rathikās different ones. That very way this third Rathikā and a bhadra oblong by four pādas.

---

### Verse 56.200

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्ताराधेन निष्कान्तं क्षोभयेद् वर्धमानत:।
वरण्डान्तरपत्रे च सार्धे(र्धे)भागेन कारयेत्।।२००।।

Vistārādhena niṣkāntaṃ kṣobhayed vardhamānataḥ।
Varaṇḍāntarapatre ca sārdhe(rdhe)bhāgena kārayet।।200।।

### Verse 56.201

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उपर्युपरि भागान् हि हीना: स्यु क्रमशोभुव: (?)।
भद्रे रथिकयोर्मध्ये सिंहकर्णो विधीयते॥२०१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

uparyupari bhāgān hi hīnāḥ syu kramaśobhuvaḥ (?) ।
bhadre rathikayormadhye siṃhakarṇo vidhīyate ॥201॥

**English Translation:**

Over and above the parts the bhuvas the terrains may be reduced in serial order. In a Bhadra in the centre of Rathikās, two Siṃhakarṇas are provided.

### Verse 56.202

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एतस्य चोच्छ्रयो भागै: पञ्चभि: परिकीर्तित:।
पार्श्र्स्थे सिंहकर्णस्थरथिके ये निवेशिते॥२०२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pārśvasthe siṃhakarṇastharathike ye niveśite ॥202॥

**English Translation:**

Of this one the height by digits five as such stands enunciated. The flank normed Rathikās (two) stationed on Siṃhakarṇas which stand incorporated.

### Verse 56.203

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विधेयमुच्छ्रयेणैतत् त्रिभागान् सप्ताथ(प)वाधिकान्॥२०३॥

**English Translation:**

On the upper part of the two, six digited broad, the roof may there be. By elevation it deserves to be created, upto three parts or seven or more.

### Verse 56.204

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सिंहं निवेशयेद् दिक्षु निखिलास्वप्ययं विधि:॥२०४॥

**English Translation:**

In the both flanks of that the two Rathikās and above that Siṃha one may incorporate in all the directions. This is the process as such.

### Verse 56.205

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मूलकर्णे तत्त्र्यार्धं शिखरं दशविस्तृतम्।

**English Translation:**

In the Mūlakarṇa and likewise in the half the roof, may be of ten breadths. By the systematic circulation deserves to be created as elevated by eleven parts being fascinating to the mind.

Ratnakūṭa is well proclaimed the temple of Śrīpati i.e. Viṣṇu. In a square shaped plot, divided into twenty digits.

The first storey of this one deserves to be created as elevated by a part triad and the other storeys reduced by a quarter norm each.

### Verse 56.231

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

Mukuṭojjvala named as such, the mansion this one is the abode of gods, in this very placement of this one, when in every Bhadra in the four quarters.

### Verse 56.232

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सिंहकर्णं परित्यज्य वर्धमानो विधीयते।
ऊर्ध्वाभवतृतीयाया:(?)¹ सप्तोच्छ्रायषडायता:॥२३२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

siṃhakarṇaṃ parityajya vardhamāno vidhīyate।
ūrdhvābhatṛtīyāyā(?)¹ saptocchrāyaṣaḍāyatāḥ॥232॥

**English Translation:**

Having evaded Siṃhakarṇa, Vardhamāna is provided of the Ūrdhvabhava as the third one, the septagonal height and hexagonal length.

> **Footnote 1:** ऊर्ध्व भवस्तृतीया: fn. 2, p.342 (GOSE).

### Verse 56.233

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऐरावतोऽयं कर्तव्यः प्रासादस्त्रिदशेसति:(शितु:)।
ऐरावत:॥
ऐरातवतस्य संस्थाने प्रासादे पूर्ववत् स्थिते॥२३३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

airāvato'yaṃ kartavyaḥ prāsādas tridaśesati(śituḥ)।
airāvataḥ॥
airātavatasya saṃsthāne prāsāde pūrvavat sthite॥233॥

**English Translation:**

Airāvata this one deserves to be created the temple of the Lord of gods i.e. Indra. The plot of Airāvata standing as a temple in the earlier norm.

---

### Verse 56.234

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वर्धमानं विहायोर्धे यदा सिंहो निवेश्यते।
शिखराणि च चत्वारि दिक्षु सर्वासु वर्जयेत्॥२३४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vardhamānaṃ vihāyordhe yadā siṃho niveśyate।
śikharāṇi ca catvāri dikṣu sarvāsu varjayet॥234॥

**English Translation:**

Having evaded Vardhamāna, on the roof when Siṃha is incorporated and the top four as such, one may evade in all the directions.

---

### Verse 56.235

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

क्षेत्राया(शमे?)विस्तारं गर्भवेश्म निवेशयेत्।
चतुर्भागायतं भद्रं निर्गमेण विभाजितम्॥२३५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kṣetrāyā(śame?)vistāraṃ garbhaveśma niveśayet।
caturbhāgāyataṃ bhadraṃ nirgameṇa vibhājitam॥235॥

**English Translation:**

Within the field within octagonal breadth, one may introduce the central apartment with four parted oblong Bhadra, divided by an outlet or an exit door.

---

### Verse 56.236

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रत्रयं प्रयुञ्जीत भित्तिभागेन वेष्टितम्।
द्वारोच्छ्रायं स(स्व)विस्ताराद् व(द्वा)राधेन समुच्छ्रय:॥२३६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadratrayaṃ prayuñjīta bhittibhāgena veṣṭitam।
dvārocchhrāyaṃ s(sv)avistārād v(dvā)rārdhena samucchrāyaḥ॥236॥

**English Translation:**

One may make use of Bhadra triad, engirt by a wall vallum Having the elevation of the

### Verse 56.237

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गवाक्षस्तत्र कर्तव्यो यथा द्वारं न लङ्घ्यते।
मध्ये चतुष्किका कार्या द्विभागायामविस्तृता॥२३७॥
प्रासादो राजहंसोऽयं ब्रह्मादीनां प्रशस्यते।
राजहंस:॥
राजहंसस्य संस्थाने तृतीये(य)रथिकोपरि॥२३८॥
