### Verse 56.237

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गवाक्षस्तत्र कर्तव्यो यथा द्वारं न लङ्घ्यते।
मध्ये चतुष्किका कार्या द्विभागायामविस्तृता॥२३७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

gavākṣastatra kartavyo yathā dvāraṃ na laṅghyate |
madhye catuṣkikā kāryā dvibhāgāyāmavisstṛtā ॥237॥

[FLAG: last word of second line — विस्तृता — IAST: vistṛtā]

gavākṣastatra kartavyo yathā dvāraṃ na laṅghyate |
madhye catuṣkikā kāryā dvibhāgāyāmavistṛtā ॥237॥

**English Translation:**

The window therein deserves to be created the way the gate may not get trespassed. In the centre a Catuṣkikā [a net square] is to be created having breadth and length of two parts.

---

### Verse 56.238

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादो राजहंसोऽयं ब्रह्मादीनां प्रशस्यते।
राजहंस:॥
राजहंसस्य संस्थाने तृतीये(य)रथिकोपरि॥२३८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādo rājahaṃso'yaṃ brahmādīnāṃ praśasyate |
rājahaṃsaḥ ॥
rājahaṃsasya saṃsthāne tṛtīye(ya)rathikopari ॥238॥

**English Translation:**

The temple is Rājahaṃsa this one belauded for Brahmā and others.

At the plot of the Rājahaṃsa over the third Rathikā.

---

### Verse 56.239

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यदार(स्य)शिखरं सप्तसमुच्छ्रायं षडायतम्।
स्यात् तदा गरुडो नाम गरुडध्वजवल्भ:॥२३९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

yadāra(sya)śikharaṃ saptasamucchrāyaṃ ṣaḍāyatam |
syāt tadā garuḍo nāma garuḍadhvajavalabhaḥ ॥239॥

**English Translation:**

When its roof may be having seven digited height and six digited length, then the Garuḍa named may be the Prāsāda dear to Garuḍadhvaja i.e. Viṣṇu.

---

### Verse 56.240

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासाद: सर्व कामार्थ: कर्तु:¹ कारियतुंस्तथा।
गरुड:॥
अस्यैव मूलशिखरं त्यक्त्वा भागद्वयोन्मितम्॥२४०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādaḥ sarva kāmārthaḥ kartuḥ kāriyatuṃstathā |
garuḍaḥ ॥
asyaiva mūlaśikharaṃ tyaktvā bhāgadvayonmitam ॥240॥

**English Translation:**

The Prāsāda may be for the acquisition of all the cherished ends of the creator or as well as the builder and the architect. And the basic roof of this very one, having evaded coming to the extent of part twain.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p.343 verse 240.

---

### Verse 56.241

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

क्रियन्ते रथिका: पू(क)र्णे तदूर्ध्वं मूलमञ्जरी।
क्रियते द्वादशोच्छ्राये (या) दशभागायता यदा॥२४१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kriyante rathikāḥ pū(ka)rṇe tadūrdhvaṃ mūlamañjarī |
kriyate dvādaśocchrāye (yā) daśabhāgāyatā yadā ॥241॥

**English Translation:**

The Rathikās² are created on the Karṇa and over that the Mūlamañjarī when is created having twelve digited in height and ten digited in length.

> **Footnote 2:** Ratha, Rathaka- a type of building or shrine or temple (EOHA, p.473, 475). Rathikā- a main street or road.

---

### Verse 56.242

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तदा स्याद् वृषभो नाम वृषभध्वजवल्भ:।
वृषभ:॥
स(श)तार्घहस्तविस्तारं ज्येष्ठं मेरुं प्रकल्पयेत्॥२४२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tadā syād vṛṣabho nāma vṛṣabhadhvajavalabhaḥ |
vṛṣabhaḥ ॥
sa(śa)tārghahastavistāraṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ meruṃ prakalpayet ॥242॥

**English Translation:**

Then may be the Prāsāda named Vṛṣabha dear to the bull-bannered god i.e. Śiva. The larger Meru one may organise, having breadth of a half hundred i.e. fifty cubits.

---


### Verse 56.243

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मध्यमे हस्तसंख्या स्यात् (पट्त्यं द्विकालाधिक: ?)।
दशत्रिगुणिता (हस्ताभ्यां ?)संख्या प्रोक्ता कनीयसि॥२४३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

madhyame hastasaṃkhyā syāt (paṭṭyaṃ dvikālādhikaḥ ?)।
daśatriguṇitā (hastābhyāṃ ?) saṃkhyā proktā kanīyasi ॥243॥

**English Translation:**

In the centre the counting of cubits may be these thirty six added by two cubits. In the smaller one the counting is reckoned with as having cubits ten multiplied by 3 i.e. thirty cubits.

---

### Verse 56.244

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे भागविंशवि(ति)भाजिते।
विस्ताराधं भवेद् गर्भगृहं(भंगृहं)भित्त्या समन्वितम्॥२४४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre bhāgaviṃśavi(ti)bhājite ।
vistārārdhaṃ bhaved garbhagṛhaṃ (bhaṃgṛhaṃ) bhittyā samanvitam ॥244॥

**English Translation:**

In a plot square shaped as such divided into twenty digits the central chamber may be of half the breadth equipped with a wall.

---

### Verse 56.245

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागप्रमितविस्तारा गर्भभित्तिर्विधीयते।
सार्धद्विभागान्या भित्तिस्तद्देवाऽन्धकारिका(: ?)॥२४५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgapramitavistārā garbhabhittirvidhīyate ।
sārdhadvibhāgānyā bhittistaddevā'ndhakārikā (ḥ ?) ॥245॥

**English Translation:**

The central wall is provided as having breadth equal in measure to the part proper i.e. single part. Another wall as of two and a half parts and that very way the Andhakārikā i.e. the blind alley.

---

### Verse 56.246

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभागा रथिका कार्या कर्णे कर्णे विजानता।
चतुर्भागा रथा भद्रेष्वेतदर्धेन(वि ?)निर्गता॥२४६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhāgā rathikā kāryā karṇe karṇe vijānatā ।
caturbhāgā rathā bhadreṣvetadardhe na (vi ?) nirgatā ॥246॥

**English Translation:**

Two parted Rathikā is to be created at every Karṇa by the knowing one and a Rathā of four parts projected by a half part in Bhadras.

---

### Verse 56.247

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रकर्णान्तयो: कार्या पादाष्टांशजं¹ जलान्तरम्।
भद्राणां रथिका: कार्या: पार्श्वयोरुभयोस्तथा॥२४७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadrakarṇāntayoḥ kāryā pādāṣṭāṃśajaṃ¹ jalāntaram ।
bhadrāṇāṃ rathikāḥ kāryāḥ pārśvayorubhayostathā ॥247॥

**English Translation:**

Of Bhadras and Karṇas deserves to be created a water channel of eight digits of that as such. The Rathikās of Bhadras deserve to be created on both the flanks likewise.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p.343, fn.16.

---

### Verse 56.248

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रथिकानां च सर्वासां स्वभद्रं विस्तराधंत:।
शृङ्गं भद्रं यथैवैकं तथा सर्विण कारयेत्॥२४८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

rathikānāṃ ca sarvāsāṃ svabhadraṃ vistarārdha taḥ ।
śṛṅgaṃ bhadraṃ yathaivaikaṃ tathā sarviṇa kārayet ॥248॥

**English Translation:**

The individual bhadra of all the Rathikās is to be half the breadth just as one Śṛṅga, that very way the bhadra and all the Rathikās one may get created.

---

### Verse 56.249

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दिक्सूत्रेषु (च) सर्वेषु वर्धमानं निवेशयेत्।
अष्टभागोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा खुरपिण्डं तदर्धत:॥२४९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

diksūtreṣu (ca) sarveṣu vardhamānaṃ niveśayet ।
aṣṭabhāgocchrītā jaṅghā khurapīṇḍaṃ tadardha taḥ ॥249॥

**English Translation:**

In all the diksūtras one may incorporate Vardhamāna. The Jaṅghā may be elevated by eight parts and the Khurapīṇḍa (the hoof globe) a half of that.

---

### Verse 56.250

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रथमा रथिकास्तत्र सपादांशः त्रयोच्छ्रिता:॥२५०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prathamā rathikāstatra sapādāṃśaḥ trayocchritāḥ ॥250॥

**English Translation:**

These two that esere the interior flaps of the slope, (Bekhalā) these may be projected by part twain. The first Rathikā there may be of three and a quarter digits ¹ elevation.

### Verse 56.251

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदपादविहीना: स्युः क्रमेणोपरिभूमय:।

दिक्सूत्रेषु सकर्णेषु क्रिया प्राग्वद् विधीयते॥२५१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padapādavihīnāḥ syuḥ krameṇoparibhūmayaḥ ।
diksūtreṣu sakarṇeṣu kriyā prāgvad vidhīyate ॥251॥

**English Translation:**

The upper storeys may be in due order as reduced by quarter or square. In the Diksūtras equipped with Karṇas the process is ordained as per earlier norm.

---

### Verse 56.252

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शिखरं दशविस्तारं भागैद्वा (गढा)दशकोच्छ्रितम्।

चतुर्गुणेन सूत्रेण वेणुकोशं समालिखेत्॥२५२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śikharaṃ daśavistāraṃ bhāgairdvā (gaḍhā)daśakocchritam ।
caturguṇena sūtreṇa veṇukośaṃ samālikhet ॥252॥

**English Translation:**

The roof having ten squared breadth, having elevation of twelve parts, by a plumb (sūtra) of four fold norm one may carve out the Veṇukośa.

---

### Verse 56.253

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्कन्धकोशान्तरं चास्य त्रिभिर्भागैर्विभाजयेत्।

ग्रीवा च पद्मशीर्षं च तावद् भागार्धमुच्छ्रयात्॥२५३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

skandhakośāntaraṃ cāsya tribhirbhāgairvibhājayet ।
grīvā ca padmaśīrṣaṃ ca tāvad bhāgārdhamucchrayāt ॥253॥

**English Translation:**

And the Skandha Kośantara of this one may divide by three parts. The Grīvā and Padmaśīrṣa, may be of a half part than the elevation.

---

### Verse 56.254

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागमामा(गं चाम)लसारं स्यात् कलशो भागमेव च।

सा(श)तभृङ्गावृतो मेरुरयं प्रासाद ईरितः॥२५४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgamāmā(gaṃ cāma)lasāraṃ syāt kalaśo bhāgameva ca ।
sā(śa)tabhṛṅgāvṛto merurayyaṃ prāsāda īritaḥ ॥254॥

**English Translation:**

The Amalasāra may be of a single part and Kalaśa of a part again. The Meru covered by a hundred peaks this mansion stands developed.

---

### Verse 56.255

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रदक्षिणीकृते न स्याद्यत्पुण्यं कनकाद्रिणा(?)।

शैलेष्टकामये तत् स्यात् कृतेऽस्मिन्नधिकं ततः॥२५५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pradakṣiṇīkṛte na syādyatpuṇyaṃ kanakādriṇā(?) ।
śaileṣṭakāmaye tat syāt kṛte'sminnādhikaṃ tataḥ ॥255॥

**English Translation:**

On that one having been circumambiated having been made of stones and bricks may be greater than the Meru.

---

### Verse 56.256

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेरु:॥

नन्दिशालस्य संस्थाने तद्रूपे समवस्थिते।

द्वितीया रथिका कार्या भागद्वयविनिर्गता॥२५६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

meruḥ ॥

nandiśālasya saṃsthāne tadrūpe samavasthite ।
dvitīyā rathikā kāryā bhāgadvayavinirgatā ॥256॥

**English Translation:**

In the plot of Nandiśāla the structure of that one having got established at similar norm, the second Rathikā is to be created projected by part twain.

---

### Verse 56.257

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेषो भद्रस्य विस्तार: स्वविस्तारोऽ(रा)र्धनिर्गत:।

अष्टांशायामविस्तार: स्वविस्तारो(रा)र्धनिर्गत:॥२५७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣo bhadrasya vistāraḥ svavistāro'(rā)rdhanirgatāḥ ।
aṣṭāṃśāyāmavistāraḥ svavistāro(rā)rdhanirgatāḥ ॥257॥

**English Translation:**

The Śeṣa is the breadth of Bhadra, projected by a half of the personal breadth. The octagonal length and breadth may be projected by a half of individual breadth.

---


### Verse 56.258

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अष्टांशायामविस्तार:(रा)शाला स्यात् पुरतः पुनः।
तस्या मध्ये भवेद् गर्भो द्विभागायामविस्तर:॥२५८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

aṣṭāṃśāyāmavistāraḥ (rā)śālā syāt purataḥ punaḥ ।
tasyā madhye bhaved garbho dvibhāgāyāmavistараḥ ॥258॥

[FLAG: The abbreviation "(रा)" appears in the Devanagari as printed; retained as-is.]

**English Translation:**

Having breadth of eight oblong and breadth, the Śālā may be in front again. In the centre of that may be the garbha¹ having breadth and length of two parts.

> **Footnote 1:** Garbha- the womb, the foundation : the chamber in a temple where the deity is placed (EOHA, p.157).

---

### Verse 56.259

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गर्भभित्तिर्भवेच्चास्य भागेनैकेन निर्गता।
बाह्यभित्तिस्तथैव स्यात् तत्समा चान्धकारिका॥२५९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

garbhabhittir bhavec cāsya bhāgenaikena nirgatā ।
bāhyabhittis tathaiva syāt tatsamā cāndhakārikā ॥259॥

**English Translation:**

Of this one the garbha bhittī (the interior wall) may be such as projected by a single part and the outer wall may also be of the same structure and a blind alley (andhakārkā).

---

### Verse 56.260

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभागा रथिकास्तस्य सलिलान्तरभूषिता:।
शेषो भद्रस्य विस्तारो भागेनैकेन निर्गम:॥२६०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhāgā rathikās tasya salilāntarabhūṣitāḥ ।
śeṣo bhadrasya vistāro bhāgenaikena nirgamaḥ ॥260॥

**English Translation:**

The Rathikā of that one may be of two parts decorated by a water channel. The remaining breadth of the Bhadra may have an exit door of one part.

---

### Verse 56.261

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घोत्सेधं (च) पीठं च विदध्यान्नन्दिसार(शाल)वत्।
रथिकास्तत्र कर्तव्या: कर्ण(र्णे) भागत्रयोच्छिता:॥२६१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghotsedhаṃ (ca) pīṭhaṃ ca vidadhyān nandiśāla(śāla)vat ।
rathikās tatra kartavyāḥ karṇa(rṇe) bhāgatrayocchitāḥ ॥261॥

[FLAG: Parenthetical insertions "(च)" and "(शाल)" and "(र्णे)" appear as printed in the original; retained as-is.]

**English Translation:**

The elevation of Jaṅghā and Pīṭha one may create like the Nandiśāla. Rathikās therein deserve to be created having height of Karṇa triad parts.

---

### Verse 56.262

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षडंशान् विस्तृत:(तं) कुर्याच्छिखरं सप्तचो(मो)च्छ्रितम्।
कार्या केसरिवच्चास्य रेखा सामलसारिका॥२६२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍaṃśān vistṛtaḥ (taṃ) kuryāc chikhараṃ saptaco(mo)cchritam ।
kāryā kesarivac cāsya rekhā sāmalasārikā ॥262॥

[FLAG: Parenthetical insertions "(तं)" and "(मो)" appear as printed in the original; retained as-is.]

**English Translation:**

The seventh elevated roof one may create having breadth of six parts. And the Rekhā² along with Amalasāraka deserves to be created of this one as one like that of Kesarī³.

> **Footnote 2:** Rekhā- Curviliniearity or Profile of a spire of a nāgara temple (Prāsāda Śikhara, p.153)

> **Footnote 3:** Kesarī- a single storeyed building having front tubernacles, the side towers at the corners of the roof, and its nose, head and neck are round or rectangular. (EOHA, p.366)

---

### Verse 56.263

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एभिर्गुणैर्युतं चैनं पार्श्वयोरपि योजयेत्।
प्रासादोऽयं लताख्य: स्यात् कर्तव्यो दानविद्विष:॥२६३॥
लताख्य:॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ebhir guṇair yutaṃ cainaṃ pārśvayorapi yojayet ।
prāsādo'yaṃ latākhyaḥ syāt kartavyo dānavidviṣaḥ ॥263॥
latākhyaḥ ॥

**English Translation:**

To this one endowed with these qualities one may make as joined by the flanks. This Prāsāda may be named as Latā⁴ or Latākhya deserves to be created of the enemy of dānavas (Viṣṇu).⁵

> **Footnote 4:** Latā- creeper design on the roof. (Prāsāda Śikhara, p.153).

> **Footnote 5:** V.S. Apte, p. 249. दानवारि: = दानविद्विष्।

---

### Verse 56.264

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अग्रेतनं यदा पश्चान्यस्येत सरिणं तदा।
भवेत् त्रिपुष्कराख्योऽयं प्रासादस्त्रिदशालयः।।२६४।।
त्रिपुष्कराख्यः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

agretanaṃ yadā paścānyasyeta sariṇaṃ tadā |
bhavet tripuṣkarākhyo'yaṃ prāsādastridaśālayaḥ || 264 ||
tripuṣkarākhyaḥ ||

**English Translation:**

The frontal one when one may assort in the rear, on the Sariṇa (fountain) or a road (Saraṇi), then this one may be the Prāsāda named Tripuṣkara an abode of gods i.e. a temple.

---

### Verse 56.265

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नन्दिशालस्य सर्वांसु दिक्षु(क्ष्व)यं केसरी यदा।
स्यात् तदा पञ्चवक्त्रोऽसौ विधेय: पद्मजन्मनः।।२६५।।
पञ्चवक्त्रः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

nandiśālasya sarvāṃsu dikṣu(kṣv)ayaṃ kesarī yadā |
syāt tadā pañcavaktro'sau vidheya: padmajannmanaḥ || 265 ||
pañcavaktraḥ ||

**English Translation:**

In all the sides of the Nandiśāla when it becomes Kesarī then that may be Pañcavaktra deserving to be creation for the lotus born one i.e. Brahmā.

---

### Verse 56.266

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यदा च पञ्चवक्त्रस्य मध्ये गर्भि(भो) न दीयते।
बाह्यलेखादिकं प्राग्वद् दिक्षु सर्वासु कल्पते।।२६६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

yadā ca pañcavaktrasya madhye garbhi(bho) na dīyate |
bāhyalekhādikaṃ prāgvad dikṣu sarvāsu kalpate || 266 ||

**English Translation:**

When in the centre of the Pañcavaktra no garbha or central space or open space is provided, and like the outer living as per earlier process, in all sides that becomes patent.

---

### Verse 56.267

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुःस्तम्भसमा कार्या मध्ये चास्य चतुष्किका।
वितानं चोपरि न्यस्येन्मध्यतस्तस्य भूषणाम्।।२६७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

catuḥstambhasamā kāryā madhye cāsya catuṣkikā |
vitānaṃ copari nyasyenmadhyatastasya bhūṣaṇām || 267 ||

**English Translation:**

In the middle of this one deserves to be created a Catuṣkikā (a grill normed network,) having similar norm of four columns (supporting it) a canopy or an awning one may assort overhead from the central part being its decoration.

---

### Verse 56.268

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हरो हिरण्यगर्भश्च हरिर्दिनकरस्तथा।
एते चतुर्मुखे स्थाप्या नापरेषां भवत्ययम्।।२६८।।
चतुर्मुखः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

haro hiraṇyagarbhaśca harirdinakarastathā |
ete caturmukhe sthāpyā nāpareṣāṃ bhavatyayam || 268 ||
caturmukhaḥ ||

**English Translation:**

Hara, (Śiva) and Hiraṇyagarbha (Brahmā), Hari (Viṣṇu) and Sun (maker of the day) these deserve to be installed on four openings or on Caturmukha (Prāsāda). This does not become practicable for others.

---

### Verse 56.269

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुःषष्टिकरे कुर्यात् क्षेत्रे मानैकविंशतिः(?)।
सप्तवर्गपदो गर्भो भित्त्या सह विधीयते।।२६९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

catuḥṣaṣṭikare kuryāt kṣetre mānaika viṃśatiḥ(?) |
saptavargapado garbho bhittyā saha vidhīyate || 269 ||

**English Translation:**

In the plot of sixty four cubits one may create a measurement of twenty one. The garbha (adyatum) having sapta vargapadas (or seven square feet) is provided with a wall.

---

### Verse 56.270

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्याद् गर्भभित्तिभागेन भागेनैवान्धकारिका।
षड्भागं कर्णिवस्तारं दशधा प्रविभाजयेत्।।२७०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

syād garbhabhittibhāgena bhāgenaivāndhakārikā |
ṣaḍbhāgaṃ karṇivistāraṃ daśadhā pravibhājayet || 270 ||

**English Translation:**

The Andhakārikā or the blind alley may be by a single part with the help of the part of the garbha bhittī (the central wall). The breadth of the Karṇa having six parts one may

### Verse 56.271

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्भिर्भागैर्भवेदस्य गर्भो भित्त्या समन्वितः।
बाह्या भित्तिर्भवेद् भागाद् भागश्चैवान्धकारिका॥२७१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍbhirbhāgairbhvedasyagarbho bhittyā samanvitaḥ।
bāhyā bhittirbhaved bhāgād bhāgaścaivāndhakārikā॥271॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN — ṣaḍbhirbhāgairbhavedasyagarbho; reading ṣaḍbhirbhāgairbhavedasyagarbho]

**IAST Transliteration (corrected):**

ṣaḍbhirbhāgairbhavedasyagarbho bhittyā samanvitaḥ ।
bāhyā bhittirbhaved bhāgād bhāgaścaivāndhakārikā ॥271॥

**English Translation:**

By six parts may be the garbha of this one endowed with a wall. The outer wall may be by a part and a part may be the Blind Alley (andhakarikā).

---

### Verse 56.272

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभागं कर्णवैपुल्यमुदकान्तरभूषितम्।
शेषो भद्रस्य विस्तारश्चतुर्थांशविनिर्गतः॥२७२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhāgaṃ karṇavaipulyamudakāntarabhūṣitam ।
śeṣo bhadrasya vistāraścaturthāṃśavinigataḥ ॥272॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN — last word: विनिर्गतः → vinirgatāḥ or vinirghataḥ; reading vinirghataḥ]

**IAST Transliteration (corrected):**

dvibhāgaṃ karṇavaipulyamudakāntarabhūṣitam ।
śeṣo bhadrasya vistāraścaturthāṃśavinirghataḥ ॥272॥

**English Translation:**

Two parted may be the thickness of the Karṇa decorated by a water channel. The remaining one as the breadth of the Bhadra, projected by a fourth part.

---

### Verse 56.273

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

क्षोभयेदर्धभागे तु तदर्धेन जलान्तरम्।
मत्तवारणकैर्विद्यात् स्तम्भैरुपरि शोभिता॥२७३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kṣobhayedardhabāge tu tadārdhena jalāntaram ।
mattavāraṇakairvidyāt stambhairupari śobhitā ॥273॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN — अर्धभागे → ardhabāge or ardhabāge; reading ardhabāge corrected to ardha­bhāge]

**IAST Transliteration (corrected):**

kṣobhayedardha­bhāge tu tadardhe­na jalāntaram ।
matta­vāraṇakair­vidyāt stambhair­upari śobhitā ॥273॥

**English Translation:**

One may dig out or excavate by a half part and by a half part of that, the water channel. And by aisles (matta vāraṇakas) one may know these as decorated by columns articles.

---

### Verse 56.274

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रथिकैका त्रिभागेन पुनः सार्धद्विभागिका।
तासां परस्परक्षेपो भागो भागो विधीयते॥२७४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

rathikaikā tribhāgena punaḥ sārdha­dvibhāgikā ।
tāsāṃ parasparākṣepo bhāgo bhāgo vidhīyate ॥274॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN — परस्परक्षेपो → parasparakṣepo]

**IAST Transliteration (corrected):**

rathikaikā tribhāgena punaḥ sārdha­dvibhāgikā ।
tāsāṃ parasparakṣepo bhāgo bhāgo vidhīyate ॥274॥

**English Translation:**

A single Rathikā by a part triad once again by a two and a half part, the mutual intercession of those, is provided by a part each.

---

### Verse 56.275

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेषं शिखरविस्तारः सार्धषट्कं तदुच्छ्रयः।
पृथक्सूत्रैस्त्रिगुणितैर्वेणुकोशं समालिखेत्॥२७५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣaṃ śikharavistāraḥ sārdhaṣaṭkaṃ taducchraya­ḥ ।
pṛthaksūtraistraguṇitairvenukoś­aṃ samālikhet ॥275॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN — त्रिगुणितैर् → triguṇitair; वेणुकोशं → veṇukośaṃ]

**IAST Transliteration (corrected):**

śeṣaṃ śikharavistāraḥ sārdhaṣaṭkaṃ taducchr­ayaḥ ।
pṛthaksūtraistriguṇitairveṇukośaṃ samālikhet ॥275॥

**English Translation:**

The Śeṣa may be having a breadth of the Śikhara (roof) with height as of six and a half parts. The Pṛthaksūtras being of triple multiple one may carve out the Veṇukośa.

---

### Verse 56.276

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्कन्धकोशान्तरं भागैश्चतुर्भिस्तस्य भाजयेत्।
ग्रीवार्धभागमुत्सेधो भागेनामलसारकम्॥२७६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

skandhakoś­āntaraṃ bhāgaiścaturbhistasya bhājayet ।
grīvārdha­bhāgamutsedho bhāgenāmalasārakam ॥276॥

**English Translation:**

One may divide by four parts the space between the Skandha kośa. The height may be of a half part of the grīvā and by a part Amalasāraka.

---

### Verse 56.277

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पद्मशीर्षस्त(ष्ट)था भागं कलशो भागसंमितः।
अर्धभागसमो(मु)त्सेधं कारयेद् बीजपूरकम्॥२७७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padmaśīrṣasta(ṣṭa)thā bhāgaṃ kalaśo bhāgasaṃmitaḥ ।
ardhabāgasamo(mu)tsedhaṃ kārayedbījapūrakam ॥277॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN — पद्मशीर्षस्त(ष्ट)था — uncertain reading, reproduced as in text; अर्धभागसमो(मु)त्सेधं → ardhabāgasamo(mu)tsedhaṃ, corrected to ardha­bhāgasamo(mu)tsedhaṃ]

**IAST Transliteration (corrected):**

padmaśīrṣasta(ṣṭa)thā bhāgaṃ kalaśo bhāgasaṃmitaḥ ।
ardha­bhāgasamo(mu)tsedhaṃ kārayed bījapūrakam ॥277॥

**English Translation:**

Padmaśīrṣa of a part and Kalaśa also comprising of a part and Bijapūraka one may create as having height of a half part.

---

### Verse 56.278

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वकर्णेषु कर्तव्याः क्रियाश्चैवं विचक्षणैः।
दिक्सूत्रबाह्यभागेषु वलभीं संत्रिवेशयेत्॥२७८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvakrarṇeṣu kartavyāḥ kriyāścaivaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।
diksūtrabāhyabhāgeṣu valabhīṃ saṃtriveśayet ॥278॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN — सर्वकर्णेषु → sarvakrarṇeṣu corrected to sarvakarṇeṣu; संत्रिवेशयेत् → saṃtriveśayet or saṃniveśayet]

**IAST Transliteration (corrected):**

sarvakarṇeṣu kartavyāḥ kriyāścaivaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ।
diksūtrabāhyabhāgeṣu valabhīṃ saṃniveśayet ॥278॥

**English Translation:**

By the experts in all the Karṇas, the wall deserves to be created. In the outer parts of Diksūtras one may incorporate Valabhī (roof).

---

### Verse 56.279

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

निर्गमे पञ्चभाग: स्यात् तिर्यक् प्रक्षिप्तभागिका:(?)।
अस्या द्विभागिको गर्भो मध्ये भागत्रयोच्छ्रित:।।२७९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

nirgame pañcabhāgaḥ syāt tiryak prakṣiptabhāgikā:(?)।
asyā dvibhāgiko garbho madhye bhāgatrayocchritaḥ।।279।।

**English Translation:**

In the exit door, a five parted development may be there and horizontal parted crouchings. The garbha of this one may be two parted one in the centre having elevation of part triad.

---

### Verse 56.280

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागार्धभागं भित्तिः स्यात् तत्समा चान्धकारिका।
तस्याष्ठाग्रे विधातव्य:(?) षड्दारुकसमन्वितम्।।२८०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgārdabhāgaṃ bhattiḥ syāt tatsamā cāndhakārikā।
tasyāṣṭhāgre vidhātavya:(?) ṣaḍdārukasamanvitam।।280।।

**English Translation:**

The wall is of a part halved of a part and equal to that the blind alley (andhakarikā). And in front of that is to be constructed a (plank) equipped with Ṣaḍdāruka (a six faggoted projection).

---

### Verse 56.281

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एकैकां रथिकां सार्धभागां कर्णेषु योजयेत्।
शेषं भद्रस्य विस्तारो भागः स्यादस्य निर्गमः।।२८१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ekaikāṃ rathikāṃ sārdabhāgāṃ karṇeṣu yojayet।
śeṣaṃ bhadrasya vistāro bhāgaḥ syādasya nirgamaḥ।।281।।

**English Translation:**

A rathikā each one may combine on the Karṇas having a part plus half. The Śeṣa one being the breadth of the Bhadra and its outlet or exit door may be of a part as such.

---

### Verse 56.282

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एवं भद्रं वि(द्वि)भागं स्यात् स्तम्भद्वयसमन्वितम्।
वलभावर्तयोर्मध्ये भागमेकं च विस्तृतम्।।२८२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

evaṃ bhadraṃ vi(dvi)bhāgaṃ syāt stambhadvayasamanvitam।
valabhāvartayormadhye bhāgamekaṃ ca vistṛtam।।282।।

**English Translation:**

This way the Bhadra may be two parted one, equipped with columns twain, and in between the Valabha and Karṇa a part alone broad as such.

---

### Verse 56.283

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तत्रोदकान्तरं कुर्याद् गुणद्वारविभूषितम्।
नवभागोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा पीठमस्य तदर्धत:।।२८३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tatrodakāntaraṃ kuryād guṇadvāravibhūṣitam।
navabhāgocchritā jaṅghā pīṭhamasya tadardhatah।।283।।

**English Translation:**

There one may create a water channel decked with a guṇa door. The Jaṅghā may be elevated to the extent of nine parts and the Pīṭha of this one by a half of that.

---

### Verse 56.284

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेखलान्तरपत्रे च कुर्याद् भागद्वयोन्मिते।
रथिका स्याद् द्विभागा च तत: साधेकभागिका।।२८४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mekhalāntarapatre ca kuryād bhāgadvayonmite।
rathikā syād dvibhāgā ca tataḥ sādhekabhāgikā।।284।।

**English Translation:**

In the Mekhalāntara Pattra measured to the extent of part twain the Rathikāmay be of part twain and from there having part one added by half.

---

### Verse 56.285

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेषं शिखरविस्तार: पञ्चांशं शिखरोच्छ्र्य:।
उपर्युपरि कर्तव्यं सर्वतोभद्रकद्वयम्।।२८५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣaṃ śikharavistāraḥ pañcāṃśaṃ śikharocchryaḥ।
uparyupari kartavyaṃ sarvatobhadrakadvayam।।285।।

**English Translation:**

The breadth of the roof as Śeṣa and five digited one the height of the roof and over and above deserves to be created Sarvatobhadraka twain.

On every Karṇa one may introduce, two Sarvatobhadras each. In all the Diksūtras the

### Verse 56.287

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तार शिखरस्याष्टौ भागात्स्यार्धसमुच्छ्रय:(?)।
पञ्चव्यासेन सूत्रेण ++++++++॥२८७॥

vistāra śikharasyāṣṭau bhāgātsyārdhasamucchrayaḥ(?)।
pañcavyāsena sūtreṇa ++++++++॥287॥

The breadth of the roof, having eight parts and half of that the height. And by the Sūtra of five Vyāsas one may carve out the Veṇukośa.¹

### Verse 56.288

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वेणुकोशान्तरं चास्य त्रिभिर्भागैर्विभाजयेत्।
ग्रीवा च पद्मशीर्षं च भागेन स्यादिदं द्वयम्॥२८८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

veṇukośāntaraṃ cāsya tribhirbhāgairvibhājayet।
grīvā ca padmaśīrṣaṃ ca bhāgena syādidaṃ dvayam॥288॥

**English Translation:**

And the space of the Veṇukośa one may subdivide by three parts. And Grīvā and Padmakośa the twain may be of a part each.

---

### Verse 56.289

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रत्येकं भागिकौ कार्यौ कलशामलसारकौ।
त(न)वात्मकोऽयं कथित: प्रासादस्त्रिदशालय:॥२८९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pratyekaṃ bhāgikau kāryau kalaśāmalasārakau।
ta(na)vātmako'yaṃ kathitaḥ prāsādasttridaśālayaḥ॥289॥

**English Translation:**

This way Kalaśa and Amalasāraka may be created as of two parts each. The abode of gods has been illustrated as of nine fold variety (Navātmaka).

---

### Verse 56.290

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नवात्मक:।
विन्यसेद् दि(दी)शमैशान्यामाग्नेय्यां पुरुषोत्तमम्।
ब्रह्माणं वायुदिग्भागे नैर्ऋते च दिवाकरम्॥२९०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

navātmakaḥ।
vinyased di(dī)śamaiśānyāmāgneyīyāṃ puruṣottamam।
brahmāṇaṃ vāyudigbhāge nairṛte ca divākaram॥290॥

**English Translation:**

One may adjust Īśa in the Aiśāni quarter (the north eastern quarter) in Āgneyī (south eastern quarter) the excellent man (i.e. Viṣṇu Puruṣottama). Brahmā in the Vāyu Koṇa (north western quarter) and in the Nairṛta (south western) the maker of the day i.e. the Sun.

---

### Verse 56.291

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मध्यगर्भे शिव: स्थाप्य: प्राच्यामपि पुरन्दर:।
धर्मोयमां(याम्यां) प्रतीच्यां च वरुण: सोम उत्तरे॥२९१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

madhyagarbhe śivaḥ sthāpyaḥ prācyāmapi purandaraḥ।
dharmoyamāṃ(yāmyāṃ) pratīcyāṃ ca varuṇaḥ soma uttare॥291॥

**English Translation:**

Within the centre of the middle part, Śiva deserves to be adjusted and in the East, Purandara (i.e. Indra). Dharma (Yama) in the Yāmyā (southern) and in the western Varuṇa and Soma (Moon) in the North.

(भक्ताठा?)² शक्तिसम्पन्न: पूर्वायतनसन्निधौ।

A devotee power endowed in the close quarter of the Eastern shrine. The mansion or temple one may get created with effort and may not torment the preliminary aspect.

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

### Verse 56.295

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अन्योन्यं दक्षिणे वेधो हीन इत्यभिधीयते।
वेधभागामृते(?)मृत्युं हीने हानिं विनिर्दिशेत्॥२९५॥

anyonyaṃ dakṣiṇe vedho hīna ityabhidhīyate |

### Verse 56.296

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हरो हिरण्यगर्भश्च हरिर्दिनकरस्तथा।

Hara, Hiranya garbha (Brahmā), Hari and Sun likewise, these are declared as gods as mutually in opposition.

### Verse 56.297

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

etā na dakṣiṇāpārśve sthāpayet puramāśritān (?) |

These one may not adjust or install in the south, as having dependence of the town nor to the left of other gods nor in the inferior temples.

### Verse 56.298

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नैतेषां दक्षिणे कुर्यादन्येषामपि चालयम्।

**English Translation:**

Nor to the south or right side of these one may erect the abodes of others. When one may cherish glory of oneself when even being inferior as superior as such.

### Verse 56.299

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तेषामुत्तरतो नू(ही)नं यदि(दी)च्छेद् देवतालयम्।

**IAST Transliteration:**

In case one may cherish the creation of an inferior temple in the north by a measurement of the square of the Prāsāda at a space of 39 or 36 cubits.

### Verse 56.300

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादं कारयेदन्यं मर्मवेधिवविर्जितान् (तम्)।

One may create a Prāsāda of different norm divested of obstruction in the vulnerable spots, in front or in the rear, or in the two flanks.

### Verse 56.301

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

महामर्माणि चत्वारि कुर्याद् यत्ताघतोत्तरे(?)।

Then one may create huge vulnerable spots four as such in the north. In all the Kṣaṇamadhyaṣa one may not get provided one material as such.

### Verse 56.302

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

### Verse 56.303

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्तृकारकयो: पीडा भवेत् पूजा न तादृशी।
तस्मात् सर्वप्रयत्नेन स्थपति: कारकोऽपि च॥३०३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kartṛkārakayoḥ pīḍā bhavet pūjā na tādṛśī।
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena sthapatiḥ kārako'pi ca॥303॥

[FLAG: English continuation spans verses 303–304 combined]

There may ensue the trouble for the builder and the Architect and no worship of that sort may be possible. Therefore by all efforts the Architected and the builder may avoid the vulnerable points with effort quite closer to the Prāsāda. Therefore, who wants to create a Prāsāda without the vulnerable spots.

### Verse 56.305

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादत: सदा तेन विधेयं महदन्तरम्।
(प्रासादां तूत्तरं कव:)² कार्यं फलपुष्पैर्विभूषितम्॥३०५॥

prāsādataḥ sadā tena dheyaṃ mahadantaram।

From the Prāsāda invariably by him should be kept a great distance. In the north of the Prāsāda, deserves to be created a spot decorated by flowers and fruits.

### Verse 56.306

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

य एतैर्लक्षणैर्युक्तं कारयेद् देवतालयम्।

Who may create such a temple endowed with such auspicious marks he may obtain wealth and crops and feel happier at ease.

Hara (Śiva), Hiraṇyagarbha (Brahmā), Hari (Viṣṇu) and Dinakara (Sun) these gods are declared as adorable for the gods even.

### Verse 56.309

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हारकेयूरसंयुक्तो रत्नमालोपशोभितः।
ऋष्यागतपुरः कार्यः पञ्चहस्तो दिवाकरः।।३०९।।

hārakeyūrasaṃyukto ratnamālopaśobhitaḥ |
ṛṣyāgatapuraḥ kāryaḥ pañcahasto divākaraḥ ||309||

Equipped with garlands, armlets and decorated with gems inlaid wreaths, the sun deserves to be created having hand occupied by lotus and having front occupied by a sage.

### Verse 56.310

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शङ्खचक्रधरो देवो वामे च मधुसूदनः।
कण्ठाभरणसंयुक्तो मूर्धा च मुकुटोज्ज्वलः।।३१०।।

The god wielding conch and quoit in the left side, Madhusūdana i.e. Kṛṣṇa equipped with neck ornaments being shining with a diadem on the crest.

---

### Verse 56.311

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ब्रह्मा पश्चिमतः कार्यो बृहज्जठरमण्डलः।

brahmā paścimatāḥ kāryo bṛhajjaṭharamaṇḍalaḥ |

Brahmā deserves to be installed on the west or in the rear, having a globular pate of a huge norm bearing the rosary of beads and Kuṇḍikās and decorated by a beard and moustaches.

### Verse 56.312

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

The Prāsādas Rucaka and others lovely ones earlier twenty have been illustrated pentad normed then thereafter headed by Kesarī borne as such have been illustrated.

---

### Verse 56.312 (continued) / Verse 56.312

षष्टिः स्याच्चतुर्निवतेति विदिता सैषा भवेत् सम्पदे।।३१२।।

Those known as Miśras or miscellaneous normed ones nine and five having been illustrated subsequently that very way be a secret nomenclature. Sixty may be there, the one added by four this way may be known as the Sampadas.

# Chapter 57 — A twenty counting of Meru and others

### Verse 57.1

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अथान्यान् कथियष्याम: समासात् सूक्ष्मलक्षणान्।
पञ्चाशतमिहोत्कृष्टान् प्रासादाञ् श्रीधरादिकान्।।१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

athānyān kathiyaṣyāmaḥ samāsāt sūkṣmalakṣaṇān |
pañcāśatamihоtkṛṣṭān prāsādāñ śrīdharādikān || 1 ||

**English Translation:**

Now we shall talk of others having minuter pre-requisites quite succinctly and fifty here, of superior order, the Prāsādas such as Śrīdharas.

---

### Verse 57.2

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

श्रीधरो हेमकूट्ञ सुभद्रो रिपुकेसरी।
पुष्पो विजयभद्रञ्च श्रीनिवास: सुदर्शन:।।२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śrīdharo hemakūṭaś ca subhadro ripukesarī |
puṣpo vijayabhadraś ca śrīnivāsaḥ sudarśanaḥ || 2 ||

**English Translation:**

Śrīdhara and Hemakūṭa, Subhadra and Ripukesarī, Puṣpa and Vijayabhadra, Śrīnivāsa and Sudarśana.

---

### Verse 57.3

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भगवत्या: प्रिया होते तथा कुसुमशेखर:।
देवस्य शम्भोर्दयित: प्रासाद: सुरसुन्दर:।।३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhagavatyāḥ priyā hote tathā kusumaśekharaḥ |
devasya śambhordayitaḥ prāsādaḥ surasundaraḥ || 3 ||

**English Translation:**

These are dear to Bhagavatī and likewise flower crested one. Dear to this god Śambhu Prāsāda Surasundara.

---

### Verse 57.4

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नन्द्यावर्तञ्च पूर्णञ्च सिद्धार्थ: सिरव(शङ्कु)वर्धन:।
त्रैलोक्यभूषणञ्चेति पद्मसु(स्तु)ब्रह्मण: प्रिय:।।४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

nandyāvartaś ca pūrṇaś ca siddhārthaḥ sirava(śaṅku)vardhanaḥ |
trailokyabhūṣaṇaśceti padmasu(stu)brahmaṇaḥ priyaḥ || 4 ||

**English Translation:**

Nandyāvartta¹ and Pūrṇa Siddhārtha and Śaṅkha Vardhana, Trailokya bhūṣaṇa and Padma beloved of Brahmā.

> **Footnote 1:** A type of building, a village, a ground plan, an entablature (EOHA, p.274).

---

### Verse 57.5

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पक्षबाहुर्विशालञ्च तथान्य: कमलोद्भव:।
हंसध्वज इति ख्याता: प्रासादा ब्रह्मण: प्रिया:।।५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pakṣabāhurviśālaś ca tathānyaḥ kamalodbhavaḥ |
haṃsadhvaja iti khyātāḥ prāsādā brahmaṇaḥ priyāḥ || 5 ||

**English Translation:**

Pakṣabāhu and Viśāla, and other one Kamalodbhava Haṃsadhvaja this way known Prāsādas are dear to Brahmā.

---

### Verse 57.6

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

लक्ष्मीधराक्ष(ख्य): प्रासादो वस्त(स)तौ मधुविद्विष:।
महावज्रो रतितनु: सिद्धकामस्तथापर:।।६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

lakṣmīdharākṣa(khya)ḥ prāsādo vasta(sa)tau madhuvidviṣaḥ |
mahāvajro ratitanuḥ siddhakāmastathāparaḥ || 6 ||

**English Translation:**

The Prāsāda named Lakṣmīdhara in Vasati, Madhuvidviṣa or an abode of enemy of Madhu (i.e., Viṣṇu), Mahāvajra, Ratitanu and Siddhakāma a different one.

---

### Verse 57.7

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चामरसंज्ञञ्च नन्दिघोषाख्य एव च।
अनुकीर्ण: सुप्रभञ्च सुरानन्दोऽथ हर्षण:।।७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcāmarasaṃjñaś ca nandighoṣākhya eva ca |
anukīrṇaḥ suprabhāś ca surānando'tha harṣaṇaḥ || 7 ||

**English Translation:**

And one named Pañcacāmara and one named Nandighoṣa, Anukīrṇa and Suprabha, Surānanda and Harṣaṇa.

---

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — verse 18 begins on previous page]

### Verse 57.18

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दुर्धरो दुर्जयश्चैव त्रिकूटो नवशेखर:।
पुण्डरीक: सुनाभश्च महेन्द्र: शिखिशेखर:॥१८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

durdharo durjayaścaiva trikūṭo navaśekharaḥ।
puṇḍarīkaḥ sunābhaśca mahendraḥ śikhiśekharaḥ॥18॥

**English Translation:**

Durdhara and Durjaya Trikūṭa and Navaśekhara Puṇdarika and Sunābha, Mahendra and Śikhi Śikhara.

---

### Verse 57.19

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वराठ: सुमुख: शुद्धश्चत्वारिंशादितीरिता:।
मिश्रकास्तु दश प्रोक्ता मिथ: कर्मप्रभेदत:॥१९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

varāṭhaḥ sumukhaḥ śuddhaścatvāriṃśāditīritāḥ।
miśrakāstu daśa proktā mithaḥ karmaprabhedataḥ॥19॥

**English Translation:**

Varāṭha and Sumukha and Śuddha these forty have been enumerated. The Miśrakas are ten enumerated as such in point of mutual activation differentiation.

---

### Verse 57.20

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विज्ञेयो नन्दसंज्ञश्च महाघोषस्तथापर:।
वृद्धिरामाभिधानश्च प्रासादोऽन्यो वसुन्धर:॥२०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vijñeyo nandasaṃjñaśca mahāghoṣastathāparaḥ।
vṛddhirāmābhidhānaśca prāsādo'nyo vasundharaḥ॥20॥

**English Translation:**

The one deserves to be known as Nandaka and the other one as Mahāghoṣa and by the names Vṛddhirāma another house is Vasundhara.

---

### Verse 57.21

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मुद्रकोऽथ बृहच्छालस्तथा(थे) व च सुधाधर:।
संवराख्य: शुकिनभस्तथा सर्वाङ्गसुन्दर:॥२१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

mudgako'tha bṛhacchālastathā(the) va ca sudhādharaḥ।
saṃvarākhyaḥ śukinabhastathā sarvāṅgasundaraḥ॥21॥

**English Translation:**

Mudgaka and Bṛhacchāla and likewise Sudhādhara and one named Saṃvara a parrot normed (Śukanibhaḥ) and likewise Sarvāṅgasundara.

---

### Verse 57.22

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चाशदेवं कथिता प्रासादानां यथाक्रमम्।
इदानीं लक्ष्मतो ब्रूम: श्रीधरं सर्वकामिकम्॥२२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcāśadevaṃ kathitā prāsādānāṃ yathākramam।
idānīṃ lakṣmato brūmaḥ śrīdharaṃ sarvakāmikam॥22॥

**English Translation:**

Fifty this way have been spoken of, (the varieties) of Prāsādas as per a descending order. Now we shall talk of as per qualities the Śrīdhara Sarvakānika i.e. all desire granting one.

---

### Verse 57.23

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वल्लभं सर्वदेवानां पुण्यानां कारणं परम्।
चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे चतुर्विंशतिभाजिते॥२३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vallabhaṃ sarvadевānāṃ puṇyānāṃ kāraṇaṃ param।
caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre caturviṃśatibhājite॥23॥

**English Translation:**

Favourite one of all the gods and an utmost source of all the merits. In a plot square shaped segmented into twenty four parts.

---

### Verse 57.24

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वादशाखिलकोणेषु कर्णशृङ्गाणि योजयेत्।
विस्तारं च चतुर्भागमेकैकस्य विनिर्दिशेत्॥२४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvādaśākhilakoṇeṣu karṇaśṛṅgāṇi yojayet।
vistāraṃ ca caturbhāgamekaikasya vinirdiśet॥24॥

**English Translation:**

In all the twelve corners one may add Śṛṅgakoṇas (the top corners or flanks) and a breadth of four parts of one each one may enjoin.

---

### Verse 57.25

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

परस्परं च निष्कोऽसौ(विष्कम्भो) द्विपदोऽत्र विधीयते।
द्व्यंशानि कर्णि(ण)भद्राणि निर्गमश्चार्धभागिक:॥२५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

parasparaṃ ca niṣko'sau(viṣkambho) dvipado'tra vidhīyate।
dvyaṃśāni karṇi(ṇa)bhadrāṇi nirgamaścārdhabhāgikaḥ॥25॥

**English Translation:**

And mutually the Viṣkambha (supporting beam of the house) is provided here of two parts, two digited Karṇabhadras and an exit gate of four digited norm.

---


### Verse 57.16

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णकर्णपदेन्यस्यात्° पदार्धिधेत(?)विस्तृत:।
वारिमग्नो विधातब्यो मध्यग: पूर्वमानयो:।।१६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇakarṇapadenyasyāt° padārddhidhet(?)vistṛtaḥ |
vārimagno vidhātabyo madhyagaḥ pūrvamānayoḥ ||16||

**English Translation:**

On every spot of each Karna one may assort broad by a half part, water logged deserving to be created or a dipping pond deserves to be created gone within the centre in two earlier measurements.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p.357, fn.10 has कर्ण: कर्णपदेऽन्य: स्यात् पदाप्येनि न विस्तृत:।

---

### Verse 57.17

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रस्य मानमुद्दिष्टं विस्तराद् दशभागिकम्।
निर्गमञ्च त्रिभिर्भांगै: समसूत्रसमाहित:।।१७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadrasya mānamudddiṣṭaṃ vistārād daśabhāgikam |
nirgamañca tribhirbhāṃgaiḥ samasūtrasamāhitaḥ ||17||

**English Translation:**

The measurement of Bhadra is enjoined as ten digited one as compared with the breadth and the exit gate by three parts well laid down by similar plumb lines.

---

### Verse 57.18

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विपादा बाह्याभिस्तिस्तु द्विपादा चान्धकारिका।
भवेच्छतपद: कन्दो गर्भ: षड्विंशदंशक:।।१८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvipādā bāhyābhististu dvipādā cāndhakārikā |
bhavecchatapadaḥ kando garbhaḥ ṣaḍviṃśadaṃśakaḥ ||18||

**English Translation:**

The outer wall of two squares and two digited one the blind alley (andhakārikā)² and Kanda should be hundred digits (a garden) and the central portion of twenty six cubits.

> **Footnote 2:** Āndhkārikā- a closed verāṇḍāh- a balcony (EOHA, p.61).

---

### Verse 57.19

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विपद: कर्णकन्दश्च प्रत्यङ्गं पदिकं स्मृतम्।
निर्गतं चार्धभागेन चतुर्दिशु व्यवस्थितम्।११९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvipadaḥ karṇakandaśca pratyaṅgaṃ padikaṃ smṛtam |
nirgataṃ cārdhabhāgena caturdikṣu vyavasthitam ||119||

[FLAG: Verse number appears as ११९ in the image, likely a misprint for १९]

**English Translation:**

The two digited flanked hedge on every part, is required as of one part, projected by a half part established in four quarters.

---

### Verse 57.20

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेन निर्गता कार्या शाला चास्य चतुष्पदा।
अभ्यन्तरं बाह्यभित्ते: कन्दस्य च तथा बहि:।।२०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgena nirgatā kāryā śālā cāsya catuṣpadā |
abhyantaraṃ bāhyabhitteḥ kandasya ca tathā bahiḥ ||20||

**English Translation:**

Projected by a part deserves to be created a hall of this one of four segments and the interior of the outer wall as also of the inner hedge being of the same exterior as such.

---

### Verse 57.21

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उभयोरन्तरं कार्यं विस्तारात् पञ्चभागिकम्।
अन्तरालं च कुर्वीत शृङ्गं तच्य चतुष्पदम्।।२१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ubhayorantaraṃ kāryaṃ vistārāt pañcabhāgikam |
antarālaṃ ca kurvīta śṛṅgaṃ tacya catuṣpadam ||21||

**English Translation:**

The interior of both deserves to be created of five segments in line with the breadth and the internal enclosure should be created with its top as of four parts.

---

### Verse 57.22

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विभागस्तादृशोऽस्य स्याद् बाह्यशृङ्गस्य यादृश:।
भित्तिकन्दान्तराले च कार्यं षड्दारुकं बुधै:।।२२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vibhāgastādṛśo'sya syād bāhyaśṛṅgasya yādṛśaḥ |
bhittikandāntarāle ca kāryaṃ ṣaḍdārukaṃ budhaiḥ ||22||

**English Translation:**

Of the outer top whatever may be the division of that type of that same norm may be in the interior of the plinth of the wall, fit to be created as Ṣaḍdāruka by the experts.

---

### Verse 57.23

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इतिकातोरणयुतं चतुदिंशु मनोरमम्।
पुरतो मण्डपं कुर्यात् सर्वलक्षणसंयुतम्।।२३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

itikātoraṇayutaṃ catudīṃśu manoramam |
purato maṇḍapaṃ kuryāt sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam ||23||

---

### Verse 57.24

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागैः पञ्चशता कुर्यादस्य मानिमहोर्ध्वगम्।
एषां मध्ये सविंशत्या प्रविधेयास्तुलोदया:।।२४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgaiḥ pañcaśatā kuryādasya mānimahordhvagam।
eṣāṃ madhye saviṃśatyā pravidheyāstulodayāḥ।।24।।

**English Translation:**

By fifty digits one may create in measurement as gone upwards. In the centre of these, by a twenty norm, deserve to be provided the beams or architraves.

---

### Verse 57.25

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तेषां मध्येऽञ्चकैः षड्भिर्वेदीबन्धो विधीयते।
नवधा भाजिते तत्र वेदीबन्धे समैः पदैः।।२५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

teṣāṃ madhye'ñcakaiḥ ṣaḍbhirvedībandho vidhīyate।
navadhā bhājite tatra vedībandhe samaiḥ padaiḥ।।25।।

**English Translation:**

In the midst of those by six digits is provided the adjustment of a platform divided into nine parts, therein on the platform ligation by equal steps.

---

### Verse 57.26

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुम्भश्चतुष्पदस्तत्र द्विपदस्तु मसूरकः।
भागेनान्तरपत्रं स्यान्मेखला द्विपदा स्मृता।।२६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kumbhaścatuṣpadastathra dvipadastu masūrakaḥ।
bhāgenāntarapatraṃ syānmekhalā dvipadā smṛtā।।26।।

**English Translation:**

A Kumbha of four steps therein and of two steps on the Masūraka (i.e. lintel) may be there. By a part may be the intermediary plate or plank, and mekhalā is known as of two parts.

---

### Verse 57.27

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मूलभागास्तु ये तैः स्याज्जङ्घा दशभिरुच्छ्रिताः।
द्विपदा मेखला प्रोक्ता द्व्यंशे चान्तरपत्रके।।२७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mūlabhāgāstu ye taiḥ syājjaṅghā daśabhirucchritāḥ।
dvipadā mekhalā proktā dvyaṃśe cāntarapatrake।।27।।

**English Translation:**

The basic parts that are there by those, may be Jaṅghā² having height of ten digits. The Mekhalā is spoken of as of two parts and the two internal planks of two parts.

> **Footnote 2:** Jaṅghā- a wall, p.150, Prāsādasikhara.

---

### Verse 57.28

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अधस्तादूर्ध्वपक्ष(ट्र)स्य तलपट्टस्य चोपरि।
षोडशांशा विधातव्यास्तत्रैतत् कर्म वार्चयेत्(?)।।२८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

adhastādūrdhvapakṣa(ṭra)sya talapaṭṭasya copari।
ṣoḍaśāṃśā vidhātavyāstatraitatkarmavārcayet(?)।।28।।

**English Translation:**

Below the upper plank and above the talapāṭṭa (the lower plank), sixteen digited ones deserve to be created : there this work may be examined.

---

### Verse 57.29

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेन रूपधारा स्यात् साढ्ढ़ा शाद्राजसेनकं।
वेदी भागत्रयोत्सेधं सार्धेनासनपट्टुकः।।२९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgena rūpadhārā syāt sārdhā śādrājasenakaṃ।
vedī bhāgatrayotsedhṃ sārdhenāsanapaṭṭukaḥ।।29।।

**English Translation:**

By a part may there be Rūpadhārā and half each may be the Rāja Senaka. The pavilion having an elevation of part triad and by a twain of that the sitting dais.

---

### Verse 57.30

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सोर्ध्व(सार्ध)भागेन कर्तव्यमूर्ध्वचन्द्रावलोकनम्।
आसनस्योर्ध्वत: स्तम्भाः सार्धपञ्चपदाः स्मृताः।।३०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sordhva(sārdha)bhāgena kartavyamūrdhvacandhrāvalookanam।
āsanasyordhvataḥ stambhāḥ sārdhapañcapadāḥ smṛtāḥ।।30।।

**English Translation:**

By a half part deserves to be created the upper moon chamber. The columns above the

---

### Verse 57.31

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेनोच्छालकं कार्यं शाष(शीर्षं) सार्धपदोत्रतम्।
पट्ट: स्याद् द्विपदोत्सेधस्त्रिपदश्छाद्यविस्तर:।।३१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgenocchālakaṃ kāryaṃ śāṣa(śīrṣaṃ) sārdhpadotrataṃ।
paṭṭaḥ syād dvipadotsedhas tripadaś chādyavistaraḥ।।31।।

**English Translation:**

By a part Ucchalaka is to be created and crest as elevated by one and a half digit. The Paṭṭa may be of an elevation of two parts and two parted Chādyavistara.

---

### Verse 57.32

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

लम्बनं तु तदर्धेन यथाशोभमथापि वा।
ऊर्ध्वनान्तर:(ध्वेनान्तर)पत्रस्य कस्य(ल्व्य)तेऽव्ययथाक्रमम्।।३२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

lambanaṃ tu tadardhe na yathāśobham athāpi vā।
ūrdhvanāntaraḥ (dhvenāntara) patrasya kasya(lvya)te'vyayathākramam।।32।।

**English Translation:**

The lambana is to be of a half structure there that or as per beatification of the Ūrdhvānantara Pattra it is provided as per unequal dimensions.

---

### Verse 57.33

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कोणेषु कूट: कर्तव्यो विविधै: कर्मसम्भ्रमै:।
विस्तार: स्याच्चतुर्भागास्तेषां षड्भाग उच्छ्रय:।।३३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

koṇeṣu kūṭaḥ kartavyo vividhaiḥ karmasambhramaiḥ।
vistāraḥ syāc caturbhāgās teṣāṃ ṣaḍbhāga ucchriyaḥ।।33।।

**English Translation:**

On the Koṇas Kūṭa is to be created by varied normed working modifications the breadth may be of four parts and the height of these by six parts.

---

### Verse 57.34

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णा घण्टासमायुक्ता: कूटमानं विधीयते।
तत्र मृत्युक्रमात्(?)कुयदिदेकैकं च तदूर्ध्वत:।।३४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇā ghaṇṭāsamāyuktāḥ kūṭamānaṃ vidhīyate।
tatra mṛtyukramāt(?)kuyadi daikaikaṃ ca tadūrdhvataḥ।।34।।

**English Translation:**

The Karṇas combined by ghaṇḍās (the bells), the measurement of Kūṭa is provided. And therein order of visibility, one may create one each over and above that.

---

### Verse 57.35

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तेषां च तुल्यता कार्या विस्तारादुच्छ्रयादपि।
चत्वार एककर्णे स्युरेवं सर्वेषु षोडश।।३५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

teṣāṃ ca tulyatā kāryā vistārād ucchriyād api।
catvāra ekakarṇe syurevaṃ sarveṣu ṣoḍaśa।।35।।

**English Translation:**

Of these a similitude deserves to be created in light of breadth as well as height. Four may be therein one Karṇa and this very way in all sixteen as such.

---

### Verse 57.36

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सिंहकर्णस्य विस्तारा(र)मानं स्यादष्टभागिकम्।
षड्भागस्तु तथोत्सेधो रथिकैश्च विभूषणम्।।३६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

siṃhakarṇasya vistārā(ra)mānaṃ syād aṣṭabhāgikam।
ṣaḍbhāgas tu tathotsedho rathikaiś ca vibhūṣaṇam।।36।।

**English Translation:**

The measurement of the breadth of the Siṃhakarṇa may be of octagonal norm and hexagonal their elevation and decoration by the Rathikās (main roads or lanes).

---

### Verse 57.37

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गुणद्वा(ता)रसमायुक्त: शूरसेनाभिधानक:।
सिंहकर्णो विधातव्य: सर्वकर्मसमाकुल:।।३७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

guṇadvā(tā)rasamāyuktaḥ śūrasenābhidhānakaḥ।
siṃhakarṇo vidhātavyaḥ sarvakarmasamākulaḥ।।37।।

**English Translation:**

Equipped with Guṇadvāra (tāra), the one named Śūrasena siṃhakarṇa deserves to be created conjoined by all the works.

---

### Verse 57.38

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सिंहकर्णोदयादूर्ध्वमुरोमञ्जरिका भवेत्।
विस्ताराद्ष्टभागासावुच्छ्रायन(यान्र)वभागिका।।३८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

siṃhakarṇodayād ūrdhvam uromaṃjarikā bhavet।
vistārād aṣṭabhāgāsāv ucchrāyana(yānra)vabhāgikā।।38।।

**English Translation:**

Above the height of the Siṃhakarṇa may be Uromaṃjarikā eight digits more than the breadth, and nine digits more than the height.

---

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the translation for verse 39 appears at the top, but the Devanagari beginning is on the previous page. Only the second half of the Devanagari is visible here.]

### Verse 57.39

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

लतापञ्चकसंयुक्ता मञ्जरी स्यात् सुशोभिता।
ग्रीवा पादोनभागा स्यादण्डकं भागमुच्छ्रितम्॥३९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

latāpañcakasaṃyuktā mañjarī syāt suśobhitā |
grīvā pādonabhāgā syādaṇḍakaṃ bhāgamucchritam || 39 ||

**English Translation:**

Equipped with Latā Pañcaka the Mañjarī may be well decorated. The grīvā may be such as of ¾ lesser norm and Āaṇḍaka (cupola) as elevated by a part.

---

### Verse 57.40

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चन्द्रिका चार्धभागेन कलशश्चैव भागिकः।
कूटमूर्ध्वे(र्धि) द्वितीया स्यादुरोमञ्जरिका तथा॥४०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

candrikā cārdhabhāgena kalaśaścaiva bhāgikaḥ |
kūṭamūrdhve(rdhi) dvitīyā syāduroмañjarikā tathā || 40 ||

**English Translation:**

Candrikā may be by a half part and Kalaśa of one part on the crest of the roof may there be a second Uroмañjarikā.

---

### Verse 57.41

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(भागाद्द्वादशविस्तीर्णा तु सार्धा न त्रिदशावाच्छिता?)।
भागमेकं भवेद् ग्रीवा सार्धभागेन चाण्डकम्॥४१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(bhāgāddvādaśavistīrṇā tu sārdhā na tridaśāvācchitā?) |
bhāgamekaṃ bhaved grīvā sārdhabhāgena cāṇḍakam || 41 ||

**English Translation:**

From a part having breadth of twelve having height thirty reduced by one and a half, the grīvā may be of part one, with Aṇḍakas of a part and a half.

> **Footnote 1:** भागान् द्वादशविस्तीर्णान् साधांस्त्रिदशचोच्छ्रितान्। (GOSE, p.353)

---

### Verse 57.42

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कपिरं(?)² चार्धभागेन कलश्च्छ द्विभागिकः।
उर:शिखरकान्य(ण्य)ष्टौ भवन्त्येवं चतुर्(दि)शम्॥४२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kapiraṃ(?)² cārdhabhāgena kalaśccha dvibhāgikaḥ |
uraḥśikharakāny(ṇy)aṣṭau bhavantyevaṃ catur(di)śam || 42 ||

**English Translation:**

A Karpara (a thatch) by a part, with Kalaśa as of two parts, eight Uraḥśikharakas, do exist here in the four quarters or sides.

> **Footnote 2:** कपिरं चार्धभागेन

---

### Verse 57.43

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वितीयकूटकस्योर्ध्वे कर्तव्या मूलमञ्जरी।
भागषोडशविस्तारा पदाष्टादशकोदय:(या)॥४३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvitīyakūṭakasyordhve kartavyā mūlamañjarī |
bhāgaṣoḍaśavistārā padāṣṭādaśakodayaḥ(yā) || 43 ||

**English Translation:**

On the top of the second Kūṭaka, deserves to be created a Mūlamañjarī having breadth of sixteen parts with elevation of eighteen squares or cubits.

---

### Verse 57.44

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्कन्धमानं हि सर्वेषां यथोक्तं शतवास्तुनि।
ग्रीवा सार्धपदांशा स्यादण्डकं द्विपदान्वितम्॥४४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

skandhamānaṃ hi sarveṣāṃ yathoktaṃ śatavāstuni |
grīvā sārdhapādāṃśā syādaṇḍakaṃ dvipadānvitam || 44 ||

**English Translation:**

Of all the measurement of the Skandha is as per enunciation in the Śatavāstu. Grīvā of a part having one and a half with Aṇḍakas equipped with part-twain.

---

### Verse 57.45

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कङ्कतीफलतुल्यानि कुयात् सर्वाण्डकानि च।
द्विपदं च(म)पिण्डकायुग्मं कार्यं सामलसारकम्॥४५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kaṅkatīphalatullyāni kuyāt sarvāṇḍakāni ca |
dvipadaṃ ca(m)piṇḍakāyugmaṃ kāryaṃ sāmalasārakam || 45 ||

**English Translation:**

One may create all the Aṇḍakas in line with Kaṅkatiphala³. The Aṇḍikā pair of two steps deserves to be created along with the Amalasāraka.

> **Footnote 3:** Sida Rhombiglia (MW, p.242)

---

### Verse 57.46

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्योपरि स्यात् कलशो वर्तुलस्त्रिपदोच्छ्रितः।
तोरणैर्मकरैः पत्रैः साग्रैश्च सव(म)रालकैः॥४६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasyopari syāt kalaśo vartulastripadocchritaḥ |
toraṇairmakарaiḥ patraiḥ sāgraiśca sava(m)rālakaiḥ || 46 ||

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the translation at the top of the page ("Over that may be Kalaśa globular and elevated by three steps by Toraṇas, Marakas, Pattras, Sāgras and Marālakas.") belongs to a verse beginning on the previous page.]


### Verse 57.47

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हस्तिमुण्डैः समाकीर्णमथ(प्स) रोगणभूषितम्।
ईदृशं श्रीधरं कुर्यात् सर्वालङ्कारभूषितम्॥४७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

hastipuṇḍaiḥ samākīrṇamatha(psa) rogaṇabhūṣitam |
īdṛśaṃ śrīdharaṃ kuryāt sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitam ||47||

[FLAG: The parenthetical "(प्स)" / "(psa)" appears in the source text and is reproduced as-is. "रोगण" may be "योगण" or another reading — IAST UNCERTAIN: rogaṇa/yogaṇa]

**English Translation:**

Overspread by elephant crests and decorated by the bevy of nymphs, such a one Śrīdhara one may create adorned by all the ornaments or decorations.

---

### Verse 57.48

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

श्रीधरं कारयेद् यस्तु कित्र्यर्थमपि मानव:।
इहैव लभते(वसत:?) सौख्यमुत्रेन्द्रत्वमानुयात्॥४८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śrīdharaṃ kārayet yastu kitryarthamapi mānavaḥ |
ihaiva labhate(vasataḥ?) saukhyamutrendratvamānuyāt ||48||

[FLAG: "(वसत:?)" appears in the source text with a query mark and is reproduced as-is. "कित्र्यर्थम्" may be "कीर्त्यर्थम्" — IAST UNCERTAIN: kitryartham/kīrtyartham]

**English Translation:**

The mānava who may create a Śrīdhara, for the sake of reputation, here he attains bliss while living and Indradom in the yonder world.

---

### Verse 57.49

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भोगान् भुक्त्वा पुमान् स्वर्गं नीयते च परे पदे।
सर्वपापविनिर्मुक्त: शान्तश्च स्यान्न संशय:॥४९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhogān bhuktvā pumān svargaṃ nīyate ca pare pade |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śāntaśca syānna saṃśayaḥ ||49||

**English Translation:**

Having enjoyed the sense objects or objects of enjoyments, a man is taken to the heaven on an upgraded norm and immune from all the sins he may be tranquil- there is no doubt in it.

---

### Verse 57.50

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

श्रीधर:॥
हेमकूटमथ ब्रूम: शुभलक्षणसंयुतम्।
सर्वविद्याधरस्थानमाश्रय: स पिनाकिन:॥५०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śrīdharaḥ ||
hemakūṭamatha brūmaḥ śubhalakṣaṇasaṃyutam |
sarvavidyādharasthānamāśrayaḥ sa pinākinaḥ ||50||

[FLAG: "श्रीधर:॥" appears as a label/colophon line before the verse proper begins.]

**English Translation:**

Now we talk of Hemakūṭa equipped with all the auspicious marks, an abode of all the Vidyādharas and a resort of the trident wielding one (i.e. Śiva).

---

### Verse 57.51

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे षड्विंशत्यंशभाजिते।
तत्र स्यु: षट्पदा: कर्णा: शाला द्वादशभागिका:॥५१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre ṣaḍviṃśatyaṃśabhājite |
tatra syuḥ ṣaṭpadāḥ karṇāḥ śālā dvādaśabhāgikāḥ ||51||

**English Translation:**

In a spot square shaped as such, divided into twenty six digits there may be six stepped Karṇas and halls of twelve parts.

---

### Verse 57.52

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

निर्गताश्च त्रिभिर्भिगैर्भवन्येताश्चतुर्दिशम्।
अष्टभागायतो भूयो निर्गमश्च त्रिभि: पदैः॥५२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

nirgatāśca tribhirbhigairbhavanyetāścaturdiśam |
aṣṭabhāgāyato bhūyo nirgamaśca tribhiḥ padaiḥ ||52||

[FLAG: "भिगैर्भवन्येताश्च" — IAST UNCERTAIN: the reading "भिगैर्" may be "भागैर्" — possible reading: tribhirbhāgairbhavantyetāś]

**English Translation:**

Projected by three parts do there exist in four sides octagonal normed in length then an additional outlet is there by three steps.

---

### Verse 57.53

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतु:स्तम्भाश्चतुष्पाश्व(श्वी) दिशु सर्वास्वयं विधि:।
कर्णशालान्तरं कार्यं पदेनैकेन विस्तृतम्॥५३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

catuḥstambhāścatuṣpāśva(śvī) diśu sarvāsvayaṃ vidhiḥ |
karṇaśālāntaraṃ kāryaṃ padenaikena vistṛtam ||53||

[FLAG: "(श्वी)" parenthetical appears in source text and is reproduced as-is.]

**English Translation:**

Four columned, four flanked, in all the directions such is the procedure. The space between the Karṇa and Śālā deserves to be created broad by one part.

---

### Verse 57.54

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रविष्टं तत् पदेनैकं तदेवात्र जलान्तरम्।
पदेन कर्णे कोण: स्यात् प्रत्यङ्घे पदिवस्तुते॥५४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

praviṣṭaṃ tat padenaikaṃ tadevātra jalāntaram |
padena karṇe koṇaḥ syāt pratyaṅghe padivastute ॥54॥

**English Translation:**

Entered therein by one part that same here a water channel, by a single part in the Karṇa a Koṇa may be there in every limb broad by one step.

---

### Verse 57.55

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

निगिते चार्धभागेन सममाने मनोरमे।
द्विपदा रथिका भद्रे निर्गतार्धपदेन सा॥५५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

nigate cārdhabhāgena samamāne manorame |
dvipadā rathikā bhadre nirgatārdhpadena sā ॥55॥

**English Translation:**

In this projected by a half part having equal measurement of fascinating norm, Dvipadā Rathikā ien the Bhadra, projected by half part as such.

---

### Verse 57.56

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुक(क्ष)णेषु कर्तव्यं (मानषेमेवंधुरु?) धीमता।
वाह्याभित्तेस्तु विस्तारत्रिपद: परिकीतित:॥५६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

catuk(kṣ)aṇeṣu kartavyaṃ (mānaṣemevāṃdhuru?) dhīmatā |
vāhyābhittestu vistāratripadaḥ parikītitaḥ ॥56॥

[FLAG: Parenthetical uncertain readings reproduced as-is from the printed text]

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 355, verse 56, pādas 1-2 चतुष्कणेषु कर्तव्य मानमेवं तु धीमता fn. 2 correction.

**English Translation:**

In four Karṇas should be created the measurement by the expert one.¹ The breadth of the outer wall is declared as of three parts.

---

### Verse 57.57

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतु:षष्टिपदो गर्भस्तद्धित्तित्रिपदा भवेत्।
त्रिपदं कर्णमानं स्याद् वारिमार्गेण संयुतम्॥५७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

catuḥṣaṣṭipado garbhastaddhitritripadā bhavet |
tripadaṃ karṇamānaṃ syād vārimārgeṇa saṃyutam ॥57॥

**English Translation:**

Sixty four stepped garbha, the wall of that may be of three steps. three stepped may be the Karṇamāna conjoined the water course.

---

### Verse 57.58

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदार्धं वारिमार्ग: स्यात् पदमस्य प्रवेशक:।
शालाष्टपदिवस्तीर्णा भागार्धेन विनिर्गता॥५८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padārdhaṃ vārimārgaḥ syāt padamasya praveśakaḥ |
śālāṣṭapadivastīrṇā bhāgārdhena vinirgatā ॥58॥

**English Translation:**

The water course may be of half steps with its entrance of one step and its compartment eight steps broad projected by a half part.

---

### Verse 57.59

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्भागायता भद्रं पुनर्भागार्धनिर्गतम्।
तलन्यासौ(सो) हेमकूटे विभक्तपदनिश्चयात्॥५९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturbhāgāyatā bhadraṃ punarbhāgārdhanirgatam |
talanyāsau(so) hemakūṭe vibhaktapadaniścayāt ॥59॥

[FLAG: Parenthetical variant (सो) reproduced as-is from the printed text]

**English Translation:**

The Bhadra of length of four parts again projected by a half part and Talanyāsa on Hemakūṭa may be by a determination of the divided part.

---

### Verse 57.60

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अस्याग्रे मण्डपं कुर्यान्महान्तं गुणपूजितम्।
ऊर्ध्वं (तु) हेमकूटस्य द्विगुणं स्यात् कलाधिकम्॥६०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

asyāgre maṇḍapaṃ kuryānmahāntaṃ guṇapūjitam |
ūrdhvaṃ (tu) hemakūṭasya dviguṇaṃ syāt kalādhikam ॥60॥

[FLAG: Parenthetical (तु) reproduced as-is from the printed text]

**English Translation:**

In the front of this one, one may create a pavilion huge one and adored by merits. The top of the Hemakūṭa may be doubled one and Kalādhika.

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhattiḥ syāt padiviaṃśatyā tatra kandasamāḥ sṛ(smṛ)tāḥ |
karṇāḥ pratyaṅku ko(kā)nyeṣāṃ pratyekaṃ padivastārāt || 89 ||

[IAST UNCERTAIN: पदिवंशत्या — possibly पद्विंशत्या; प्रत्यङ्कु को(का) — parenthetical in source]

**English Translation:**

The wall may be by twenty padas therein is recognised as equal to Kanda. the Karṇa and every component of these ones, each one from the breadth of the pada.

---

### Verse 57.90

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्व्यंशो मध्यगविस्तार उभयोर्निर्गित(म): पदम्।
द्व्यंश: सरसि(?)विस्तार आक्रान्तपदनिर्गमैः॥९०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvyaṃśo madhyagavistāra ubhayornirgita(m) padam |
dvyaṃśaḥ sarasi(?)vistāra ākrāntapadanirgamaiḥ || 90 ||

[IAST UNCERTAIN: निर्गित(म) — parenthetical in source; सरसि(?) — query mark in source]

**English Translation:**

Two digited the breadth of the madhyaṅga (the central part)¹ emerged out or projected on both sides, being a pada or square. Two digited one the breadth on the crest² by the exit doors of the squares occupied as such.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 358, verse 90, pāda 1. द्व्यंशो मध्यंगविस्तार: for मध्यगविस्तार: fn. 3. (moulding of the door).

> **Footnote 2:** द्व्यंश: शिरासिविस्तार: GOSE, p. 358, fn.5 for सरसिविस्तार:।

---


### Verse 57.91

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विपदा बाह्याभित्तिः स्याद् विस्तारेण सुशोभिता(:?)।
चतुष्पदायतः कर्णो भद्रं तस्य द्विभागिकम्॥९१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvipādā bāhyābhattiḥ syād vistāreṇa suśobhitā(ḥ?)।
catuṣpadāyataḥ karṇo bhadraṃ tasya dvibhāgikam॥91॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: बाह्याभित्तिः — could be bāhyābhattiḥ or bāhyābhittiḥ]

**English Translation:**

The outer wall may be two digited one, adorned by a breadth. The Karṇa oblong by four parts the bhadra of that one of two parts.

---

### Verse 57.92

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

निर्गमोऽस्यार्धभागेन स्यादेवं सुन्दरं कृतम्।
कर्णे कोणासु(स्तु) पदिका दिक्षु सर्वासु शोभना॥९२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

nirgamo'syārdhabhāgena syādevaṃ sundaraṃ kṛtam।
karṇe koṇāsu(stu) padikā dikṣu sarvāsu śobhanā॥92॥

**English Translation:**

The exit door of this one may be of a half part, this way beautified as such. On the Karṇa Koṇas may be of one part good looking in all the direction.

---

### Verse 57.93

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

निगूढविस्तरः कार्यः सार्ध(र्ध)पञ्चपदोन्मितः।
द्विपदो निर्गमस्तत्र सर्वदिक्षु विधीयते॥९३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

nigūḍhvistaraḥ kāryaḥ sārdha(rdha)pañcapadonmitaḥ।
dvipado nirgamastatra sarvadikṣu vidhīyate॥93॥

**English Translation:**

The private breadth of a chamber deserves to be created measured to the extent of five padas and the two digited exit door of that one there in all sides is provided as such.

---

### Verse 57.94

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सलिलान्तरकं कुर्यादन्तरे कर्णभद्रयोः।
प्रविष्टं पद्मानेन पद्पादेन विस्तृतम्॥९४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

salilāntarakaṃ kuryādantare karṇabhadrayoḥ।
praviṣṭaṃ padmānena padapādena vistṛtam॥94॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: पद्मानेन — could be padmānena; पद्पादेन likely padapādena]

**English Translation:**

The water course one may create within the space of Karṇa and Bhadra, incorporated by the measurement of a pada broad by the square of a pada.

---

### Verse 57.95

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वमानमयैतस्य यथावदभिधीयते।
राजपीठं विधातव्यं भागार्धेनातिसुन्दरम्॥९५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūrdhvamānamayaitasya yathāvadabhidhīyate।
rājapīṭhaṃ vidhātavyaṃ bhāgārdhenātiSundaram॥95॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: ऊर्ध्वमानमयैतस्य — possibly ūrdhvamānamayaitasya]

**English Translation:**

The upper measurement of this one, is being declared as per sequence. The Rāja Pīṭha (Royal pedestal or throne) deserves to be created by a half part highly beautiful.

---

### Verse 57.96

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वभागेन खुरकपीठं स्याच्चतुरंशकम्।
द्विपदः कुम्भकोत्सेधः पादोनं सोम(स्यान्म)सूरकम्॥९६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūrdhvabhāgena khurakapīṭhaṃ syāccaturaṃśakam।
dvipādaḥ kumbhakotsedhaḥ pādonaṃ soma(syānma)sūrakam॥96॥

**English Translation:**

By the upper part the Khuraka Pīṭha may be four digited one. Two parted the elevation of the Kumbhaka and reduced by a quarter i.e. 3/4th may be the Masūraka¹.

> **Footnote 1:** The piece of timber or stone that covers an opening and supports a weight above it. It is sometimes used as an Adhiṣṭhāna or base (EOHA, p.452).

---

### Verse 57.97

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागार्धेनान्तरं पत्रं पादोनांशेन मेखला।
षड्भागमुच्छ्रिता जङ्घा भागेन ग्रासपट्टिका:(?)॥९७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgārdhenāntaraṃ patraṃ pādanāṃśena mekhalā।
ṣaḍbhāgamucchrītā jaṅghā bhāgena grāsapaṭṭikā(ḥ?)॥97॥

**English Translation:**

By a half part the antarapattra and Mekhalās by 3/4th of a part. The Jaṅghā may be elevated by six parts and by a part the grāsapaṭṭikās.

---

### Verse 57.98

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेखलान्तरपत्रे च प्रत्येकं पदके स्मृते।
पट्टादधरखुरादूर्ध्वं भागा सैकां दशान्तरम्। (?)॥९८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

mekhalāntarapatre ca pratyekaṃ padake smṛte।
paṭṭādadharakhurādūrdhvaṃ bhāgā saikāṃ daśāntaram। (?)॥98॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: पट्टादधरखुरादूर्ध्वं — paṭṭādadharakhurādūrdhvaṃ; reading uncertain from image]

### Verse 57.99

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

राजासनं पदे प्रोक्तमुत्सेधं(धे)नातिशोभनम्।
अर्धेनात्यधिके कार्ये द्वे पदे वेदिकोच्छ्रूय:।।१९९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

rājāsanaṃ pade proktamutsedhaṃ(dhe)nātiśobhanam |
ardhenātyadhike kārye dve pade vedikocc hrūyaḥ || 199 ||

[FLAG: Verse number reads ॥१९९॥ — visually appears as ११९९ or similar; number uncertain. The verse numeral appears to be ॥९९॥ based on context. IAST UNCERTAIN for verse number.]

**English Translation:**

Rājāsana (throne) is spoken of as a single unit in elevation highly gorgeous. By a half highly exceeding ones deserve to be created two padas with the elevation of a dais or pavilion or enclosure.

---

### Verse 57.100

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदार्धमासनं कार्यं द्व्यंशं चन्द्रावलोकनम्।
ऊर्ध्वमासनपटुस्य स्तम्भ: पञ्चपदान्वित:।।१०० ।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

padārdhamāsanaṃ kāryaṃ dvyaṃśaṃ candrāvalokanam |
ūrdhvamāsanapaṭusya stambhaḥ pañcapadānvitaḥ || 100 ||

**English Translation:**

The seat deserves to be created as of half pada digit and two digited Candrīvalokana and the column of the upper squatting platform may be of five squared norm.

---

### Verse 57.101

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भरणं स्तम्भशीर्षं च पदेन स्यात् समुच्छ्रितम्।
छाद्यकेनावृतं कुर्यात् पदेनैकेन पटुकम्।।१०१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bharaṇaṃ stambhaśīrṣaṃ ca padena syāt samucchritam |
chādyakenāvṛtaṃ kuryāt padenaikena paṭukam || 101 ||

> **Footnote:** (पञ्चपदान्वित: ?)

**English Translation:**

Bharaṇa and Stambhasīrṣa may be elevated by a single pada or square and the paṭṭaka (the platform) may one create covered by a Chādyaka of one Padaka norm.

---

### Verse 57.102

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विपदस्य: प्र(च्छाद्य)विस्तार: पदेनैकेन लम्बनम्।
ऊर्ध्वमन्तरपत्रस्य कथयामो यथास्थितम्।।१०२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvipadasya pracchadyavistāraḥ padenaikena lambanam |
ūrdhvamantarapatrasya kathayāmo yathāsthitam || 102 ||

**English Translation:**

Two square normed may be the breadth of the Pracchādya (roof) and by a single square the Lambana (the plumb line) of the upper and intermediary space, we shall talk of as established.

---

### Verse 57.103

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुष्पदेषु कर्णेषु ये कर्णा: पदिका: स्थिता:।
तेषु (घिखरका:?) कार्या विस्तारोच्छ्रायत: पदम्।।१०३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

catuṣpadeṣu karṇeṣu ye karṇāḥ padikāḥ sthitāḥ |
teṣu (śikharakāḥ?) kāryā vistārocchhrāyataḥ padam || 103 ||

[FLAG: तेषु (घिखरका:?) — the bracketed term with ? is reproduced as printed; likely śikharakāḥ. IAST UNCERTAIN for this term.]

**English Translation:**

In Karṇas of Catuṣpadas (four squares), the Karṇas that stand as of one square, among them the roofs (Śikharakas) deserve to be created as single squared as compared with the height of the breadth or height and breadth.

---

### Verse 57.104

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कलशोनं तथा ग्रीवा पदार्धेन समुच्छ्रिता।
द्विपद: सिंहकर्णस्तु विस्तारोच्छ्रायत: सम:।।१०४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kalaśonaṃ tathā grīvā padārdhena samucchritā |
dvipadaḥ siṃhakarṇastu vistārocchhrāyataḥ samaḥ || 104 ||

> **Footnote 1:** Kalaśa- A pitcher, a cupola, a finial, a dome, a pinnacle, a tower or a type of round building (EOHA p.118).

**English Translation:**

And likewise the grīvā may be smaller than the Kalaśa¹ elevated by a half of the square. The Siṃhakarṇa may be of two squared norm, equal in size of the breadth and height.

---

[FLAG: The verse beginning **शिखिराधो विधातव्या त्रिपदी कर्णमञ्जरी।** is visible at the bottom of the page but its second line and English translation continue on the next page.]

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शिखिराधो विधातव्या त्रिपदी कर्णमञ्जरी।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śikhirādho vidhātavyā tripadī karṇamañjarī |

### Verse 57.105

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वं च त्रिपदा स (सा) स्याद् द्विपदा स्कन्धविस्तृतिः॥१०५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūrdhvaṃ ca tripadā sa (sā) syād dvipadā skandhavisstṛtiḥ ॥105॥

[FLAG: This appears to be only the second half of a verse; first pāda likely continued from previous page.]

**English Translation:**

Below the roof (Śikharādho) deserves to be created Tripadī Karṇamañjarī. And above that may be Tripadā, dvipadā as Skandhavistṛti¹.

> **Footnote 1:** The breadth of the Skandha, a type of gate house (EOHA, p.582).

---

### Verse 57.106

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सार्धभागेन कर्तव्यं समीवं कलशाण्डकम्।
सिंहप्रासादवत् कर्णा विधेयाः शुभलक्षणाः॥१०६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sārdhabhāgena kartavyaṃ samīvaṃ kalaśāṇḍakam ।
siṃhāprāsādavat karṇā vidheyāḥ śubhalakṣaṇāḥ ॥106॥

**English Translation:**

The Kalaśaṇḍaka equipped with a grīvā deserves to be created by a half a square. Like the Siṃha Prāsāda, deserve to be created the Karṇas of auspicious norm.

---

### Verse 57.107

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मूलमानेन विस्तीर्णा निर्गू(ग)ढस्योपरि² स्थिताः।
द्वितीयश्च तृतीयश्च तदूर्ध्वं च समुच्छ्रितः॥१०७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

mūlamānena vistīrṇā nirgū(ga)ḍhasyopari² sthitāḥ ।
dvitīyaś ca tṛtīyaś ca tadūrdhvaṃ ca samucchritaḥ ॥107॥

**English Translation:**

Broad by the original measurement stationed above the Niryūha³ (the projection on the columns or gates), the second and the third one over that elevated as such.

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE, p.359 fn.7 निर्यूहस्य for निर्गूढ
> **Footnote 3:** V.S. Apte, p.295.

---

### Verse 57.108

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णस्थकलशादूर्ध्वं कर्तव्या मूलमञ्जरी।
विस्तारो दशभागांशानु(ग:स्यादु)च्छ्रायो द्वादशांशक:॥१०८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇasthakaḷaśādūrdhvaṃ kartavyā mūlamañjarī ।
vistāro daśabhāgāṃśānu(gaḥ syādu)cchāyo dvādaśāṃśakaḥ ॥108॥

**English Translation:**

Above the Karṇasthakalāśa deserves to be created Mūlamañjarī. The breadth may be of ten parts with elevation of twelve norms.

---

### Verse 57.109

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

लताभिः पञ्च(वि?)भिर्युक्ता विचित्रैश्चापि कर्मभिः।
षट्पदोऽस्य स्मृतः स्कन्धो ग्रीवा चास्य चतुष्पदा॥१०९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

latābhiḥ pañca(vi?)bhiryuktā vicittraiścāpi karmabhiḥ ।
ṣaṭpado'sya smṛtaḥ skandho grīvā cāsya catuṣpadā ॥109॥

**English Translation:**

Equipped with five latās by variegated ten times, the Skandha of this one is known as of six padas and grīvā of that one as of four padas.

---

### Verse 57.110

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तारेण समाख्याता पादोनं पदमुच्छिता।
अण्डकां(कं) साद्यिग्भागेन(भवि?) षड्भागविस्तृतम्॥११०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistāreṇa samākhyātā pādonaṃ padamucchitā ।
aṇḍakāṃ(kaṃ) sādigbhāgena(bhavi?) ṣaḍbhāgavistṛtam ॥110॥

**English Translation:**

By breadth has been detailed the lesser by a pada in elevation, and Aṇḍaka by the part of the root six parts in width.

---

### Verse 57.111

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पादोनं चन्द्रिकाभागं कलशश्च द्विभागिकः।
प्रासादं ये सुभद्राख्यं कारयन्ति सुलक्षणम्॥१११॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pādonaṃ candrikābhāgaṃ kalaśaś ca dvibhāgikaḥ ।
prāsādaṃ ye subhadrākhyaṃ kārayanti sulakṣaṇam ॥111॥

**English Translation:**

The Candrikābhāga being reduced by a part and Kalaśa of part-twain, the Prāsāda named Subhadra who arrange to get built, is endowed with auspicious marks.

---

### Verse 57.112

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कल्पकोटिसहस्राणि भद्रं तेषां शिवाग्रतः।
सुभद्रः॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kalpakōṭisahasrāṇi bhadraṃ teṣāṃ śivāgrataḥ ।
subhadraḥ ॥

### Verse 57.112

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वपापक्षयकरस्त्रिषु लोकेषु कीर्तितः॥११२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarvapāpakṣayakarastriṣu lokeṣu kīrtitaḥ ॥112॥

**English Translation:**

For thousands of crores of aeons, their well being gets ensured before Śiva. An obviator of all sins well reputed in the triad of worlds.

[FLAG: First pāda of verse 112 continues from previous page; only second pāda visible here.]

---

### Verse 57.113

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रिपुकेसरिसंज्ञोऽयं प्रासाद: परिकीर्त्यते।
चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे भागविंशतिभाजिते॥११३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ripukesarisaṃjño'yaṃ prāsādaḥ parikīrtyate ।
caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre bhāgaviṃśatibhājite ॥113॥

**English Translation:**

This one known as Ripukesarī the Prāsāda stands enunciated. In the field square shaped divided into twenty parts.

---

### Verse 57.114

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विपदा बाह्याभित्ति: स्यान्मध्याभित्तिश्च तावता(ती)।
भ्रमणी द्विपदा कार्या विस्तारात् सर्विदग्रता:(ggता)॥११४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvipadā bāhyābhittiḥ syānmadhyābhittiśca tāvatā(tī) ।
bhramaṇī dvipadā kāryā vistārāt sarvidagratā(ggta) ॥114॥

[FLAG: The words "सर्विदग्रता:(ggता)" appear garbled/uncertain in the source; rendered as visible.]

**English Translation:**

Two folded outer wall may be there and the central wall also of the same extent. Bhramaṇī deserves to be created as of square twain in breadth as gone over to all the direction.

---

### Verse 57.115

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गर्भोऽष्टविस्तृत: कर्ण:(+कन्द?) सार्धभागिक:१।
चतुर्भागायतं भद्रं कुर्याद् भागेन निर्गतम्॥११५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

garbho'ṣṭavistṛtaḥ karṇaḥ (+kanda?) sārdhabhāgikaḥ ।
caturbhāgāyataṃ bhadraṃ kuryād bhāgena nirgatam ॥115॥

[FLAG: "(+कन्द?)" appears in the source text itself as an editorial insertion with query mark; preserved verbatim.]

**English Translation:**

The garbha is to be of octagonal breadth and Karṇa of one and half part oblong by four parts one may create the Bhadra projected by one part.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p.360 fn.7 has कर्ण: साद्धिंभागिक:गर्भ: – Adyatum, a chamber in a temple where the deity is placed (EOHA, p.157).

### Verse 57.116

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रथकोऽसौ समुद्दिष्टो विधातव्यश्चतुर्दिशम्।
पार्श्वयोश्च प्रतिरथौ कार्यौ सार्धपदायतौ॥११६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

rathako'sau samuddiṣṭo vidhātavyaścaturdiśam ।
pārśvayośca pratirathou kāryau sārdhapadāyatau ॥116॥

**English Translation:**

Rathaka as that one stands defined and deserves to be created in all the four directions as also on the two flanks the counter rathas (Pratirathas), oblong by one and a half square.

---

### Verse 57.117

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदार्धेन विनिष्कान्तौ +++ विविधेष्वपि२।
कर्णायामश्चतुर्भागो द्विपदं कर्णभद्रकम्॥११७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padārdhena viniṣkāntau +++ vividheṣvapi ।
karṇāyāmaścaturbhāgo dvipadaṃ karṇabhadrakam ॥117॥

[FLAG: "+++" appears in the source text itself, indicating illegible/missing text; preserved verbatim.]

**English Translation:**

Projected out by a half square, such a norm is valid even in case of Rathas. The length of the Karṇas may be of four parts and Karṇa bhadraka of two parts.

> **Footnote 2:** पदार्धेन विनिष्क्रान्तो विधिरेष रथष्वपे। fn.9, p.360, verse 117 pāda2.

---

### Verse 57.118

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदार्धेन विनिष्कान्तं बाह्यकर्णे व्यवस्थितम्।
पद(:?)पादेन विस्तीर्णं प्रविष्टं पदमात्रकम्॥११८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padārdhena viniṣkāntaṃ bāhyakarṇe vyavasthitam ।
pada(ḥ?)pādena vistīrṇaṃ praviṣṭaṃ padamātrakam ॥118॥

[FLAG: "पद(:?)" appears in the source text itself with query mark; preserved verbatim.]

**English Translation:**

Projected by a half part and well laid down as the outer Karṇa, the pada broad by a square norm entered inside by a single pada.

---

### Verse 57.119

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कार्यं जलान्तरं मध्ये कर्णस्य तिलकस्य च।
द्वचंशस्तिलकविस्तार: पदेनैकेन निर्गम:॥११९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kāryaṃ jalāntaraṃ madhye karṇasya tilakasya ca ।
dvacaṃśastilakavistāraḥ padenaekena nirgamaḥ ॥119॥
