### Verse 57.121

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सुवर्णिता: स्युस्तिलका भद्रकोणव्यवस्थिता:।
अष्टभागं भवेद् भद्रं पद्त्रयविनिर्गतम्।।१२०।।

[FLAG: The verse number visible in the text is ॥१२०॥ — I am reading the numeral carefully. The verse shown here is numbered 120.]

**IAST Transliteration:**

suvarṇitāḥ syustilakā bhadrakoṇavyavasthitāḥ।
aṣṭabhāgaṃ bhaved bhadraṃ padtrayavinirgatam।।120।।

**English Translation:**

The Tilaka may be well described or coloured adjusted well in the Bhadrakoṇa. Bhadra may be of octagonal normed angles projected by part triad.

---

### Verse 57.122

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतसृष्वपि तद् दिक्षु कर्तव्यं स्तम्भभूषितम्।
ऊर्ध्वमानमथ बूम: प्रतिपत्रं सुखावहम्।।१२१।।

[FLAG: Verse number reads ॥१२१॥]

**IAST Transliteration:**

catasṛṣvapi tad dikṣu kartavyaṃ stambhabhūṣitam।
ūrdhvamānamatha būmaḥ pratipatraṃ sukhāvaham।।121।।

**English Translation:**

In the four quarters deserves to be created as decorated by columns. The upper measurement we know explain part as blissful.

---

### Verse 57.123

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वप्रमाणं द्विगुणं कर्तव्यं द्विकलाधिकम्।
भागैरेकोनविंशत्या मध्ये कार्यस्तलोदय:।।१२२।।

[FLAG: Verse number reads ॥१२२॥]

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūrdhvapramāṇaṃ dviguṇaṃ kartavyaṃ dvikalādhikam।
bhāgairekonaviṃśatyā madhye kāryastalodayaḥ।।122।।

**English Translation:**

The upper measurement or the measurement of the upper portion or roof deserves to be done exceeding by two Kalās and the talodaya (the elevation of the floor or platform) is to be created in the centre by a nineteen parts.

---

### Verse 57.124

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एभ्यो मध्याद् विधातव्या वेदीबशा: सुशोभना:।
द्विपद: कुम्भक: साधं पदं तु कलशो भवेत्।।१२३।।

[FLAG: Verse number reads ॥१२३॥]

**IAST Transliteration:**

ebhyo madhyād vidhātavyā vedībaśāḥ suśobhanāḥ।
dvipadaḥ kumbhakaḥ sārdhaṃ padaṃ tu kalaśo bhavet।।123।।

**English Translation:**

In the centre from these deserve to be created the Vedī bandhas (the pavilion structures) of beautiful norm and Kumbhaka¹ of pada twain and Kalaśa may be of one and a half pada.

> **Footnote 1:** The base of a coloumn (EOHA, p.138).

---

### Verse 57.125

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेखलान्तरपत्रे तु कार्ये सार्धपदोन्नते।
नवभागोन्रता जङ्घा द्विपदा रूपपट्टिका।।१२४।।

[FLAG: Verse number reads ॥१२४॥]

**IAST Transliteration:**

mekhalāntarapatre tu kārye sārdhapadonnate।
navabhāgonratā jaṅghā dvipadā rūpapaṭṭikā।।124।।

**English Translation:**

The two Mekhalāntarapattras deserves to be created elevated by one and a half padas. The Jaṅghā may be elevated by nine parts and pada twained Rūpapaṭṭikā.

---

### Verse 57.126

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेखलान्तरपत्रे तु विदधीत पदद्वयम्।
मध्यं स्यात् षोडशपदं (मानाया:?)खुरके(को)ऽस्य च।।१२५।।

[FLAG: Verse number reads ॥१२५॥. The text contains editorial annotations "(मानाया:?)" and "(को)" as printed in the source.]

**IAST Transliteration:**

mekhalāntarapatre tu vidadhīta padadvayam।
madhyaṃ syāt ṣoḍaśapadaṃ (mānāyāḥ?)khurake(ko)'sya ca।।125।।

**English Translation:**

In the Mekhalāntarapattra one may create pada-twain. The Madhya (or centre) may be of sixteen padas, of Mālā and Khuruka (fn. 8, corrected p. 361).

> **Footnote 2:** मालाया: खुरकस्य (fn.8)

---

### Verse 57.127

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

राजसेना तथा वेदी तद्वदासनपट्टकम्।
पदै: पञ्चभिरेतानि विदध्यादूर्ध्वमानत:।।१२६।।

[FLAG: Verse number reads ॥१२६॥]

**IAST Transliteration:**

rājasanā tathā vedī tadvadāsanapaṭṭakam।
padaiḥ pañcabhiretāni vidadhyādūrdhvamānataḥ।।126।।

**English Translation:**

Rājasanā and likewise Vedī and that very way the Āsanapaṭṭaka, by five padas these one may create by a higher measurement.

### Verse 57.127

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चन्द्रावलोकनं कुर्याद् द्विपदं भागमानतः।
उपर्यासनपट्टस्य स्तम्भः स्यात् सप्तभागिकः॥१२७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

candrāvalokanaṃ kuryād dvipadaṃ bhāgamānataḥ |
uparyāsanapaṭṭasya stambhaḥ syāt saptabhāgikaḥ ||127||

**English Translation:**

Candrāvalokana one may create of pada twain by a divisional measure. Above the āsanapaṭṭaka the column or pillar may be of seven parts.

---

### Verse 57.128

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भरणं स्तम्भशीर्षं च द्विपदं चोर्ध्वमानतः।
द्विपदः पट्टपिण्डः स्यात् त्रिपदश्छाद्यविस्तरः॥१२८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bharaṇaṃ stambhaśīrṣaṃ ca dvipadaṃ cordhvamānataḥ |
dvipadaḥ paṭṭapiṇḍaḥ syāt tripadaścchādyavistaraḥ ||128||

**English Translation:**

Bharaṇa and Stambhaśīrṣa may be pada-twained by a higher measurement. The Paṭṭapiṇḍa maybe pada-twained and pada-triad normed the breadth of the Chādya (roof).

---

### Verse 57.129

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वमन्तरपत्रस्य साम्प्रतं परिकीर्त्यते।
चतुष्पदः कर्णशृङ्गमायामोच्छ्रायतः ++ (?)॥१२९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūrdhvamantarapatrasya sāmprataṃ parikīrtyate |
catuṣpadaḥ karṇaśṛṅgamāyāmocchhrāyataḥ ++ (?) ||129||

**English Translation:**

Now the elevation of the antarapattra is being dilated upon now. The Karṇa Śṛṅga of four padas as compared with the height and length.

---

### Verse 57.130

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवाण्डकं च भागेन चन्द्रिकार्धपदेन च।
कलशश्छार्धभागेन कर्तव्योऽत्र न संशयः॥१३०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvāṇḍakaṃ ca bhāgena candrikārdhapadena ca |
kalaśaścchārdhabhāgena kartavyo'tra na saṃśayaḥ ||130||

**English Translation:**

And Grīvāṇḍaka¹ by a parts and Candrikā by a half part. Kalaśa by a half part deserves to be created here. There is no doubt in it.

> **Footnote 1:** The spire of the neck.

---

### Verse 57.131

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अस्योर्ध्वतः प्रकर्तव्या द्वितीया कर्णमञ्जरी।
त्रिपदायामविस्तारा ग्रीवाण्डकलशा(:) पद्म्॥१३१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

asyordhvataḥ prakartavyā dvitīyā karṇamañjarī |
tripadāyāmavistarā grīvāṇḍakalaśā(:) padm ||131||

[FLAG: The last word/syllable of the second pāda is unclear — appears to read पद्म् or similar; transliteration uncertain: `[IAST UNCERTAIN: padm]`]

**English Translation:**

Above this deserves to be created the second Karṇamañjarī and having breadth and length of pada-triad the structure of Grīvāṇḍakalaśa.

---

### Verse 57.132

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रकर्णाश्रिते ह्यंशो विस्तारिस्तलके स्मृतः।
उच्छ्रयिस्त्रिपदस्तस्य द्वितीयः स्यात् तदूर्ध्वतः॥१३२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadrakarṇāśrite hyaṃśo vistāristalake smṛtaḥ |
ucchrayistripadastasya dvitīyaḥ syāt tadūrdhvataḥ ||132||

**English Translation:**

On the Tilaka recumbent upon Bhadrakarṇa the breadth is remembered as of two digits. The elevation of that is of three padas and the second one may be above that.

---

### Verse 57.133

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सार्धद्विपद उच्छ्रायो विस्तारो द्विपदः स्मृतः।
सप्तभागोन्नतं तद्वद् विस्ताराद्ष्टभागिकम्॥१३३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sārdhadvipada ucchhrāyo vistāro dvipadaḥ smṛtaḥ |
saptabhāgonnatam tadvad vistārādaṣṭabhāgikam ||133||

**English Translation:**

The height may be of two padas along with one and a half and the breadth is known as of two padas. Having elevation of seven parts that very way may be the octagonal spot in light of the breadth.

---

### Verse 57.134

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सिंहकर्णौ(र्णं) प्रकुर्वीत सुसूत्रमानपूर्वकम्(?)।
उरोमञ्झरिका कार्या द्वितीया तिलकोर्ध्वतः॥१३४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

siṃhakarṇau(rṇaṃ) prakurvīta susūtramānapūrvakam(?) |
uromañjharikā kāryā dvitīyā tilakordhvataḥ ||134||

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

**English Translation:**

One may create the Siṃhakarṇa equipped with the plumb line measurement. Uromaṇjarīkā deserves to be created the second one, above the Tilaka.

---

### Verse 57.135

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अर्धभागायता मूले नवभागमितोच्छ्रया(: ?)।
तस्यास्तु स्कन्धविस्तारो भागैः स्यादर्धपञ्चमैः॥१३५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ardhabhāgāyatā mūle navabhāgamitocc̈hrāyā(: ?) |
tasyāstu skandhavistarō bhāgaiḥ syādardhapañcamaiḥ ॥135॥

[FLAG: The word उच्छ्रया has a visarga and question mark in the text: उच्छ्रया(: ?)]

**IAST Transliteration (revised):**

ardhabhāgāyatā mūle navabhāgamitocc̈hrāyā(: ?) |
tasyāstu skandhavistaraḥ bhāgaiḥ syādardhapañcamaiḥ ॥135॥

**English Translation:**

At root oblong by a half part, with elevation measuring a nine parts, may be there. The breadth of the Skandha of that one, may there be by parts two and a half (ardhapañcaka).

---

### Verse 57.136

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवार्धभागमुत्सेधाद् भागेनामू(म)लसारकम्।
चन्द्रिका चार्धभागेन कलशो भागमुच्छितः॥१३६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvārdhabhāgamutsedhād bhāgenāmū(m)lasārakam |
candrikā cārdhabhāgena kalaśo bhāgamucchitaḥ ॥136॥

**English Translation:**

From the elevation, the Amalasāraka may be by a part with a half part of the Grīvā. Candrikā may be by a half part and Kalaśa elevated by a part.

---

### Verse 57.137–138

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वितीया कर्णशृङ्गस्य स्यादूर्ध्वे मूलमञ्जरी।
भागा(ग)द्वादशविस्तारा कलयाभ्यधिकोर्ध्वतः॥१३७॥
स्कन्धः सप्तदश(दः) प्रोक्तो ग्रीवा भागसमुच्छिता।
अण्डस्य द्वचंश उत्सेधो विस्तारः सप्तभागिकः॥१३८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvitīyā karṇaśṛṅgasya syādūrdhve mūlamañjarī |
bhāgā(ga)dvādaśavistārā kalayābhyadhikordhvataḥ ॥137॥
skandhaḥ saptadaśa(daḥ) prokto grīvā bhāgasamucchitā |
aṇḍasya dvacaṃśa utsedhō vistaraḥ saptabhāgikaḥ ॥138॥

**English Translation:**

Skandha is declared of seven padas and Grīvā elevated by a part. The elevation may be of digit twain as compared with the Aṇḍa and breadth of seven parts.

---

### Verse 57.139

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चन्द्रिकैकेन भागेन कलशस्तु द्विभागिकः।
तला(लता)नागरिका कार्यानात्यन्तक(नान्यत् क)मात्रं योजयेत्॥१३९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

candrikaikena bhāgena kalaśastu dvibhāgikaḥ |
talā(latā)nāgarikā kāryānātyantaka(nānyat ka)mātraṃ yojayet ॥139॥

**English Translation:**

Candrikā by a single part and Kalaśa of part-twain. Latā deserves to be created as nāgarikā and no other arrangement may be made.

---

### Verse 57.140

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जेस्मिन्(?) विजयमिच्छन्ति भोगान् सुविपुलानपि।
सर्वपापप्रणाशं च कार्यो वा रिपुकेसरी॥१४०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jesminn(?) vijayamiccanti bhogān suvipulānapi |
sarvapāpapraṇāśaṃ ca kāryo vā ripukesarī ॥140॥

Those¹ who want victory in it as also the enjoyments of fabulous norm, and the obviation of all the sins by him may be created the Ripukesarī.

### Verse 57.141

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रिपुकेसरी॥
इदानीं प्रेष(पुष्प)कं नाम प्रासादमभिदध्महे।
निर्मितं(तो) धनदस्यार्थे (यः) पूर्वं विश्वकर्मणा॥१४१॥

ripukesarī ॥
idānīṃ preṣa(puṣpa)kaṃ nāma prāsādamabhidadhmahi |
nirmitaṃ(to) dhanadasvārthe (yaḥ) pūrvaṃ viśvakarmaṇā ॥141॥

Now one named Puṣpaka a Prāsāda we shall talk of the one that stood built for the benefit of Dhanada (i.e. Kubera) earlier by Viśvakarmā.

### Verse 57.143

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णमकर्णगभांद्य²(?) सूत्रं तेन समानि च।
रक्तानि दत्त्वा सूत्राणि तद्यद्वितयः(?) स्मृताः।।१४३।।

karṇamakarṇagabhāṃdya²(?) sūtraṃ tena samāni ca |

Gone within the Karṇa, a plumb line for the purpose of the scenting of the Karṇa and equal to that having applied threads or plumbs ruddy hued, known as helpful at the front.

### Verse 57.144

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सूत्रेण चतुर कर्णान् दिक्स्थानुत्यात(द)येत् ततः।
चत्वारोऽन्ये पुनः कर्णाः संसिद्धा सर्वमे (ए)व हि।।१४४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sūtreṇa catura karṇān dikasthānutyāta(da)yet tataḥ |
catvāro'nye punaḥ karṇāḥ saṃsiddhā sarvame (e)va hi || 144 ||

**English Translation:**

By the plumb line one may join four Karṇas fixed in the quarters. Four other Karṇas added as remain calculated as such.

---

### Verse 57.145

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एवमष्टदला(न्) कर्णान् वृत्तानेवं(व) प्रकल्पयेत्।
भागप्रवेशविस्तारं कर्णान्ते च जलान्तरम्।।१४५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

evamāṣṭadalā(n) karṇān vṛttānevaṃ(va) prakalpayet |
bhāgapraveśavistāraṃ karṇānte ca jalāntaram || 145 ||

**English Translation:**

This way one may create circular or globular normed Karṇas of octagonal flanks. The breadth of the introduction of a part and at the end of the Karṇa a Jalāntara (water course).

---

### Verse 57.146

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शाला स्यात् षट्पदायामा त्रिपदोऽस्यच्छ निर्गमः।
द्विपदा बाह्यभित्तिः स्यात् षट्पदा कन्दिविस्तृतिः।।१४६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śālā syāt ṣaṭpadāyāmā tripadо'syaccha nirgamaḥ |
dvipādā bāhyabhittiḥ syāt ṣaṭpadā kandivistṛtiḥ || 146 ||

**English Translation:**

The hall may be oblong by hexagonal norm and its outlet or exit door may be three digited as such. The outer wall of two digited norm and hexagonal normed the breadth of the Kanda.

---

### Verse 57.147

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कन्दगर्भस्थितं तत्रं भ्रामयेत् कर्णकंवत(?)³।
उत्पद्यते ततो वृत्तं समसूत्रं सुशोभनम्।।१४७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kandagarbhasthitaṃ tatraṃ bhrāmayet karṇakaṃvat(?)³ |
utpadyate tato vṛttaṃ samasūtraṃ suśobhanam || 147 ||

**English Translation:**

The tantra may be stationed on the centre of the Kanda one may rotate from the curvature of the Kanda. Then does get generation the circle, of similar dimensions beautiful as such.

> **Footnote 3:** कन्दगर्भस्थितं पजं भ्रामयेत् कर्णकन्दत: GOSE, p. 363, verse 148, pādas 1-2.

---

### Verse 57.148

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्वीत तस्य मध्ये तु कन्दं षोडशपत्रकम्।
भित्तिकन्दान्तराले यच्छेषं + स्याद्भ्रमन्तिका(?)⁴।।१४८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kurvīta tasya madhye tu kandaṃ ṣoḍaśapatrakam |
bhittikandāntarāle yaccheṣaṃ + syādbhramantikā(?)⁴ || 148 ||

**English Translation:**

In the centre of that one, one may create a Kanda of sixteen pattrakas. And the Śeṣa that may be within the interval of the Kanda of the wall, or that may be Bhramantikā.⁵

> **Footnote 4:** शेषं सा स्याद्भ्रमन्तिका GOSE, p. 363, verse 148, pādas 3-4.
> **Footnote 5:** सा स्याद्भ्रमन्तिका।

### Verse 57.149

पुष्पकस्य तलन्यासः पञ्चपुष्पाकृतिर्भवेत्।
इदानीमूर्ध्वमस्यैव कथ्यते मानपूर्वकम्॥१४९॥

puṣpakasya talanyāsaḥ pañcapuṣpākṛtirbhavet |
idānīmūrdhvamasvaiva kathyate mānapūrvakam || 149 ||

**English Translation:**

The function of the ground of Puṣpaka may be of the shape of five flowers. Now the top of this one is being dilated upon inline with the measurement.

### Verse 57.150

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(एकोनत्रिंशदन्यूर्ध्वं सा स्युः पदान् पूर्वं?) यथाक्रमम्।
अतो बहिर्विधातव्यः पीठबन्धः पदत्रयम्॥१५०॥

(ekonatriṃśadanyūrdhvaṃ sā syuḥ padān pūrvaṃ?) yathākramam |
ato bahirvidhātavyaḥ pīṭhabandhaḥ padatrayam || 150 ||

**English Translation:**

Thirty padas of this one may be overhead as per descending order and on the exterior of this one deserves to be created the Pīṭhabandha having pada triad.

> **Footnote 1:** triṃśadya syuḥ padānyūrdhvaṃ

### Verse 57.151

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(जयो?) कुम्भः सपादांशः पादोनः स्यान्मसूरकः।
अर्धेनान्तरपत्रं स्यात् कपोताली च तत्समा॥१५१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(jayo?) kumbhaḥ sapādāṃśaḥ pādonaḥ syānmasūrakaḥ |
ardhenāntarapatraṃ syāt kapotālī ca tatsamā || 151 ||

**English Translation:**

The Jaṅghākaṇṭha of one and a quarter digits and lesser by as pāda may be masūraka (पादान: स्यान्मसूरक:). The Antarapatra may be by a half, and Kapotalī equal to that.

---

### Verse 57.152

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

माला विद्याधरी कार्या पुष्पहस्तैरलङ्कृता।
पद्द्वादशकोत्सेधः (सगुरु:?) स्यात् तुलोदयः॥१५२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

mālā vidyādharī kāryā puṣpahastairalāṅkṛtā |
paddvādaśakotsedhaḥ (saguru?) syāt tulodayaḥ || 152 ||

**English Translation:**

Mālā deserves to be created as Vidyādharī decorated by floral hands or floral cubits. The elevation may be of twelve padas and perforated may be the beam elevation or architrave.

---

### Verse 57.153

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अस्य मध्ये विधातव्यो वेदीबन्धस्त्रिभागिकः।
पदार्धं खुरकं कुर्याद् भागेनैकेन कुम्भकम्॥१५३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

asya madhye vidhātavyo vedībandhastrибhāgikaḥ |
padārdhaṃ khurakaṃ kuryād bhāgenaikena kumbhakam || 153 ||

**English Translation:**

In the centre of this is to be created a Vedībandha (a pavilion structure) of part-triad. The Khuraka one may create of half a pada and Kumbhaka by a part alone.

---

### Verse 57.154

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मसूरकं पदार्धेन मेखला पदमानतः।

**IAST Transliteration:**

masūrakaṃ padārdhena mekhalā padamānataḥ |
ṣaḍbhāgasamucchritā jaṅghā puṣpake parikīrtitā || 154 ||

**English Translation:**

Masūraka may be by a single part and Mekhalāby a measure of one pada. The Jaṅghā may be elevated by six parts illustrated over the Puṣpaka.

---

### Verse 57.155

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

व(म)रालग्रास(ह)मकरैः पुष्पविद्याधरैरपि।
सूक्ष्मकर्णसमा कर्णा° (?) चास्य जङ्घा विधीयते॥१५५॥

va(ma)rālagrāsa(ha)makaraiḥ puṣpavidyādharairapi |
sūkṣmakarṇasamā karṇā° (?) cāsya jaṅghā vidhīyate || 155 ||

**English Translation:**

By Puṣpavidyādharas, alligators, crocodiles and swans the Jaṅghā of this one is provided equal in norm to the Karṇas as similar to minuter Karṇas.

### Verse 57.157

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वतसतलपटुस्य पटुस्योर्ध्वस्य मस्तकम्।

ūrdhvatasatalapaṭusya paṭusyordhvasya mastakam।

Above the talapaṭṭa, the mastaka of the above lying paṭṭaka, having eleven parts deserves to be created Karṇa carcitā (kā).

### Verse 57.158

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेन राजसेनं स्याद् द्विभागो वेदिकोच्छृता:(?)।

bhāgena rājasenaṃ syād dvibhāgo vedikocchṛtā(?)।
bhavedāsanapaṭuśa bhāgārdhena samunnataḥ॥158॥

By a part may be less Rājasena having elevation of two parts and may be āsanapaṭṭa elevated by a part half.

---

### Verse 57.159

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चन्द्रावलोकनं कुर्याद् भागिकं त्र्यंशलम्बितम्।
आसनस्योर्ध्वत: कुर्यात् स्तम्भं पञ्चपदं शुभम्॥१५९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

candrāvalokanaṃ kuryād bhāgikaṃ tryaṃśalambbitam।
āsanasyordhvataḥ kuryāt stambhaṃ pañcapadaṃ śubham॥159॥

**English Translation:**

One may create the Candrāvalokana one parted and having the equipment of digit triad. Above the āsana one may create, a column five stepped and auspicious.

---

### Verse 57.160

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हीरग्रहणकं शीर्षं द्वयं सार्धेकभागत:।
गुला(गल)पटुश भागेन मळ्काद्यं द्विभागिकम्॥१६०॥

hīragrahaṇakaṃ śīrṣaṃ dvayaṃ sārdhekabhāgataḥ।

**English Translation:**

Hiragrahaṇaka and Śīrṣa the twain by one and half part and Galapatta (tulāpaṭṭa) by a part and Mallachādya by part twain.

> **Footnote 1:** तुलापटुश भागेन मलछाद्यं द्विभागिकम्। (fn.6,7 p.364, verse 160 pādas 1-2)

### Verse 57.161

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेन लम्बितं तत् स्यात् सुसृष्टं सुमनोरमम्।

etasyoparibhāgena kartavyā cheda(chādya)paṭikā॥161॥

**English Translation:**

That may be pendulous by a part well knit and nicely fascinating and above the upper part of this one, deserves to be created the Chādyapaṭṭikā.

---

### Verse 57.162

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदेनैकेन चास्योर्ध्वं कपोताल्यन्तरच्छदे।

padenaiekena cāsyordhvaṃ kapotālyantaracchade।
bhāgaṣaṭkena vistīrṇe pañcabhāgasamunnataṃ(te)॥162॥

**English Translation:**

By a single part its upper part, with the roof of the space of the eaves on broad by six parts and elevated by five parts.

---

### Verse 57.163

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शूरसेनं प्रकुर्वीत मध्यवर्तालितोरणम्।
वराहमासमकरैर्वराहगजसुण्डकै:(?)॥१६३॥

śūrasenaṃ prakurvīta madhyavartālitoraṇam।
varāhamāsamakarairvarāhagajasūṇḍakai(?)॥163॥

One may create Śūrasena having an arched portal existing within the central rows having Rhinoceroses, elephants, boars, crocodiles and alligators and swans (painted) as such.

### Verse 57.164

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एवमादिभराकीर्णमतिदस्योपरिस्थितम्।
कोणं कुर्यात् पुष्पकूटं पुष्पकर्मनिरन्तरम्।।१६४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

evamādibharākīrṇamatidasyopariśthitam।
koṇaṃ kuryāt puṣpakūṭaṃ puṣpakarmanirantaram।।164।।

**English Translation:**

Promiscuous by such and that, stationed above the balcony, the Koṇa, one may create as Puṣpakūṭa jagged by floral designing.

---

### Verse 57.165

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतस्रो भूमयोऽस्य स्ताछ न्यूनाः पुरः पुरः।
प्रथमा भूमिका +++++ स्यात् परा परा।।१६५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

catasro bhūmayo'sya stācha nyūnāḥ puraḥ puraḥ।
prathamā bhūmikā +++++ syāt parā parā।।165।।

**English Translation:**

Four storeys of this one may be there and those may be reduced as they go above. The first¹ storey may be lesser in extent by a part reduced i.e. 3/4th having step pentad. Reduced by a half part the storey one after the other.

> **Footnote 1:** प्रथमा भूमिकोच्छ्रायातपादोन पद पञ्चकम्। भागार्द्धेन विहीना स्यात् भूमिका स्यात् परापरा:।

---

### Verse 57.166

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

आद्यस्य कोणकूटस्य विस्तारस्त्रिपदः स्मृतः।
परेषां पुनरेषां स्यात् क्रमादूनो ऽङ्घ्रिणाङ्घ्रिणा।।१६६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ādyasya koṇakūṭasya vistārastripadaḥ smṛtaḥ।
pareṣāṃ punareṣāṃ syāt kramādūno 'ṅghriṇāṅghriṇā।।166।।

**English Translation:**

Of the preliminary Koṇakūṭa the breadth is estimated as of three padas. Of those other the breadth may be reduced by square by square.

---

### Verse 57.167

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

बाह्यात् परस्परं क्षेपमंशेनांशेन योजयेत्।
मध्ये लतास्य कर्तव्या भागषट्केन विस्तृता।।१६७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bāhyāt parasparaṃ kṣepamaṃśenāṃśena yojayet।
madhye latāsya kartavyā bhāgaṣaṭkena vistṛtā।।167।।

**English Translation:**

From the exterior mutually the Kṣepa² one may combine by a single digit. In the centre of this Latā deserves to be created, broad by a hexagonal digit.

> **Footnote 2:** Kṣepa = Kṣepaṇa- A projection : a moulding above the plinth in pedestal generally placed between a dado and acyma or fillet or petal and fillet (EOHA p.146).

---

### Verse 57.168

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्कन्धे द्विपदविस्तारां विदध्यान्मध्यमञ्जरीम्।
षड्गुणं सूत्रमादाय लतारेखां समालिखेत्।।१६८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

skandhe dvipadavistārāṃ vidadhyānmadhyamañjarīm।
ṣaḍguṇaṃ sūtramādāya latārekhāṃ samālikhet।।168।।

**English Translation:**

The Madhyamañjarī one may provide, on the Skandha as having breadth of two padas. Having got hold of the Sūtra (plumb line) six-fold, one may carve out the lining of the latā.

---

### Verse 57.169

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

आलेखं च ततः कुर्यात् सुशुद्धं भागसुन्दरम्।
भागेन वेदिकोत्सेधः षड्भागः स्कन्धविस्तरै(र):।।१६९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ālekhyaṃ ca tataḥ kuryāt suśuddhaṃ bhāgasundaram।
bhāgena vedikotsedhāḥ ṣaḍbhāgaḥ skandhavistarai(r)aḥ।।169।।

**English Translation:**

Painting or plastering (ālekhyaṃ) then one may perform, absolutely pure and good looking in parts. By a part the elevation of the Vedikā hexagonal and broad at the Skandha.

---

### Verse 57.170

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेनैकेन च ग्रीवा द्वाभ्यां सामलसारकम्।
विशालपद्मसदृशं विधेयं पद्मशीर्षकम्।।१७०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgenaikena ca grīvā dvābhyāṃ sāmalasārakam।
viśālapadmasadṛśaṃ vidheyaṃ padmaśīrṣakam।।170।।

**English Translation:**

By a part the grīvā and by two Amalasāraka and equal in norm to the Viśālapadma deserves to be created the Padmaśīrṣaaka.

---

### Verse 57.171

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the Devanagari for verse 171 appears to start here but may have begun on the previous page. Only the last two lines of Devanagari are visible.]

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चन्द्रिका पद्यपत्राभा द्वयमेतत् पदोच्छ्रितम्।
त्र्यंशः स्यात् (कलशोमूर्त्या जनकैरवकुड्मल:?)॥१७१॥

tryaṃśaḥ syāt (kalaśomūrtyā janakairavakuḍmalaḥ?)॥171॥

Candrikā having gleam of the lotus petal, this twain elevated by a pada, three parted one may be the Kalaśa by framework by the creators of sprouted norms.

### Verse 57.172

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एवंविधं विधत्ते यः पुष्पकं सुमनोरमम्।
तुष्येत् तस्य धनाधीशः शुभैर्याति व्रजेच्च स:(?)॥१७२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

evaṃvidhaṃ vidhatte yaḥ puṣpakaṃ sumanoramam।
tuṣyet tasya dhanādhīśaḥ śubhairyāti vrajecca saḥ(?)॥172॥

**English Translation:**

Of such a normed Puṣpaka whosoever bears, highly gorgeous, unto him may get gratified the lord of riches i.e. Kubera and by auspicious norms may he not go ahead.

---

### Verse 57.173

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पुष्पक:॥
बूमो विजयभद्रस्य सुभद्रस्य च लक्षणम्।
वल्लभः षण्मुखस्यायं बहुपुण्यविधायकः॥१७३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

puṣpakaḥ॥
brūmo vijayabhadrasya subhadrasya ca lakṣaṇam।
vallabhaḥ ṣaṇmukhasya āyaṃ bahupuṇyavidhāyakaḥ॥173॥

**English Translation:**

We now talk of the definition of Vijayabhadra and Subhadra. The devotee of the six faced one i.e. Gaṇeśa is highly merit inducing.

---

### Verse 57.174

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुःश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे साष्टविंशतिभाजिते।
कुर्यादष्टपदं कर्ण(ण्)भद्रं वास्य चतुष्पदम्॥१७४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

catuḥśrīkṛte kṣetre sāṣṭaviṃśatibhājite।
kuryādaṣṭapadaṃ karṇa(ṇ)bhadraṃ vāsya catuṣpadam॥174॥

**English Translation:**

In the four squared field, divided into twenty eight one may create the octagonal Karṇa and Bhadra of this one as four parted one.

---

### Verse 57.175

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदेनैकेन निर्यातं सर्वकोणेष्वयं विधिः।
उदकान्तरकं कार्यं पदवलृपतं पदायतम्॥१७५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padenaekena niryātaṃ sarvakoṇeṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ।
udakāntarakaṃ kāryaṃ padavalṛptaṃ padāyatam॥175॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: पदवलृपतं — reading uncertain, possibly पदवलृप्तं padavalṛptaṃ]

**English Translation:**

Issued out by a single part in all the koṇa as such is the process Udakāntaraka deserves to be created, accomplished by a pada or square and oblong by a part.

---

### Verse 57.176

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दशभागायतं भद्रं कार्यं त्रिपदनिर्गतम्।
दिक्षु(येव?) विधेयः˒ स्यान्मुखतो मुखमण्डप:॥१७६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

daśabhāgāyataṃ bhadraṃ kāryaṃ tripadanirgataṃ।
dikṣu(yev?) vidheyaḥ syānmukhato mukhamaṇḍapaḥ॥176॥

[FLAG: दिक्षु(येव?) — transcribed with (?) as printed in the text]

**English Translation:**

The Bhadra oblong by ten parts, deserves to be created as projected by a pada-triad. The opening pavilion deserves to be created at the opening in quarters.

> **Footnote 1:** विधिरेषः स्यात्

---

### Verse 57.177

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

त्रिपदा बाह्यतो भित्तिस्त्रिपदा चाध्यकारिका।
मध्ये प्रासादमानं तु कर्तव्यं षोडशांशकम्॥१७७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tripadā bāhyato bhittistripádā cāndhakārikā।
madhye prāsādamānaṃ tu kartavyaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśakam॥177॥

**English Translation:**

Three pada normed the outer wall and three pada normed Andhakārikā (blind alley). In the centre the measurement of the Prāsāda deserves to be created of sixteen digits.

---

### Verse 57.178

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णा चतुष्पदा(?)कन्दे भद्राण्येषां पदद्वयम्।
निःक्रान्तानि पदेन स्युः कन्दकर्णाश्रितानि हि॥१७८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇā catuṣpadā(?)kande bhadrāṇyeṣāṃ padadhvayam।
niḥkrāntāni padena syuḥ kandakarṇāśritāni hi॥178॥

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

In the Kanda Karnas are to be four squared ones, and bhadras of these of pada-twain, deserve to be projected by a pada and recumbent on the Kanda and Karnas Kandakarṇa.

---

### Verse 57.179

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षट्पदं मध्यमङ्गं स्याद् द्विपदश्चास्य निर्गम:।
कर्णशालान्तरं यत् स्यात् (सा गन्धमार्गें?) विधीयते॥१७९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaṭpadaṃ madhyamaṅgaṃ syād dvipadaścāsya nirgamaḥ ।
karṇaśālāntaraṃ yat syāt (sā gandhamārge?) vidhīyate ॥179॥

**English Translation:**

Hexagonal squared may be the middle part and two squared outlets or exit doors and the space between the Karṇa and śālā that verily becomes an outlet for water.¹

> **Footnote 1:** कार्यस्तुलोदयस्यति वेदिबन्धोशससकम्। fn.5, p.366, verse 182, pādas 1-2 (GOSE).

---

### Verse 57.180

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विपदा कन्दभित्ति: स्याद् गर्भो द्वादश भागिक:।
ऊर्ध्वमानमिह प्रोक्तं द्विगुणं द्विकलाधिकम्॥१८०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvipadā kandabhittiḥ syād garbho dvādaśa bhāgikaḥ ।
ūrdhvamānamiha proktaṃ dviguṇaṃ dvikalādhikam ॥180॥

**English Translation:**

Two squared may be the wall of the Kanda and the interior as twelve parted one. The measurement of the upper part or roof is spoken of redoubled as exceeding by two Kalās.

---

### Verse 57.181

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्विंशतिभागान्ते तुलोच्छ्रायस्य मध्यत:।
ऊर्ध्वमानं तु यत् प्रोक्तं ग्रीवाण्डाद्यं ततो बहि:॥१८१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturviṃśatibhāgānte tulocchāyasya madhyataḥ ।
ūrdhvamānaṃ tu yat proktaṃ grīvāṇḍādyaṃ tato bahiḥ ॥181॥

**English Translation:**

At the end of the twenty four parts in the centre of the elevation or height of the architrave or beam, the measurement of the roof that stands spoken headed by Grīvāṇḍa and the like on the outskirts of that.

---

### Verse 57.182

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कार्यं(तुलोदयं स्यांच?) वेदीबध्योंऽशसप्तकम्।
त्रिभागं कुम्भक: सूत्रं(?)सार्धभागो मसूरक:॥१८२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kāryaṃ (tulodayaṃ syāṃc?) vedībaddhyo'ṃśasaptakam ।
tribhāgaṃ kumbhakaḥ sūtraṃ (?) sārdhabāgo masūrakaḥ ॥182॥

**English Translation:**

At the end of this height of the architrave deserves to be created the height of the architrave of septagonal digits. Three parted therein the Kumbhaka and Masūraka of a part and a half.

---

### Verse 57.183

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेनान्तरपत्रं स्यात् सार्धभागेन मेखला।
जङ्घा द्वादशिभिर्भागैर्द्व्यंशा वा गलपट्टिका(:?)॥१८३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgenāntarapatraṃ syāt sārdabhāgena mekhalā ।
jaṅghā dvādaśibhirbhāgairdvyaṃśā vā galapaṭṭikā (ḥ?) ॥183॥

**English Translation:**

By a part the Antarapattra, and by a part and a half Mekhalā Jaṅghā may be by twelve parts and two parted may be galapaṭṭikā.

---

### Verse 57.184

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अन्धारिका च भागार्धं सार्धभागा वरिण्डका।
भागेनान्तरपत्रं स्याद् रूपकर्मसमाकुलम्॥१८४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

andhārikā ca bhāgārdhaṃ sārdabhāgā variṇḍakā ।
bhāgenāntarapatraṃ syād rūpakarmasamākulam ॥184॥

**English Translation:**

Andhakārikāmay be of a half part and Variṇḍakā of apart and a half. By a part may be Antarapattra, and the rūpaka of unequal norm.

---

### Verse 57.185

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊध्वाध:पट्टयोर्मध्ये(ध्य)भागो भागैकविंशति:।
अतो मध्याद् विधातव्यं द्विपदं राजसेनकम्॥१८५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūdhvādhaḥpaṭṭayormadhye(dhya)bhāgo bhāgaikāviṃśatiḥ ।
ato madhyād vidhātavyaṃ dvipadaṃ rājasenakam ॥185॥

**English Translation:**

In the centre of the upper and lower paṭṭa, the part of twenty one parts may there be. From the centre of this deserves to be created two parted Rājasenaka.

---

### Verse 57.186

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वेदी चतुष्पदा प्रोक्ता भागेनासनपट्टक:।
पद्द्वयेन सार्धेन कार्यं चन्द्रावलोकनम्॥१८६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vedī catuṣpadā proktā bhāgenāsanapaṭṭakaḥ ।
padvayena sārdhena kāryaṃ candrāvalokanam ॥186॥

**English Translation:**

The VedI is spoken of as of four squares, and the āsanapaṭṭaka of one square and by pada twain having one and a half deserves to be created Candrāvalakana.

---

### Verse 57.187

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नवभागोच्छितः स्तम्भः पत्रकर्मसमाकुलः।
भागेनैकेन भरणं शीर्षकं च द्विभागिकम्॥१८७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

navabhāgocchitaḥ stambhaḥ patrakarmsamākulaḥ ।
bhāgenaikena bharaṇaṃ śīrṣakaṃ ca dvibhāgikam ॥187॥

**English Translation:**

The column having height of nine parts, having carving of floral patterns and by a part the Bharaṇa and Śīrṣaka as of part-twain.

---

### Verse 57.188

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उच्छालकमुभौ भागौ हारग्रहणमासिकम् (?)।
द्वंशा पट्टोच्छितिर्भागचतुष्का बाह्यविस्तृतिः॥१८८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

uccālakamubhau bhāgau hāragrahaṇamāsikam (?) ।
dvaṃśā paṭṭocchitibhāgacatuṣkā bāhyavistṛtiḥ ॥188॥

**English Translation:**

Ucchalaka or Utkālaka on both the sides hiragrahna¹ by a part. The height of paṭṭaka of two digits and the outer breadth as of part square.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 366, fn. 12 has हीरग्रहणमाशिकं for हारग्रहणमासिकम्।

---

### Verse 57.189

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(द्वंशास्यालम्बनोर्ध्वे² तु रूपकं चक पटिका: ?)।
सा च भागत्रयेण स्यात् सुश्लिष्टा साधुचित्रिता॥१८९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(dvaṃśāsyālambanodhve² tu rūpakaṃ caka paṭikā ?) ।
sā ca bhāgatrayeṇa syāt suśliṣṭā sādhucitritā ॥189॥

**English Translation:**

The Rūpaka and Balapaṭṭikā have roof of a height of two digits. And that may be nicely chequered or painted well knit as such by a part-triad.

> **Footnote 2:** द्वयंशांशलम्बनोधर्वे तु रूपकैचैकबल पटिका। (fn.9, p.367, GOSE).

---

### Verse 57.190

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णकर्णेषु शृङ्गाणां विस्तृतिर्द्विपदा भवेत्।
ऊर्ध्वमानं त्रिभागं स्याद् ग्रीवाण्डकलशैः सह॥१९०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇakarṇeṣu śṛṅgāṇāṃ vistṛtirdvipadā bhavet ।
ūrdhvamānaṃ tribhāgaṃ syād grīvāṇḍakalaśaiḥ saha ॥190॥

**English Translation:**

On the Karṇa Karṇas of the Śṛṅgas the breadth may be of two squares. The upper measurement may be of three parts along with grīvāṇḍaka Kalaśas.

---

### Verse 57.191

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मध्ये चतुष्पदा कर्मा(णां)दुरोमञ्जरिका भवेत्।
उच्छ्राय: षट्पदस्तस्या ग्रीवाण्डं द्विपदोच्छितम्॥१९१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

madhye catuṣpadā karmā(ṇāṃ)duromañjarikā bhavet ।
ucchrāyaḥ ṣaṭpadastasyā grīvāṇḍaṃ dvipadocchitam ॥191॥

**English Translation:**

In the centre from the Karṇas of four squares, there may be Uramañjarikā. The height of that may be hexagonal and grīvāṇḍa elevated by two parts.

---

### Verse 57.192

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागेन कलशोत्सेधः स्यादेवं कर्णनिर्मित(ति):।
कर्णाश्चि पिण्डिका कार्या भद्रदेशे तथोच्छितः॥१९२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgena kalaśotsedhaḥ syādevaṃ karṇanirmita(ti)ḥ ।
karṇāści piṇḍikā kāryā bhadrade śe tathocchitaḥ ॥192॥

**English Translation:**

By a part the elevation of the Kalaśa and this very way may be the construction of the Karṇa. The Karṇas and piṇḍikā deserves to be created in the Bhadradéśa elevated likewise.

---

### Verse 57.193

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्तव्यः सप्तिभिर्भागैः सिंहकर्णः सुचिर्चितः।
कर्णद्वये तथा शृङ्गं तयोरूर्ध्वं चतुर्दिशम्॥१९३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kartavyaḥ saptibhirbhāgaiḥ siṃhakarṇaḥ sucircitaḥ ।
karṇadvaye tathā śṛṅgaṃ tayorūrdhvaṃ caturdiśam ॥193॥

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the first paragraph "Deserves to be created by seven parts Simhakarṇa well plastered. The Karṇa twain and likewise Śṛṅga twain over them on four direction." belongs to a verse begun on the previous page.]


### Verse 57.194

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उरोमञ्जरिकायामादुदया(?)दश पञ्च च।
तस्याश्चाष्टपद: कन्दो ग्रीवा भागसमुन्नता॥१९४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

uromañjarikāyāmādudayā(?)daśa pañca ca।
tasyāścāṣṭapadaḥ kando grīvā bhāgasamunnatā॥194॥

**English Translation:**

From the length of the Uramañjarikā the Udayas may be ten and five and of that Kanda is of octagonal norm, and grīvā as elevated by a part.

---

### Verse 57.195

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्विभागमण्डकं कार्यं चन्द्रिका पदमुच्छ्रृता।
त्रिपदा: कलशास्तेषां मध्यगान्तरमञ्जरी॥१९५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvibhāgamaṇḍakaṃ kāryaṃ candrikā padamucchṛtā।
tripadāḥ kalaśāsteṣāṃ madhyagāntaramañjarī॥195॥

**English Translation:**

Part twained is to be created the Aṇḍaka and Candrikā as elevated by a square. The Kalaśas of those are triple squared and Mañjarī within the central part.

---

### Verse 57.196

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तला(लता)पञ्चकसंयुक्ता चरटक्रिययान्विता।
भागविंशतिविस्तीर्णा कर्तव्या मूलमञ्जरी॥१९६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

talā(latā)pañcakasaṃyuktā caraṭakriyayānvitā।
bhāgaviṃśativistīrṇā kartavyā mūlamañjarī॥196॥

**English Translation:**

Latā equipped with a pentad equipped with Caratakriyā and Mūlamañjarī is to be created as broad by twenty parts.

---

### Verse 57.197

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वाविंशतिसमुत्सेधा सकण्ठो द्वादशभागिक:।
मध्या लता सु(शू)रसेनकर्मरूपसमाकुला॥१९७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvāviṃśatisamutsedhā sakaṇṭho dvādaśabhāgikaḥ।
madhyā latā su(śū)rasenakarmārūpasamākulā॥197॥

**English Translation:**

The skandha may have elevation of twenty two (parts) having twelve segments. The central latā is to be created as equipped with the framework of Śūrasena.

---

### Verse 57.198

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवा सार्धपदोत्सेधा कार्या द्विपदमण्डकम्।
भागेन च(म)णिडकां कुर्यात् कलशं तु चतुष्पदम्॥१९८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvā sārdhpadotsedhā kāryā dvipadamaṇḍakam।
bhāgena ca(ma)ṇiḍakāṃ kuryāt kalaśaṃ tu catuṣpadam॥198॥

**English Translation:**

Grīvā may be having an elevation of one and half padas required to be created as such and Aṇḍaka of pada twain. By a single pada one may create Maṇḍikā¹ and Kalaśa of four padas.

> **Footnote 1:** Maṇḍi = Maṇḍikā- the crowning part of the capital of a column, a market place, a market (EOHA, p.447).

---

### Verse 57.199

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एकोनत्रिंशदण्डोऽयं प्रासाद: शुभलक्षण:।
षट्पदं पीठमाख्यातं चरितं पूर्वकर्मवत्॥१९९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ekonatriṃśadaṇḍo'yaṃ prāsādaḥ śubhalaṣaṇaḥ।
ṣaṭpadaṃ pīṭhamākhyātaṃ caritaṃ pūrvakarmavat॥199॥

**English Translation:**

Having nineteen normed Aṇḍaka the Prāsāda is of auspicious marks. The Pīṭha is declared as of six padas plastered² as per earlier norm.

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE, p. 367, verse 99, pādas 4 has चरितं corrected by चर्चितम् in fn. 18.

---

### Verse 57.200

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

आरोग्यं पुत्रलाभश्च भवेद् विजयकारिणाम्।
तेषां तुष्यति षड्वक्त्रो भक्त्या ये विदधत्यमुम्॥२००॥
विजयभद्र:॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ārogyaṃ putralābhaśca bhaved vijayakāriṇām।
teṣāṃ tuṣyati ṣaḍvaktro bhaktyā ye vidadhaty amum॥200॥
vijayabhadraḥ॥

**English Translation:**

---

### Verse 57.201

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अधुना श्रीनिवासाख्यः प्रासादः सम्यगुच्यते।
तृप्त्यर्थमेव क्रियते जयश्रीस्तत्र तिष्ठति॥२०१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

adhunā śrīnivāsākhyaḥ prāsādaḥ samyagucyate ।
tṛptyarthamevakriyate jayaśrīstatra tiṣṭhati ॥201॥

**English Translation:**

Now is being described in details the Prāsada named Śrīnivāsa. It is created only for the sake of satisfaction and victory and glory abide therein.

---

### Verse 57.202

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुरश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे भक्तेऽष्टादशभिः पदैः।
अन्धारिका च भित्ते(ति)श्च पदे द्वे द्वे यथाक्रमम्॥२०२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturaśrīkṛte kṣetre bhakte'ṣṭādaśabhiḥ padaiḥ ।
andhārikā ca bhitte(ti)śca pade dve dve yathākramam ॥202॥

**English Translation:**

In a field four square shaped divided by eighteen segments, Andhakarikā and bhitti (wall) are of pada twain each.

---

### Verse 57.203

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गर्भश्च षट्पदः कार्यः श्रीनिवासस्य सुन्दरः।
कन्दे स्युद्विपदाः कर्णा भागेन सलिलान्तरम्॥२०३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

garbhaśca ṣaṭpadaḥ kāryaḥ śrīnivāsasya sundaraḥ ।
kande syudvipadāḥ karṇā bhāgena salilāntaram ॥203॥

**English Translation:**

The garbha is to be created as hexagonal normed of Śrīnivāsa the beautiful one and on Kunda may be pada twained Karṇas and by apart the water channel.

---

### Verse 57.204

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रं चतुष्पदं कार्यं पदेनैकेन निर्गतम्।
बाह्याः स्युस्त्रिपदाः कर्णा भद्रैश्च द्विपदैर्युताः॥२०४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadraṃ catuṣpadaṃ kāryaṃ padenaikena nirgatam ।
bāhyāḥ syustripadāḥ karṇā bhadhraiśca dvipadairyutāḥ ॥204॥

**English Translation:**

Bhadra should be created as four squared projected by a single part. The outer Karṇa may be of part-triad equipped with Bhadras of pada-twain.

---

### Verse 57.205

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रं चतुर्थकर्णेषु(तु) पदेनैकेन निर्गतम्।
कोणकोणाश्च कर्तव्याः पदस्यार्धं समायताः॥२०५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadraṃ caturthakārṇeṣu(tu) padenaikena nirgatam ।
koṇakoṇāśca kartavyāḥ padasyārdhaṃ samāyatāḥ ॥205॥

**English Translation:**

The bhadra in the fourth Karṇa is to be projected by one part and Koṇa Koṇas deserve to be created as equally oblong by a half part.

---

### Verse 57.206

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदेनाम्बुधरः कार्यः प्रवेशाद् विस्तृतेरपि।
विस्तारात् षट्पदं भद्रं पद्द्वितयनिर्गमम्॥२०६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padenāmbudharaḥ kāryaḥ praveśād vistṛterapi ।
vistārāt ṣaṭpadaṃ bhadraṃ paddvitayanir gamam ॥206॥

**English Translation:**

By a pada Ambudhara decease to be created from the entrance of the breadth and Bhadra is to be of hexagonal norm from the breadth having an exit door of pada twain.

---

### Verse 57.207

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

नीरस्यालान्तरे(?) कार्या(:) तिलका द्व्यंशविस्तृताः।
पदेनैकेन निष्काताः शोभिताश्चारुकर्मणा॥२०७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

nīrasyālāntare(?) kāryā(ḥ) tilakā dvyaṃśavistṛtāḥ ।
padenaikena niṣkātāḥ śobhitāścārukārmaṇā ॥207॥

**English Translation:**

Within the Nirasyāla deserves to be created Tilakas two digited normed broad projected by a single part, and beautified by good workmanship.

> **Footnote 1:** षड्वक्त्रः। षडानन:।

---

### Verse 57.208

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ऊर्ध्वमानमथ बूमः श्रीनिवासे यथाक्रमम्।
खुरकस्योच्छ्रितिः पीठे पादोनं पदमिष्यते।।२०८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ūrdhvamānamatha brūmaḥ śrīnivāse yathākramam |
khurakasyocchritiḥ pīṭhe pādonaṃ padamiṣyate ||208||

**English Translation:**

Now we talk of the upper measurement in Śrīnivāsa as per serial order on the Pīṭha and the height of Khuraka is desired as of digits of 3/4ᵗʰ norm.

---

### Verse 57.209

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्यात् सपादो(दे)नांशेन जाङ्गकुम्भ(:?)समुच्छ्रितम्।
भागेनान्तरपत्रे तु भागेनैकेन मेखलाम्।।२०९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kuryāt sapādo(de)nāṃśena jāṅgakumbha(ḥ?)samucchhritam |
bhāgenāntarapatre tu bhāgenaikena mekhalām ||209||

**English Translation:**

The Jādya Kumbha elevated as such deserves to be created by a quarter normed digit. By apart the Antarapattra and by a part the Mekhalā.

---

### Verse 57.210

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पीठोत्सेधश्चतुर्भागः श्रीनिवासे भवेदिति।
वेदीबन्धस्य खुरको भागार्धेन समुच्चतः।।२१०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pīṭhotsedhaścaturbhāgaḥ śrīnivāse bhavediti |
vedībandhasya khurako bhāgārdhena samuccataḥ ||210||

**English Translation:**

The Pīṭhotsedha of four parts may there be in Śrīnivāsa and the Khuraka of Vedībandha may be elevated by a half part Kumbhaka by a half part and by a half of that Masūraka.

> **Footnote 1:** Text with English Translation brought out as Appendix at the end of this volume.

# Chapter 58 — The panegyric of palaces

### Verse 58.1

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादानां चतुःषष्टिरिदानीमभिधीयते।
या पूर्वं ब्रह्मणा दत्ता प्रा(प्र)सादा विश्वकर्मणे।।१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādānāṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiridānīmabhidhīyate |
yā pūrvaṃ brahmaṇā dattā prā(pra)sādā viśvakarmaṇe ||1||

**English Translation:**

Now of palaces or mansions (Prāsādas) the sixty four (calculation) is being talked of. The Prāsādas that were earlier given by Brahmā to Viśvakarmā.

---

### Verse 58.2

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मर्मवेधस्थिता वास्तुदेवाः पूज्या यथोचितम्।
पूज्यता च स्मृता तेषां प्रासादे मण्डपे ध्वजे।।२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

marmavedhasthitā vāstudevāḥ pūjyā yathocitam |
pūjyatā ca smṛtā teṣāṃ prāsāde maṇḍape dhvaje ||2||

**English Translation:**

Stationed on the obstruction of the vulnerable points the Vāstu devatas or Architectural gods deserve to be adored as per suitability. And adorability of those is well known in light of Prāsāda, Maṇḍapa and Dhvaja.

---

### Verse 58.3

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

आसने वाहने तद्वत् सर्वोपकरणेष्वपि।
प्रासादे यादृश(ऋछून्दस्तादृङ्मन्दपीठयोः:?)।।३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

āsane vāhane tadvat sarvopakraṇeṣvapi |
prāsāde yādṛśa(ṛchūndastādṛṅmandapīṭhayoḥ ?) ||3||

**English Translation:**

On their seat, vehicle that very way and on all the allied equipments for a Prāsāda of the

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the translation at the top of the page ("norm that is the Chandaḥ of that very hue as of the manda and pīṭha.") belongs to a verse beginning on the previous page.]

---

### Verse 58.4

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तथा वास्तुविरुद्धं स्यात् प्रासादाङ्गे हिते(?)विदु:।
अश्वष्टौ स्मृतास्तेषु त्रिदशानां पृथक् पृथक्।।४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tathā vāstuviruddhaṃ syāt prāsādāṅge hite(?)vidu ḥ |
aśvaṣṭau smṛtāsteṣu tridaśānāṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||4||

**English Translation:**

And likewise the framework of the Prāsāda having been planned, that may prove contrary to the architectural norm. Eight each are allocated then the deities quite distinctly.

---

### Verse 58.5

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शाम्भो(हेरे)विरिञ्जस्य ग्रहाणाम(घिपस्य च)।
चण्डिकाया गणेशस्य श्रियाः सर्वेदिवौकसाम्।।५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śāmbho(here)viriñjasya grahāṇāma(ghipasya ca) |
caṇḍikāyā gaṇeśasya śriyāḥ sarvedivaukasām ||5||

**English Translation:**

Of Śambhu, Hari (Śiva and Viṣṇu) of Indra (Viriñci) as also of the Lord of planets (moon), of Caṇḍikā and Gaṇeśa of Śrī (goddess of fortune) and all the gods.

---

### Verse 58.6

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमानः सर्वतोभद्रो गजपृष्ठोऽथ पद्मकः।
वृषभो मुक्तकोणश्च नलिनो द्राविडस्तथा।।६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vimānaḥ sarvatobhadro gajapṛṣṭho'tha padmakaḥ |
vṛṣabho muktakoṇaśca nalino drāviḍastathā ||6||

**English Translation:**

Vimāna Sarvatobhadra, Gajapṛṣṭha and Padmaka-vṛṣabha and Muktakoṇa, Nalina and Drāviḍa.

---

### Verse 58.7

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इत्येतेऽष्टौ समुद्दिष्टाः प्रासादास्त्रिपुरद्रुहः।
गरुडो वर्धमानश्च शङ्खावर्तोऽथ पुष्पकः।।७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ityete'ṣṭau samuddiṣṭāḥ prāsādāstripurudruhaḥ |
garuḍo vardhamānaśca śaṅkhāvarto'tha puṣpakaḥ ||7||

**English Translation:**

This eight have been allocated this way the palaces of Śiva (Tripura druha) [the enemy of Tripura] Garuḍa and Vardhamāna, Śankhāvartaka and Puṣpaka.

---

### Verse 58.8

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गृहर(राट्) स्वस्तिकश्चैव रुचकः पुण्डूवर्धनः।
कार्या जनार्दनस्याष्टौ प्रासादाः पुरभूषणाः।।८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

gṛhara(rāṭ) svastikascaiva rucakaḥ puṇḍūvardhanaḥ |
kāryā janārdanasyāṣṭau prāsādāḥ purabhūṣaṇāḥ ||8||

**English Translation:**

The palace king Svastika, Rucaka and Puṇḍra Vardhana deserve to be created of Janārdana (Viṣṇu) the eight palaces, the ornaments of the towns.

---

### Verse 58.9

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मेरुमन्दरकैलासा हंसाख्यो भद्र एव च।
उदुञ्जो मिश्रकश्चैव तथा मालाधरोऽष्टमः।।९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

merumandara kailāsā haṃsākhyo bhadra eva ca |
uduñjo miśrakaścaiva tathā mālādharo'ṣṭamaḥ ||9||

**English Translation:**

Meru, Mandara and Kailāsa (śa) and one named Haṃsakha and Miśraka and likewise, Māladhara, the eighth.

---

### Verse 58.10

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

इत्यष्टौ ब्रह्मणः प्रोक्ताः प्रासादाः पुरमध्यगाः।
गवयश्चित्रकूटश्च किरणः सर्वसुन्दरः।।१०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ityaṣṭau brahmaṇaḥ proktāḥ prāsādāḥ puramadhyagāḥ |
gavayaścitrakūṭaśca kiraṇaḥ sarvasundaraḥ ||10||

**English Translation:**

This way the eight illustrated by Brahmā are the Prāsādas as created in the centre of the town or capital. Gavaya and Citrakūṭa, and Karṇa gorgeous of all or Sarvasundara.

---

### Verse 58.11

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

श्रीवत्सः पद्मनाभश्च वैराजो वृत्त एव च।
एते कार्या रवेरष्टौ प्रासादाः शुभलक्षणाः।।११।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śrīvatsaḥ padmanābhaśca vairājo vṛtta eva ca |
ete kāryā ravereṣṭau prāsādāḥ śubhalakṣaṇāḥ ||11||

**English Translation:**

Śrīvatsa and Padmanābha, Vairāja and likewise Vṛtta, these deserve to be created eight Prāsādas of the Sun of auspicious marks.

---

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — Verse 58.12 begins on previous page, only partial Devanagari and translation visible here]

### Verse 58.12

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(नन्द्यावर्तश्चैव चलभश्चर्णदिख्य: ?)¹ सिंह एव च।
विचित्रो योगपीठश्च घण्टानादपताकिनौ॥१२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(nandyāvartaścaiva calabhaścarṇadikhy: ?)¹ siṃha eva ca |
vicitro yogapīṭhaśca ghaṇṭānādapātākinau ॥12॥

**English Translation:**

Nandyavartta and Valabhya, as also Suparṇa (Garuḍa), likewise, and Siṃhavicitra and yogapīṭha Ghaṇṭānāda and Pātākin.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE p.433, fn.2 has नन्द्यावर्तश्चैव सुपर्णरख्य: सिंह एव च।

---

### Verse 58.13

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अष्टावेते विधातव्याश्चण्डिकाया: सुरालया:।
(गुहारसलोकश्च ?)² वेणुभद्रोऽथ कुञ्जर:॥१३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

aṣṭāvete vidhātavyāścaṇḍikāyāḥ surālayāḥ |
(guhārasalokaśca ?)² veṇubhadro'tha kuñjaraḥ ॥13॥

**English Translation:**

Eight these deserve to be created as the temples of Caṇḍikā, Guhādhara, Śālaka Veṇubhadra and Kuñjara.

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE p.433, fn.5 has गुहाधरशालाक

---

### Verse 58.14

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तथा च हर्षविजयावुदकुम्भोऽथ मोदक:।
एतान् विनायकस्याष्टौ प्रासादान् कारयेच्छुभान्॥१४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tathā ca harṣavijayāvudakumbho'tha modakaḥ |
etān vināyakasyāṣṭau prāsādān kārayecchubhān ॥14॥

**English Translation:**

And likewise Hara and Vijaya Udakumbha and Modaka, these eight of Vinayaka (Gaṇeśa) the Prāsādas one may create auspicious ones.

---

### Verse 58.15

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

महापद्म(हर्म्यतल)मुज्जयन्तस्तथा पर:।
गन्धमादनसंज्ञं च(ञ्छ)शतशृङ्गानवद्यकौ(नवद्यकौ)॥१५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

mahāpadma(harmyatala)mujjayantastathā paraḥ |
gandhamādanasaṃjñaṃ ca(ñcha)śataśṛṅgānavadyakau(navadyakau) ॥15॥

**English Translation:**

Mahāpadma and Harmyatala and another one Ujjayanta and one named Gandhamādana, Śataśṛṅga and Anavadyaka.

---

### Verse 58.16

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सु(म)विभ्रान्तो (मनो)हारीत्यष्टौ लक्ष्म्या: प्रकीर्तिता:।
वृत्तो वृत्तायतश्चैत्य: किङ्किणीलयनाभिध:॥१६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

su(ma)vibhrānto (mano)hārītyaṣṭau lakṣmyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
vṛtto vṛttāyataścaitya: kiṅkiṇīlayanābhidhaḥ ॥16॥

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English translation for verse 16 is merged with verse 17 below]

---

### Verse 58.17

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पट्टिशो विभवाख्यश्च तत्था(स्ता)रागणा(णोऽ)ष्टम:।
कुर्वीत सर्वदेवानां प्रासादान् वास्तुशास्त्रवित्॥१७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

paṭṭiśo vibhavākhyaśca tatthā(stā)rāgaṇā(ṇo'ṣṭa)maḥ |
kurvīta sarvadevānāṃ prāsādān vāstuśāstravit ॥17॥

**English Translation:**

Suvibhranta and Manohārī these eight deserve to be created for Lakṣmī well known as such. Vṛttāyata Vṛtta and oblong and circular, one named Kiṅkinī Layana. Paṭṭiśa and one named Vibhava and likewise may be Tārā-gaṇa as the eighth. These Prāsādas the one expert in Science of Architecture may create for all the gods.

# Chapter 59 — The Definition of vimanas amounting sixty four

### Verse 59.1

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमानमथ वक्ष्याम: प्रासादं शम्भुवल्लभम्।
स्वर्गपातालमत्यांनां त्रयाणामपि भूषणम्॥१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vimānamatha vakṣyāmaḥ prāsādaṃ śambhuvallabham |
svargapātālamatyānāṃ trayāṇāmapi bhūṣaṇam || 1 ||

**English Translation:**

The Vimāna now we shall talk of the Prāsāda dear to Śambhu, the very ornament of Heaven Hades and the mortal people.

---

### Verse 59.2

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सर्वेषां गृहवास्तूनां प्रासादानां च सर्वत:।
प्रासादो मूलभूतोऽयं तथा च परिकर्मणाम्॥२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sarveṣāṃ gṛhavāstūnāṃ prāsādānāṃ ca sarvataḥ |
prāsādo mūlabhūto'yaṃ tathā ca parikarmṇām || 2 ||

**English Translation:**

Of all the building Architectures as also of the mansions, in entirety the mansion this is the original structure as also among other structural norms.

---

### Verse 59.3

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एकाशीतिपदे(दं) वास्तु विमाने पञ्चभूमिके।
कर्णान्तयो: शतपदं प्रासादेष्वपरेषु तु॥३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ekāśītipade(daṃ) vāstu vimāne pañcabhūmike |
karṇāntayoḥ śatapadaṃ prāsādeṣvapareṣu tu || 3 ||

**English Translation:**

In the mansion of five storeys the architectural norm is of eighty one squares. The hundred norm is in the two ends of the Karṇas as also in case of other Prāsādas.

---

### Verse 59.4

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ब्रह्मासृजत् पञ्चभौमिवमानानि पुरा रवे:।
मूलकर्णानुगैभद्रैर्द्विगुणोच्छ्रायवन्ति च॥४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

brahmāsṛjat pañcabhaumivamānāni purā raveḥ |
mūlakarṇānugaibhadrairdviguṇocchrāyavanti ca || 4 ||

**English Translation:**

Brahmā earlier created five storeyed buildings of Sun (Ravi) by the Bhadras following the original Karṇas having elevation of redoubled norm.

---

### Verse 59.5

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेषभद्रस्य निष्कासो भद्रदेवचतुष्टयम्(?)।
आकाशदेवताधार (चरकप्राथ?) विदिक्षु च॥५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣabhadrasya niṣkāso bhadradevacatuṣṭayam(?) |
ākāśadevatadhāra (carakaprātha?) vidikshu ca || 5 ||

**English Translation:**

The projection of the Śeṣabhadra, a square of Bhadradeva Ākāśa devatadhāra and Carakaprātha in specific directions.

---

### Verse 59.6

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

दशधा कृतिविस्तारो विमाने सम्प्रकीर्तित:।
पञ्चभाग्य(ग)प्रमाणञ्च गर्भे भित्तिसतदर्धत:॥६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

daśadhā kṛtivistāro vimāne samprakīrtitaḥ |
pañcabhāgya(ga)pramāṇañca garbhe bhittisatadardhatāḥ || 6 ||

**English Translation:**

Having breadth of ten fold norm, in a Vimāna stands stipulated and five parted extent in the centre and the wall by a half of that.

---

### Verse 59.7

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्राग्रीवं भित्तिविस्तारं गर्भायामत्तथाग्रत: (?)।
तत: प्राग्रीविविस्तार: क्षोभणीय: कराङ्गुलै:॥७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāgrīvaṃ bhittivistāraṃ garbhāyāmatathāgrataḥ (?) |
tataḥ prāgrīvivistāraḥ kṣobhaṇīyaḥ karāṅgulaiḥ || 7 ||

**English Translation:**

The entrance hall having the breadth of the wall and the centre and the wall by a half of that. Then the width of the entrance hall is to be measured or carved out by cubits.

---

### Verse 59.8

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागिको रथिविस्तार: कर्णिका चार्धभागिकी।
भागपञ्चकविस्तारं भद्रं यत् (तत्) प्रकीतितम्॥८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgiko rathivistāraḥ karṇikā cārdhabhāgikī |
bhāgapañcakavistāraṃ bhadraṃ yat (tat) prakīrtitam || 8 ||

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the translation at the top of the page ("Single parted one as the breadth of the Ratha...") belongs to a verse begun on the previous page.]


### Verse 59.9

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(भूणस्तोवहेस्तस्य?)¹ निर्गमो भागिक: स्मृत:।
भागार्धेन विधातव्यः क्षोभणो जलवर्त्मन:।।९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

(bhūṇastovahestasya?) [IAST UNCERTAIN: first word unclear] nirgamo bhāgikaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
bhāgārdhena vidhātavyaḥ kṣobhaṇo jalavatmanaḥ ।।9।।

> **Footnote 1:** तूर्णसीग्रो बहिस्तस्य निर्गमोभागिक: स्मृत:।

**English Translation:**

On the exterior of the border of the Tūrṇa, the exit door is deemed as of one part. And by a half part deserves to be created the Kṣobhaṇa of the water course.

---

### Verse 59.10

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णिकां जलमार्गं (च) समसूत्रेण मापयेत्।
अथोच्यते भूमिकानां स्तम्भानां चेह लक्षणम्।।१०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

karṇikāṃ jalamārgaṃ (ca) samasūtreṇa māpayet ।
athocyate bhūmikānāṃ stambhānāṃ ceha lakṣaṇam ।।10।।

**English Translation:**

One may measure the Karṇikā and water course by a similar plumb line. Now is being defined the mark of the poles or pillar of storeys.

---

### Verse 59.11

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्ताराद् द्विगुण: स्कन्ध: सर्वस्मिन् बुद्धनागरे।
++++पञ्चभागा स्याज्जङ्घास्वसमुच्छ्रित:।।११।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistārād dviguṇaḥ skandhaḥ sarvasmin buddhanāgare ।
[FLAG: ++++ marks four illegible syllables] pañcabhāgā syājjaṅghāsvasamucchritaḥ ।।11।।

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE, p. 434, verse 11, pāda 2, fn. 10 शुद्धनागरे for बुद्धनागरे।

**English Translation:**

The Skandha is to be double fold than the breadth in the entire Śuddha nāgara² and five parted may be Jaṅghā having individual height.

---

### Verse 59.12

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तिलकानां तथोच्छ्रायो विधातव्यो द्विभागिक:।
तिलकस्य शिरोघण्टां चैकसूत्रेण मापयेत्।।१२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tilakānāṃ tathocchhrāyo vidhātavyo dvibhāgikaḥ ।
tilakasya śiroaghaṇṭāṃ caikasūtreṇa māpayet ।।12।।

**English Translation:**

Of the tilakas likewise the elevation deserves to be created as twin-parted one and the ghaṇṭā of tilaka one may measure by a single thread or plumb.

---

### Verse 59.13

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घामानत्रिभागेन खुरिपण्डीं प्रकल्पयेत्।
खुरकं वेदिबस्यं च समसूत्रेण मापयेत्।।१३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghāmānatribhāgena khuripaṇḍīṃ prakalpayet ।
khurakaṃ vedibasyaṃ ca samasūtreṇa māpayet ।।13।।

**English Translation:**

By a part triad of the measurement of Jaṅghā one may create the Khurapinḍī. The Khuraka and Vedībandha, one may measure by a similar thread.

---

### Verse 59.14

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घामानत्रिभागेन खुरिपण्डीं प्रकल्पयेत्।
खुरकं वेदिबस्यं च समसूत्रेण सूत्रयेत्।।१४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghāmānatribhāgena khuripaṇḍīṃ prakalpayet ।
khurakaṃ vedibasyaṃ ca samasūtreṇa sūtrayet ।।14।।

**English Translation:**

By a triad of measurement of the Jaṅghā one may create the Khurapinḍī and Khuraka and Vedībandha, one may initiate by a similar thread.

---

### Verse 59.15

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वितीयभूमिकोत्सेधं सिंहकर्णं विभूषयेत्।
मस्तके घण्टया युक्ता चतुर्भागोच्छ्रिता च सा।।१५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvitīyabhūmikotsedhaṃ siṃhakarṇaṃ vibhūṣayet ।
mastake ghaṇṭayā yuktā caturbhāgocchritā ca sā ।।15।।

**English Translation:**

The elevated one the second storey one may decorate the Siṃhakarṇa on the Mastaka equipped with a ghaṇṭā and that may be elevated by four parts.

---

### Verse 59.16

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ततस्तृतीयभूत्सेधः पदतुल्यांशवर्जितः।
चतुर्थी भूमिका कार्या सार्धभागत्रयोच्छ्रिता।।१६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tatas tṛtīyabhūtsedhḥ padatulyāṃśavarjitaḥ।
caturthī bhūmikā kāryā sārdhabhāgatrayocchrītā।।16।।

**English Translation:**

Then the elevation of the third storey divested of a part equal to a square, the fourth storey deserves to be created elevated by a part and a half.

---

### Verse 59.17

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मञ्जरीस्तम्भयोर्मध्ये सवातायनमेखला।
द्वितीया भूमिका या सा सिंहकर्णैरलङ्कृता।।१७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mañjarīstambhayormadhye savātāyanamekhalā।
dvitīyā bhūmikā yā sā siṃhakarṇairlalaṅkṛtā।।17।।

**English Translation:**

In the centre of the Mañjarī and Stambha a mekhalā along with a window, and the second storey that may be that may be decorated by Siṃhakarṇas.

---

### Verse 59.18

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तस्या द्वारं विधातव्यं कपाटद्वयसंयुतम्।
(स्यावद्धातृ?)पाटितं द्वारं तृतीयायां सदा भुवि।।१८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tasyā dvāraṃ vidhātavyaṃ kapāṭadvayasaṃyutam।
(syāvaddhātṛ?)pāṭitaṃ dvāraṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ sadā bhuvi।।18।।

[FLAG: The second pāda opening reads "(स्यावद्धातृ?)पाटितं" — the bracketed portion is marked uncertain in the source with "(?)"]

**English Translation:**

A door of that deserves to be created equipped with two door panels. In the third storey always a door may be half opened.

---

### Verse 59.19

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पादे(दो)नद्विपदोत्सेधा तदूर्ध्वं वेदिमेखला।
कैरवाणां दलैर्युक्ता कर्तव्या दृष्टिहारिभिः।।१९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pāde(do)nadvipādotsedhā tadūrdhvaṃ vedimekhalā।
kairavāṇāṃ dalairyuktā kartavyā dṛṣṭihāribhiḥ।।19।।

[FLAG: "पादे(दो)न" — the "(दो)" is an editorial insertion/correction marked in the source]

**English Translation:**

The Vedīmekhalā above that may be such as having elevation two padas reduced by one pāda equipped with the sprouts of Kairavas (white lotuses) fascinating for the eyes.

---

### Verse 59.20

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वेदिका पञ्चविस्तारा कार्या भागसमुच्छ्रिता।
ग्रीवार्धभागिकोत्सेधा घण्टैकं भागसमुच्छ्रिता।।२०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vedikā pañcavistārā kāryā bhāgasamucchrītā।
grīvārdhabhāgikotsedhā ghaṇṭaikaṃ bhāgasamucchrītā।।20।।

**English Translation:**

Vedikā deserves to be created as having breadth of five having elevation of a part having elevation of the partition of a half of grīvā and a ghaṇṭā elevated by a part.

---

### Verse 59.21

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चभाग+विस्तारा घण्टाकोटिर्विधीयते।
(कुमादं̐ ? )वेदिबध्यं च घण्टाग्रं मस्तकोदयम्।।२१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcabhāga+vistārā ghaṇṭākoṭirvidhīyate।
(kumādaṃ?)vedibaddhyaṃ ca ghaṇṭāgraṃ mastakodayam।।21।।

[FLAG: "(कुमादं̐ ?)" is marked uncertain in the source with "(?)"; "+" in पञ्चभाग+विस्तारा is reproduced as it appears]

**English Translation:**

Having breadth of five parts deserves to be created elevated by a part. Kumuda and Vedībandha having frontal ghaṇṭā with the elevation of mastaka.

> **Footnote 2:** GOSE, p.435, verse 21 has कुमदं

---

### Verse 59.22

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मापयेत् समसूत्रेण समन्ताद् भूमिपञ्चके।
व्यासार्धहस्तसङ्ख्यानि(प्रवेशा?)प्रथमं क्षितेः।।२२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

māpayet samasūtreṇa samantād bhūmipañcake।
vyāsārdhahastasakkhyāni(praveśā?)prathamaṃ kṣiteḥ।।22।।

[FLAG: "(प्रवेशा?)" is marked uncertain in the source with "(?)"]

**English Translation:**

One may measure by a similar thread all around on the pentad of storeys. The entrance of the earth of foreground may be of the cubits in half of the breadth.

---

### Verse 59.23

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अङ्गुलानि तदा ऋचर्धो द्वितीयायाः प्रकीर्तिताः (?)।
संयोगाद्नयोर्यः स्यात् तृतीयायास्तमादिशेत्।।२३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

aṅgulāni tadā ṛcardho dvitīyāyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ (?)।
saṃyogādnayoryaḥ syāt tṛtīyāyāstamādiśet।।23।।

[FLAG: "(?)"] is reproduced as it appears in the source after "प्रकीर्तिताः"]

**English Translation:**

[FLAG: English continues on next page]

---

### Verse 59.24

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तदध्यर्धश्चतुथ्यास्तु पञ्चम्या: शेष ईरित:।
स्वमूलविस्तृतेर्भागस्तृतीयो वेदिकोर्ध्वत:।।२४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tadadhyardhaścatuthyāstu pañcamyāḥ śeṣa īritaḥ |
svamūlavistrterbhāgastṛtīyo vedikorddhvataḥ || 24 ||

**English Translation:**

A half more than that of the fourth one and of the fifth the Śeṣa stands developed. In the broad area of the root of its own, the third part is the upper part of Vedikā.

---

### Verse 59.25

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

लतया विस्तृतिभद्रे युक्ताया जालवर्त्मन: (?)।
(वेदिदोग्रविधातव्यो ?) सार्धसविस्तृती।।२५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

latayā vistṛtibhadre yuktāyā jālavartmanaḥ (?) |
(vedidogravídhātavyo ?) sārdhasavistṛtī || 25 ||

**English Translation:**

By a latā the breadth on the Bhadra of the one combined on the window routes. The Vedī and the upper part deserve to be created having breadth of one and a half.

---

### Verse 59.26

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मञ्जर्या: स्तम्भसीमानां क्षोभयेत् पुष्टिमानत: (?)।
(वेद्यां भागे?) शालायां निष्कामो मूलकोणत:।।२६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mañjaryāḥ stambhasīmānāṃ kṣobhayet puṣṭimānataḥ (?) |
(vedyāṃ bhāge?) śālāyāṃ niṣkāmo mūlakoṇataḥ || 26 ||

**English Translation:**

To the extremity of the Mañjarī one may develop by a measurement of the Puṣṭi on the portion of the Vedī, in a Hall the exit door may be from the corner of the root as such.

---

### Verse 59.27

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्थानैर्विचित्ररूपै: स्यात् सिंहकर्णैश्च भूषित:।
पञ्चव्यासेन सूत्रेण रेखामस्य समालिखेत्।।२७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sthānairvitravarūpaiḥ syāt siṃhakarṇaiśca bhūṣitaḥ |
pañcavyāsena sūtreṇa rekhāmasya samālikhet || 27 ||

**English Translation:**

By spots of variegated norm and decorated by Siṃhakarṇas, by a Sūtra of Pañcavyāsa (five breadths) one may carve out its boundary (Rekhā).

---

### Verse 59.28

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एतद् विमानं ललितं देवदेवस्य कारयेत्।
विमानम्।। संस्थानं सर्वतोभद्रस्येदानीमभिधीयते।।२८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

etad vimānaṃ lalitaṃ devadevasya kārayet |
vimānam || saṃsthānaṃ sarvatobhadrasye dānīmabhidhīyate || 28 ||

**English Translation:**

This Vimāna as Lalita one may create of Devadeva (Brahmā, Śiva and Viṣṇu)¹. The establishment spot now of Sarvatobhadra is being talked of today.

> **Footnote 1:** V.S. Apte, p. 259.

---

### Verse 59.29

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जठरं बाह्यसीमा च तथा भित्त्यन्धकारिका।
जङ्घोत्सेधश्च कर्णौ च यथा मेरोस्तथा भवेत्।।२९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṭharaṃ bāhyasīmā ca tathā bhittyandhakaārikā |
jaṅghotsedhāśca karṇau ca yathā merostathā bhavet || 29 ||

**English Translation:**

Jaṭhara and Bāhya Simā, and likewise the wall and blind alley (andhakārika) the elevation of the Jaṅghā and the two Karṇas may be the way as those of Meru.

---

### Verse 59.30

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तथैव भद्रविस्तार: षड्भागेन समनतत:।
रथिके च द्विभागे स्त: कोणसंज्ञे च पार्श्वयो:।।३०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tathaiva bhadravistāraḥ ṣaḍbhāgena samantat aḥ |
rathike ca dvibhāge staḥ koṇasaṃjñe ca pārśvayoḥ || 30 ||

**English Translation:**

And likewise the breadth of the Bhadra by six parts all around, the two Rathikās may be of two parts and the two Koṇas on two flanks.

---

---

### Verse 59.31

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मुष्टिप्रमाणं(ण)विस्तारं कर्तव्यमुदकान्तरम्।
विस्ताराद् द्विगुणः स्कन्धस्योच्छ्रायो भागविंशतिः (?)॥३१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

muṣṭipramāṇaṃ(ṇa)vistāraṃ kartavyamudakāntaram |
vistārād dviguṇaḥ skandhasyocchrāyo bhāgaviṃśatiḥ (?)॥31॥

**English Translation:**

The breadth of the size of Muṣṭi deserves to be created having a water channel double fold than the breadth is to be the height of the Skandha as twenty parts.

---

### Verse 59.32

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चभागसमुत्सेधा जङ्घा कार्या सदा बुधैः।
मेखलान्तरपत्रं च सार्धभागसमुच्छ्रितम्॥३२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcabhāgasamutsedhā jaṅghā kāryā sadā budhaiḥ |
mekhalāntarapatraṃ ca sārdhabhāgasamucchhritam॥32॥

**English Translation:**

By the wise ones the Jaṅghā deserves to be created as having elevation of five parts and the Mekhalāntarapatrā having height of a part and a half.

---

### Verse 59.33

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शृङ्गं भागत्रयोत्सेधं सग्रीवामलसारकम्।
मूलशृङ्गस्य गर्भेण न्यस्येदुपरिभूमिकाम्॥३३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śṛṅgaṃ bhāgatrayotsedhāṃ sagrīvāmalasārakam |
mūlaśṛṅgasya garbheṇa nyasyedupari bhūmikām॥33॥

**English Translation:**

The Śṛṅga having elevation of a part triad accompanied by an entrance hall and Amalasāraka. By the centre of the Mūlaśṛṅga one may create the upper storey.

---

### Verse 59.34

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वितीयभूमिविस्तारं दशधा प्रविभाजयेत्।
द्वौ भागौ शृङ्गविस्तारौ(रो) विधेयः पार्श्वयोर्द्वयोः॥३४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvitīyabhūmivistāraṃ daśadhā pravibhājayet |
dvau bhāgau śṛṅgavistārau(ro) vidheyaḥ pārśvayor dvayoḥ॥34॥

**English Translation:**

The breadth of the second storey one may divide by ten parts. Śṛṅgavistāra deserves to be created in two parts on both the flanks.

---

### Verse 59.35

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सग्रीवामलसारस्य तैः शृङ्गस्योदयस्थितिः।
तस्य शृङ्गस्य गर्भेण कर्तव्योपरिभूमिका॥३५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

sagrīvāmalasārasya taiḥ śṛṅgasyodayasthitiḥ |
tasya śṛṅgasya garbheṇa kartavyoparibhūmikā॥35॥

**English Translation:**

Of Amalasāraka along with the entrance hall, and Śṛṅga the elevation may be by those. By the centre of the Śṛṅga of that one, deserves to be created the upper storey.

### Verse 59.36

toraṇānyatha mālyāni tasyālaṅkūraṇāni ca।
nīlotpaladalaākārā mañjaryaḥ sarvaśobhanāḥ॥6 4॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: "alaṅkūraṇāni" — the word may be "alaṅkuraṇāni" (alaṅkṛraṇāni); reading as written]

**English Translation:**

The arched portals and the garlands and the ornaments of that one, of the shape of blue lotuses, the Mañjarīs all beautiful.

---

### Verse 59.5

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमानमपरं होतद् योनिरेकस्तयोर्द्वयो:।
केवलं भद्रभेदेन मणिको द्राविडोऽप्ययम्॥६ ५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vimānamaparaṃ hotad yonirekastayordvayoḥ।
kevalaṃ bhadrabhedena maṇiko drāviḍo'pyayam॥6 5॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: "hotad" — may be "haitad" or "hyetad"; reading as visible]

**English Translation:**

The other Vimāna is of a different type, the parent source of the two is one. Alone by the differentiation of Bhadra Maṇika is Drāviḍa as such.

---

### Verse 59.6

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मणिक:॥

प्रासादमथ वक्ष्यामो गरुडं सर्वसुन्दरम्।
दशधा क्षेत्रविस्तारं तस्य पूर्व विभाजयेत्॥६ ६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

maṇikaḥ॥

prāsādamatha vakṣyāmo garuḍaṃ sarvasundaram।
daśadhā kṣetravistāraṃ tasya pūrva vibhājayet॥6 6॥

**English Translation:**

We shall now talk of the Prāsāda Garuḍa all beautiful. The breadth of Kṣetra or plot of that one, one may divide into ten parts.

---

### Verse 59.7

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वौ भागौ रथिका: कार्या मूलकण्णाद् विनिस्सृता:।
भद्रं षड्(भाग)विस्तारं पक्षवंशादिभेदितम्॥६ ७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvau bhāgau rathikāḥ kāryā mūlakaṇṇād vinissṛtāḥ।
bhadraṃ ṣaḍ(bhāga)vistāraṃ pakṣavaṃśādibheditam॥6 7॥

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]
"Two parted Rathikās deserve to be created projected out of Mūlakarṇa. The Bhadra having breadth of six parts distinguished from the flanked diagonal."

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]
"The exit door of the balcony deserves to be created by a half of the boundary on four sides. The boundary at the root deserves to be crated deprived of the water-coarse."

### Verse 59.68 (॥६८॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अलिन्दनिर्गम: कार्य: सीमार्धेन चतुर्दिशम्।
मूलसीमा तु कर्तव्या सलिलान्तरवर्जिता।।६८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

alindanigamaḥ kāryaḥ sīmārdhena caturdiśam |
mūlasīmā tu kartavyā salilāntaravarjitā ||68||

**English Translation:**

The exit door of the balcony deserves to be created by a half of the boundary on four sides. The boundary at the root deserves to be crated deprived of the water-coarse.

---

### Verse 59.69 (॥६९॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

स्यान्मूलसीमविस्तारात् स्कन्ध: स्याद् द्विगुणोच्छ्रिति:।
प्रासादस्य समुच्छ्रायात् त्रिभागेन समेखलाम्।।६९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

syānmūlasīmavistārāt skandhaḥ syād dviguṇocchṛitiḥ |
prāsādasya samucchhrāyāt tribhāgena samekhālam ||69||

**English Translation:**

There may be Skandha from the breadth of the root boundary and the elevation may be redoubled, from the elevation of the Prāsāda, by a part triad, accompanied by Mekhalā.

---

### Verse 59.70 (॥७०॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घामन्तरपत्रेण (कुं+युक्तं कुर्वस्तसा?)।
जी (ही)रेकं वेदिबन्धं च भागत्रयसमुच्छ्रितम्।।७०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghāmantarapatreṇa (kuṃ+yuktaṃ kurvas tasā?) |
jī (hī)rekaṃ vedibandhaṃ ca bhāgatrayasamucchritam ||70||

**English Translation:**

By the internal plate of the Jaṅghā one may create that one Hīraka and Vedībandha, may be elevated by a part-triad.

---

### Verse 59.71 (॥७१॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अलिम्बा(न्दा)नां समुत्सेधं शे(शि)खरार्धेन कारयेत्।
षड्भागं स्कन्धविस्तारं विदद्धीत विचक्षण:।।७१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

alimbā(ndā)nāṃ samutsedhaṃ śe(śi)kharārdhena kārayet |
ṣaḍbhāgaṃ skandhavistāraṃ vidaddhīta vicakṣaṇaḥ ||71||

**English Translation:**

The elevation of balconies one may create by a half of the roof. The skilled are may create the breadth of Skandha as having six parts.

---

### Verse 59.72 (॥७२॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवार्धभाग(मुत्से)धाद् भागमामलसारकम्।
कुमुदं चार्धभागेन कुम्भ: स्यादेकभागिक:।।७२।।

गरुड:।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvārdhabhāga(mutse)dhād bhāgamāmalasārakam |
kumudaṃ cārdhabhāgena kumbhaḥ syādekabhāgikaḥ ||72||

garuḍaḥ ||

**English Translation:**

The Amalasāraka may be of one part as compared with the elevation of the half part of Grīvā. Kumuda may be by a half part and Kumbha of a single part.

---

### Verse 59.73 (॥७३॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अथोच्यते वर्धमानो दशधा तं विभाजयेत्।
पादोनांशद्वयं कुर्यात् पार्श्वयो: कर्णिविस्तृतिम्।।७३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

athocyate vardhamāno daśadhā taṃ vibhājayet |
pādonāṃśadvayaṃ kuryāt pārśvayoḥ karṇivistṛtim ||73||

**English Translation:**

Now is being illustrated Vardhamāna. One may divided that in ten parts and the breadth of the Karṇas in the flanks one may create as having part-twain reduced by a part.

---

### Verse 59.74 (॥७४॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सपादपदिवस्तारौ रथकौ वामदक्षिणौ।
चतुर्भागोन्मितं भद्रं विस्तारेण प्रकीर्तितम्।।७४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sapādapadivistārau rathakau vāmadakṣiṇau |
caturbhāgonmitaṃ bhadraṃ vistāreṇa prakīrtitam ||74||

**English Translation:**

The two Rathakas on the left and right may have breadths of square normed parts. The Bhadra may be measured as of four parts by way of breadth declared as such.

---

### Verse 59.75 (॥७५॥)

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तारो(राद्) द्विगुणोच्छ्रायं स्कन्धं यावत् प्रकल्पयेत्।

vistāro(rād) dviguṇocchhrāyaṃ skandhaṃ yāvat prakalpayet |
khurakasyātha jaṅghāyā mañjarīskhandhayorapi ||75||

**English Translation:**

### Verse 59.76

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ग्रीवामलसारकादेः प्रमाणं गरुडे यथा।
वर्धमान: ।।
द्वाविंशतिकरायामः शङ्खावर्तोऽथ कथ्यते।।७६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

grīvāmalasārakādeḥ pramāṇaṃ garuḍe yathā |
vardhamānaḥ ||
dvāviṃśatikarāyāmaḥ śaṅkhāvarto'tha kathyate || 76 ||

**English Translation:**

As also of Grīvā and Amalasāraka and the like, the extent as much is, in the Garuḍa, that much may be therein (in Vardhamāna). The Śaṅkhāvartta is being illustrated having lengths of twenty two cubits.

---

### Verse 59.77

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मूलसीमावृत्तनाहस्तस्य स्यात् पद्मके यथा।
भित्तिगर्भस्य विस्तार: (पादेनार्धनवकमात्?)।।७७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mūlasīmāvṛttanāhastasya syāt padmake yathā |
bhittigarbhasya vistāraḥ (pādenārdhanavakamāt?) || 77 ||

**English Translation:**

And the enclosure of the circular norm of the boundary at the root, may be as it is in Padamaka. The breadth of curvature of the wall may be by a half square.

---

### Verse 59.78

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अलिन्दमग्रतः कुर्यात् सिंहकर्णविभूषितम्।
उत्सेधत्र्यंशतो जङ्घा (वेद्यं?) तत्र विभागतः।।७८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

alindamagrataḥ kuryāt siṃhakarṇavibhūṣitam |
utsedhātryaṃśato jaṅghā (vedyaṃ?) tatra vibhāgataḥ || 78 ||

**English Translation:**

In front of the balcony one may create decorated by a Siṃhakarṇa, the Jaṅghā Vedya (may be Vedha) from the part triad of the elevation as per divisibility.

---

### Verse 59.79

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

आस्कन्धं वेदिकाबन्धाद् विस्तृतेर्द्विगुणोच्छ्रितः।
मेखलान्तरपत्रं च जङ्घामध्ये विधीयते।।७९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

āskandhаṃ vedikābandhād vistṛterdviguṇocchritaḥ |
mekhalāntarapatraṃ ca jaṅghāmadhye vidhīyate || 79 ||

**English Translation:**

Upto the Skandha right from the Vedikābandha, of breadth may be the redoubled height. The Mekhalāntarapattra is provided within the centre of the Jaṅghā.

---

### Verse 59.80

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भ्रमयेत् कर्णसूत्रेण बहिर्वृत्तं समन्ततः।
कर्णदिक्पालयोर्मध्यं वृत्तसूत्रेण वर्तयन् (येत्)।।८०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhramayet karṇasūtreṇa bahirvṛttaṃ samantataḥ |
karṇadikpālayormadhyaṃ vṛttasūtreṇa vartayan (yet) || 80 ||

**English Translation:**

One may rotate by the Karṇasūtra a circle on the outside all over, and the centre of the Karṇa and Dikpāla, one may measure by a circular tape.

---

### Verse 59.81

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अवशिष्टं त(ल)च्छन्दं स्वस्तिकस्येव कारयेत्।
ग्रीवा(म)मलसारं च कलशं वारिनिर्गमम्।।८१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

avaśiṣṭaṃ ta(la)cchandaṃ svastikasyeva kārayet |
grīvā(ma)malasāraṃ ca kalaśaṃ vārinirgamam || 81 ||

**English Translation:**

The Avaśiṣṭa talacchada one may create like that of Svastika, the Grīvā and Amalasāraka as also Kalaśa and water sewerage.

---

### Verse 59.82

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्वीत स्वस्तिकस्येव विस्तारोत्सेधमानतः।
शू(मू)लसीमानुसारेण च्छेदे संवरणं भवेत्।।८२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kurvīta svastikasyeva vistārotsedhamānataḥ |
śū(mū)lasīmānusāreṇa cchede saṃvaraṇaṃ bhavet || 82 ||

**English Translation:**

One may create like the Svastika as per breadth, and elevations measurement. As per the condignment of the original boundary at the Cheda, the Saṃvaraṇa may be there.

### Verse 59.83

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शड्ढावर्त:॥
बूमोऽथ पुष्पकं स स्याद् विमानसदृशाकृति:॥८३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tadūpameva latinaṃ vartayed valanākṛtim।
śaḍḍhāvartaḥ॥
būmo'tha puṣpakaṃ sa syād vimānasadṛśākṛtiḥ॥83॥

Of the same structure may be the latina to which he may rotate as per shape of circulations. Now we shall talk of Puṣpaka that may have a form similar to that of Vimāna.

---

### Verse 59.84

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तावत्प्रमाणस्तद्वृद्धि: पञ्चभूशतुरश्रक:।
(विमानेन मानयुक्त?) यन्मञ्जर्या यच्च लक्षणम्॥८४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tāvatpramāṇastadvṛddhiḥ pañcabhūśaturaśrakaḥ।
(vimānena mānayukt?) yanmañjaryā yacca lakṣaṇam॥84॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: पञ्चभूशतुरश्रक: — possibly पञ्चभूश्चतुरश्रक: = pañcabhūścaturaśrakaḥ]

**English Translation:**

Of that norm may be the structure the enlongative of that one and may have five storeys square shaped as such equal in measurement to that of Vimāna the way it may be of Mañjarī in light of its definition.

---

### Verse 59.85

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तत् कार्यमत्र मञ्जर्या नतु कार्यं जलान्तरम्।
पुष्पक:॥
गृहराजमथ बूम: स स्यात् कैलाससन्निभ:॥८५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tat kāryamatra mañjaryā natu kāryaṃ jalāntaram।
puṣpakaḥ॥
gṛharājamatha būmaḥ sa syāt kailāsasannibhaḥ॥85॥

**English Translation:**

That deserves to be created of Mañjarī and no water coarse is to be created. We now talk of Gṛharāja. That may be similar to Kailāśa.

---

### Verse 59.86

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विटङ्कूनिर्गमाधारनिर्यूहै: सर्वतो वृत:।
वलभ्या भूषितो मध्ये गवाक्षद्वारसंयुत:॥८६॥

viṭaṅkūnirgamādhāraniryūhaiḥ sarvato vṛtaḥ।

All around circular owing to the niryūhas (projections on the columns of gates) or aisles or (mattavāraṇaka or Upāśraya)¹ being the basement of the loftiest points or pinnacles decorated by roof in the centre equipped with the gate of the window.

> **Footnote 1:** V.S. Apte, p. 295.

### Verse 59.87

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वेदिकाखण्डजालाद्यं क ++ परितो भवेत्॥८७॥

vedikākhaṇḍajālādyaṃ ka ++ parito bhavet॥87॥

**English Translation:**

Having at the extremities dove cot columns decorated by bracket female figures and headed by networks or lattices fragmentary on the pavilions all around a curvature may be there.

---

### Verse 59.88

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(कुवीत्य?) मलकच्छाद्यै: सिंहकणैश्च भूषित:।

One may create by the swan like or cup like coverages and decorated by Siṃhakarṇas as per distinction of the balconies the scholars enunciate as Gṛharāja.

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कैलासस्येव संस्थानं स्यादस्योर्ध्वमधोऽपि च।

### Verse 59.89

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

बूमोऽथ स्वस्तिकं तस्य पूर्ववन्मानलक्षणम्॥८९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

brūmo'tha svastikaṃ tasya pūrvavan māna-lakṣaṇam ॥89॥

**English Translation:**

The plot like that of Kailāsa may be of it at the top as well as at the root. Now we talk of Svastika and of that the measurement and definition as per earlier norm.

---

### Verse 59.90

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तेनैव लतिनं सर्वं कुर्वीतैनं विचक्षण:।
यथा मूले विभक्ता: स्युर्लतिनस्वस्तिकादय:॥९०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tenaiva latinaṃ sarvaṃ kurvītainaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
yathā mūle vibhaktāḥ syur latina-svastikādayaḥ ॥90॥

**English Translation:**

By that one may create Latina in all regards, the expert one the way at the root divided may be Latina Svastika and the like.

---

### Verse 59.91

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तथैषां स्कन्धभक्तानां मध्ये रेखां प्रकल्पयेत्।
प्रासाद: स्वस्तिको नाम स्यादेवं लक्षणान्वित:॥९१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tathaişāṃ skandha-bhaktānāṃ madhye rekhāṃ prakalpayet ।
prāsādaḥ svastiko nāma syādevaṃ lakṣaṇānvitaḥ ॥91॥

**English Translation:**

That very way in the centre of the divided Skandhas one may create a Rekhā. The Prāsāda Svastika by name may be this way endowed with marks or illustrations.

---

### Verse 59.92

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(सुधानासोदय:?)¹ स्वस्य कर्तव्य: स+भागिक:।
स्कन्धं यावत् समुत्सेधो विस्ताराद् द्विगुणो भवेत्॥९२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(sudhānāsodayaḥ?) [IAST UNCERTAIN: first word] svasy kartavyaḥ sa+bhāgakaḥ ।
skandhaṃ yāvat samutsedho vistārād dviguṇo bhavet ॥92॥

**English Translation:**

The Aquiline nosed parrot normed an elevation of this one deserves to be created as of equal partitions. The elevation of the type of Skandha or the elevation upto the Skandha, may be redoubled as per comparison of breadths.

> **Footnote 1:** fn.6 has शुकनासोदय:।

### Verse 59.93

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(तयृ?)द्वागोच्छ्रता जङ्घा मेखला चा + भागिकी।
मध्यशाला द्विभागाश्च मूलसूत्रविभागत:॥९३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(tayṛ?) [IAST UNCERTAIN: first word] dvāgocchṛtā jaṅghā mekhalā cā + bhāgikī ।
madhyaśālā dvibhāgāśca mūlasūtra-vibhāgataḥ ॥93॥

**English Translation:**

The Jaṅghā may be elevated to the partition of three digits and Mekhalā also of a part as such. The Madhyaśāla (the central chamber) of two partitions as per division of the original plumb line.

> **Footnote 2:** fn.2 has मुखभागे।

### Verse 59.94

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्णा द्विभागिकाछ्ऐवं जलमार्गस्तु षोडश।
