karṇā dvibhāgikācch aivaṃ jalamārgas tu ṣoḍaśa ।
aṣṭau śālā bhavanty asmin karṇi-chāṣṭau samanataḥ ॥94॥

**English Translation:**

The Karṇas may be of two partitions and this way the latticed ventilator of sixteen eight halls do exist in it and Karṇas eight all over.

kalaśaś-chiṇḍakā grīvā tadvad āmalasārakaḥ ॥95॥

The entrance hall one may create on the out precinct on the frontal part, the expert one. Kalaśa and Aṇḍikā, grīvā and that very way Amalasāraka.

### Verse 59.96

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

रुचकं बूमहे तस्य विभागो दशधा भवेत्॥१९६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

rucakaṃ brūmahe tasya vibhāgo daśadhā bhavet ॥196॥

**English Translation:**

The roof and the upper portion, extent, the way it is of earlier norm may be of that norm. Rucaka we shall talk of the division of that may be of ten-fold norm.

---

### Verse 59.97

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागद्वयिमती कर्णौ भद्रं षड्भागसम्मितम्।
तेषां विनिर्गमं विद्या (हस्तमात्रा?)¹ प्रमाणतः॥१९७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgadvayimatī karṇau bhadraṃ ṣaḍbhāgasammitam ।
teṣāṃ vinigamaṃ vidyā (hastamātrā?) pramāṇataḥ ॥197॥

**English Translation:**

The two Karṇas may be of part-twain, and Bhadra measuring six parts and of those the exit door may be known as a cubit normed in extent.

> **Footnote 1:** हस्तमात्रं GOSE, p.442, fn.5.

---

### Verse 59.98

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्यादुदकमार्गांश्च प्रासादे रुचके क्वचित्।
स्कन्धावशिष्टमुत्सेधो विस्ताराद् द्विगुणो भवेत्॥१९८॥

kuryādudakamārgāṃśca prāsāde rucake kvacit ।
skandhāvaśiṣṭamutsedhо vistārād dviguṇo bhavet ॥198॥

**English Translation:**

One may create the water coarses or channels in the Prāsāda Rucaka somewhere. The elevation may be of the specific norm of Skandha redoubled as per breadth.

---

### Verse 59.99

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वेदिकायास्तु विस्तार: स्कन्धे षड्भागिक: स्मृत:।
तृतीयाशेन कुर्वीत जङ्घामूर्ध्वं सुरोदयान्॥१९९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vedikāyāstu vistāraḥ skandhe ṣaḍbhāgikaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
tṛtīyāśena kurvīta jaṅghāmūrdhvaṃ surodayān ॥199॥

**English Translation:**

The breadth of the Vedikā, however, at the Skandha is remembered as six parted one. By a third digit one may create the Jaṅghā above rising up like a god.

---

### Verse 59.100

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घायाश्च त्रिभागेन कार्या खुरखरण्डिका।
मेखलान्तरपत्रं च कुर्याद्ध्यर्धभागिकम्॥१९१००॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghāyāśca tribhāgena kāryā khurakharaṇḍikā ।
mekhālāntarapatraṃ ca kuryāddhy ardhabhāgikam ॥1 9 100॥

**English Translation:**

Of the Jaṅghā Khurakharandikā deserves to be created by three parts and Mekahlāntarapattra one may create as a part more than a half.

---

### Verse 59.101

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सार्धत्रिगुणसूत्रेण पूर्वा कर्कटना भवेत्।
(चतुर्गुणं मूलसूत्रेण मध्ये कर्कटना स्मृतौ² ?)॥१०१॥

By a triple plumb, of one and a half, the first Karkaṭanā may be there and by a four-fold sūtras, the central Karkaṭanā is well known.

> **Footnote 2:** चतुर्गिणेन सूत्रेण मध्य कर्कटना स्मृता। GOSE, p.442, verse 100, pādas 3-4.

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

vibhajya daśadhā skandhavistāraṃ taiḥ prakalpayет ।

### Verse 59.104

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुम्भं भागेन कुर्वीत प्रासादे रुचके बुध:।

kumbhaṃ bhāgena kurvīta prāsāde rucake budhaḥ ।
sādhāraṇo'yaṃ sarveṣāṃ prāsādastu divaukasām ॥104॥

**English Translation:**

Kumbha one may create by a part in the Prāsāda Rucaka, the expert one. The Prāsāda is normally allocated to the divinities.

---

### Verse 59.105

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पुण्डूवर्धनकं ब्रूम: प्रासादं वल्भं हरे:।
भ्रमयेन्मूलसीमास्पृग्वृत्तमादौ समन्तत:॥१०५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

puṇḍūvardhanakam brūmaḥ prāsādaṃ valbhaṃ hareḥ ।
bhramayenmūlasīmāspṛgvṛttamādau samantataḥ ॥105॥

**English Translation:**

We now talk of Puṇḍra Vardhanaka the Prāsāda, favourite one of Hari (Viṣṇu). One may make it rotate as cordoning the root boundary and circular at the opening all over.

---

### Verse 59.106

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तच्छालाकर्णसंयुक्तं कर्तव्यं सर्वतोदिशम्।
यच्छन्द: स्वस्तिकेऽस्य(स) स्याद् द्विगुण: पुण्डूवर्धने॥१०६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tacchālākarṇasaṃyuktaṃ kartavyaṃ sarvatodisam ।
yacchandaḥ svastike'sya(s) syād dviguṇaḥ puṇḍūvardhane ॥106॥

**English Translation:**

That one equipped with Halls and Karṇas (śālās and karṇas) deserves to be created all over the directions. Whatever Chandaḥ may there be in Svastika of this one that same may be as such in Puṇḍra vardhana as redoubled.

---

### Verse 59.107

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घोदकान्तभद्राणामुच्छ्रायो विस्तृतिश्च या।
स्वस्तिके कथिता सैव विज्ञेया पुण्डूवर्धने॥१०७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghodakāntabhadrāṇāmucChrāyo vistṛtiśca yā ।
svastike kathitā saiva vijñeyā puṇḍūvardhane ॥107॥

**English Translation:**

Jaṅghā of the Udakāntabhadras, the height as well as the breadth that is the Svastika has been illustrated as such, and deserves to be understood in Puṇḍravardhana.

---

### Verse 59.108

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अथाभिधीयते मेरुर्दशधा तत्र भाजयेत्।
सीमा(न) तस्य कुर्वीत शृङ्गं चापि (वि?) भागिकम्॥१०८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

athābhidhīyate meruradaśadhā tatra bhājayet ।
sīmā(na) tasya kurvīta śṛṅgaṃ cāpi (vi?) bhāgikam ॥108॥

**English Translation:**

Now is being talked of Meru ten-fold that may one divide and boundary of that one may create and Śṛṅga even of a part.

---

### Verse 59.109

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शेषं+भागिकं भद्र(मायामेन?) विधीयते।
पदस्य षोडशौसेन(शांशेन) कर्तव्यमुदकान्तरम्॥१०९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śeṣaṃ+bhāgikaṃ bhadra(māyāmena?) vidhīyate ।
padasya ṣoḍaśausena(śāṃśena) kartavyamudakāntaram ॥109॥

**English Translation:**

Śeṣa as six parted Bhadra by length is ordained as such. By the sixth part of the pada deserves to be created the water coarse.

---

### Verse 59.110

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पदै: षोडशिभर्गभो विधातव्य: पदं पदम्।
भित्तिरन्धारिका बाह्यभित्तिश्चास्य विधीयते॥११०॥

**English Translation:**

By padas sixteen Garbha deserves to be created from pada to pada. Bhitti (wall) Andhakarikā (blind alley) and the outer wall, of this one is provided as such.

### Verse 59.111

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागषट्कोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा मेखला चैकभागिकी।
श्रृङ्गं च त्रिपदोत्सेधं शिखरं स्याद् दशोच्छ्रितम्॥१११॥

śṛṅgaṃ ca tripadotsedhaṃ śikharaṃ syād daśocchritam॥111॥

**English Translation:**

The Jaṅghā may be elevated by a part hexagonal and Bekhalā of one part only. And Śṛṅga of elevation of 3 padas and Śikhara may be elevated by ten.

---

### Verse 59.112

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्तव्यं वास्तुशास्त्रज्ञैस्तस्यैकादशभूमिकम्।
अर्धपञ्चमविस्तारः स्कन्धो ग्रीवार्थभागिका:॥११२॥

kartavyaṃ vāstuśāstrajñaistasyaikādaśabhūmikam।
ardhapañcamavistāraḥ skandho grīvārthabāgikāḥ॥112॥

**English Translation:**

Deserves to be created by those expert in Science of Architecture of that one of eleven storeys. The Skandha of breadth of half fives and grīvā of half a part.

---

### Verse 59.113

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उच्छ्रायेण विधातव्या भा(गि)कोत्सेधमण्डकम्।
भागाधि(ध)कं च कुमुदं भागिका कलशोच्छ्रितिः॥११३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ucchāyeṇa vidhātavyā bhā(gi)kotsedhamaṇḍakam।
bhāgādhi(dha)kaṃ ca kumudaṃ bhāgikā kalaśocchritiḥ॥113॥

**English Translation:**

By elevation deserves to be created having Utsedha and maṇḍaka as of one part and Kumuda of a part half and one parted the height of Kalaśa.

---

### Verse 59.114

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्गुणेनैव सूत्रेण रेखा तस्य प्रकीर्तिता।
मेरुं मेरुगिरिप्रख्यमेवं यः कारयेदिमम्॥११४॥

meruṃ merugiriprakhyamevaṃ yaḥ kārayedimam॥114॥

**English Translation:**

By a six stringed thread the Rekhā of that one is illustrated as such. To Meru well known as Merugiri, (Meru mountain) who may create this way.

---

### Verse 59.115

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

शिलाभिरिष्टकाभिर्वा स महत् पुण्यमानुयात्।
मेरुः॥
लक्षणं मन्दरस्याथ प्रासादस्याभिधीयते॥११५॥

meruḥ॥
lakṣaṇaṃ mandarasyātha prāsādasvābhidhīyate॥115॥

**English Translation:**

With stone slabs or bricks one may acquire great merit. Now the definition of the Prāsāda mandara is being illustrated.

---

### Verse 59.116

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गर्भस्यार्धेन निष्क्रान्तं भद्रं कुर्वीत मन्दरे।
९+++मेरुसङ्कुशं विन्यस्येत् सर्वतोदिशम्॥११६॥

garbhasyārdhena niṣkrāntaṃ bhadraṃ kurvīta mandare।
9+++merusaṅkuśaṃ vinyasyet sarvatodisam॥116॥

**English Translation:**

In Mandara, the Bhadra one may create as projected out by a half of the garbha. The temple or palace equal in norm to Meru, one may assort in all the direction.

> **Footnote 1:** fn.3, p.443, GOSE- मन्दिरम्

### Verse 59.117

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वलभी मध्यदेशे तु शिखरोर्ध्वसमुद्धृता।
सर्वमा(म)न्यत् प्रमाणं तु मेरोरिव भवेदिह॥११७॥

valabhī madhyadeśe tu śikharordhvasamuddhrṛtā।
sarvamā(ma)nyat pramāṇaṃ tu meroriva bhavedha॥117॥

**English Translation:**

The roof in the central region carved out on the upper top, the entire other structure may be of the same extent as of Meru.

### Verse 59.118

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्रं षड्भागविस्तारं मध्यदेशे विनिःसृतम्॥११८॥

We shall now talk of Kailāśa. that one may divide into ten parts. The bhadra may be of breadth of six parts, projected in the central part.

### Verse 59.119

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्ण(र्णा)द्विभागविस्तारा: सलिलान्तरवर्जिता:।
गर्भस्यार्धेन निष्कास: कार्यो भद्रस्य सर्वत:॥११९॥

garbhasyārdhena niṣkāsaḥ kāryo bhadrasya sarvataḥ ॥119॥

The Karṇas having breadth of two parts devoid of water coarses, and an outlet of a half of the garbha, deserves to be done all around the Bhadra.

### Verse 59.120

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

¹(लतिस्याखरं?) मध्ये शिखरार्धसमोदयम्।
भित्तिगर्भभ्रमन्तीनां जङ्घामेखलयोरपि॥१२०॥

bhittigarbhabhramantīnāṃ jaṅghāmekhalayorapi ॥120॥

**English Translation:**

Lati may be in the centre of the roof, having elevation of the equaling of half of the roof the bhitti garbha bhramantis², (the revolving modes in the centre of the walls) of Jaṅghā andIekhalā even.

> **Footnote 1:** लति: स्याच्छिखरं मध्ये, GOSE, p.444, fn.6, verse 120, pāda I. (Lati = Latā- creeper design on the roof, prāsāda śikhara, p.153).
> **Footnote 2:** A surrounding terrace, a circular path, an enclosure verāṇḍaḥ (EOHA, p.419).

### Verse 59.121

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्तारमुदयं चास्मिन् विदध्यात् स्कन्धशृङ्गयो:।
मेरोरिवास्मिन् प्रासादे (ववचद्व?³)ग्रीवाण्डकस्य च॥१२१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistāramudayaṃ cāsmin vidadhyāt skandhaśṛṅgayoḥ ।
merorivāsmin prāsāde (vavacadva?³)grīvāṇḍakasya ca ॥121॥

The breadth as well as height in this one may create of Skandha and Śṛṅga, like Meru in this Prāsāda (mansion) of Aṇḍaka and Grīvā enclosed as such.

By the cubit extents of the Prāsādas in every case the exit door of a bhadra is to be there two cubited, three cubited or even four cubited as such.

### Verse 59.146

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(भली)मध्ये तु मञ्जर्य: कार्या: पद्मदलोपमा:।
मर्वत: परिकर्म स्याद् रथिका कर्णसंश्रया॥१४६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(bhalī)madhye tu mañjaryaḥ kāryāḥ padmadalopamāḥ ।
marvataḥ parikarm syād rathikā karṇasaṃśrayā ॥146॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: मर्वत: — possibly सर्वत: (sarvataḥ); visually reads as मर्वत: but may be typographic. The word परिकर्म is printed without inherent a-ending clarity.]

**English Translation:**

Within the centre of the Śālā (hall)¹ Mañjarīs deserve to be created similar to the lotus sprouts. All around a decorative designing may be there and Rathikā recumbent on Karṇa.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 447, fn. 1 has शाला for भली।

---

### Verse 59.147

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

आमलिश्चन्द्रशालाभि:² स्कन्धान्तं परिपूरयेत्(?)।
खुरपिण्डा च जङ्घा च कुम्भाग्रं शिखरादि च॥१४७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

āmaliścandraśālābhiḥ skandhāntaṃ paripūrayet(?) ।
khuapiṇḍā ca jaṅghā ca kumbhāgraṃ śikharādi ca ॥147॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: आमलिश्चन्द्रशालाभि: — the compound is unusual; may be āmalaś-candraśālābhiḥ or āmaliś-candraśālābhiḥ. Footnote 2 notes: आमलैश्चन्द्रशालाभि:, GOSE p.447, fn.3, verse 147, pāda 1.]

**IAST Transliteration (with footnote variant):**

āmalaiścandraśālābhiḥ skandhāntaṃ paripūrayet(?) ।
khuapiṇḍā ca jaṅghā ca kumbhāgraṃ śikharādi ca ॥147॥

**English Translation:**

By Āmalas and Candraśālās (Moon chambers) one may replenish the end or corner of the Skandha Khuapiṇḍā and Jaṅghā Kumbhāgra and Śikhara and the like.

> **Footnote 2:** आमलैश्चन्द्रशालाभि: GOSE, p.447, fn.3, verse 147, pāda 1.

### Verse 59.148

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यत्किञ्चित् तत् प्रमाणेन वर्धमानसमं भवेत्।
नन्द्यावर्त:॥
बूमो वलभ्यं स भवेद् गृहराजस्य सन्निभ:॥१४८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

yatkiñcit tat pramāṇena vardhamānasamaṃ bhavet ।
nandyāvartaḥ ॥
būmo valabhyaṃ sa bhaved gṛharājasya sannibhaḥ ॥148॥

[FLAG: The layout of this verse is unusual — "नन्द्यावर्त:॥" appears on a separate line between the two half-verses, possibly a label or heading. Transcribed as printed.]

**English Translation:**

whatever may be by extent that may be similar to Vardhamāna. We talk of Valabhī³ that may be similar structured as Gṛharāja.

> **Footnote 3:** Puṣpaka rectangular building (EOHA, p.372).

---

### Verse 59.149

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

आव(य)तश्चतुरश्रो वा प्रमाणेनैकत: सम:।
चतुरश्रस्तु विस्ताराद्उदयो(ये) द्विगुणो भवेत्॥१४९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

āva(ya)taścaturaśro vā pramāṇenaikataḥ samaḥ ।
caturaśrastu vistārādudayo(ye) dviguṇo bhavet ॥149॥

**English Translation:**

Oblong or square shaped, in structure equitable in a single norm, or on one side, the square shaped one in comparison to breadth may be redoubled as such.

---

### Verse 59.150

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

(आध्यतन्रस्य पुन: सार्ध: स्कन्धोच्छ्रायो विधीयते?)।
(विस्तारं दशधा समक्ष+तुरश्र समन्त:?)॥१५०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

(ādhyatanrasya punaḥ sārdhaḥ skandhocchāyo vidhīyate?) ।
(vistāraṃ daśadhā samakṣa+turaśra samantaḥ?) ॥150॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: आध्यतन्रस्य — unclear compound; possibly आद्यतन्त्रस्य (ādyatantrasya). The +++  mark in the translation corresponds to the + in the Devanagari line.]

**English Translation:**

From the start, of that one, again the elevation of the Skandha is ordained. the breadth may be ten-fold in front and all around as such.

### Verse 59.151

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विभजे(भागे) स्यात् ततो मानं पूर्वप्रासादसन्निभम्।
स्वरूपं तस्य वक्ष्याम: श्रीवत्समिव तं भवे(जे)त्॥१५१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vibhaje(bhāge) syāt tato mānaṃ pūrvaprāsādasannibham ।
svarūpaṃ tasya vakṣyāmaḥ śrīvatsam iva taṃ bhave(je)t ॥151॥

**English Translation:**

By division then may be measurement, equal in norm to the earlier Prāsāda, we shall

### Verse 59.152

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यद्वा विमानरुचकवर्धमानादिसद्मना।
छन्देनैकस्य कस्यापि प्रासादस्य विभाजयेत्।।१५२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

yadvā vimānarucakavardhamānādisadmanā।
chandenaika sya kasyāpi prāsādasya vibhājayet।।152।।

**English Translation:**

Or of Vimāna, Rucaka, Vardhamāna and the like palaces, by the concord of one some one, a division of the Prāsāda one may create.

---

### Verse 59.153

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भूस्तम्भपरिकर्माणि विस्तारोत्सेधमेखला:।
सिंहकर्णरथा घण्टा तथा कुम्भाग्रषण्डकम्।।१५३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhūstambhaparikarmāṇi vistārotsedhamekhalāḥ।
siṃhakarṇarathā ghaṇṭā tathā kumbhāgraṣaṇḍakam।।153।।

**English Translation:**

The structure of the ground columns, breadth elevation and central parts, Siṃhakarṇarathaaas and ghaṇṭās, and likewise Kumbhāgraṣaṇḍaka.

---

### Verse 59.154

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यत्किञ्चित् तत् प्रमाणेन यथैवाद्यं तथा भवेत्।
वलभ्य:।।
सुपर्णस्य (स्व)रूपं च प्रमाणं चाभिधीयते।।१५४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

yatkiñcit tat pramāṇena yathaivādyaṃ tathā bhavet।
valabhyaḥ।।
suparṇasya (sva)rūpaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ cābhidhīyate।।154।।

**English Translation:**

Whatever something may there be by the extent that may be like the one it was earlier. The structure of Suparṇa and extent are being narrated.

---

### Verse 59.155

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विभक्तं सिंहरूपेण सर्वभद्रं निवेशयेत्।
भागैश्चतुर्भिर्निष्क्रान्तं भद्रं (गभियत:?¹)समम्।।१५५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vibhaktaṃ siṃharūpeṇa sarvabhadraṃ niveśayet।
bhāgaiścaturbhirniṣkrāntaṃ bhadraṃ (gabhiyataḥ?¹)samam।।155।।

**English Translation:**

Divided by the form of Siṃha one may introduce Sarvabhadra projected by four parts equal in size to "Gavaya".

> **Footnote 1:** गवयत:। GOSE, p.447, fn.11, verse 155, pāda 4.

---

### Verse 59.156

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वौ भागौ मूलकर्णौ तु षड्भागा भद्रविस्तृति:।
पञ्चभागोच्छ्रिता जङ्घा मेखला तस्य भागिका।।१५६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvau bhāgau mūlakarṇau tu ṣaḍbhāgā bhadravistṛtiḥ।
pañcabhāgocchritā jaṅghā mekhalā tasya bhāgikā।।156।।

**English Translation:**

Two padas may stand divided from the height in all quarters. In the Mūlakarṇas of the Śṛṅgas three parted may be the elevation.

---

### Verse 59.157

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मूलजङ्घात्रिभागेन खुरवेदिसमुच्छ्रिति:।
(सच?) मध्ये तु शृङ्गे द्वे कर्तव्ये वामदक्षिणे।।१५७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mūlajaṅghātribhāgena khuravedisamucchritiḥ।
(sac?) madhye tu śṛṅge dve kartavye vāmadakṣiṇe।।157।।

**English Translation:**

Two parted Mūlakarṇas and the breadth of Bhadra as hexagonal parted one and Jaṅghā elevated by five parts and Mekhalā of that one of one part.

---

### Verse 59.158

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उच्छ्रायाद् द्विपदे स्यातां विभक्ते सर्वतोदिशम्।
मूलकर्णेषु शृङ्गाणां त्रिपदा स्यात् समुच्छ्रिति:।।१५८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

ucchāyād dvipade syātāṃ vibhakte sarvotodiśam।
mūlakarṇeṣu śṛṅgāṇāṃ tripadā syāt samucchritiḥ।।158।।

**English Translation:**

The Mūlajaṅghā by a part triad having height of Khura Vedī. In the centre of that two Śṛṅgas deserve to be created to the left and right.

### Verse 59.159

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page — the Devanagari for this verse begins at the top of the page; the verse number suggests this is verse 159 of the chapter, but the opening pāda may be cut off from the previous page.]

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कुर्याज्जाला(ध?)विस्तारं श्रीवत्से नन्दने यथा॥१५९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kuryājjālā(dh?)vistāraṃ śrīvatse nandane yathā ॥159॥

[FLAG: (ध?) — unclear akṣara, possibly ध, marked with query in original]

**English Translation:**

In the Śrivatsa the breadth of lattices may be the same as in Nandana of four cubits in line with the elevation.

---

### Verse 59.160

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विस्ताराद् द्विगुणोत्सेध: स्कन्ध: षड्भागविस्तृत:।
उत्सेधस्य त्रिभागेन जङ्घोत्सेधो विधीयते॥१६०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistārād dviguṇotsedhaḥ skandhaḥ ṣaḍbhāgavistṛtaḥ ।
utsedhasyatribhāgena jaṅghotse dho vidhīyate ॥160॥

**English Translation:**

The Utsedha (elevation) may be twice as the breadth and Skandha as broad by six parts. By the part triad of the elevation, the elevation of Jaṅghā is being ordained.

---

### Verse 59.161

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तृतीयांशेन जङ्घाया: कुर्वंत खुरपिण्डिकाम्।
मेखलान्तरपत्रं च विदध्यात् सार्धभागिकम्॥१६१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tṛtīyāṃśena jaṅghāyāḥ kurvaṃta khurapinḍikām ।
mekhalāntarapatraṃ ca vidadhyāt sārdhabhāgikam ॥161॥

**English Translation:**

By the third part of the Jaṅghā one may create the Khura piṇḍikā and may create Mekhalāntarapattra as of a part and a half.

---

### Verse 59.162

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विभाज्य दशभिर्भगै: पूर्ववत् स्कन्धविस्तृति:।
सार्धद्विगुणविस्तृत्या पूर्वा कर्कटना भवेत्॥१६२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vibhājya daśabhirbhagaiḥ pūrvavat skandhavistṛtiḥ ।
sārdhadviguṇavistṛtyā pūrvā karkaṭanā bhavet ॥162॥

**English Translation:**

The breadth of the Skandha deserves to be created as per earlier norm by ten parts divided as such. The eastern or earlier Karkaṭanā may be by a breadth of two and a half norm.

---

### Verse 59.163

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्गुणेन सूत्रेण मध्या कर्कटना भवेत्।
श्री(ग्री)वार्धभागमुत्सेधात् कुमुदं कुम्भकं पुन:॥१६३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturguṇena sūtreṇa madhyā karkaṭanā bhavet ।
śrī(grī)vārdhabhāgamutsedhāt kumudaṃ kumbhakaṃ punaḥ ॥163॥

[FLAG: श्री or ग्री — uncertain reading, both given in original with parenthetical variant]

**English Translation:**

By a four-fold Sūtra the Madhyākarkaṭanā may be there. From the elevation the grīvā may be of a half part and Kumuda and Kumbhaka again.

---

### Verse 59.164

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अस्मिन्नामलसारं च यथा चाद्ये तथा भवेत्।
श्रीवत्स:॥
पद्मनाभमथ ब्रूम: पद्मशालाभिरन्वितम्॥१६४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

asminnāmalasāraṃ ca yathā cādye tathā bhavet ।
śrīvatsaḥ ॥
padmanābhamatha brūmaḥ padmaśālābhiranvitam ॥164॥

[FLAG: "श्रीवत्स:॥" appears as a label/heading mid-verse in the original, possibly marking the end of the Śrīvatsa section and beginning of Padmanābha description]

**English Translation:**

In this the Amalasāraka may be as it is in the earlier norm. Now we shall talk of Padmanābha endowed with Padmaśālās.

---

### Verse 59.165

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

द्वितीय: पद्मको होष पद्ममालाधर: शुभ:।
सर्वमन्यत् प्रमाणं तु पद्मस्वस्तिकयोर्यथा॥१६५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dvitīyaḥ padmako hoṣa padmamālādharaḥ śubhaḥ ।
sarvamanyat pramāṇaṃ tu padmasvastikayoryathā ॥165॥

**English Translation:**

The second may be Padmaka the one auspicious one as equipped with lotus wreaths. All other in extent, however, may be the one as of Padma and Svastika.

---

### Verse 59.166

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पद्मनाभ:॥
वैराजमथ वक्ष्यामि स विज्ञेयो विमानवत्।
(रूपशिख+मुत्सेधस्तम्भग्रीव+रूपकम्?)॥१६६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

padmanābhaḥ ॥
vairājamatha vakṣyāmi sa vijñeyo vimānavat ।
(rūpaśikha+mutsedhastambhagrīva+rūpakam?) ॥166॥

[FLAG: "पद्मनाभ:॥" appears as a section label. The second pāda contains a heavily corrupted or editorially marked reading with + signs and query mark as in the original: (रूपशिख+मुत्सेधस्तम्भग्रीव+रूपकम्?)]
[FLAG: English continues on next page]

**English Translation:**

I shall now talk of Vairāja. That may be understood as one like Vimāna Rūpaśikha

### Verse 59.167

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

¹सभातोरणनिर्यूहसिंहकर्णा(णैं) तादृशैः।
साधारं चतुरश्रं च तं कुर्यात् पञ्चभूमिकम्॥१६७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

¹sabhātaraṇaniryūhasiṃhakarṇā(ṇaiṃ) tādṛśaiḥ।
sādhāraṃ caturaśraṃ ca taṃ kuryāt pañcabhūmikam॥167॥

**English Translation:**

Columns, arched portals, pinnacles or matta vāranakas (aisles) by the Siṃhakarṇas of that type. Equipped with basement and being square shaped one may create that having five storeys.

> **Footnote 1:** स्तम्भतोरणनिर्यूहसिंहकर्णांश्च तादृशैः। GOSE, p.448, fn.11, verse 167, pāda 1.

### Verse 59.168

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमानसदृशाकारो वैराजोऽयमुदाहृतः।
वैराजः॥
बूमोऽथ वृत्तकं मूले चतुरश्रः प्रकीर्तितः॥१६८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vimānasadṛśākāro vairājo'yamudāhṛtaḥ।
vairājaḥ॥
bhrūmo'tha vṛttakaṃ mūle caturaśraḥ prakīrtitaḥ॥168॥

[FLAG: The label "वैराजः॥" appears to be a prose heading/label between the two pādas, not part of the verse metre.]

**English Translation:**

Having shape like that of Vimāna, has been illustrated this one as "Vairāja". Now we talk of "Vṛttaka" declared as square shaped at the basement.

---

### Verse 59.169

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

जङ्घामूले ततोऽष्टाभिस्त्रि(श्रि)वृत्तो भागतृतीयके।
मूलमध्याग्रतः पूर्णं तं कुर्यात् सर्वतोदिशम्॥१६९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

jaṅghāmūle tato'ṣṭābhistri(śri)vṛtto bhāgatṛtīyake।
mūlamadhyāgrataḥ pūrṇaṃ taṃ kuryāt sarvatodisam॥169॥

**English Translation:**

At the basement of the Jaṅghā by eight being circular at the part-triad. Fulsome at root or basement, centre and front, to that one may create in all the directions.

---

### Verse 59.170

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भद्राकारं च भद्रेषु विभागे चतुरश्रके।
²(अश्श्रिवज्जकाकारा?) वृत्ते स्वस्तिकसन्निभम्॥१७०॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhadrākāraṃ ca bhadreṣu vibhāge caturaśrake।
²(aśśrivajjakākārā?) vṛtte svastikasannibham॥170॥

**English Translation:**

In Bhadras of the shape of Bhadra, having square shaped division, in the octagonal one of the shape of Vajra and in Vṛtta like that of Svastika.

> **Footnote 2:** अष्टश्रीवज्रकाकारम्, GOSE, p.449, verse 170, pāda 3.

---

### Verse 59.171

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यथा मूलविभागेन लतिनः स्वस्तिकोदयः।
तथा वृद्धिप्रमाणाभ्यामयमप्याद्यवद् भवेत्॥१७१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

yathā mūlavibhāgena latinaḥ svastikodayaḥ।
tathā vṛddhipramāṇābhyāmayamapyādyavad bhavet॥171॥

**English Translation:**

Just as by the division of the basement of Latina, the height of Svastika that very way by growth and extent, this one may also be of the same type as earlier one.

---

### Verse 59.172

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

वृत्तकः॥
नन्द्यावर्तमथ बूमो दशधा तं विभाजयेत्।

### Verse 59.244

ध्वजच्छत्रविमानादैः किङ्किणीभिर्विराजितम्।
यत्किञ्चित् सुन्दरं सर्वं तदत्र विनिवेशयेत्॥२४४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

dhvajacchatravimānādaiḥ kiṅkiṇībhirvirājitam।
yatkiñcit sundaraṃ sarvaṃ tadatra viniveśayet॥244॥

**English Translation:**

Decorated by bells and equipments such as aerial cars, parasols and flags or pennons, whatever may be the beatific material all that one may incorporate.

---

### Verse 59.245

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तारागण:॥
अशष्टकैर्द्वे च विशेषयोगात्
प्रासादषष्ठिश्चतुरन्वितैषा।
विमानमुख्याः कथिता य एतान्
(जातायस्यैस?¹) शिल्पिगणाग्रणीः स्यात्॥२४५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tārāgaṇaḥ॥
aṣaṣṭakairddve ca viśeṣayogāt
prāsādaṣaṣṭiścaturaṃvitaiṣā।
vimānamukhyāḥ kathitā ya etān
(jātāyasyais?¹) śilpigaṇāgraṇīḥ syāt॥245॥

**English Translation:**

By eight eights i.e. sixty four with twain added specifically, with sixty four Prāsādas added as such, these chief mansions that have been spoken of he who knows those, he may be the fore-runner among the architects.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p.456, fn 2, verse 245, pāda 4 has जातास्यसौ।

# Chapter 60 — The Definition of Thirty six Prasadas headed by Sri Kuta

### Verse 60.1

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

षड्विं(त्रिं)शतमथ ब्रूमः प्रासादान् नागरक्रियान्।
साधारान् प्रथमस्तेषु श्रीकूटः श्रीमुखस्तत:॥१॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ṣaḍviṃ(triṃ)śatamatha brūmaḥ prāsādān nāgarakriyān।
sādhārān prathamaste ṣu śrīkūṭaḥ śrīmukhastataḥ॥1॥

**English Translation:**

Now we shall talk of thirty six Prāsādas having activities of the townsmen entailing Sādhārās. The first among them is Śrīkūṭa and Śrīmukha after that.

---

### Verse 60.2

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

श्रीधरो बदरश्चैव तथाभ्य:(न्य:) प्रियदर्शिन:।
कुलनन्दोऽन्तरिक्षश्च पुष्पभासो विशालक:॥२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

śrīdharo badaraścaiva tathābhya(nya)ḥ priyadarśinaḥ।
kulanandoṃntarikṣaśca puṣpabhāso viśālakaḥ॥2॥

**English Translation:**

Śrīdhara, and Badara and likewise Priyadarśana, Kulananda nd Antarikṣa, Puṣpabhāsa and Viśālaka.

---


### Verse 60.3

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सङ्कीर्णोऽठ महानन्दो नन्द्यावर्तस्तथापर:।
सौभाग्यश्च विभङ्गश्च विभवस्तदनन्तरम्॥३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

saṅkīrṇo'ṭha mahānando nandyāvartastathāparaḥ।
saubhāgyaśca vibhaṅgaśca vibhavastadanantaram॥3॥

**English Translation:**

Saṅkirṇa and Mahānanda and the other one Nandyāvartta. Saubhāgya and Vibhaṅga and Vibhava subsequently.

---

### Verse 60.4

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

बीभत्सकोऽठ श्रीतुङ्गो मानतुङ्गस्तथापर:।
(भवतो रुद्र?) संज्ञश्च (भवद्बाह्योदरस्तच:?)॥४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bībhatsako'ṭha śrītuṅgo mānatuṅgastathāparaḥ।
(bhavato rudra?) saṃjñaśca (bhavadbāhyodarastacaḥ?)॥4॥

**English Translation:**

Vibhatsaka and Śrītuṅga and the other one Manatuṅga and one named Sarvatobhadra¹ and likewise may be Bāhyodara one.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, Ch. 60, verse 4 pada 3 has in fn. 9-10 सर्वतोभद्र: इति स्यात् for भवतोरुद्र p. 456.

---

### Verse 60.5

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

निर्यूहोदर(संज्ञोऽन्य)स्ततो ज्ञेय: समोदर:।
नन्दिभद्रो भद्रकोशश्चित्रकूटस्तत: परम्॥५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

niryūhodara(saṃjño'nya)stato jñeyaḥ samodaraḥ।
nandibhadro bhadrakośaścitrakūṭastataḥ param॥5॥

**English Translation:**

The other one is "Niryūhodara" named as such, and then may be known as Samodara. Nandibhadra and Bhadrakośa and Citrakūṭa after that.

---

### Verse 60.6

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विमलो हर्षणो भद्रसङ्कीर्णस्तदनन्तरम्।
ततो भद्रविशालाख्यो भद्रविष्कम्भ एव च॥६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vimalo harṣaṇo bhadrasaṅkīrṇastadanantaram।
tato bhadraviśālākhyo bhadraviṣkambha eva ca॥6॥

**English Translation:**

Vimala, Harṣaṇa, Bhadra Saṅkirṇa following that. Thereafter one named Bhadra Viśāla as also Bhadra Viṣkambha.

---

### Verse 60.7

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उज्जयन्ताभिधानश्च सुखे(मे)रुरथ मन्दर:।
कैलास: कुम्भकाक्ष(ख्य)श्च गृहराजश्च नामत:॥७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

ujjayantābhidhānaśca sukhe(me)ruratha mandaraḥ।
kailāsaḥ kumbhakākṣa(khya)śca gṛharājaśca nāmataḥ॥7॥

**English Translation:**

One named Ujjayanta Sumeru and Mandara Kailāśa and one named Kumbha and one by name gṛharāja.

---

### Verse 60.8

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

एतेषां(षट्) त्रिंशदुदिष्टा लक्षणं कश्यतेऽधुना।
चतुःश्रीकृते क्षेत्रे द्वादशांशिविभाजिते॥८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

eteṣāṃ(ṣaṭ) triṃśadudiṣṭā lakṣaṇaṃ kaśyate'dhunā।
catuḥśrīkṛte kṣetre dvādaśāṃśivibhājite॥8॥

**English Translation:**

Of these thirty six have been named. Now their definition is being dilated upto. In a square shaped field, divided into twelve parts.

---

### Verse 60.9

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादं विभजेत् प्राज्ञ: श्रीकू(टे) नाम शोभने (नम्)।
ज्येष्ठ: स्याद् विंशतिहंस्ता² मध्यमो दश पञ्च च॥९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādaṃ vibhajet prājñaḥ śrīkū(ṭe) nāma śobhane (nam)।
jyeṣṭhaḥ syād viṃśatihastā² madhyamo daśa pañca ca॥9॥

**English Translation:**

The expert may devise a Prāsāda named Śrīkūṭa, the gorgeous one. The largest one may be of twenty cubits and the middling one of ten and five i.e. fifteen cubits.

> **Footnote 2:** विंशतिहस्तो may be the correct expression.

### Verse 60.11

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तिलकं भागिकं कार्यं भागेनैकेन निर्गतम्।
तस्माद् भागेन निष्क्रान्तं भद्रमस्य विधीयते॥११॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

tilakam̐ bhāgikaṃ kāryaṃ bhāgenaikena nirgatam।
tasmād bhāgena niṣkrāntaṃ bhadramasya vidhīyate॥11॥

**English Translation:**

Tilaka deserves to be created as a single parted one, projected by a part as such. On that projected as such, the Bhadra of this one stands provided.

---

### Verse 60.12

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागिकी बाह्यभित्ति: स्याद् द्विपदा चान्धकारिका।
भागिकी गर्भभित्तिश्च गर्भ: कार्यश्चतुष्पद:॥१२॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgikī bāhyabhittiḥ syād dvipadā cāndhakārikā।
bhāgikī garbhabhittiśca garbhaḥ kāryaścatuṣpadaḥ॥12॥

**English Translation:**

The outer wall may be of one part and of two padas Andhakārikā¹. The central wall of one part and the garbha (adyatum) deserves to be created as of four padas.

> **Footnote 1:** May be Andhārikā- a closed verāṇḍaḥ or balcony (EOHA, p.60-61).

---

### Verse 60.13

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

अधश्छन्द: समुदिष्ट ऊर्ध्वच्छन्दोऽभिधीयते।
विस्तारार्धेन जङ्घा स्यान्मेखला चैकभागिका॥१३॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

vistārārdhena jaṅghā syānmekhalā caikabhāgikā॥13॥

**English Translation:**

Adhaśchanda has been illustrated. Now Ūrdhva Chanda is being talked of. The Jaṅghā may be by a half of the breadth and Mekhalā may be by one part only.

---

### Verse 60.14

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागत्रयोच्छ्रितं शृङ्गं द्वितीयमपि तादृशम्।

**English Translation:**

The Śṛṅga may be elevated by a part-triad and the second one also of the same norm. In the centre of the first Śṛṅga that deserves to be created as such by the experts.

---

### Verse 60.16

स्यादुरोमञ्जरी सप्तभागोत्सेधा षडायता।
स्याद् भागिकमस्छा(धश्छा)द्यं मञ्जर्या या तु विस्तृति:॥१६॥

A half parted Udaya is to be created the Tilaka and the other one of the same norm over the roof of the second Tilaka equipped with well knit framework, may be Uromañjarī having elevation of seven parts and oblong by six parts. The lower Chādya may be one parted and of Mañjarī that may be the breadth.

### Verse 60.27

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मण्डपं कारयेदेवं श्रीकूटस्य विचक्षण:।
श्रीकूटस्य विचक्षणं भूषणं स मण्डप: समात्।

**IAST Transliteration:**

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सौभाग्य:।।

विधीयते यदास्यैव विना भद्रमलिन्दकः।
तदा विभङ्गको नाम प्रासादः स्यात् सुशोभनः।।५४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

saubhāgyaḥ।।

vidhīyate yadāsyaiva vinā bhadramalindakaḥ।
tadā vibhaṅgako nāma prāsādaḥ syāt suśobhanaḥ।।54।।

**English Translation:**

When without Bhadra a balcony is created then one named Vibhaṅgaka Prāsāda may be highly gorgeous.

---

### Verse 60.55

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विभङ्गकः।।

यदि भद्रस्य निष्कासः क्रियतेऽस्य तदा पुनः।
प्रासादो विभवो नाम जायते परमोत्तमः।।५५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vibhaṅgakaḥ।।

yadi bhadrasya niṣkāsaḥ kriyate'sya tadā punaḥ।
prāsādo vibhavo nāma jāyate paramottamaḥ।।55।।

**English Translation:**

In case the exit door of the Bhadra is created of this one again, the Prāsāda becomes Vibhava highly excellent.

---

### Verse 60.56

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

विभव:।।

भागद्वयविनिष्क्रान्ता नन्दिका क्रियते यदि।
तदा वदन्ति बीभत्ससंज्ञं प्रासादमुत्तमम्।।५६।।

बीभत्सः।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

vibhavaḥ।।

bhāgadvayaviniṣkrāntā nandikā kriyate yadi।
tadā vadanti bībhatsasaṃjñaṃ prāsādamuttamam।।56।।

bībhatsaḥ।।

**English Translation:**

Projected by a part-twain, if Nandikā is created then, they call it as Bībhatsa a Prāsāda very excellent.

---


### Verse 60.57

[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यदा निर्गमविस्तारसमा भवति नन्दिका।
श्रीतुङ्गइति विज्ञेयस्तदा प्रासादसत्तमः।।५७।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

yadā nirgamavistārasamā bhavati nandikā।
śrītuṅga iti vijñeyastadā prāsādasattamaḥ।।57।।

**English Translation:**

When Nandikā becomes such as equal in norm to the breadth of the exit door, Śrītuṅga this way may be called the Prāsāda quite superior one.

---

### Verse 60.58

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

श्रीतुङ्गः।।
यदा त्वलिन्दकोऽस्यैव क्रियते न विनिर्गतः।
प्रासादो मानतुङ्गाख्यस्तदानीमुपजायते।।५८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

śrītuṅgaḥ।।
yadā tv alindako 'syaiva kriyate na vinirgatah।
prāsādo mānatuṅgākhyastadānīmupajāyate।।58।।

**English Translation:**

When the balcony of this one is not created as projected one, the Prāsāda named Mānatuṅga then does get into being.

---

### Verse 60.59

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मानतुङ्गः।।
ब्रूमोऽथ सर्वतोभद्रं दशधा तं विभाजयेत्।
षड्विधान्त्या (विंशत्या) भवेज्ज्येष्ठः कनीयान् दशभिः करैः।।५९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

mānatuṅgaḥ।।
brūmo'tha sarvatobhadraṃ daśadhā taṃ vibhājayet।
ṣaḍvidhāntyā (viṃśatyā) bhavejjyeṣṭhaḥ kanīyān daśabhiḥ karaiḥ।।59।।

**English Translation:**

Now we talk of Sarvatobhadra. One may divide it into ten parts. The largest one may be of twenty six cubits and the smallest of ten cubits.

---

### Verse 60.60

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

हस्तैस्तथाष्टादशभिर्मध्यम: परिकीर्तितः।
कर्णा द्विभागिकाः कार्या अलिन्दाः षट्पदोन्मिताः।।६०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

hastaistatthāṣṭādaśabhirmadhyamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ।
karṇā dvibhāgikāḥ kāryā alindāḥ ṣaṭpadonmitāḥ।।60।।

**English Translation:**

By 18 cubits then the middle one is enunciated. Karṇas are to be created as two parted ones, and balconies as of six padas.

---

### Verse 60.61

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

चतुर्भागानि भद्राणि वि(द्वि) भागस्तद्विनिर्गमः।
गर्भभित्तिर्बहिर्भित्तिरन्धारी च पदं पदम्।।६१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

caturbhāgāni bhadrāṇi vi(dvi)bhāgastadvinirg​amaḥ।
garbhabhittirb​ahirbhittirandhārī ca padaṃ padam।।61।।

**English Translation:**

The Bhadras of four parts the exit door of that part-twain garbha bhitti and bāhyabhitti and Andhārī¹ of one pada each.

> **Footnote 1:** A closed veranda : a balcony (EOHA, p.61).

---

### Verse 60.62

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गर्भस्तु षोडशपद इत्येवं छन्द ईरितः।
विस्तारार्घेन जङ्घा स्यान्मेखला चैकभागिका।।६२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

garbhastu ṣoḍaśapada ityevaṃ chanda īritaḥ।
vistārārghena jaṅghā syānmekhālā caikabhāgikā।।62।।

**English Translation:**

The garbha is to be of sixteen padas, this way is declared Chandaḥ. The Jaṅghā is to be by a half of the breadth and Mekhalā as of one part.

---

### Verse 60.63

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रथमं कल्पयेच्छृङ्गं विस्तारात् सार्धमुच्छ्रितम्।
द्वितीय शृङ्गं तत्राल्पं पूर्वशृङ्गस्य मध्यगम्।।६३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prathamaṃ kalpayecchṛṅgaṃ vistārāt sārdhamucchritam।
dvitīya śṛṅgaṃ tatrālpaṃ pūrvaśṛṅgasya madhyagam।।63।।

**English Translation:**

The Śṛṅga may one create as the first one elevated as half of the breadth. The second Śṛṅga there may be shorter one, intertwined within the first Śṛṅga.

---

### Verse 60.78

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तदानीं भद्रसंकीर्णः प्रासादो जायते शुभः।
भद्रसंकीर्णः।।
अस्यैव तु यदा भद्रं भागेनैकेन निर्गतम्।।७८।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tadānīṃ bhadrasaṃkīrṇaḥ prāsādo jāyate śubhaḥ।
bhadrasaṃkīrṇaḥ।।
asyaiva tu yadā bhadraṃ bhāgenaikena nirgatam।।78।।

**English Translation:**

Then Bhadra Saṅkīrṇa, a Prāsāda becomes nicely decorated or auspicious one. Of this very one when Bhadra is such as projected by one part.

---

### Verse 60.79

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भवेत् तदानीं प्रासादो नाम्ना भद्रविशालकः।
भद्रविशालकः।।
+++ भद्रैश्च विना यदा त्वेष विधीयते।।७९।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhavet tadānīṃ prāsādo nāmnā bhadraviśālakaḥ।
bhadraviśālakaḥ।।
+++ bhadraiśca vinā yadā tveṣa vidhīyate।।79।।

**English Translation:**

The Prāsāda then may be named Bhadraviśālaka¹ and without Bhadras and alindas (balconies) when it becomes tenable.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 464, verse 79, pāda 1, fn. 5 भद्रैरलिन्दैश्च विना यदा त्वेषविधीयते।

---

### Verse 60.80

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

तदानीं भद्रविष्कम्भः प्रासादः स्यात् सुखप्रदः।
भद्रविष्कभः।।
चित्रकूटादिषट्टकम्।
चतुरश्रे समे क्षेत्रे विभक्तेऽष्टभिरष्टकैः।।८०।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

tadānīṃ bhadraviṣkambhaḥ prāsādaḥ syāt sukkhapradaḥ।
bhadraviṣkabhaḥ।।
citrakūṭādiṣaṭṭakam।
caturśre same kṣetre vibhakte'ṣṭabhiraṣṭakaiḥ।।80।।

**English Translation:**

Then Bhadraviṣkambha Prāsāda may be bliss endowing.

Citrakūṭa and the like a hexagon.

In the field square shaped and equilateral and divided by eight octagons.

---

### Verse 60.81

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

प्रासादं (चयेद्वा?) उज्जयन्तं सुशोभनम्।
पदमेकं भवेत् कर्णस्तिलकस्तादेव च।।८१।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

prāsādaṃ (cayedvā?) ujjayantaṃ suśobhanam।
padamekaṃ bhavet karṇastilakastādeva ca।।81।।

**English Translation:**

Prāsāda one may create² as Ujjayanta very beautiful. The Karṇa may be of a single pada and Tilaka likewise of the same norm.

> **Footnote 2:** प्रासादंरचयेद् विद्वान् उञ्जयन्त सुशोभनम्। verse 81, pāda 1.

---

### Verse 60.82

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

सभित्तिगर्भमानेन भद्रं कुर्याद् विचक्षणः।
बाह्याभित्तिर्भवेद् भागं भागमेकं प्रदक्षिणा।।८२।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

sabhittigarbhamānena bhadraṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ।
bāhyābhittirbhaved bhāgaṃ bhāgamekaṃ pradakṣiṇā।।82।।

**English Translation:**

By a measurement of the garbha of Bhitti (wall) the expert may create Bhadra. The outer wall may be of one part having part one as clockwise.

---

### Verse 60.83

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागिका गर्भभित्तिश्च गर्भमध्ये चतुष्पदम्।
पञ्चभागोन्मिता जङ्घा भागं तत्रैव मेखला।।८३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgikā garbhabhittiśca garbhamadhye catuṣpadam।
pañcabhāgonmitā jaṅghā bhāgaṃ tatraiva mekhalā।।83।।

**English Translation:**

A single parted may be the internal wall, being of four parts in the middle of the garbha.

[FLAG: English continues on next page]


[FLAG: English continuation from previous page]

The Jaṅghā may be of the measurement of five parts and Mekhalā there of one part.

---

### Verse 60.84

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कर्तव्यं भागिकं शृङ्गमण्डकं चार्धभागिकम्।
द्वितीयं साण्डकं शृङ्गं तत्समं पदमध्यगम्॥८४॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kartavyaṃ bhāgikaṃ śṛṅgamaṇḍakaṃ cārdhabhāgikam।
dvitīyaṃ sāṇḍakaṃ śṛṅgaṃ tatsamaṃ padamadhyagam॥84॥

**English Translation:**

The Śṛṅga (roof) is to be created as of one part, and Aṇḍaka (cupola) of one and a half part. The second Śṛṅga with an aṇḍaka similar to that projected to a pada.

---

### Verse 60.85

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

मल्लच्छाद्यं विधातव्यमुत्सेधेनार्धभागिकम्।
पादोत्सेधं च शिखरं गर्भभित्तिसमं भवेत्॥८५॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

mallacchādyaṃ vidhātavyamutsedhenārdhabhāgikam।
pādotsedhāṃ ca śikharaṃ garbhabhittisaṃaṃ bhavet॥85॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: पादोत्सेधं — could be pādotsedhāṃ or pādotsedhāṃ; second line ending भवेत् is clear]

**English Translation:**

Mallacchādya deserves to be created as half parted inline with elevation and Śikhara of the elevation of a pada equal in norm to the garbha bhitti.

---

### Verse 60.86

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागिक: कलश: कार्यो ध्वजशं + स्य तत्सम:।
एतस्य मूलशिखरं कुर्यात् षड्भागविस्तृतम्॥८६॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgikaḥ kalaśaḥ kāryo dhvajaśaṃ + sya tatsaṃaḥ।
etasya mūlaśikharaṃ kuryāt ṣaḍbhāgavistṛtam॥86॥

[FLAG: ध्वजशं + स्य — the + sign appears in the printed text itself, likely indicating a lacuna or scribal mark; retained as printed]

**English Translation:**

The Kalaśa is to be created as of one part and the diagonal (vaṃśa) of the Dhvaja of the same size. The mūlaśikhara of this one, one may create as of breadth of six parts.

> **Footnote 1:** GOSE, p. 465, verse 86, pāda 2. Vide fn. 1 has ध्वजवंशश्च तत्सम:।

> **Footnote 2:** Dhvaja stambha = Dhvaja vaṃśa – free pillars erected generally by the worshippers of Śiva or a pillar decorated with banner or flags on the top. (EOHA, p.263).

---

### Verse 60.87

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

भागाधिकसमुत्सेधं कार्यं कल्याणमिच्छता।
ऊर्ध्वं तिलकशृङ्गस्य शिखरं स्यात् पदोच्छ्रितम्॥८७॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

bhāgādhikasamutsedhāṃ kāryaṃ kalyāṇamicchatā।
ūrdhvaṃ tilakaśṛṅgasya śikharaṃ syāt padocchritam॥87॥

**English Translation:**

By one cherishing beneficence, this one deserves to be created as having elevation of an additional part and the Śikhara at the top (i.e. roof at the top) may be elevated by a pada.

---

### Verse 60.88

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

पञ्चाशद्विस्तृतं(?) शेषं श्रीवत्सस्येव कारयेत्।
इत्येष कथितः सम्यगुज्जयन्तोऽभिधानतः॥८८॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

pañcāśadvistṛtaṃ(?) śeṣaṃ śrīvatsasyeva kārayet।
ityeṣa kathitaḥ samyagujjayanto'bhidhānataḥ॥88॥

**English Translation:**

The Śeṣa broad by five digits³ one may create as that of Śrīvatsa. This way has been spoken of correctly Ujjayanta by nomenclature.

> **Footnote 3:** पञ्चांशिवस्तृतं शेषं pāda 1 of verse 87, fn. 3, p. 465.

---

### Verse 60.89

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कार्योऽयं सर्वदेवस्य प्रासादः शुभलक्षणः।
उज्जयन्तः॥
चित्रकूटा(द) यथोत्पन्नाः प्रासादा विमलादयः॥८९॥

**IAST Transliteration:**

kāryo'yaṃ sarvadevasya prāsādaḥ śubhalaṃkṣaṇaḥ।
ujjayantaḥ॥
citrakūṭā(d) yathotpannāḥ prāsādā vimalādayaḥ॥89॥

[FLAG: IAST UNCERTAIN: चित्रकूटा(द) — the (द) appears in the printed text as a bracketed syllable, indicating a scribal/editorial insertion; retained as printed]

**English Translation:**

Should be created this Prāsāda of Sarvadeva or all the gods or invariably⁴ of auspicious marks. Sprung out of Citrakūṭa are the Prāsādas Vimala and the like the way–

> **Footnote 4:** GOSE, p. 465, verse 89, pāda 1, fn. 4 has सर्वदेव स्यात्।

### Verse 60.90

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

उज्जयन्तात् तथा पञ्च मेरुप्रभृतयो मताः।
मेरुश्च मन्दरश्चैव कैलासः कुम्भ एव च॥९०॥

meruśca mandaraścaiva kailāsaḥ kumbha eva ca॥90॥

### Verse 60.91

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

गृहराज इति प्रोक्ता: प्रासादास्ते सुलक्षणा:।
अशेत्तरिमहोहिष्टं प्रासादानां शतं बुधै:।।९ १।।

[FLAG: The second line reading "अशेत्तरिमहोहिष्टं" is uncertain — likely "अशीत्युत्तरमहोहिष्टं" or similar; read as closely as possible from image.]

gṛharāja iti proktāḥ prāsādāste sulakṣaṇāḥ ।
aśettarimahohiṣṭaṃ prāsādānāṃ śataṃ budhaiḥ ॥91॥

**English Translation:**

Gṛharāja this way illustrated as such the Prāsādas these are of auspicious marks and eight added to a hundred of Prāsādas has been illustrated by the wise ones (i.e. experts) here.

---

### Verse 60.92

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ज्येष्ठमध्यकनिष्ठानां साधाराणां तथैव च।
तेष्वलिन्दैर्युता: केचिद् भद्रै: केचिच्च वेष्टिता:।।९ २।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jyeṣṭhamadhyakaniṣṭhānāṃ sādhāraṇānāṃ tathaiva ca ।
teṣvalindairyutāḥ kecid bhadraiḥ kecicca veṣṭitāḥ ॥92॥

**English Translation:**

Of larger, middle ones and smaller ones, and also of those equipped with supports (Sēdhāras). Among them some are equipped with balconies and some surrounded by the Bhadras (porticos or mouldings)¹.

> **Footnote 1:** EOHA, p.407 on Bhadra or medium projection on plan (Prāsādaśikhara, p.152).

---

### Verse 60.93

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

केचिद् वर्णसमा: कार्या: प्रासादा: सर्वशोभना:।
सर्वेऽप्येते विधातव्या (रिज?)² भागप्रतिष्ठिता:।।९ ३।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

kecid varṇasamāḥ kāryāḥ prāsādāḥ sarvaśobhanāḥ ।
sarve'pyete vidhātavyā (rija?)² bhāgapratiṣṭhitāḥ ॥93॥

**English Translation:**

Some having similar colours or of similar normed for the castes, deserve to be created the Prāsādas auspicious ones in all respects. All these deserve to be created straight ones established in parts.

> **Footnote 2:** ऋजुभागप्रतिष्ठिता:।

---

### Verse 60.94

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

कोणा न विषमा: कार्या वर्गभेदश्च नेष्यते।
एकहस्ता द्विहस्ता ++++ ये प्रकीर्तिता:।।९ ४।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

koṇā na viṣamāḥ kāryā vargabhedaśca neṣyate ।
ekahastā dvihastā ++++ ye prakīrtitāḥ ॥94॥

**English Translation:**

The Koṇas are not to be created as of dissimilar structure and the distinction of vargas is not cherished. One cubit normed and two cubit normed are these that are illustrated as such.

---

### Verse 60.95

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

यक्षनारागृहादीनां(?) रक्षसां च भवन्ति ते।
भागेन (धूम: स्तु?) त्रेधा विनिर्दिशेत्।।९ ५।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

yakṣanārāgṛhādīnāṃ (?) rakṣasāṃ ca bhavanti te ।
bhāgena (dhūmaḥ stu?) tredhā vinirdiśet ॥95॥

[IAST UNCERTAIN: "नारागृहादीनां" and "धूम: स्तु?" are uncertain readings from the image.]

**English Translation:**

Of Yakṣas and naras and the like and of Rākṣasas, the houses that happen to be, by a part as triple seated ones they may be organised.

---

### Verse 60.96

**Sanskrit (Devanagari):**

ज्येष्ठं मध्यं कनीयश्च ज्ञेयं तच्चांशमानत:।
ज्येष्ठं साधित्रिहस्तं स्यात् त्रिहस्तं मध्यमं विदु:।।९ ६।।

**IAST Transliteration:**

jyeṣṭhaṃ madhyaṃ kanīyaśca jñeyaṃ taccāṃśamānataḥ ।
jyeṣṭhaṃ sādhitrihastaṃ syāt trihastaṃ madhyamaṃ viduḥ ॥96॥

**English Translation:**

The larger one, the middle one and the smaller one, deserves to be recognised by the measurement of a digit. The larger one may be of 3½ cubits and the middle one of 3 cubits recognised as such.
